    or (syn and syn.queue_on_teleport)

    end
end)

local function FM_queueReloadAfterTeleport()
    if typeof(FM_QUEUE_TP) ~= "function" then
        return false
    end
    local payload = [[
if not game:IsLoaded() then game.Loaded:Wait() end
task.wait(0.5)
local file = "FayyMeng_MineAMountain.lua"
local ok, src = false, nil
if typeof(readfile) == "function" then
    ok, src = pcall(readfile, file)
end
if ok and type(src) == "string" and #src > 100 then
    local fn, err = loadstring(src)
    if fn then
        pcall(fn)
    end
elseif typeof(getgenv) == "function" and type(getgenv().FayyMeng_SRC) == "string" and #getgenv().FayyMeng_SRC > 100 then
    local fn = loadstring(getgenv().FayyMeng_SRC)
    if fn then pcall(fn) end
end
]]
    local ok = pcall(FM_QUEUE_TP, payload)
    return ok == true
end

local function FM_setNoCameraShake(enabled)
    if type(S) ~= "table" then
        return
    end
    S.noCameraShake = enabled == true

    local SHAKE_STEPS = {
        "BigCrystalShake",
        "CameraShaker",
        "CameraShakerUpdate",
        "CameraShakerUpdate", 
    }

    local function unbindAllShake()
        for _, name in ipairs(SHAKE_STEPS) do
            pcall(function()
                RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep(name)
            end)
        end
    end

    local function blockShakeRemote(remote)
        if not remote then return end
        pcall(function()
            if typeof(getconnections) == "function" then
                for _, c in ipairs(getconnections(remote.OnClientEvent)) do
                    pcall(function()
                        if c.Disable then c:Disable() end
                    end)
                    pcall(function()
                        if c.Disconnect then c:Disconnect() end
                    end)
                end
            end
        end)
        pcall(function()
            if remote:IsA("RemoteEvent") and not remote:GetAttribute("FayyMengShakeBlocked") then
                remote:SetAttribute("FayyMengShakeBlocked", true)
                remote.OnClientEvent:Connect(function()
                end)
            end
        end)
    end

    local function disableShakeScripts()
        local roots = {}
        pcall(function()
            local ps = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerScripts")
            if ps then roots[#roots + 1] = ps end
        end)
        pcall(function()
            local pg = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
            if pg then roots[#roots + 1] = pg end
        end)
        pcall(function()
            roots[#roots + 1] = ReplicatedStorage
        end)
        pcall(function()
            local sp = game:GetService("StarterPlayer")
            local sps = sp and sp:FindFirstChild("StarterPlayerScripts")
            if sps then roots[#roots + 1] = sps end
        end)

        for _, root in ipairs(roots) do
            pcall(function()
                for _, inst in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
                    local n = inst.Name
                    if type(n) == "string" then
                        local lower = string.lower(n)
                        local isShake = lower:find("camerashake", 1, true)
                            or lower:find("shakecontroller", 1, true)
                            or n == "Shake"
                            or n == "CameraShaker"
                            or n == "CameraShakeInstance"
                            or n == "CameraShakePresets"
                            or n == "CameraShakeController"
                        if isShake and (inst:IsA("LocalScript") or inst:IsA("Script") or inst:IsA("ModuleScript")) then
                            if inst:IsA("LocalScript") or inst:IsA("Script") then
                                pcall(function()
                                    inst.Disabled = true
                                end)
                            end
                        end
                        if isShake and inst:IsA("LocalScript") then
                            pcall(function()
                                inst.Disabled = true
                            end)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
    end

    local function hookCameraShake()
        unbindAllShake()
        blockShakeRemote((ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Remotes") or ReplicatedStorage):FindFirstChild("BigCrystalShake"))
        blockShakeRemote(ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("BigCrystalShake", true))
        disableShakeScripts()
    end

    if S.noCameraShake then
        pcall(hookCameraShake)

        if S._shakeGuardConn then
            pcall(function() S._shakeGuardConn:Disconnect() end)
            S._shakeGuardConn = nil
        end
        if S._shakeHeartbeatConn then
            pcall(function() S._shakeHeartbeatConn:Disconnect() end)
            S._shakeHeartbeatConn = nil
        end

        local shakeAcc = 0
        S._shakeGuardConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
            if not S.noCameraShake then return end
            shakeAcc += dt
            if shakeAcc < 0.4 then return end
            shakeAcc = 0
            unbindAllShake()
            pcall(function()
                local remotes = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
                blockShakeRemote(remotes and remotes:FindFirstChild("BigCrystalShake"))
            end)
        end)
        S._shakeHeartbeatConn = nil

        if not S._shakeScriptWatch then
            pcall(function()
                local ps = LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerScripts", 5)
                if ps then
                    S._shakeScriptWatch = ps.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
                        if not S.noCameraShake then return end
                        task.defer(function()
                            disableShakeScripts()
                            unbindAllShake()
                        end)
                    end)
                end
            end)
        end
    else
        if S._shakeGuardConn then
            pcall(function() S._shakeGuardConn:Disconnect() end)
            S._shakeGuardConn = nil
        end
        if S._shakeHeartbeatConn then
            pcall(function() S._shakeHeartbeatConn:Disconnect() end)
            S._shakeHeartbeatConn = nil
        end
        if S._shakeScriptWatch then
            pcall(function() S._shakeScriptWatch:Disconnect() end)
            S._shakeScriptWatch = nil
        end
    end
end

task.defer(function()
    local tries = 0
    while tries < 80 do
        tries += 1
        if type(S) == "table" and type(FM_setNoCameraShake) == "function" then
            pcall(function()
                FM_setNoCameraShake(true)
            end)
            break
        end
        task.wait(0.1)
    end
end)

local afkConns = {}
local afkRunning = true

do
    local VirtualUser = game:GetService("VirtualUser")

    local function silenceIdle()
        local ok, list = pcall(function()
            return getconnections(LocalPlayer.Idled)
        end)

        if not ok or type(list) ~= "table" then
            return
        end

        for _, connection in ipairs(list) do
            pcall(function()
                connection:Disable()
            end)
        end
    end

    local function nudge()
        pcall(function()
            VirtualUser:CaptureController()
            VirtualUser:ClickButton2(Vector3.new())
        end)
    end

    silenceIdle()

    pcall(function()
        local idled = LocalPlayer.Idled
        if idled then
            afkConns[#afkConns + 1] = idled:Connect(nudge)
        end
    end)

    task.spawn(function()
        while afkRunning do
            task.wait(60)

            if not afkRunning or not LocalPlayer.Parent then
                break
            end

            pcall(silenceIdle)
            pcall(nudge)
        end
    end)
end

function FM.resolveGuiRoot()
    local ok, hidden = pcall(function()
        return gethui()
    end)
    if ok and typeof(hidden) == "Instance" then
        return hidden
    end

    local playerGui = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
    if playerGui then
        return playerGui
    end

    return LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui") or CoreGui
end

local GuiRoot = FM.resolveGuiRoot()
print("GuiRoot ready")

for _, container in ipairs({ GuiRoot, CoreGui }) do
    for _, name in ipairs({ "FayyMengESPGui", "FayyMengCrystalEsp" }) do
        pcall(function()
            local existing = container:FindFirstChild(name)
            if existing then
                existing:Destroy()
            end
        end)
    end
end

local _remoteCache = {}
local _remoteFolder = nil
local _remoteFolderAt = 0
local _remoteMissAt = {}

function FM.clearRemoteCache()
    table.clear(_remoteCache)
    if _remoteMissAt then table.clear(_remoteMissAt) end
    _remoteFolder = nil
end

function FM.findRemote(name)
    name = tostring(name or "")
    if name == "" then
        return nil
    end
    local now = os.clock()
    local cached = _remoteCache[name]
    if cached == false then
        local missAt = (_remoteMissAt and _remoteMissAt[name]) or 0
        if (now - missAt) < 1.5 then
            return nil
        end
        _remoteCache[name] = nil
        cached = nil
    elseif typeof(cached) == "Instance" then
        if cached.Parent then
            return cached
        end
        _remoteCache[name] = nil
    end
    if not _remoteFolder or not _remoteFolder.Parent or (now - (_remoteFolderAt or 0)) > 30 then
        _remoteFolder = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
        if not _remoteFolder then
            _remoteFolder = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Remotes", 2)
        end
        _remoteFolderAt = now
    end
    if not _remoteFolder then
        _remoteMissAt = _remoteMissAt or {}
        _remoteMissAt[name] = now
        _remoteCache[name] = false
        return nil
    end
    local r = _remoteFolder:FindFirstChild(name)
    if not r then
        r = _remoteFolder:WaitForChild(name, 0.75)
    end
    if r then
        _remoteCache[name] = r
        if _remoteMissAt then _remoteMissAt[name] = nil end
        return r
    end
    _remoteMissAt = _remoteMissAt or {}
    _remoteMissAt[name] = now
    _remoteCache[name] = false
    return nil
end

local FluentLibrary
do
    local ok, lib = pcall(function()
        return loadstring(game:HttpGetAsync("https://github.com/ActualMasterOogway/Fluent-Renewed/releases/latest/download/Fluent.luau"))()
    end)
    if not ok or type(lib) ~= "table" then
        ok, lib = pcall(function()
            return loadstring(game:HttpGet("https://github.com/ActualMasterOogway/Fluent-Renewed/releases/latest/download/Fluent.luau"))()
        end)
    end
    if not ok or type(lib) ~= "table" then
        warn("Fluent Renewed load FAILED:", lib)
        return
    end
    FluentLibrary = lib
    print("Fluent loaded")
end

local FluentWindow
do
    local isMobile = false
    pcall(function()
        isMobile = UserInputService.TouchEnabled and not UserInputService.KeyboardEnabled
    end)
    
    local winSize = isMobile and UDim2.fromOffset(560, 430) or UDim2.fromOffset(700, 480)
    local okWin, winOrErr = pcall(function()
        return FluentLibrary:CreateWindow({
            Title = "Mine A Mountain",
            SubTitle = "FayyMeng",
            TabWidth = isMobile and 120 or 140,
            Size = winSize,
            Resize = true,
            MinSize = Vector2.new(460, 360),
            Acrylic = false,
            Theme = "Dark",
            MinimizeKey = Enum.KeyCode.RightControl,
            
            Mobile = {
                Size = UDim2.fromOffset(44, 44),
                GetIcon = function(isMinimized)
                    local off = Vector2.new(0, 0)
                    local size = Vector2.new(48, 48)
                    return {
                        Image = "rbxassetid://10734897102",
                        ImageRectOffset = off,
                        ImageRectSize = size,
                        ImageRectOffsetX = 0,
                        ImageRectOffsetY = 0,
                        ImageRectSizeX = 48,
                        ImageRectSizeY = 48,
                    }
                end,
            },
        })
    end)
    if okWin then
        FluentWindow = winOrErr
        print("CreateWindow OK")
        pcall(function()
            if getgenv then getgenv().FayyMeng_FluentWindow = FluentWindow end
        end)
        
        task.defer(function()
            pcall(function()
                local gui = FluentLibrary.GUI or FluentLibrary.WindowFrame
                if not gui then
                    local ok, g = pcall(function()
                        return FluentLibrary.CreatedWindow and FluentLibrary.CreatedWindow.Root
                    end)
                    if ok then gui = g end
                end
                
                local frame = nil
                pcall(function()
                    if FluentLibrary.CreatedWindow and FluentLibrary.CreatedWindow.Root then
                        frame = FluentLibrary.CreatedWindow.Root
                    end
                end)
                pcall(function()
                    if not frame and FluentWindow and FluentWindow.Root then
                        frame = FluentWindow.Root
                    end
                end)
                if frame and frame:IsA("GuiObject") then
                    frame.Size = winSize
                end
            end)
        end)
    else
        warn("CreateWindow FAILED:", winOrErr)
        return
    end
end

do
    local guiVisible = true
    local parentGui = nil
    pcall(function()
        if typeof(gethui) == "function" then
            parentGui = gethui()
        end
    end)
    if not parentGui then
        parentGui = (LocalPlayer and LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")) or CoreGui
    end

    pcall(function()
        local oldBtn = parentGui and parentGui:FindFirstChild("FayyMeng_GuiToggle")
        if oldBtn then oldBtn:Destroy() end
    end)

    local screen = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    screen.Name = "FayyMeng_GuiToggle"
    screen.ResetOnSpawn = false
    screen.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
    screen.DisplayOrder = 1000
    screen.IgnoreGuiInset = true
    screen.Parent = parentGui

    
    local EYE_OPEN = "rbxassetid://7734053495"   
    local EYE_CLOSED = "rbxassetid://7734057141" 
    
    pcall(function() end)

    local btn = Instance.new("ImageButton")
    btn.Name = "ToggleBtn"
    btn.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(48, 48)
    btn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -62, 0.42, 0)
    btn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 35)
    btn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.2
    btn.BorderSizePixel = 0
    btn.Image = EYE_OPEN
    btn.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit
    btn.Parent = screen

    local corner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
    corner.Parent = btn

    local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(100, 100, 120)
    stroke.Thickness = 1.2
    stroke.Parent = btn

    
    local dragging, dragStart, startPos = false, nil, nil
    btn.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
            or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            dragging = true
            dragStart = input.Position
            startPos = btn.Position
            input.Changed:Connect(function()
                if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
                    dragging = false
                end
            end)
        end
    end)
    UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if not dragging then return end
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
            or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            local d = input.Position - dragStart
            btn.Position = UDim2.new(
                startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + d.X,
                startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + d.Y
            )
        end
    end)

    local function setVisible(vis)
        guiVisible = vis == true
        btn.Image = guiVisible and EYE_OPEN or EYE_CLOSED

        
        pcall(function()
            if FluentLibrary.Minimize and guiVisible == false then
                FluentLibrary:Minimize()
                return
            end
        end)
        pcall(function()
            if FluentWindow and FluentWindow.Minimize and guiVisible == false then
                FluentWindow:Minimize()
            end
        end)

        pcall(function()
            if FluentLibrary.GUI then
                FluentLibrary.GUI.Enabled = guiVisible
            end
        end)
        pcall(function()
            local cw = FluentLibrary.CreatedWindow
            if cw then
                if cw.Root then cw.Root.Visible = guiVisible end
                if cw.Frame then cw.Frame.Visible = guiVisible end
            end
        end)
        pcall(function()
            if FluentWindow then
                if FluentWindow.Root then FluentWindow.Root.Visible = guiVisible end
                if FluentWindow.Frame then FluentWindow.Frame.Visible = guiVisible end
            end
        end)
        
        local function scan(container)
            if not container then return end
            for _, g in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
                if g:IsA("ScreenGui") and g.Name ~= "FayyMeng_GuiToggle"
                    and g.Name ~= "FayyMengESPGui" and g.Name ~= "FayyMengCrystalEsp" then
                    local n = string.lower(g.Name)
                    if n:find("fluent", 1, true) or n:find("library", 1, true) then
                        g.Enabled = guiVisible
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        pcall(function() scan(parentGui) end)
        pcall(function() scan(CoreGui) end)
    end

    local lastClick, pressPos = 0, nil
    btn.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
            or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            pressPos = input.Position
        end
    end)
    btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        if pressPos and dragStart then
            local moved = (pressPos - dragStart).Magnitude
            if moved > 14 then return end
        end
        local now = os.clock()
        if now - lastClick < 0.2 then return end
        lastClick = now
        setVisible(not guiVisible)
    end)

    FM.setGuiVisible = setVisible
    FM.isGuiVisible = function()
        return guiVisible
    end
end

local FluentOptions = FluentLibrary.Options or {}

local function FluentNotify(opts)
    opts = opts or {}
    local s = string.lower(tostring(opts.Title or "") .. " " .. tostring(opts.Content or ""))
    if s:find("hidden", 1, true) or s:find("reopen the interface", 1, true) then
        return
    end
    pcall(function()
        FluentLibrary:Notify({
            Title = tostring(opts.Title or "FayyMeng"),
            Content = tostring(opts.Content or ""),
            Duration = tonumber(opts.Duration) or 3,
        })
    end)
end

local WindUI = {
    Notify = function(_, opts)
        FluentNotify(opts)
    end,
}

local FLUENT_TAB_ICONS = {
    ESP = "eye",
    Boulder = "mountain",
    Money = "coins",
    Pickup = "package",
    Dig = "pickaxe",
    Runes = "sparkles",
    Shop = "store",
    Movement = "footprints",
    Teleports = "map-pin",
    Misc = "wrench",
    Button = "touchpad",
    Vein = "gem",
    Fav = "star",
    Suggestions = "message-circle",
    Settings = "settings",
    Config = "save",
}

local _fluentId = 0
local function fluentFlag(prefix, title)
    
    if type(title) == "string" and title ~= "" then
        local slug = title:gsub("[^%w]+", "_"):gsub("^_+", ""):gsub("_+$", "")
        if slug ~= "" then
            return (prefix or "fm") .. "_" .. slug
        end
    end
    _fluentId += 1
    return (prefix or "fm") .. tostring(_fluentId)
end

local function wrapTab(fluentTab)
    local T = { _raw = fluentTab, _fluent = fluentTab }

    function T:Section(o)
        o = o or {}
        local title = (type(o) == "string" and o) or (o.Title or "Section")
        pcall(function()
            fluentTab:CreateParagraph(fluentFlag("sec"), {
                Title = tostring(title),
                Content = " ",
            })
        end)
        return true
    end

    function T:Button(o)
        o = o or {}
        pcall(function()
            fluentTab:CreateButton({
                Title = o.Title or "Button",
                Description = o.Desc or o.Description or "",
                Callback = o.Callback or function() end,
            })
        end)
        return { Lock = function() end, Unlock = function() end, SetLocked = function() end }
    end

    function T:Toggle(o)
        o = o or {}
        local flag = o.Flag or o.Index or fluentFlag("tg", o.Title)
        local obj
        pcall(function()
            obj = fluentTab:CreateToggle(flag, {
                Title = o.Title or "Toggle",
                Description = o.Desc or o.Description or "",
                Default = o.Value == true,
                Callback = function(v)
                    if type(o.Callback) == "function" then
                        o.Callback(v == true)
                    end
                end,
            })
        end)
        return {
            Set = function(_, v)
                pcall(function()
                    if FluentOptions[flag] and FluentOptions[flag].SetValue then
                        FluentOptions[flag]:SetValue(v == true)
                    elseif obj and obj.SetValue then
                        obj:SetValue(v == true)
                    end
                end)
            end,
            Get = function()
                local opt = FluentOptions[flag]
                return opt and opt.Value == true
            end,
        }
    end

    function T:Slider(o)
        o = o or {}
        local flag = o.Flag or o.Index or fluentFlag("sl", o.Title)
        local min = (o.Value and o.Value.Min) or 0
        local max = (o.Value and o.Value.Max) or 100
        local def = (o.Value and o.Value.Default) or min
        local flag = flag
        local obj
        pcall(function()
            obj = fluentTab:CreateSlider(flag, {
                Title = o.Title or "Slider",
                Description = o.Desc or o.Description or "",
                Default = def,
                Min = min,
                Max = max,
                Rounding = 1,
                Callback = function(v)
                    if type(o.Callback) == "function" then
                        o.Callback(v)
                    end
                end,
            })
        end)
        return {
            Set = function(_, v)
                pcall(function()
                    if obj and obj.SetValue then obj:SetValue(v) end
                end)
            end,
            Get = function() return def end,
        }
    end

    function T:Input(o)
        o = o or {}
        local flag = o.Flag or o.Index or fluentFlag("in", o.Title)
        local obj
        pcall(function()
            obj = fluentTab:CreateInput(flag, {
                Title = o.Title or "Input",
                Default = tostring(o.Value or ""),
                Placeholder = o.Placeholder or "...",
                Numeric = false,
                Finished = true,
                Callback = function(v)
                    if type(o.Callback) == "function" then
                        o.Callback(v)
                    end
                end,
            })
        end)
        return {
            Set = function(_, v)
                pcall(function()
                    if obj and obj.SetValue then obj:SetValue(tostring(v)) end
                end)
            end,
            Get = function() return o.Value end,
        }
    end

    function T:Dropdown(o)
        o = o or {}
        local flag = o.Flag or o.Index or fluentFlag("dd", o.Title)
        local values = o.Values or {}
        local multi = o.Multi == true or o.MultipleOptions == true
        local default = o.Value
        if multi then
            if type(default) ~= "table" then default = {} end
        else
            if type(default) == "table" then
                default = default[1] or values[1]
            elseif default == nil or default == "" then
                default = values[1]
            end
        end
        local obj
        pcall(function()
            obj = fluentTab:CreateDropdown(flag, {
                Title = o.Title or "Dropdown",
                Description = o.Desc or o.Description or "",
                Values = values,
                Multi = multi,
                Default = default,
                Callback = function(v)
                    if type(o.Callback) == "function" then
                        o.Callback(v)
                    end
                end,
            })
        end)
        return {
            Set = function(_, v)
                pcall(function()
                    if obj and obj.SetValue then obj:SetValue(v) end
                end)
            end,
            Get = function() return default end,
        }
    end

    function T:Paragraph(o)
        o = o or {}
        local title = o.Title or "Info"
        local desc = o.Content or o.Desc or ""
        local flag = fluentFlag("pg")
        local para
        pcall(function()
            para = fluentTab:CreateParagraph(flag, {
                Title = tostring(title),
                Content = tostring(desc),
            })
        end)
        local holder = { Title = title, Desc = desc, Content = desc, _para = para }
        function holder:SetText(text)
            text = tostring(text or "")
            self.Desc = text
            self.Content = text
            pcall(function()
                if self._para and self._para.SetValue then
                    self._para:SetValue(text)
                end
            end)
        end
        return holder
    end

    function T:CreateSection(title)
        return self:Section({ Title = title })
    end

    return T
end

local function createTabSafe(name)
    local icon = FLUENT_TAB_ICONS[name] or "circle"
    local ok, tab = pcall(function()
        return FluentWindow:CreateTab({ Title = name, Icon = icon })
    end)
    if ok and tab then return tab end
    ok, tab = pcall(function()
        return FluentWindow:CreateTab({ Title = name })
    end)
    if ok and tab then return tab end
    warn("CreateTab failed for", name)
    return nil
end

local Window = {}
local LunaWindow = FluentWindow
local Luna = FluentLibrary

function Window:Tab(opts)
    opts = opts or {}
    local name = opts.Title or "Tab"
    local tab = createTabSafe(name)
    if not tab then
        return wrapTab({
            CreateParagraph = function() end,
            CreateButton = function() end,
            CreateToggle = function() end,
            CreateSlider = function() end,
            CreateInput = function() end,
            CreateDropdown = function() end,
        })
    end
    return wrapTab(tab)
end

function Window:Destroy()
    pcall(function()
        if FluentLibrary.Destroy then FluentLibrary:Destroy() end
    end)
end

function Window:Notify(opts)
    FluentNotify(opts)
end

    end
end)

local FeatureManager = {
    features = {},
    order = {},
}

-- Multi-owner movement gate: several farm features may share noclip without
-- one Release/stop killing another's ownership (Pickup never acquires movement).
local MovementOwner = { owners = {}, owner = nil }

function MovementOwner:Acquire(name)
    if type(name) ~= "string" or name == "" then return false end
    self.owners = self.owners or {}
    self.owners[name] = true
    self.owner = name -- last acquirer (compat with single-owner checks)
    return true
end

function MovementOwner:Release(name)
    if type(name) ~= "string" then return false end
    self.owners = self.owners or {}
    local had = self.owners[name] == true
    self.owners[name] = nil
    if self.owner == name then
        self.owner = next(self.owners)
    end
    return had
end

function MovementOwner:Is(name)
    if type(name) ~= "string" then return false end
    if self.owners and self.owners[name] then return true end
    return self.owner == name
end

function MovementOwner:HasAny()
    if self.owners then
        if next(self.owners) ~= nil then return true end
    end
    return self.owner ~= nil
end

function MovementOwner:Clear()
    self.owners = {}
    self.owner = nil
end

FM.MovementOwner = MovementOwner

-- Respawn: never leave new character stuck in previous NoClip state
pcall(function()
    LocalPlayer.CharacterRemoving:Connect(function()
        pcall(function()
            if Move and Move.glideStop then Move.glideStop() end
        end)
    end)
    LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
        task.defer(function()
            if MovementOwner and MovementOwner:HasAny() then
                pcall(function()
                    if Move and Move.setNoclip then Move.setNoclip(true, false) end
                end)
            else
                pcall(function()
                    if Move and Move.setNoclip then Move.setNoclip(false, false) end
                end)
            end
        end)
    end)
end)

-- ============================================================================
-- SYSTEM REGISTRY — isolated diagnostics (no cross-stop)
-- ============================================================================
FM.SystemRegistry = FM.SystemRegistry or {
    names = { "Boulder", "Money", "BoulderPickup", "MoneyPickup", "RunePickup", "AutoDig", "AutoVein", "AutoFav" },
}

function FM.getSystemDiagnostics()
    local out = {}
    local function row(name, sys)
        if not sys then
            out[name] = { present = false }
            return
        end
        out[name] = {
            present = true,
            Enabled = sys.Enabled == true,
            Active = sys.Active == true,
            Session = tonumber(sys.Session) or 0,
            Target = sys.Target ~= nil,
            Connections = type(sys.Connections) == "table" and #sys.Connections or 0,
            Status = sys.Runtime and sys.Runtime.status or nil,
        }
    end
    row("Boulder", FM.BoulderSystem)
    row("Money", FM.MoneySystem)
    row("BoulderPickup", FM.BoulderPickupSystem)
    row("MoneyPickup", FM.MoneyPickupSystem)
    row("RunePickup", FM.RunePickupSystem)
    out.MovementOwners = {}
    if MovementOwner and MovementOwner.owners then
        for k, v in pairs(MovementOwner.owners) do
            if v then out.MovementOwners[#out.MovementOwners + 1] = k end
        end
    end
    out.Flags = {
        boulderFarmActive = S.boulderFarmActive == true,
        moneyFarmActive = S.moneyFarmActive == true,
        autoFavActive = S.autoFavActive == true,
        fastDigActive = S.fastDigActive == true,
    }
    return out
end

function FM.printSystemDiagnostics()
    local d = FM.getSystemDiagnostics()
    print("[Systems] Movement owners:", table.concat(d.MovementOwners or {}, ", "))
    for _, name in ipairs({ "Boulder", "Money", "BoulderPickup", "MoneyPickup", "RunePickup" }) do
        local r = d[name]
        if r and r.present then
            print(string.format(
                "[Systems] %s enabled=%s active=%s session=%s conns=%s target=%s status=%s",
                name, tostring(r.Enabled), tostring(r.Active), tostring(r.Session),
                tostring(r.Connections), tostring(r.Target), tostring(r.Status)
            ))
        end
    end
end


local function FM_safeCall(featureName, phase, fn, ...)
    if type(fn) ~= "function" then return true end
    local ok, err = pcall(fn, ...)
    if not ok then
        warn(string.format("[Feature:%s] %s failed: %s", tostring(featureName), tostring(phase), tostring(err)))
        return false, err
    end
    return true
end

function FeatureManager:Register(name, feature)
    if type(name) ~= "string" or name == "" or type(feature) ~= "table" then return end
    feature.name = name
    feature.connections = feature.connections or {}
    feature.session = tonumber(feature.session) or 0
    feature.enabled = feature.enabled == true
    feature.active = feature.active == true
    if not self.features[name] then
        self.order[#self.order + 1] = name
    end
    self.features[name] = feature
end

function FeatureManager:Get(name)
    return self.features[name]
end

function FeatureManager:Track(name, connection)
    local feature = self.features[name]
    if not feature or not connection then return connection end
    feature.connections[#feature.connections + 1] = connection
    return connection
end

function FeatureManager:DisconnectAll(name)
    local feature = self.features[name]
    if not feature then return end
    for i = #feature.connections, 1, -1 do
        local c = feature.connections[i]
        feature.connections[i] = nil
        pcall(function()
            if c and c.Disconnect then c:Disconnect() end
        end)
    end
    table.clear(feature.connections)
end

function FeatureManager:BumpSession(name)
    local feature = self.features[name]
    if not feature then return 0 end
    feature.session = (feature.session or 0) + 1
    return feature.session
end

function FeatureManager:IsCurrent(name, sessionId)
    local feature = self.features[name]
    return feature ~= nil and feature.session == sessionId
end

function FeatureManager:StopRuntime(name)
    local feature = self.features[name]
    if not feature then return end
    self:BumpSession(name)
    feature.active = false
    FM_safeCall(name, "StopRuntime", feature.StopRuntime or feature.Stop, feature)
    self:DisconnectAll(name)
    FM_safeCall(name, "Cleanup", feature.Cleanup, feature)
end

function FeatureManager:ResetRuntime(name)
    local feature = self.features[name]
    if not feature then return end
    FM_safeCall(name, "ResetRuntime", feature.ResetRuntime or feature.Reset, feature)
    feature.active = false
end

function FeatureManager:StartRuntime(name)
    local feature = self.features[name]
    if not feature then return end
    if not feature.enabled then return end
    local session = self:BumpSession(name)
    FM_safeCall(name, "Initialize", feature.Initialize, feature)
    FM_safeCall(name, "StartRuntime", feature.StartRuntime or feature.Start, feature, session)
    feature.active = true
end

function FeatureManager:SetEnabled(name, enabled)
    local feature = self.features[name]
    if not feature then return end
    enabled = enabled == true
    feature.enabled = enabled
    if enabled then
        self:ResetRuntime(name)
        self:StartRuntime(name)
    else
        self:StopRuntime(name)
        self:ResetRuntime(name)
    end
end

function FeatureManager:CaptureEnabled()
    local snap = {}
    for _, name in ipairs(self.order) do
        local f = self.features[name]
        if f then snap[name] = f.enabled == true end
    end
    return snap
end

function FeatureManager:RestoreEnabled(snap)
    if type(snap) ~= "table" then return end
    for _, name in ipairs(self.order) do
        local f = self.features[name]
        if f and snap[name] ~= nil then
            f.enabled = snap[name] == true
        end
    end
end

function FeatureManager:StopAllRuntime()
    for i = #self.order, 1, -1 do
        self:StopRuntime(self.order[i])
    end
end

function FeatureManager:ResetAllRuntime()
    for _, name in ipairs(self.order) do
        self:ResetRuntime(name)
    end
end

function FeatureManager:StartAllEnabled()
    for _, name in ipairs(self.order) do
        local f = self.features[name]
        if f and f.enabled then
            self:StartRuntime(name)
        end
    end
end

function FeatureManager:RefreshAll()
    local snap = self:CaptureEnabled()
    S._globalSession = (S._globalSession or 0) + 1
    self:StopAllRuntime()
    self:ResetAllRuntime()
    pcall(function()
        if FM.clearRemoteCache then FM.clearRemoteCache() end
    end)
    S._filterCandCache = nil
    S._veinDeposits = nil
    S._veinFarmTarget = nil
    S._veinLootPhase = false
    S._slowMove = false
    S.miningBoulder = nil
    S.farmActive = false
    S.boulderFarmActive = false
    S.moneyFarmActive = false
    S.rootPart = nil
    MovementOwner:Clear()
    pcall(function()
        if Move and Move.glideStop then Move.glideStop() end
        if Move and Move.forceClearFarmMovement then Move.forceClearFarmMovement() end
        if Move and Move.setFly then Move.setFly(false) end
        if Move and Move.setNoclip then Move.setNoclip(false, false) end
    end)
    task.wait(0.12)
    pcall(function()
        if FM.getRoot then FM.getRoot() end
    end)
    self:RestoreEnabled(snap)
    self:StartAllEnabled()
end

function FeatureManager:Stop(name) self:StopRuntime(name) end
function FeatureManager:Start(name) self:StartRuntime(name) end
function FeatureManager:Reset(name) self:ResetRuntime(name) end
function FeatureManager:StopAll() self:StopAllRuntime() end
function FeatureManager:ResetAll() self:ResetAllRuntime() end
function FeatureManager:StartEnabled() self:StartAllEnabled() end

FM.FeatureManager = FeatureManager

-- ============================================================================
-- ISOLATED SUBSYSTEMS (rebuild) — Boulder / Money / Pickup fully separated
-- ============================================================================
do
    local Players = game:GetService("Players")
    local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
    local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")
    local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

    local VALUE_PRESETS = {
        ["None"] = 0,
        ["100k"] = 1e5,
        ["500k"] = 5e5,
        ["1m"] = 1e6,
        ["5m"] = 5e6,
        ["10m"] = 1e7,
        ["50m"] = 5e7,
        ["100m"] = 1e8,
        ["1b"] = 1e9,
    }
    local LUCK_PRESETS = {
        ["None"] = 0,
        ["1%"] = 1,
        ["5%"] = 5,
        ["10%"] = 10,
        ["25%"] = 25,
        ["50%"] = 50,
        ["100%"] = 100,
    }
    local TIER_OPTIONS = {
        "All", "Common", "Uncommon", "Rare", "Epic", "Legendary", "Mythic",
        "Empyrean", "Pulsar", "Quasar",
    }
    local FARM_KINDS = { "Mossite", "Voltite", "Gildrite", "Rimeveil", "Nocturnite" }

    local function newConfig()
        return {
            PresetValue = "None",
            PresetLuck = "None",
            Tier = { All = true },
            MinValue = 0,
            MinLuck = 0,
            Targets = { Mossite = true, Voltite = true, Gildrite = true, Rimeveil = true, Nocturnite = true },
        }
    end

    local function bump(self)
        self.Session = (self.Session or 0) + 1
        return self.Session
    end

    local function disconnectAll(list)
        if type(list) ~= "table" then return end
        for i = #list, 1, -1 do
            local c = list[i]
            list[i] = nil
            pcall(function()
                if c and c.Disconnect then c:Disconnect() end
            end)
        end
    end

    local function track(self, conn)
        if not conn then return end
        self.Connections = self.Connections or {}
        self.Connections[#self.Connections + 1] = conn
        return conn
    end

    -- Shared movement multi-owner (feature-scoped names only)
    local Mov = FM.MovementOwner or { owners = {}, owner = nil }
    if not Mov.Acquire then
        Mov.owners = Mov.owners or {}
        function Mov:Acquire(n) self.owners[n] = true; self.owner = n; return true end
        function Mov:Release(n) self.owners[n] = nil; if self.owner == n then self.owner = next(self.owners) end end
        function Mov:HasAny() return next(self.owners or {}) ~= nil end
        function Mov:Clear() self.owners = {}; self.owner = nil end
        FM.MovementOwner = Mov
    end

    local function resolvePresetNumber(opt, presetMap)
        if type(opt) == "table" then
            opt = opt[1] or opt.Value or opt.Name or opt.Text or ""
        end
        opt = tostring(opt or ""):gsub("%s+", "")
        if opt == "" or opt == "None" or opt == "Off" then
            return 0, "None"
        end
        if presetMap and presetMap[opt] ~= nil then
            return tonumber(presetMap[opt]) or 0, opt
        end
        -- case-insensitive key match
        local lower = string.lower(opt)
        if presetMap then
            for k, v in pairs(presetMap) do
                if string.lower(tostring(k)) == lower then
                    return tonumber(v) or 0, tostring(k)
                end
            end
        end
        -- parse "50m" / "500k" style
        if FM.parseValue then
            local p = FM.parseValue(opt)
            if p then return p, opt end
        end
        local n = tonumber(opt:match("[%d%.]+"))
        return n or 0, opt
    end

    local function crystalPasses(cfg, inst)
        if not inst or not inst.Parent then return false end
        if FM.getAttr and FM.getAttr(inst, "Collected") == true then return false end
        if FM.isCrystal and not FM.isCrystal(inst) then return false end

        local value = 0
        pcall(function()
            value = tonumber(FM.crystalValue and FM.crystalValue(inst)) or 0
        end)
        if value <= 0 then
            local raw = FM.getAttr and FM.getAttr(inst, "Value")
            value = tonumber(raw) or 0
        end

        local luck = 0
        pcall(function()
            local l = FM.crystalLuck and FM.crystalLuck(inst) or 0
            luck = (tonumber(l) or 0) * 100
        end)

        local minV = tonumber(cfg and cfg.MinValue) or 0
        local minL = tonumber(cfg and cfg.MinLuck) or 0

        -- Strict: below minimum = reject
        if minV > 0 and value < minV then
            return false
        end
        if minL > 0 and luck < minL then
            return false
        end

        local tiers = cfg.Tier
        if type(tiers) == "table" and not tiers.All and next(tiers) then
            local tname = tostring(FM.getAttr and (FM.getAttr(inst, "TierName") or "") or "")
            local tierN = tonumber(FM.getAttr and FM.getAttr(inst, "Tier"))
            local ok = false
            if tname ~= "" and tiers[tname] then ok = true end
            if not ok and tierN and TIER_OPTIONS[tierN + 1] and tiers[TIER_OPTIONS[tierN + 1]] then ok = true end
            -- also map by index names
            if not ok then
                local names = TIER_OPTIONS
                for _, n in ipairs(names) do
                    if n ~= "All" and tiers[n] then
                        local idx = nil
                        for i, nn in ipairs(names) do if nn == n then idx = i - 1 end end
                        if idx and tierN == idx then ok = true end
                    end
                end
            end
            if not ok then return false end
        end
        return true
    end

    -- -------------------- BoulderPickupSystem --------------------
    local BoulderSystem, MoneySystem
    local BoulderPickupSystem = {
        Enabled = false,
        Active = false,
        Session = 0,
        Connections = {},
        Target = nil,
        Config = newConfig(),
        Runtime = { lastAt = 0 },
    }

    function BoulderPickupSystem:SetEnabled(on)
        on = on == true
        if self.Enabled == on and self.Active == on then return end
        self.Enabled = on
        if on then
            self:StartRuntime()
        else
            self:StopRuntime()
        end
    end

    function BoulderPickupSystem:StartRuntime()
        if not self.Enabled then return end
        if not FM.isWorldReady or not FM.isWorldReady() then
            FM.ensureWorldReadyThen(function()
                if self.Enabled then self:StartRuntime() end
            end)
            return
        end
        local sid = bump(self)
        self.Active = true
        disconnectAll(self.Connections)
        self.Connections = {}
        track(self, RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if not self.Enabled or not self.Active or self.Session ~= sid then return end
            -- Boulder pickup path only (does NOT touch Auto Fav)
            if not (BoulderSystem and BoulderSystem.Active) then return end
            local now = os.clock()
            if now - (self.Runtime.lastAt or 0) < 0.25 then return end
            self.Runtime.lastAt = now
            local root = FM.getRoot and FM.getRoot()
            if not root then return end
            local free = FM.backpackFree and FM.backpackFree() or 0
            if free <= 0 then return end
            local cfg = self.Config
            local minV = tonumber(cfg.MinValue) or 0
            local minL = tonumber(cfg.MinLuck) or 0
            local origin = root.Position
            local list = FM.pickupCandidates and FM.pickupCandidates(free, origin, function(inst)
                return crystalPasses(cfg, inst)
            end) or {}

            -- Prefer nearest matching crystal
            local best, bestD
            for _, entry in ipairs(list) do
                local inst = entry.inst
                if inst and inst.Parent and crystalPasses(cfg, inst) then
                    local d = tonumber(entry.distance) or 1e9
                    if not bestD or d < bestD then
                        best, bestD = entry, d
                    end
                end
            end

            if not best or not best.inst then
                return
            end

            local inst = best.inst
            local dist = bestD or 0
            local GRAB_OK = 16
            self.Target = inst

            -- If matching crystal is too far (missed after boulder break), teleport then grab
            if dist > GRAB_OK then
                local pos = nil
                pcall(function()
                    if FM.resolveWorldPosition then
                        pos = FM.resolveWorldPosition(inst)
                    end
                end)
                if typeof(pos) ~= "Vector3" then
                    pcall(function()
                        if inst:IsA("BasePart") then pos = inst.Position end
                    end)
                end
                if typeof(pos) == "Vector3" then
                    local stand = pos + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
                    local teleported = false
                    if FM.teleportTo then
                        teleported = pcall(function()
                            FM.teleportTo(stand)
                        end) and true
                    end
                    if not teleported then
                        pcall(function()
                            root.CFrame = CFrame.new(stand)
                            root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                        end)
                    end
                    -- brief settle then grab
                    task.delay(0.08, function()
                        if not self.Enabled or not self.Active then return end
                        if inst and inst.Parent and FM.grabCrystal then
                            pcall(FM.grabCrystal, inst, best.prompt)
                        end
                    end)
                    return
                end
            end

            if FM.grabCrystal then
                pcall(FM.grabCrystal, inst, best.prompt)
            end
        end))
    end

    function BoulderPickupSystem:StopRuntime()
        bump(self)
        self.Active = false
        self.Target = nil
        disconnectAll(self.Connections)
        self.Connections = {}
    end

    function BoulderPickupSystem:Reset()
        self:StopRuntime()
        self.Runtime = { lastAt = 0 }
        self.Target = nil
    end

    -- -------------------- MoneyPickupSystem --------------------
    local MoneyPickupSystem = {
        Enabled = false,
        Active = false,
        Session = 0,
        Connections = {},
        Target = nil,
        Config = newConfig(),
        Runtime = { lastAt = 0 },
    }

    function MoneyPickupSystem:SetEnabled(on)
        on = on == true
        if self.Enabled == on and self.Active == on then return end
        self.Enabled = on
        if on then
            self:StartRuntime()
        else
            self:StopRuntime()
        end
    end

    function MoneyPickupSystem:StartRuntime()
        if not self.Enabled then return end
        if not FM.isWorldReady or not FM.isWorldReady() then
            FM.ensureWorldReadyThen(function()
                if self.Enabled then self:StartRuntime() end
            end)
            return
        end
        local sid = bump(self)
        self.Active = true
        disconnectAll(self.Connections)
        self.Connections = {}
        track(self, RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if not self.Enabled or not self.Active or self.Session ~= sid then return end
            if not (MoneySystem and MoneySystem.Active) then return end
            local now = os.clock()
            if now - (self.Runtime.lastAt or 0) < 0.28 then return end
            self.Runtime.lastAt = now
            local root = FM.getRoot and FM.getRoot()
            if not root then return end
            local free = FM.backpackFree and FM.backpackFree() or 0
            if free <= 0 then return end
            local cfg = self.Config
            local minV = tonumber(cfg.MinValue) or 0
            local list = FM.pickupCandidates and FM.pickupCandidates(free, root.Position, function(inst)
                return crystalPasses(cfg, inst)
            end) or {}
            for _, entry in ipairs(list) do
                local inst = entry.inst
                if inst and FM.grabCrystal then
                    local val = tonumber(entry.value) or 0
                    if minV <= 0 or val >= minV then
                        if crystalPasses(cfg, inst) then
                            self.Target = inst
                            pcall(FM.grabCrystal, inst, entry.prompt)
                            break
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end))
    end

    function MoneyPickupSystem:StopRuntime()
        bump(self)
        self.Active = false
        self.Target = nil
        disconnectAll(self.Connections)
        self.Connections = {}
    end

    function MoneyPickupSystem:Reset()
        self:StopRuntime()
        self.Runtime = { lastAt = 0 }
        self.Target = nil
    end

    -- -------------------- RunePickupSystem --------------------
    local RunePickupSystem = {
        Enabled = false,
        Active = false,
        Session = 0,
        Connections = {},
        Target = nil,
        Runtime = { lastAt = 0 },
    }

    function RunePickupSystem:SetEnabled(on)
        on = on == true
        self.Enabled = on
        if on then self:StartRuntime() else self:StopRuntime() end
    end

    function RunePickupSystem:StartRuntime()
        if not self.Enabled then return end
        if not FM.isWorldReady or not FM.isWorldReady() then
            FM.ensureWorldReadyThen(function()
                if self.Enabled then self:StartRuntime() end
            end)
            return
        end
        local sid = bump(self)
        self.Active = true
        disconnectAll(self.Connections)
        self.Connections = {}

        -- Drive Mountain auto-grab (legacy heartbeat path)
        pcall(function()
            if Mountain and Mountain.setAutoGrab then
                Mountain.setAutoGrab(true)
            end
        end)

        track(self, RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if not self.Enabled or not self.Active or self.Session ~= sid then return end
            local now = os.clock()
            if now - (self.Runtime.lastAt or 0) < 0.25 then return end
            self.Runtime.lastAt = now

            local grabbed = 0

            -- Correct API: Mountain.grabNear (exports grabRunes internally)
            if Mountain then
                if Mountain.grabNear then
                    local ok, n = pcall(function()
                        return Mountain.grabNear(80)
                    end)
                    if ok and type(n) == "number" then
                        grabbed = n
                    end
                elseif Mountain.grabRunes then
                    local ok, n = pcall(function()
                        return Mountain.grabRunes(80)
                    end)
                    if ok and type(n) == "number" then
                        grabbed = n
                    end
                end
            end

            -- Fallback: nearby rune prompts only (no full GetDescendants every tick)
            if grabbed <= 0 then
                local root = FM.getRoot and FM.getRoot()
                if root then
                    local origin = root.Position
                    local range = 80
                    local fired = 0
                    local params = OverlapParams.new()
                    params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
                    pcall(function()
                        local char = LocalPlayer.Character
                        if char then
                            params.FilterDescendantsInstances = { char }
                        end
                    end)
                    local parts = nil
                    pcall(function()
                        parts = Workspace:GetPartBoundsInRadius(origin, range, params)
                    end)
                    if type(parts) == "table" then
                        for _, part in ipairs(parts) do
                            if fired >= 8 then break end
                            local owner = part
                            local parent = part.Parent
                            if parent and parent ~= Workspace then
                                if FM.isRuneLike and (FM.isRuneLike(parent) or FM.isRuneLike(part)) then
                                    owner = FM.isRuneLike(parent) and parent or part
                                elseif type(parent.Name) == "string" and parent.Name:find("Rune", 1, true) then
                                    owner = parent
                                else
                                    owner = nil
                                end
                            elseif not (FM.isRuneLike and FM.isRuneLike(part)) then
                                owner = nil
                            end
                            if owner then
                                local prompt = owner:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ProximityPrompt", true)
                                if not prompt and part then
                                    prompt = part:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ProximityPrompt", true)
                                end
                                if prompt and FM.firePrompt then
                                    if pcall(FM.firePrompt, prompt) then
                                        fired += 1
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end))
    end

        function RunePickupSystem:StopRuntime()
        bump(self)
        self.Active = false
        self.Target = nil
        disconnectAll(self.Connections)
        self.Connections = {}
        pcall(function()
            if Mountain and Mountain.setAutoGrab then
                Mountain.setAutoGrab(false)
            end
        end)
    -- Rune path is independent from Auto Fav / Boulder / Money
    end

    function RunePickupSystem:Reset()
        self:StopRuntime()
        self.Runtime = { lastAt = 0 }
    end

    -- -------------------- BoulderSystem --------------------
    BoulderSystem = {
        Enabled = false,
        Active = false,
        Session = 0,
        Connections = {},
        Target = nil,
        Config = newConfig(),
        Runtime = { status = "Idle" },
        Pickup = BoulderPickupSystem,
    }

    function BoulderSystem:SetEnabled(on)
        on = on == true
        self.Enabled = on
        if on then
            self:StartRuntime()
        else
            -- Parent off also stops child pickup worker (not the reverse)
            self:StopRuntime()
            if BoulderPickupSystem.Active then
                BoulderPickupSystem:StopRuntime()
            end
        end
    end

    function BoulderSystem:StartRuntime()
        if not self.Enabled then return end

        local sid = bump(self)
        self.Active = true
        self.Runtime.status = "Starting"
        self.Runtime.approachDone = true
        self.Runtime.approachRunning = false
        self.Target = nil

        pcall(function()
            if FM.ensureFastDigPasses then FM.ensureFastDigPasses() end
        end)

        -- Stop any previous farm session cleanly before restart
        pcall(function()
            local eng = FM.Farm
            if eng and eng.setActive then eng.setActive(false) end
        end)
        pcall(function()
            if Move and Move.glideStop then Move.glideStop() end
        end)

        S.boulderFarmActive = true
        pcall(function() if FM.syncFarmActiveFlag then FM.syncFarmActiveFlag() end end)

        -- Movement + NoClip ownership (refcount) — only while Boulder is Active
        pcall(function() Mov:Acquire("Boulder") end)
        pcall(function()
            if FM.ownedSetNoclip then
                FM.ownedSetNoclip("Boulder", true, false)
            end
        end)

        -- Expand streaming so boulder folder has more models loaded (large scan radius)
        task.spawn(function()
            if not (self.Enabled and self.Active and self.Session == sid) then return end
            pcall(function()
                local center = nil
                if FM.mountainSpot then center = FM.mountainSpot() end
                if typeof(center) ~= "Vector3" then
                    local root = FM.getRoot and FM.getRoot()
                    center = root and root.Position
                end
                if typeof(center) == "Vector3" then
                    LocalPlayer:RequestStreamAroundAsync(center, 12)
                    if FM.requestStream then FM.requestStream(center) end
                end
            end)
            -- secondary stream points for wide mountain coverage (not a tween path)
            local STREAM_POINTS = {
                Vector3.new(-19.3, 120, 534.8),
                Vector3.new(41.7, 200, 246.6),
                Vector3.new(0, 300, 400),
            }
            for _, pt in ipairs(STREAM_POINTS) do
                if not (self.Enabled and self.Active and self.Session == sid) then return end
                pcall(function()
                    LocalPlayer:RequestStreamAroundAsync(pt, 10)
                end)
                task.wait(0.15)
            end
        end)

        local function startFarmEngine()
            if not (self.Enabled and self.Active and self.Session == sid) then return end
            self.Runtime.status = "Farming"
            pcall(function()
                local eng = FM.Farm
                if eng and eng.setActive then
                    S.pickupValueActive = false
                    S.pickupLuckActive = false
                    S.pickupRarityActive = false
                    S.autoPickupActive = false
                    eng.setActive(true)
                end
            end)
            if BoulderPickupSystem.Enabled then
                pcall(function() BoulderPickupSystem:StartRuntime() end)
            end
        end

        startFarmEngine()

        disconnectAll(self.Connections)
        self.Connections = {}
        track(self, RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if not self.Enabled or not self.Active or self.Session ~= sid then return end
            S.boulderFarmActive = true
            if self.Runtime.status == "Farming" then
                local eng = FM.Farm
                if eng and eng.setActive then
                    local isOn = true
                    pcall(function()
                        if eng.isActive then isOn = eng.isActive() == true end
                    end)
                    if not isOn then
                        pcall(function() eng.setActive(true) end)
                    end
                end
            end
        end))
    end

    function BoulderSystem:StopRuntime()
        bump(self)
        self.Active = false
        self.Target = nil
        self.Runtime.status = "Idle"
        self.Runtime.approachDone = false
        self.Runtime.approachRunning = false
        S.boulderFarmActive = false
        pcall(function() if FM.syncFarmActiveFlag then FM.syncFarmActiveFlag() end end)

        disconnectAll(self.Connections)
        self.Connections = {}

        pcall(function()
            local eng = FM.Farm
            if eng and eng.setActive then
                eng.setActive(false)
            elseif eng and eng.stop then
                eng.stop()
            end
        end)

        pcall(function()
            if Move and Move.glideStop then Move.glideStop() end
        end)
        pcall(function()
            if Move and Move.forceClearFarmMovement then Move.forceClearFarmMovement() end
        end)
        pcall(function()
            local char = LocalPlayer.Character
            local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if hum then
                hum.PlatformStand = false
                pcall(function() hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp) end)
            end
            local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if root then
                root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
            end
        end)

        -- Release Boulder ownership only
        pcall(function() Mov:Release("Boulder") end)
        pcall(function()
            if FM.ownedSetNoclip then
                FM.ownedSetNoclip("Boulder", false, false)
            end
        end)

        -- If nothing else owns movement/noclip, hard-restore collision
        pcall(function()
            if Mov and not Mov:HasAny() then
                if Move and Move.setNoclip then
                    Move.setNoclip(false, false)
                end
                if Move and Move.setFly and not S._flyWanted then
                    Move.setFly(false)
                end
            end
        end)
    end

    function BoulderSystem:Reset()
        self:StopRuntime()
        self.Runtime = { status = "Idle" }
        self.Target = nil
    end

    function BoulderSystem:ApplyConfig()
        -- Config-only: never stop Money / never StopAll
        local cfg = self.Config
        -- map targets for legacy Farm if exposed
        pcall(function()
            local eng = FM.Farm
            if eng and eng.setTargets and cfg.Targets then
                eng.setTargets(cfg.Targets)
            end
        end)
    end

    -- -------------------- MoneySystem --------------------
    MoneySystem = {
        Enabled = false,
        Active = false,
        Session = 0,
        Connections = {},
        Target = nil,
        Config = newConfig(),
        Runtime = { status = "Idle" },
        Pickup = MoneyPickupSystem,
    }

    function MoneySystem:SetEnabled(on)
        on = on == true
        self.Enabled = on
        if on then
            -- Stop Auto Favorite once (no UI spam — prevents Fluent freeze)
            pcall(function()
                if FM.setAutoFavorite then
                    FM.setAutoFavorite(false)
                else
                    S.autoFavActive = false
                end
            end)
            pcall(function()
                if FM.setAutoFavToggleUI then
                    FM.setAutoFavToggleUI(false)
                elseif type(FM._autoFavToggle) == "table" and FM._autoFavToggle.SetValue then
                    local cur = FM._autoFavToggle.Value
                    if cur ~= false then
                        S._autoFavUiLock = true
                        FM._autoFavToggle:SetValue(false)
                        task.defer(function()
                            S._autoFavUiLock = false
                        end)
                    end
                end
            end)
            self:StartRuntime()
        else
            self:StopRuntime()
            if MoneyPickupSystem.Active then
                MoneyPickupSystem:StopRuntime()
            end
        end
    end

    function MoneySystem:StartRuntime()
        if not self.Enabled then return end
        if not FM.isWorldReady or not FM.isWorldReady() then
            self.Runtime.status = "WaitingWorld"
            FM.ensureWorldReadyThen(function()
                if self.Enabled then
                    self:StartRuntime()
                end
            end)
            return
        end
        local sid = bump(self)
        self.Active = true
        self.Runtime.status = "Starting"
        pcall(function()
            if FM.ensureFastDigPasses then FM.ensureFastDigPasses() end
        end)
        S.moneyFarmActive = true
        pcall(function() if FM.syncFarmActiveFlag then FM.syncFarmActiveFlag() end end)
        pcall(function() Mov:Acquire("Money") end)
        pcall(function() if FM.ownedSetNoclip then FM.ownedSetNoclip("Money", true, false) end end)

        pcall(function()
            local eng = FM.Money
            if eng and eng.setActive then
                S.pickupValueActive = false
                S.pickupLuckActive = false
                S.pickupRarityActive = false
                S.autoPickupActive = false
                eng.setActive(true)
            else
                warn("[MoneySystem] Money engine not ready")
            end
        end)

        if MoneyPickupSystem.Enabled then
            MoneyPickupSystem:StartRuntime()
        end

        disconnectAll(self.Connections)
        self.Connections = {}
        track(self, RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if not self.Enabled or not self.Active or self.Session ~= sid then return end
            S.moneyFarmActive = true
            local eng = FM.Money
            if eng and eng.isActive and not eng.isActive() and self.Enabled then
                pcall(function() eng.setActive(true) end)
            end
        end))
    end

    function MoneySystem:StopRuntime()
        bump(self)
        self.Active = false
        self.Target = nil
        self.Runtime.status = "Idle"
        S.moneyFarmActive = false
        pcall(function() if FM.syncFarmActiveFlag then FM.syncFarmActiveFlag() end end)
        disconnectAll(self.Connections)
        self.Connections = {}
        pcall(function()
            local eng = FM.Money
            if eng and eng.setActive then
                eng.setActive(false)
            elseif eng and eng.stop then
                eng.stop()
            end
        end)
        pcall(function() Mov:Release("Money") end)
        pcall(function() if FM.ownedSetNoclip then FM.ownedSetNoclip("Money", false, false) end end)
    end

    function MoneySystem:Reset()
        self:StopRuntime()
        self.Runtime = { status = "Idle" }
        self.Target = nil
    end

    function MoneySystem:ApplyConfig()
        -- config only; never touches Boulder
    end

    -- Export
    FM.BoulderSystem = BoulderSystem
    FM.MoneySystem = MoneySystem
    FM.BoulderPickupSystem = BoulderPickupSystem
    FM.MoneyPickupSystem = MoneyPickupSystem
    FM.RunePickupSystem = RunePickupSystem

    FM.Systems = {
        Boulder = BoulderSystem,
        Money = MoneySystem,
        BoulderPickup = BoulderPickupSystem,
        MoneyPickup = MoneyPickupSystem,
        RunePickup = RunePickupSystem,
        VALUE_PRESETS = VALUE_PRESETS,
        LUCK_PRESETS = LUCK_PRESETS,
        TIER_OPTIONS = TIER_OPTIONS,
        FARM_KINDS = FARM_KINDS,
    }

    function FM.refreshIsolatedSystems()
        local snap = {
            b = BoulderSystem.Enabled,
            bp = BoulderPickupSystem.Enabled,
            m = MoneySystem.Enabled,
            mp = MoneyPickupSystem.Enabled,
            r = RunePickupSystem.Enabled,
        }
        BoulderSystem:StopRuntime()
        MoneySystem:StopRuntime()
        BoulderPickupSystem:StopRuntime()
        MoneyPickupSystem:StopRuntime()
        RunePickupSystem:StopRuntime()
        BoulderSystem.Enabled = snap.b
        BoulderPickupSystem.Enabled = snap.bp
        MoneySystem.Enabled = snap.m
        MoneyPickupSystem.Enabled = snap.mp
        RunePickupSystem.Enabled = snap.r
        if snap.b then BoulderSystem:StartRuntime() end
        if snap.m then MoneySystem:StartRuntime() end
        if snap.bp and snap.b then BoulderPickupSystem:StartRuntime() end
        if snap.mp and snap.m then MoneyPickupSystem:StartRuntime() end
        if snap.r then RunePickupSystem:StartRuntime() end
    end
end



function FM.anyFarmRunning()
    return S.boulderFarmActive == true
        or S.moneyFarmActive == true
        or S.veinFarmActive == true
        or S.miningBoulder ~= nil
end

function FM.syncFarmActiveFlag()
    S.farmActive = FM.anyFarmRunning()
end

function FM.getPickupTiming()
    local mode = (S and S.pickupMode) or "Legit"
    if mode == "Burst" then
        return PICK.burstCooldown, PICK.burstBurst
    end
    return PICK.legitCooldown, PICK.legitBurst
end

function FM.getDropPickupTiming()
    local mode = (S and S.pickupMode) or "Legit"
    if mode == "Burst" then
        return {
            dropEquip = 0.02,
            dropGap = 0.02,
            pickGap = 0.04,
            pickBurst = 12,
            pickRadius = 90,
        }
    end
    return {
        dropEquip = 0.04,
        dropGap = 0.06,
        pickGap = 0.1,
        pickBurst = 4,
        pickRadius = 70,
    }
end

function FM.valueLuckFilter(inst)
    if not inst or not S then
        return false
    end
    if S.pickupRarityActive then
        if FM.rarityAllowed and not FM.rarityAllowed(inst) then
            return false
        end
    end
    if S.pickupValueActive then
        local value = FM.crystalValue(inst)
        if value < (S.pickupMinValue or 0) then
            return false
        end
    end
    if S.pickupLuckActive then
        local ok, luck = pcall(FM.crystalLuck, inst)
        local luckPct = (ok and luck or 0) * 100
        if luckPct < (S.pickupMinLuck or 0) then
            return false
        end
    end
    return true
end

function FM.crystalFingerprint(inst)
    if not inst then
        return nil
    end
    local value = FM.crystalValue(inst)
    local weight = FM.crystalWeight(inst)
    local tier = FM.crystalTier(inst)
    local name = tostring(FM.crystalName(inst) or inst.Name or "")
    local mut = tostring(FM.getAttr(inst, "Mutation") or "")
    return string.format("%s|%.4f|%.4f|%d|%s", name, value, weight, tier, mut)
end

function FM.rememberDroppedCrystal(toolOrInst)
    if not S.recentDrops then
        S.recentDrops = {}
    end
    local fp = FM.crystalFingerprint(toolOrInst)
    if not fp then
        return
    end
    S.recentDrops[#S.recentDrops + 1] = {
        fp = fp,
        at = os.clock(),
        value = FM.crystalValue(toolOrInst),
        weight = FM.crystalWeight(toolOrInst),
        name = tostring(FM.crystalName(toolOrInst) or ""),
    }
    while #S.recentDrops > 80 do
        table.remove(S.recentDrops, 1)
    end
end

function FM.isOurRecentDrop(inst, maxAge)
    maxAge = maxAge or 120
    if not inst or not S.recentDrops or #S.recentDrops == 0 then
        return false
    end
    local now = os.clock()
    local fp = FM.crystalFingerprint(inst)
    if not fp then
        return false
    end
    for i = #S.recentDrops, 1, -1 do
        local entry = S.recentDrops[i]
        if now - entry.at > maxAge then
            table.remove(S.recentDrops, i)
        elseif entry.fp == fp then
            return true
        end
    end
    return false
end

function FM.isInDroppedFolder(inst)
    local p = inst
    while p do
        if p.Name == "DroppedCrystals" then
            return true
        end
        p = p.Parent
    end
    return false
end

function FM.reportError(context, err)
    local now = os.clock()
    if now - S.lastReport < 5 then
        return
    end
    S.lastReport = now
    warn(string.format("%s: %s", context, tostring(err)))
end

function FM.formatShort(n, prefix)
    n = tonumber(n) or 0
    prefix = prefix or ""

    local sign = n < 0 and "-" or ""
    n = math.abs(n)

    if n < 1000 then
        return string.format("%s%s%d", sign, prefix, math.floor(n + 0.5))
    end

    local index = 0
    while n >= 1000 and index < #SUFFIXES - 1 do
        n /= 1000
        index += 1
    end

    return string.format("%s%s%.2f%s", sign, prefix, n, SUFFIXES[index + 1])
end

function FM.formatWeight(kg)
    kg = tonumber(kg) or 0
    if kg >= 1000 then
        return FM.formatShort(kg) .. "kg"
    end
    return string.format("%.1fkg", kg)
end

function FM.formatDistance(studs)
    studs = tonumber(studs) or 0
    if studs >= 1000 then
        return string.format("%.1fkm", studs / 1000)
    end
    return string.format("%dm", math.floor(studs + 0.5))
end

function FM.formatLuck(score)
    local pct = (tonumber(score) or 0) * 100

    if pct <= 0 then
        return "+0%"
    end
    if pct < 1 then
        return string.format("+%.2f%%", pct)
    end
    if pct < 10 then
        return string.format("+%.1f%%", pct)
    end
    return string.format("+%.0f%%", pct)
end

function FM.parseValue(text)
    if type(text) ~= "string" then
        return nil
    end

    local cleaned = text:lower():gsub("[%s,%$_]", "")
    if cleaned == "" then
        return 0
    end

    local number, suffix = cleaned:match("^(%d*%.?%d+)(%a*)$")
    if not number then
        return nil
    end

    local base = tonumber(number)
    if not base then
        return nil
    end

    if suffix == "" then
        return base
    end

    local multiplier = PARSE_MULTIPLIERS[suffix]
    if not multiplier then
        return nil
    end

    return base * multiplier
end

-- ============================================================================
-- CHARACTER MANAGER — single source of truth for Character / Humanoid / Root
-- Session bumps on death/removal/respawn so stale workers can exit.
-- ============================================================================
local CharacterManager = {
    Character = nil,
    Humanoid = nil,
    RootPart = nil,
    Session = 0,
    Ready = false,
    _charConn = nil,
    _removingConn = nil,
    _childConn = nil,
    _diedConn = nil,
    _readyCallbacks = {},
}

function CharacterManager:_clearCharacterRefs(reason)
    self.Character = nil
    self.Humanoid = nil
    self.RootPart = nil
    self.Ready = false
    S.rootPart = nil
    S.tpState = nil
    if self._childConn then
        pcall(function() self._childConn:Disconnect() end)
        self._childConn = nil
    end
    if self._diedConn then
        pcall(function() self._diedConn:Disconnect() end)
        self._diedConn = nil
    end
end

function CharacterManager:_bindDied(human)
    if self._diedConn then
        pcall(function() self._diedConn:Disconnect() end)
        self._diedConn = nil
    end
    if not human then
        return
    end
    self._diedConn = human.Died:Connect(function()
        -- Invalidate session; do NOT stop feature config / GUI
        self.Session += 1
        self:_clearCharacterRefs("died")
        -- Soft-pause character-dependent movement only if no owners left will re-acquire on ready
        pcall(function()
            if Move and Move.glideStop then
                Move.glideStop()
            end
        end)
    end)
end

function CharacterManager:_setReady(char, human, root)
    self.Character = char
    self.Humanoid = human
    self.RootPart = root
    self.Ready = true
    S.rootPart = root
    self:_bindDied(human)
    for _, cb in ipairs(self._readyCallbacks) do
        pcall(cb, char, human, root, self.Session)
    end
    -- Notify isolated systems to drop stale targets (config Enabled preserved)
    pcall(function()
        if FM.BoulderSystem and FM.BoulderSystem.Active then
            FM.BoulderSystem.Target = nil
            if FM.BoulderSystem.Runtime then
                FM.BoulderSystem.Runtime.status = "CharReady"
            end
        end
        if FM.MoneySystem and FM.MoneySystem.Active then
            FM.MoneySystem.Target = nil
            if FM.MoneySystem.Runtime then
                FM.MoneySystem.Runtime.status = "CharReady"
            end
        end
        if FM.BoulderPickupSystem then
            FM.BoulderPickupSystem.Target = nil
        end
        if FM.MoneyPickupSystem then
            FM.MoneyPickupSystem.Target = nil
        end
    end)
    -- Re-apply god mode on new body without full script reset
    pcall(function()
        if S.godMode and type(FM._godReapply) == "function" then
            FM._godReapply(char)
        end
    end)
end

function CharacterManager:Refresh(char)
    char = char or LocalPlayer.Character
    if not char or not char.Parent then
        self:_clearCharacterRefs("missing")
        return false
    end
    local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local human = char:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Humanoid")
    if root and human then
        -- New character instance → new session
        if self.Character ~= char then
            self.Session += 1
        end
        self:_setReady(char, human, root)
        return true
    end
    self.Character = char
    self.Ready = false
    S.rootPart = root
    if self._childConn then
        pcall(function() self._childConn:Disconnect() end)
        self._childConn = nil
    end
    self._childConn = char.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
        if child.Name == "HumanoidRootPart" or child:IsA("Humanoid") then
            task.defer(function()
                self:Refresh(char)
            end)
        end
    end)
    return false
end

function CharacterManager:GetSession()
    return self.Session
end

function CharacterManager:IsCurrent(session)
    return session == self.Session and self.Ready == true
end

function CharacterManager:OnReady(cb)
    if type(cb) == "function" then
        self._readyCallbacks[#self._readyCallbacks + 1] = cb
    end
end

function CharacterManager:Install()
    if self._charConn then
        return
    end
    self:Refresh(LocalPlayer.Character)
    self._charConn = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)
        self.Session += 1
        self:_clearCharacterRefs("CharacterAdded")
        S.tpState = nil
        task.defer(function()
            -- Wait briefly for Humanoid + HRP replication
            local tries = 0
            while tries < 50 do
                tries += 1
                if self:Refresh(character) then
                    return
                end
                task.wait(0.1)
            end
            self:Refresh(character)
        end)
    end)
    pcall(function()
        self._removingConn = LocalPlayer.CharacterRemoving:Connect(function()
            self.Session += 1
            self:_clearCharacterRefs("CharacterRemoving")
        end)
    end)
end

CharacterManager:Install()
FM.CharacterManager = CharacterManager

function FM.bindCharacter(character)
    CharacterManager:Refresh(character or LocalPlayer.Character)
end

function FM.getRoot()
    local cm = CharacterManager
    if cm.RootPart and cm.RootPart.Parent then
        return cm.RootPart
    end
    cm:Refresh(LocalPlayer.Character)
    if cm.RootPart and cm.RootPart.Parent then
        return cm.RootPart
    end
    return nil
end

function FM.getHumanoid()
    local cm = CharacterManager
