    if cm.Humanoid and cm.Humanoid.Parent then
        return cm.Humanoid
    end
    cm:Refresh(LocalPlayer.Character)
    if cm.Humanoid and cm.Humanoid.Parent then
        return cm.Humanoid
    end
    return nil
end

function FM.getCharacter()
    local cm = CharacterManager
    if cm.Character and cm.Character.Parent then
        return cm.Character
    end
    cm:Refresh(LocalPlayer.Character)
    return cm.Character
end

function FM.charSession()
    return CharacterManager.Session
end

-- ============================================================================
-- WORLD / TERRAIN READY — delay main features until mountain terrain is present
-- ============================================================================
function FM.isWorldReady()
    if S.worldReady == true then
        return true
    end
    local cx = Workspace:GetAttribute("MountainCenterX")
    local cz = Workspace:GetAttribute("MountainCenterZ")
    if typeof(cx) ~= "number" or typeof(cz) ~= "number" then
        return false
    end
    local Terrain = Workspace:FindFirstChildOfClass("Terrain") or Workspace.Terrain
    if not Terrain then
        return false
    end
    local ok = pcall(function()
        local center = Vector3.new(cx, 40, cz)
        local half = Vector3.new(48, 48, 48)
        local region = Region3.new(center - half, center + half):ExpandToGrid(4)
        local mats, occs = Terrain:ReadVoxels(region, 4)
        -- need some non-air occupancy near mountain center
        local solid = 0
        for x = 1, math.min(8, #mats) do
            for y = 1, math.min(8, #mats[x]) do
                for z = 1, math.min(8, #mats[x][y]) do
                    local o = occs[x][y][z]
                    if o and o > 0.05 then
                        solid += 1
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        if solid < 3 then
            error("not enough terrain")
        end
    end)
    if ok then
        S.worldReady = true
        return true
    end
    return false
end

function FM.waitForWorldReady(timeoutSec)
    timeoutSec = tonumber(timeoutSec) or 90
    if FM.isWorldReady() then
        return true
    end
    local t0 = os.clock()
    while os.clock() - t0 < timeoutSec do
        if FM.isWorldReady() then
            return true
        end
        task.wait(0.35)
    end
    -- soft allow after timeout so script is not permanently stuck
    S.worldReady = true
    return false
end

function FM.ensureWorldReadyThen(fn)
    if type(fn) ~= "function" then
        return
    end
    task.spawn(function()
        FM.waitForWorldReady(90)
        pcall(fn)
    end)
end



function FM.getAttr(inst, name)
    local ok, value = pcall(inst.GetAttribute, inst, name)
    if ok then
        return value
    end
    return nil
end

function FM.crystalValue(inst)
    return tonumber(FM.getAttr(inst, "Value")) or 0
end

function FM.crystalWeight(inst)
    return tonumber(FM.getAttr(inst, "WeightKg")) or 0
end

function FM.crystalTier(inst)
    local t = tonumber(FM.getAttr(inst, "Tier")) or 0
    if t > 0 then
        return t
    end
    local name = FM.getAttr(inst, "TierName")
    if type(name) == "string" and name ~= "" and TIER_NAMES then
        local lower = string.lower(name)
        for i, n in ipairs(TIER_NAMES) do
            if string.lower(n) == lower then
                return i
            end
        end
    end
    return 0
end

function FM.crystalRarity(inst)
    local name = FM.getAttr(inst, "TierName")
    if type(name) == "string" and name ~= "" then
        return name
    end
    return TIER_NAMES[FM.crystalTier(inst)] or "Unknown"
end

function FM.crystalName(inst)
    local name = FM.getAttr(inst, "CrystalName")
    if type(name) == "string" and name ~= "" then
        return name
    end
    return inst.Name
end

function FM.crystalColor(inst)
    local r = tonumber(FM.getAttr(inst, "TierColorR"))
    local g = tonumber(FM.getAttr(inst, "TierColorG"))
    local b = tonumber(FM.getAttr(inst, "TierColorB"))
    if r and g and b then
        return Color3.fromRGB(r, g, b)
    end
    return COLORS.default
end

function FM.mutationLuck(name)
    if type(name) ~= "string" or name == "" then
        return 1
    end
    return MUTATION_LUCK[name] or 1
end

function FM.combinedLuckMult(inst)
    local mutation = FM.getAttr(inst, "Mutation")
    local roll = tonumber(FM.getAttr(inst, "MutationLuckRoll"))
    local multiplier = (roll and roll > 0) and roll or FM.mutationLuck(mutation)

    local extra = FM.getAttr(inst, "ExtraMutations")
    if type(extra) == "string" and extra ~= "" then
        for name in string.gmatch(extra, "[^,]+") do
            if name ~= "" then
                multiplier *= FM.mutationLuck(name)
            end
        end
    end

    if FM.getAttr(inst, "IsBloodCrystal") == true then
        multiplier *= LUCK.blood
    end

    if FM.getAttr(inst, "AdminMutation") == "Radioactive" and mutation ~= "Radioactive" then
        local hasRadioactive = type(extra) == "string" and extra:find("Radioactive", 1, true) ~= nil
        if not hasRadioactive then
            multiplier *= FM.mutationLuck("Radioactive")
        end
    end

    return multiplier
end

function FM.computeLuck(inst)
    local tier = FM.crystalTier(inst)
    if tier <= 0 then
        return 0
    end

    local weight = math.max(0.01, FM.crystalWeight(inst))
    local rarityMult = LUCK.rarity[tier]
    if not rarityMult then
        rarityMult = LUCK.rarity[math.min(tier, #LUCK.rarity)] or LUCK.rarity[1] or 1
    end
    local base = rarityMult * (math.min(weight, LUCK.cap) ^ LUCK.exponent) * LUCK.base

    if FM.getAttr(inst, "BombCrystal") == true then
        base *= LUCK.bomb
    end

    local mult = 1
    pcall(function()
        mult = FM.combinedLuckMult(inst) or 1
    end)
    return base * mult
end

function FM.luckLabel(inst)
    local hover = inst:FindFirstChild("CrystalHover")
    if not hover then
        return nil
    end

    local label = hover:FindFirstChild("LuckBoost")
    if not label or not label:IsA("TextLabel") then
        return nil
    end

    return label
end

function FM.luckLabelText(inst)
    local label = FM.luckLabel(inst)
    if not label then
        return nil
    end
    return label.Text
end

function FM.rarityAllowed(inst)
    local filter = S.rarityFilter
    if type(filter) ~= "table" then
        return true
    end

    local selected = {}
    local count = 0
    local hasAll = false
    for k, v in pairs(filter) do
        local name = nil
        if type(k) == "string" and (v == true or v == "true") then
            name = k
        elseif type(v) == "string" then
            name = v
        elseif type(k) == "number" and type(v) == "string" then
            name = v
        end
        if name and name ~= "" then
            local lower = string.lower(name)
            if lower == "all" then
                hasAll = true
            else
                selected[lower] = true
                count += 1
            end
        end
    end

    if hasAll or count == 0 then
        return true
    end

    local rarity = FM.crystalRarity and FM.crystalRarity(inst) or "Unknown"
    local rarityLower = string.lower(tostring(rarity))

    if selected[rarityLower] then
        return true
    end

    local tier = FM.crystalTier and FM.crystalTier(inst) or 0
    if tier > 0 and TIER_NAMES and TIER_NAMES[tier] then
        if selected[string.lower(TIER_NAMES[tier])] then
            return true
        end
    end

    return false
end

function FM.mutationList(inst)
    local list = {}
    if not inst then
        return list
    end
    local primary = FM.getAttr(inst, "Mutation")
    if type(primary) == "string" and primary ~= "" then
        list[#list + 1] = primary
    end
    local extra = FM.getAttr(inst, "ExtraMutations")
    if type(extra) == "string" and extra ~= "" then
        for name in string.gmatch(extra, "[^,]+") do
            name = name:match("^%s*(.-)%s*$") or name
            if name ~= "" then
                local dup = false
                for _, existing in ipairs(list) do
                    if existing == name then
                        dup = true
                        break
                    end
                end
                if not dup then
                    list[#list + 1] = name
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return list
end

function FM.mutationColor(name)
    if type(name) ~= "string" or name == "" then
        return COLORS.default
    end
    local c = MUTATION_COLORS and MUTATION_COLORS[name]
    if typeof(c) == "Color3" then
        return c
    end
    return COLORS.default
end

function FM.mutationRichText(inst)
    local list = FM.mutationList(inst)
    if #list == 0 then
        return ""
    end
    local parts = {}
    for i, name in ipairs(list) do
        local c = FM.mutationColor(name)
        local hex = string.format("%02X%02X%02X",
            math.floor(c.R * 255 + 0.5),
            math.floor(c.G * 255 + 0.5),
            math.floor(c.B * 255 + 0.5))
        parts[#parts + 1] = string.format('<font color="#%s">%s</font>', hex, name)
        if i == 1 and #list > 1 then
            parts[#parts + 1] = '<font color="#00FF00"><b>+</b></font>'
        elseif i > 1 and i < #list then
            parts[#parts + 1] = '<font color="#AAAAAA">, </font>'
        end
    end
    
    if #list == 1 then
        local c = FM.mutationColor(list[1])
        local hex = string.format("%02X%02X%02X",
            math.floor(c.R * 255 + 0.5),
            math.floor(c.G * 255 + 0.5),
            math.floor(c.B * 255 + 0.5))
        return string.format('<font color="#%s">(%s)</font>', hex, list[1])
    end
    local first = list[1]
    local c1 = FM.mutationColor(first)
    local hex1 = string.format("%02X%02X%02X",
        math.floor(c1.R * 255 + 0.5),
        math.floor(c1.G * 255 + 0.5),
        math.floor(c1.B * 255 + 0.5))
    local extras = {}
    for i = 2, #list do
        local c = FM.mutationColor(list[i])
        local hex = string.format("%02X%02X%02X",
            math.floor(c.R * 255 + 0.5),
            math.floor(c.G * 255 + 0.5),
            math.floor(c.B * 255 + 0.5))
        extras[#extras + 1] = string.format('<font color="#%s">%s</font>', hex, list[i])
    end
    return string.format('<font color="#%s">(%s)</font> <font color="#00FF00"><b>+</b></font> %s',
        hex1, first, table.concat(extras, ", "))
end

function FM.crystalLuck(inst)
    local labelText = FM.luckLabelText(inst)
    if type(labelText) == "string" then
        local pct = tonumber(labelText:match("([%d%.]+)%s*%%"))
        if not pct then
            pct = tonumber(labelText:match("([%d%.]+)"))
        end
        if pct and pct > 0 then
            if pct > 1 then
                return pct / 100
            end
            return pct
        end
    end
    return FM.computeLuck(inst)
end

function FM.getOwnedPlotRegion()
    local things = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Things")
    local plots = things and things:FindFirstChild("Plots")
    local slots = plots and plots:FindFirstChild("Slots")
    if not slots then
        return nil, nil
    end
    local plot = slots:FindFirstChild(LocalPlayer.Name)
    if not plot then
        for _, slot in ipairs(slots:GetChildren()) do
            local owner = slot:GetAttribute("Owner") or slot:GetAttribute("OwnerName")
            if owner == LocalPlayer.Name or owner == LocalPlayer.UserId or tostring(owner) == tostring(LocalPlayer.UserId) then
                plot = slot
                break
            end
        end
    end
    if not plot then
        return nil, nil
    end
    local region = plot:FindFirstChild("Region")
    if region and region:IsA("BasePart") then
        return plot, region
    end
    return plot, nil
end

function FM.crystalPlotPos(region, objectSize)
    local cf = region.CFrame
    local size = region.Size
    local half = size * 0.5
    local padX = math.max(half.X - 1.5, 1)
    local padZ = math.max(half.Z - 1.5, 1)
    local oh = (objectSize or Vector3.new(2, 2, 2)) * 0.5
    local lx = (math.random() - 0.5) * 2 * padX
    local lz = (math.random() - 0.5) * 2 * padZ
    local ly = oh.Y + 0.05
    return cf:PointToWorldSpace(Vector3.new(lx, ly - half.Y, lz))
end

function FM.isCrystalTool(tool)
    if not tool or not tool:IsA("Tool") then
        return false
    end
    local name = tool.Name
    if type(name) == "string" and name:find("Rune", 1, true) then
        return false
    end
    if FM.getAttr(tool, "Tier") ~= nil then
        return true
    end
    -- fallback: crystals often have Value / WeightKg or names like "[M] Name [21.5kg]"
    if FM.getAttr(tool, "Value") ~= nil then
        return true
    end
    if FM.getAttr(tool, "WeightKg") ~= nil then
        return true
    end
    if type(name) == "string" and name:find("%[.-kg%]") then
        return true
    end
    return false
end

function FM.resolveWorldPosition(target)
    if typeof(target) == "Vector3" then
        return target
    end
    if typeof(target) == "CFrame" then
        return target.Position
    end
    if typeof(target) ~= "Instance" or not target.Parent then
        return nil
    end
    if target:IsA("BasePart") then
        return target.Position
    end
    if target:IsA("Model") then
        local ok, pivot = pcall(function()
            return target:GetPivot().Position
        end)
        if ok and typeof(pivot) == "Vector3" then
            return pivot
        end
        local part = target.PrimaryPart or target:FindFirstChild("Handle") or target:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
        if part then
            return part.Position
        end
    end
    if target:IsA("Tool") then
        local handle = target:FindFirstChild("Handle")
        if handle and handle:IsA("BasePart") then
            return handle.Position
        end
        local part = target:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
        if part then
            return part.Position
        end
    end
    local part = target:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
    if part then
        return part.Position
    end
    return nil
end

function FM.standNearCrystal(target)
    local pos = FM.resolveWorldPosition(target)
    if typeof(pos) ~= "Vector3" then
        return nil
    end
    
    if pos.X ~= pos.X or pos.Y ~= pos.Y or pos.Z ~= pos.Z then
        return nil
    end
    if math.abs(pos.X) > 50000 or math.abs(pos.Z) > 50000 then
        return nil
    end
    if pos.Y > 2500 or pos.Y < -500 then
        return nil
    end
    
    return pos + Vector3.new(0, 3.5, 0)
end

function FM.moveTo(dest, lookAt)
    local pos
    if typeof(dest) == "Vector3" then
        pos = dest
    elseif typeof(dest) == "CFrame" then
        pos = dest.Position
    elseif typeof(dest) == "Instance" then
        pos = FM.resolveWorldPosition(dest)
    else
        return false
    end
    if typeof(pos) ~= "Vector3" then
        return false
    end
    if typeof(lookAt) == "Instance" then
        lookAt = FM.resolveWorldPosition(lookAt) or pos
    elseif typeof(lookAt) ~= "Vector3" then
        lookAt = pos
    end

    local mode = (S and S.farmMode) or "Tween"

    if mode == "Teleport" then
        S._forceTeleport = true
        local ok = FM.secureTeleportTo(pos, lookAt)
        S._forceTeleport = false
        return ok
    end

    
    pcall(function()
        if Move and Move.setNoclip then
            Move.setNoclip(true, false)
        end
    end)
    if Move and type(Move.glide) == "function" then
        local ok, result = pcall(function()
            return Move.glide(CFrame.lookAt(pos, typeof(lookAt) == "Vector3" and lookAt or pos))
        end)
        if ok and result ~= false then
            return true
        end
    end

    local root = FM.getRoot and FM.getRoot()
    if not root then
        return false
    end
    local lookPos = typeof(lookAt) == "Vector3" and lookAt or pos
    local goal = CFrame.lookAt(pos, lookPos)
    local dist = (root.Position - pos).Magnitude
    local speed = (S and S._slowMove) and 36 or 90
    local durMin = (S and S._slowMove) and 0.5 or 0.15
    local durMax = (S and S._slowMove) and 4.0 or 1.35
    local dur = math.clamp(dist / speed, durMin, durMax)
    local tweenOk = false
    pcall(function()
        local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")
        local tw = TweenService:Create(
            root,
            TweenInfo.new(dur, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
            { CFrame = goal }
        )
        tw:Play()
        tw.Completed:Wait()
        tweenOk = true
    end)
    if tweenOk then
        return true
    end
    
    pcall(function()
        root.CFrame = goal
        root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
    end)
    return true
end

function FM.travelTo(dest, lookAt)
    return FM.moveTo(dest, lookAt)
end

function FM.goPlotUnderground(region)
    local plot = nil
    if region and region.Parent then
        plot = region.Parent
    end
    if not plot then
        local p, r = FM.getOwnedPlotRegion()
        plot = p
        region = r or region
    end
    if not plot then
        return false
    end

    local dest = nil
    local sign = plot:FindFirstChild("Sign", true)
    if sign then
        if sign:IsA("BasePart") then
            dest = sign.Position
        elseif sign:IsA("Model") then
            local ok, pivot = pcall(function()
                return sign:GetPivot().Position
            end)
            if ok and pivot then
                dest = pivot
            else
                local part = sign.PrimaryPart or sign:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
                if part then
                    dest = part.Position
                end
            end
        end
    end

    if not dest and region and region:IsA("BasePart") then
        dest = region.Position
    end

    if not dest then
        return false
    end

    dest = dest + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)

    pcall(function()
        if Move and Move.setNoclip then
            Move.setNoclip(true, false)
        end
    end)
    
    FM.travelTo(dest + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0), dest)
    task.wait(0.2)
    return true
end

function FM.placeCrystalTool(tool, region)
    local remote = PlotPlaceRequest or FM.findRemote("PlotPlaceRequest")
    if not remote or not tool or not region then
        return false
    end

    local humanoid = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if humanoid and tool.Parent ~= LocalPlayer.Character then
        pcall(function()
            humanoid:EquipTool(tool)
        end)
        local waited = 0
        while waited < 0.45 do
            task.wait(0.05)
            waited += 0.05
            if LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild(tool.Name) then
                break
            end
        end
    end

    local held = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild(tool.Name)
    held = held or tool
    if not held or not held.Parent then
        return false
    end

    local handle = held:FindFirstChild("Handle")
    local objectSize = (handle and handle:IsA("BasePart") and handle.Size) or Vector3.new(2, 2, 2)

    for attempt = 1, 3 do
        local pos = FM.crystalPlotPos(region, objectSize)
        local ok = pcall(function()
            remote:FireServer(held.Name, pos, 0, held)
        end)
        if ok then
            task.wait(0.18)
            if not held.Parent or held.Parent == Workspace then
                return true
            end
            task.wait(0.12)
        else
            task.wait(0.1)
        end
    end
    return false
end

function FM.placeAllBackpackCrystals()
    local remote = PlotPlaceRequest or FM.findRemote("PlotPlaceRequest")
    local plot, region = FM.getOwnedPlotRegion()
    if not remote then
        WindUI:Notify({ Title = "Plant", Content = "PlotPlaceRequest missing", Duration = 3, Icon = "alert-circle" })
        return
    end
    if not region then
        WindUI:Notify({ Title = "Plant", Content = "Own plot Region not found", Duration = 3, Icon = "alert-circle" })
        return
    end
    if S._placingCrystals then
        return
    end
    S._placingCrystals = true
    S._placeLockAt = os.clock()
    task.spawn(function()
        FM.goPlotUnderground(region)
        local tools = {}
        local function scan(container)
            if not container then return end
            for _, child in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
                if FM.isCrystalTool(child) then
                    tools[#tools + 1] = child
                end
            end
        end
        scan(LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack"))
        scan(LocalPlayer.Character)
        local placed = 0
        for _, tool in ipairs(tools) do
            if tool and tool.Parent then
                local root = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if root and region then
                    local plot = region.Parent
                    local targetPos = region.Position + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
                    if plot then
                        local sign = plot:FindFirstChild("Sign", true)
                        if sign then
                            if sign:IsA("BasePart") then
                                targetPos = sign.Position + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
                            elseif sign:IsA("Model") then
                                local ok, pivot = pcall(function() return sign:GetPivot().Position end)
                                if ok and pivot then
                                    targetPos = pivot + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    if (root.Position - targetPos).Magnitude > 25 then
                        FM.goPlotUnderground(region)
                    end
                end
                if FM.placeCrystalTool(tool, region) then
                    placed += 1
                end
            end
            task.wait(0.35)
        end
        S._placingCrystals = false
        WindUI:Notify({
            Title = "Plant Crystals",
            Content = string.format("Placed %d crystal(s)", placed),
            Duration = 3,
            Icon = placed > 0 and "check" or "alert-circle",
        })
    end)
end

function FM.plantHeldCrystal()
    local remote = PlotPlaceRequest or FM.findRemote("PlotPlaceRequest")
    local plot, region = FM.getOwnedPlotRegion()
    if not remote then
        WindUI:Notify({ Title = "Plant", Content = "PlotPlaceRequest missing", Duration = 3, Icon = "alert-circle" })
        return
    end
    if not region then
        WindUI:Notify({ Title = "Plant", Content = "Own plot Region not found", Duration = 3, Icon = "alert-circle" })
        return
    end
    local held = nil
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    if char then
        for _, child in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
            if FM.isCrystalTool(child) then
                held = child
                break
            end
        end
    end
    if not held then
        WindUI:Notify({ Title = "Plant", Content = "Hold a crystal first", Duration = 2, Icon = "alert-circle" })
        return
    end
    task.spawn(function()
        FM.goPlotUnderground(region)
        local ok = FM.placeCrystalTool(held, region)
        WindUI:Notify({
            Title = "Plant Crystal",
            Content = ok and ("Placed " .. held.Name) or "Failed to place",
            Duration = 2,
            Icon = ok and "check" or "alert-circle",
        })
    end)
end

function FM.meetsFilter(inst, value)
    if S.valueFilter then
        if (value or FM.crystalValue(inst)) < S.minValue then
            return false
        end
    end
    return true
end

function FM.ownsGamepass(name)
    local folder = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("GamepassesOwned")
    if not folder then
        return false
    end

    local flag = folder:FindFirstChild(name)
    return flag ~= nil and flag:IsA("BoolValue") and flag.Value == true
end

-- Ensure GamepassesOwned flags exist client-side (FastDig speeds dig systems)
-- Path: LocalPlayer.GamepassesOwned.<Name> (BoolValue)
function FM.ensureGamepass(name, value)
    value = value ~= false
    name = tostring(name or "")
    if name == "" then
        return false
    end
    local folder = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("GamepassesOwned")
    if not folder then
        folder = Instance.new("Folder")
        folder.Name = "GamepassesOwned"
        folder.Parent = LocalPlayer
    end
    local flag = folder:FindFirstChild(name)
    if not flag then
        flag = Instance.new("BoolValue")
        flag.Name = name
        flag.Parent = folder
    end
    if flag:IsA("BoolValue") then
        flag.Value = value == true
        return true
    end
    return false
end

function FM.ensureFastDigPasses()
    -- Intentionally disabled: no FastDig / gamepass spoof.
    -- Dig speed is controlled only via S.digSpeedMult (Auto Dig slider).
    return
end

function FM.realStat(name)
    local data = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerData")
    local stats = data and data:FindFirstChild("RealStats")
    local entry = stats and stats:FindFirstChild(name)
    if not entry then
        return nil
    end
    return tonumber(entry.Value)
end

function FM.hasActiveRune(keyword)
    local data = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerData")
    local plot = data and data:FindFirstChild("PlotData")
    local runes = plot and plot:FindFirstChild("Runes")
    if not runes then
        return false
    end

    for _, child in ipairs(runes:GetChildren()) do
        local runeName = child:GetAttribute("RuneName")
        if type(runeName) == "string" and runeName:find(keyword, 1, true) then
            if (tonumber(child:GetAttribute("Remaining")) or 0) > 0 then
                return true
            end
        end
    end

    return false
end

function FM.backpackCapacity()
    if LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("InfBackpack") == true then
        return math.huge
    end

    local base = FM.realStat("CarryWeight") or 10
    if FM.ownsGamepass("CarryKgPlus4") then
        base *= 4
    end

    local total = base + (FM.realStat("CarryWeightBonus") or 0)

    if FM.hasActiveRune("Weight") then
        return total * 2
    end

    return total
end

function FM.backpackWeight()
    local total = 0

    local function scan(container)
        if not container then
            return
        end
        for _, child in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
            if child:IsA("Tool") and FM.getAttr(child, "Tier") ~= nil then
                local kg = tonumber(FM.getAttr(child, "WeightKg"))
                if kg then
                    total += kg
                end
            end
        end
    end

    scan(LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack"))
    scan(LocalPlayer.Character)

    return total
end

function FM.backpackFree()
    local capacity = FM.backpackCapacity()
    if capacity == math.huge then
        return math.huge
    end
    return capacity - FM.backpackWeight()
end

function FM.looksLikeCrystal(inst)
    if not inst:IsA("BasePart") then
        return false
    end
    return inst.Name:find("Crystal", 1, true) ~= nil
end

local crystalFlags = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })

function FM.isCrystal(inst)
    local cached = crystalFlags[inst]
    if cached ~= nil then
        return cached
    end

    local result = false
    if inst and inst:IsA("BasePart") then
        local parent = inst.Parent
        if parent then
            if parent:IsA("Model") and parent:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then
                crystalFlags[inst] = false
                return false
            end
            if parent:IsA("Tool") or parent:IsA("Accoutrement") then
                crystalFlags[inst] = false
                return false
            end
        end

        local value = FM.getAttr(inst, "Value")
        local hasValue = typeof(value) == "number"
        local tier = FM.getAttr(inst, "Tier")
        local hasTier = typeof(tier) == "number" and tier > 0
        local cname = FM.getAttr(inst, "CrystalName")
        local hasName = type(cname) == "string" and cname ~= ""
        local weight = FM.getAttr(inst, "WeightKg")
        local hasWeight = typeof(weight) == "number"
        local nameHit = type(inst.Name) == "string" and inst.Name:find("Crystal", 1, true) ~= nil

        if hasValue then
            result = true
        elseif hasName and (hasTier or hasWeight or nameHit) then
            result = true
        elseif hasTier and hasWeight then
            result = true
        else
            result = false
        end
    end

    crystalFlags[inst] = result
    return result
end

local containerList = {}
local containerClock = 0

function FM.rebuildContainers()
    table.clear(containerList)

    local seen = {}

    local function push(container)
        if not container or seen[container] then
            return
        end
        if container == Workspace then
            return
        end
        seen[container] = true
        containerList[#containerList + 1] = container
    end

    for _, name in ipairs(CONTAINER_NAMES) do
        push(Workspace:FindFirstChild(name))
    end

    local things = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Things")
    if things then
        for _, name in ipairs(CONTAINER_NAMES) do
            push(things:FindFirstChild(name))
        end
        push(things:FindFirstChild("DroppedCrystal"))
        push(things:FindFirstChild("WorldCrystals"))
    end

    local drops = Workspace:FindFirstChild("DroppedCrystal")
    push(drops)
end

function FM.eachContainer(fn)
    local now = os.clock()
    local stale = #containerList == 0 or now - containerClock >= 1.0

    if not stale then
        for _, container in ipairs(containerList) do
            if not container.Parent and container ~= Workspace then
                stale = true
                break
            end
        end
    end

    if stale then
        containerClock = now
        FM.rebuildContainers()
    end

    for _, container in ipairs(containerList) do
        fn(container)
    end
end

function FM.newLabel(name, parent, order, total, color, rich, maxText)
    local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    label.Name = name
    label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    label.BorderSizePixel = 0
    label.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1 / total, 0)
    label.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, order / total, 0)
    label.Font = ESP.font
    label.TextScaled = true
    label.TextTransparency = 0
    label.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
    label.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    label.TextColor3 = color
    label.RichText = rich == true
    label.Text = ""
    label.Parent = parent

    local constraint = Instance.new("UITextSizeConstraint")
    constraint.MaxTextSize = maxText
    constraint.MinTextSize = 8
    constraint.Parent = label

    return label, constraint
end

function FM.crystalGuiSize()
    return UDim2.fromOffset(ESP.width * S.espScale, ESP.height * S.espScale)
end

function FM.crystalTextSize()
    return math.max(6, math.floor(ESP.text * S.espScale + 0.5))
end

function FM.playerGuiSize()
    return UDim2.fromOffset(PLAYER.width * S.playerScale, PLAYER.height * S.playerScale)
end

function FM.playerTextSize()
    return math.max(6, math.floor(PLAYER.text * S.playerScale + 0.5))
end

function FM.applyRadarGradient(label, style)
    if not label then
        return
    end
    style = style or RADAR_STYLE.Crystal
    local g = label:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIGradient")
    if not g then
        g = Instance.new("UIGradient")
        g.Name = "RadarGradient"
        g.Parent = label
    end
    g.Color = ColorSequence.new(style.a, style.b)
    g.Rotation = 15
    g.Enabled = true
end

function FM.createEntry(inst)
    local billboard = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
    billboard.Name = "FayyMengEsp"
    billboard.Adornee = inst
    billboard.AlwaysOnTop = true
    billboard.ResetOnSpawn = false
    billboard.LightInfluence = 0
    billboard.Size = FM.crystalGuiSize()
    billboard.StudsOffsetWorldSpace = ESP.offset
    billboard.MaxDistance = math.huge
    billboard.Parent = EspHolder

    local textSize = FM.crystalTextSize()
    local rarity, rarityConstraint = FM.newLabel("Rarity", billboard, 0, 3, COLORS.default, true, textSize)
    local info, infoConstraint = FM.newLabel("Info", billboard, 1, 3, COLORS.money, false, textSize)
    local extra, extraConstraint = FM.newLabel("Extra", billboard, 2, 3, COLORS.extra, true, textSize)

    return {
        gui = billboard,
        rarity = rarity,
        info = info,
        extra = extra,
        constraints = { rarityConstraint, infoConstraint, extraConstraint },
        signature = false,
        luckText = "+0%",
        distanceText = false,
    }
end

function FM.destroyEntry(inst, entry)
    entry = entry or S.espCache[inst]
    if not entry then
        return
    end

    if entry.gui then
        entry.gui:Destroy()
    end

    S.espCache[inst] = nil
    S.espCount -= 1
    S.statsDirty = true
end

function FM.applyEspScale()
    local size = FM.crystalGuiSize()
    local textSize = FM.crystalTextSize()

    for _, entry in pairs(S.espCache) do
        if entry.gui then
            entry.gui.Size = size
        end
        for _, constraint in ipairs(entry.constraints) do
            constraint.MaxTextSize = textSize
        end
    end
end

function FM.applyPlayerScale()
    local size = FM.playerGuiSize()
    local textSize = FM.playerTextSize()

    for _, entry in pairs(S.playerCache) do
        if entry.gui then
            entry.gui.Size = size
        end
        for _, constraint in ipairs(entry.constraints) do
            constraint.MaxTextSize = textSize
        end
    end
end

function FM.applyExtra(entry, distanceText)
    entry.distanceText = distanceText
    entry.extra.Text = string.format(
        '<font color="#%s">%s</font>  \u{2022}  <font color="#%s">%s</font>',
        COLORS.hexDistance,
        distanceText,
        COLORS.hexLuck,
        entry.luckText
    )
end

function FM.buildTitle(inst)
    local rarity = FM.crystalRarity(inst)
    local name = FM.crystalName(inst)
    local muts = FM.mutationList and FM.mutationList(inst) or {}
    if #muts > 0 then
        local mutStr = table.concat(muts, "+")
        return string.format("[%s] %s (%s)", rarity, name, mutStr)
    end
    return string.format("[%s] %s", rarity, name)
end

function FM.applyDetails(inst, entry, origin)
    local rarityName = FM.crystalRarity(inst) or "Unknown"
    local money = FM.formatShort(FM.crystalValue(inst), "$")
    local weight = FM.formatWeight(FM.crystalWeight(inst))
    local mutRich = FM.mutationRichText and FM.mutationRichText(inst) or ""
    local mutList = FM.mutationList and FM.mutationList(inst) or {}

    if mutRich ~= "" then
        entry.rarity.Text = string.format("[%s] %s", rarityName, mutRich)
        entry.rarity.RichText = true
    else
        entry.rarity.Text = string.format("[%s]", rarityName)
        entry.rarity.RichText = true
    end
    local tier = FM.crystalTier(inst) or 0
    local tierColor = (TIER_COLORS and TIER_COLORS[tier]) or FM.crystalColor(inst) or COLORS.default
    if #mutList > 0 then
        local mc = FM.mutationColor(mutList[1])
        if typeof(mc) == "Color3" then
            tierColor = mc
        end
    end
    entry.rarity.TextColor3 = tierColor

    entry.info.Text = string.format("%s  ·  %s", money, weight)
    entry.info.TextColor3 = COLORS.money

    local okLuck, luck = pcall(FM.crystalLuck, inst)
    entry.luckText = FM.formatLuck(okLuck and luck or 0)
    if origin then
        entry.distanceText = FM.formatDistance((inst.Position - origin).Magnitude)
    else
        entry.distanceText = entry.distanceText or "--"
    end
    FM.applyExtra(entry, entry.distanceText)
end

function FM.crystalSignature(inst)
    return table.concat({
        tostring(FM.getAttr(inst, "Tier")),
        tostring(FM.getAttr(inst, "TierName")),
        tostring(FM.getAttr(inst, "CrystalName")),
        tostring(FM.getAttr(inst, "Value")),
        tostring(FM.getAttr(inst, "WeightKg")),
        tostring(FM.getAttr(inst, "Mutation")),
        tostring(FM.getAttr(inst, "ExtraMutations")),
        tostring(FM.luckLabelText(inst)),
    }, "|")
end

function FM.markDirty(inst)
    S.dirty[inst] = true
end

function FM.untrackCrystal(inst)
    local conns = S.registry[inst]
    if not conns then
        return
    end

    for _, connection in ipairs(conns) do
        connection:Disconnect()
    end

    S.registry[inst] = nil
    S.registryCount -= 1
    S.dirty[inst] = nil
    S.candidates[inst] = nil
    S.statsDirty = true

    FM.destroyEntry(inst)
end

function FM.trackCrystal(inst)
    if S.registry[inst] then
        return
    end
    if not FM.isCrystal(inst) then
        return
    end

    local conns = {}
    S.registry[inst] = conns
    S.registryCount += 1
    S.statsDirty = true

    local ok = pcall(function()
        conns[#conns + 1] = inst.Destroying:Connect(function()
            FM.untrackCrystal(inst)
        end)

        conns[#conns + 1] = inst.AncestryChanged:Connect(function()
            if not inst:IsDescendantOf(Workspace) then
                FM.untrackCrystal(inst)
            end
        end)

        for _, name in ipairs(WATCHED_ATTRIBUTES) do
            conns[#conns + 1] = inst:GetAttributeChangedSignal(name):Connect(function()
                FM.markDirty(inst)
            end)
        end

        local label = FM.luckLabel(inst)
        if label then
            conns[#conns + 1] = label:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
                FM.markDirty(inst)
            end)
        end
    end)

    if not ok then
        FM.untrackCrystal(inst)
        return
    end

    FM.markDirty(inst)
end

function FM.syncCrystal(inst)
    S.dirty[inst] = nil
    if not inst or not FM.isCrystal(inst) then
        if inst then
            FM.untrackCrystal(inst)
        end
        return
    end

    if not S.registry[inst] then
        return
    end

    if not inst.Parent then
        FM.untrackCrystal(inst)
        return
    end

    local entry = S.espCache[inst]
    local hidden = not S.espActive or FM.getAttr(inst, "Collected") == true

    if not hidden then
        hidden = not FM.meetsFilter(inst)
    end
    if not hidden then
        hidden = not FM.rarityAllowed(inst)
    end
    if not hidden then
        hidden = not FM.espLuckAllowed(inst)
    end

    if hidden then
        if entry then
            FM.destroyEntry(inst, entry)
        end
        return
    end

    if not entry then
        local built, result = pcall(FM.createEntry, inst)
        if not built then
            FM.reportError("billboard", result)
            return
        end

        entry = result
        S.espCache[inst] = entry
        S.espCount += 1
        S.statsDirty = true
    end

    local signature = FM.crystalSignature(inst)
    if signature == entry.signature then
        return
    end

    local root = FM.getRoot()
    local ok, err = pcall(FM.applyDetails, inst, entry, root and root.Position or nil)
    if ok then
        entry.signature = signature
    else
        FM.reportError("details", err)
    end
end

local sweepSeen = {}

function FM.sweep()
    local seen = sweepSeen
    table.clear(seen)

    FM.eachContainer(function(container)
        for _, child in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
            if not seen[child] and FM.isCrystal(child) then
                seen[child] = true
                if not S.registry[child] then
                    FM.trackCrystal(child)
                end
            end
        end
    end)

    local stale
    for inst in pairs(S.registry) do
        if not seen[inst] then
            stale = stale or {}
            stale[#stale + 1] = inst
        end
    end

    if stale then
        for _, inst in ipairs(stale) do
            FM.untrackCrystal(inst)
        end
    end
end

local lastDistanceOrigin

function FM.updateDistances()
    local root = FM.getRoot()
    if not root then
        return
    end

    local origin = root.Position

    if lastDistanceOrigin and (origin - lastDistanceOrigin).Magnitude < 1 then
        return
    end

    lastDistanceOrigin = origin

    for inst, entry in pairs(S.espCache) do
        if inst.Parent then
            local text = FM.formatDistance((inst.Position - origin).Magnitude)
            if text ~= entry.distanceText then
                FM.applyExtra(entry, text)
            end
        end
    end
end

local clearLuckEsp

function FM.clearEsp()
    for inst, entry in pairs(S.espCache) do
        FM.destroyEntry(inst, entry)
    end
    S.espCache = {}
    S.espCount = 0
    S.statsDirty = true
end

function FM.clearRegistry()
    local all
    for inst in pairs(S.registry) do
        all = all or {}
        all[#all + 1] = inst
    end

    if all then
        for _, inst in ipairs(all) do
            FM.untrackCrystal(inst)
        end
    end

    FM.clearEsp()
    clearLuckEsp()

    S.registry = {}
    S.candidates = {}
    S.dirty = {}
    S.registryCount = 0
    S.statsDirty = true
end

function FM.requestRefresh()
    for inst in pairs(S.registry) do
        S.dirty[inst] = true
    end
    S.sweepAccumulator = math.huge
end

function FM.luckEspGuiSize()
    return UDim2.fromOffset(ESP.width * S.espScale, math.floor(ESP.height * S.espScale * 0.85))
end

function FM.createLuckEntry(inst)
    local billboard = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
    billboard.Name = "FayyMengLuckEsp"
    billboard.Adornee = inst
    billboard.AlwaysOnTop = true
    billboard.ResetOnSpawn = false
    billboard.LightInfluence = 0
    billboard.Size = FM.luckEspGuiSize()
    billboard.StudsOffsetWorldSpace = ESP.offset
    billboard.MaxDistance = math.huge
    billboard.Parent = EspHolder

    local textSize = FM.crystalTextSize()
    local label, constraint = FM.newLabel("Luck", billboard, 0, 2, Color3.fromRGB(255, 196, 0), false, textSize)
    local distLabel, distConstraint = FM.newLabel("Distance", billboard, 1, 2, COLORS.extra, false, textSize)

    return {
        gui = billboard,
        label = label,
        distLabel = distLabel,
        constraint = constraint,
        constraints = { constraint, distConstraint },
        luckText = "",
        distanceText = "",
    }
end

function FM.destroyLuckEntry(inst)
    local entry = S.luckEspCache[inst]
    if not entry then return end
    if entry.gui then entry.gui:Destroy() end
    S.luckEspCache[inst] = nil
    S.luckEspCount -= 1
    S.statsDirty = true
end

clearLuckEsp = function()
    for inst, entry in pairs(S.luckEspCache) do
        if entry.gui then entry.gui:Destroy() end
    end
    S.luckEspCache = {}
    S.luckEspCount = 0
    S.statsDirty = true
end

function FM.luckMeetsFilter(inst)
    local minPct = tonumber(S.minLuck) or 0
    if minPct <= 0 then
        return true
    end
    local ok, luck = pcall(FM.crystalLuck, inst)
    local score = ok and luck or 0
    local pct = score * 100
    if pct < 1 and score > 1 then
        pct = score
    end
    return pct >= minPct
end

function FM.espLuckAllowed(inst)
    return FM.luckMeetsFilter(inst)
end

function FM.syncLuckCrystal(inst)
    if not S.luckEspActive
        or not inst
        or not inst.Parent
        or not FM.isCrystal(inst)
        or FM.getAttr(inst, "Collected") == true
        or not FM.luckMeetsFilter(inst)
    then
        FM.destroyLuckEntry(inst)
        return
    end

    local entry = S.luckEspCache[inst]
    if not entry then
        local built, result = pcall(FM.createLuckEntry, inst)
        if not built or not result then
            return
        end
        entry = result
        S.luckEspCache[inst] = entry
        S.luckEspCount += 1
        S.statsDirty = true
    end

    if entry.gui and entry.gui.Adornee ~= inst then
        entry.gui.Adornee = inst
    end

    local ok, luck = pcall(FM.crystalLuck, inst)
    local luckScore = ok and luck or 0
    local luckText = FM.formatLuck(luckScore)
    local rarityName = FM.crystalRarity(inst) or "Unknown"
    local root = FM.getRoot()
    local distanceText = "--"
    if root then
        distanceText = FM.formatDistance((inst.Position - root.Position).Magnitude)
    end

    local textSize = FM.crystalTextSize()
    entry.gui.Size = FM.luckEspGuiSize()
    if entry.constraint then
        entry.constraint.MaxTextSize = textSize
    end
    if entry.constraints then
        for _, c in ipairs(entry.constraints) do
            c.MaxTextSize = textSize
        end
    end

    local topText = string.format("[%s] %s", rarityName, luckText)
    if entry.luckText ~= topText then
        entry.luckText = topText
        entry.label.Text = topText
        local tier = FM.crystalTier(inst) or 0
        entry.label.TextColor3 = (TIER_COLORS and TIER_COLORS[tier]) or Color3.fromRGB(255, 196, 0)
    end
    if entry.distanceText ~= distanceText then
        entry.distanceText = distanceText
        if entry.distLabel then
            entry.distLabel.Text = distanceText
            entry.distLabel.TextColor3 = COLORS.extra
        end
    end
end

function FM.refreshLuckEsp()
    for inst in pairs(S.registry) do
        FM.syncLuckCrystal(inst)
    end
    FM.eachContainer(function(container)
        for _, child in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
            if FM.isCrystal(child) and not S.registry[child] then
                FM.syncLuckCrystal(child)
            end
        end
    end)
end

function FM.trackingEnabled()
    return S.espActive or S.luckEspActive
end

function FM.onContainerChild(child)
    if FM.looksLikeCrystal(child) then
        S.candidates[child] = os.clock() + ESP.ttl
    end
end

function FM.watchContainers()
    for container, connection in pairs(S.containerConns) do
        if not container:IsDescendantOf(game) then
            connection:Disconnect()
            S.containerConns[container] = nil
        end
    end

    FM.eachContainer(function(container)
        if S.containerConns[container] then
            return
        end
        S.containerConns[container] = container.ChildAdded:Connect(FM.onContainerChild)
    end)
end

function FM.unwatchContainers()
    for container, connection in pairs(S.containerConns) do
        connection:Disconnect()
        S.containerConns[container] = nil
    end
end

function FM.updateTracking()
    if FM.trackingEnabled() then
        S.sweepAccumulator = math.huge
        FM.watchContainers()
        FM.requestRefresh()
    else
        FM.unwatchContainers()
        FM.clearRegistry()
    end
end

local espConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(deltaTime)
    if S.statsDirty and S.StatsLabel then
        S.statsDirty = false
        S.StatsLabel:SetText(string.format("Tracking: %d  |  Shown: %d", S.registryCount, S.espCount))
    end

    if not FM.trackingEnabled() then
        return
    end

    local now = os.clock()

    if next(S.candidates) ~= nil then
        for inst, expiry in pairs(S.candidates) do
            if not inst.Parent then
                S.candidates[inst] = nil
            elseif FM.isCrystal(inst) then
                S.candidates[inst] = nil
                FM.trackCrystal(inst)
            elseif now > expiry then
                S.candidates[inst] = nil
            end
        end
    end

    if next(S.dirty) ~= nil then
        local deadline = now + ESP.budget
        local processed = 0
        for inst in pairs(S.dirty) do
            local ok, err = pcall(FM.syncCrystal, inst)
            if not ok then
                S.dirty[inst] = nil
                FM.reportError("sync", err)
            end
            processed += 1
            if processed >= 4 or os.clock() > deadline then
                break
            end
        end
    end

    S.sweepAccumulator += deltaTime
    if S.sweepAccumulator >= ESP.sweep then
        S.sweepAccumulator = 0
        local ok, err = pcall(function()
            FM.watchContainers()
            FM.sweep()
            if S.luckEspActive then
                FM.refreshLuckEsp()
            end
        end)
        if not ok then
            FM.reportError("sweep", err)
        end
    end

    if S.espCount > 0 then
        S.distanceAccumulator += deltaTime
        if S.distanceAccumulator >= PACE.distance then
            S.distanceAccumulator = 0
            local ok, err = pcall(FM.updateDistances)
            if not ok then
                FM.reportError("distance", err)
            end
        end
    end
end)

function FM.destroyPlayerEntry(player)
    local entry = S.playerCache[player]
    if not entry then
        return
    end

    if entry.highlight then
        pcall(function()
            entry.highlight:Destroy()
        end)
        entry.highlight = nil
    end
    if entry.gui then
        entry.gui:Destroy()
    end

    S.playerCache[player] = nil
end

function FM.createPlayerEntry(player)
    local billboard = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
    billboard.Name = "FayyMengPlayerEsp"
    billboard.AlwaysOnTop = true
    billboard.ResetOnSpawn = false
    billboard.LightInfluence = 0
    billboard.Size = FM.playerGuiSize()
    billboard.StudsOffsetWorldSpace = PLAYER.offset
    billboard.MaxDistance = math.huge
    billboard.Parent = EspHolder

    local textSize = FM.playerTextSize()
    local nameLabel, nameConstraint = FM.newLabel("Name", billboard, 0, 2, COLORS.player, false, textSize)
    local distanceLabel, distanceConstraint = FM.newLabel("Distance", billboard, 1, 2, COLORS.extra, false, textSize)

    nameLabel.Text = player.DisplayName

    local highlight = Instance.new("Highlight")
    highlight.Name = "FayyMengPlayerOutline"
    highlight.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
    highlight.FillTransparency = 0.85
    highlight.OutlineTransparency = 0
    highlight.FillColor = COLORS.player
    highlight.OutlineColor = COLORS.player
    highlight.Parent = EspHolder
    if player.Character then
        highlight.Adornee = player.Character
    end

    return {
        gui = billboard,
        name = nameLabel,
        distance = distanceLabel,
        constraints = { nameConstraint, distanceConstraint },
        nameText = player.DisplayName,
        distanceText = false,
        highlight = highlight,
    }
end

function FM.clearPlayerEsp()
    for player in pairs(S.playerCache) do
        FM.destroyPlayerEntry(player)
    end
    S.playerCache = {}
end

function FM.updatePlayerEsp()
    if not S.playerEspActive then
        return
    end

    local root = FM.getRoot()
    local origin = root and root.Position or nil

    for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if player ~= LocalPlayer then
            local character = player.Character
            local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local target = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

            if target and humanoid and humanoid.Health > 0 then
                local entry = S.playerCache[player]
                if not entry then
                    local built, result = pcall(FM.createPlayerEntry, player)
                    if built then
                        entry = result
                        S.playerCache[player] = entry
                    else
                        FM.reportError("player", result)
                    end
                end

                if entry then
                    if entry.gui.Adornee ~= target then
                        entry.gui.Adornee = target
                    end
                    if entry.highlight then
                        if entry.highlight.Adornee ~= character then
                            entry.highlight.Adornee = character
                        end
                    end

                    if entry.nameText ~= player.DisplayName then
                        entry.nameText = player.DisplayName
                        entry.name.Text = player.DisplayName
                    end

                    local text = origin and FM.formatDistance((target.Position - origin).Magnitude) or "--"
                    if text ~= entry.distanceText then
                        entry.distanceText = text
                        entry.distance.Text = text
                    end
                end
            else
                FM.destroyPlayerEntry(player)
            end
        end
    end

    local gone
    for player in pairs(S.playerCache) do
        if player == LocalPlayer or not player.Parent then
            gone = gone or {}
            gone[#gone + 1] = player
        end
    end

    if gone then
        for _, player in ipairs(gone) do
            FM.destroyPlayerEntry(player)
        end
    end
end

local streamMark = 0
local streamSpot

function FM.requestStream(position)
    if typeof(position) ~= "Vector3" then
        return
    end

    local now = os.clock()

    if streamSpot and now - streamMark < 0.3 and (streamSpot - position).Magnitude < 32 then
        return
    end

    streamMark = now
    streamSpot = position

    task.spawn(function()
        pcall(function()
            LocalPlayer:RequestStreamAroundAsync(position, 1)
        end)
    end)
end

function FM.applyPivot(cframe)
    local character = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not character then
        return false
    end

    FM.requestStream(cframe.Position)

    local root = FM.getRoot()
    if not root then
        return false
    end

    local moved = pcall(function()
        character:PivotTo(cframe)
    end)

    if not moved then
        moved = pcall(function()
            root.CFrame = cframe
        end)
    end

    if not moved then
        return false
    end

    pcall(function()
        root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
        root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
    end)

    return true
end

function FM.findClearGoal(position, ignore)
    local params = OverlapParams.new()
    params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
    params.FilterDescendantsInstances = ignore
    params.MaxParts = 1

    for _, offset in ipairs(TP.clear) do
        local candidate = position + offset
        local ok, hits = pcall(function()
            return Workspace:GetPartBoundsInRadius(candidate, 2.5, params)
        end)

        if ok and #hits == 0 then
            return candidate
        end
    end

    return position + TP.clear[#TP.clear]
end

function FM.finishTeleport()
    if not S.tpState then
        return
    end

    local root = FM.getRoot()
    if root then
        pcall(function()
            root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
        end)
    end

    local character = LocalPlayer.Character
    local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if humanoid then
        pcall(function()
            humanoid.PlatformStand = false
            humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall, true)
            humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown, true)
            humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll, true)
            humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
        end)
    end

    S.tpState = nil
end

function FM.teleportTo(target)
    
    local position = FM.resolveWorldPosition and FM.resolveWorldPosition(target) or nil
    if typeof(position) ~= "Vector3" then
        if typeof(target) == "Vector3" then
            position = target
        elseif typeof(target) == "Instance" and target.Parent then
            local ok, p = pcall(function()
                return target.Position
            end)
            if ok and typeof(p) == "Vector3" then
                position = p
            end
        end
    end
    if typeof(position) ~= "Vector3" then
        return false
    end
    if position.X ~= position.X or position.Y ~= position.Y or position.Z ~= position.Z then
        return false
    end

    local character = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not character then
        return false
    end
    local root = FM.getRoot and FM.getRoot()
    if not root then
        return false
    end

    pcall(function()
        if FM.finishTeleport then FM.finishTeleport() end
    end)
    pcall(function()
        if Move and Move.glideStop then Move.glideStop() end
    end)

    local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if humanoid then
        pcall(function()
            if humanoid.SeatPart or humanoid.Sit then
                humanoid.Sit = false
            end
            humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown, false)
            humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll, false)
        end)
    end

    local ignore = { character }
    if typeof(target) == "Instance" then
        ignore[#ignore + 1] = target
    end

    local base = position + (TP and TP.offset or Vector3.new(0, 4.5, 0))
    local clearPos = base
    if FM.findClearGoal then
        clearPos = FM.findClearGoal(base, ignore) or base
    end
    local goalFrame = CFrame.new(clearPos)

    local mode = (S and S.farmMode) or "Tween"
    if mode == "Tween" and Move and type(Move.glide) == "function" then
        pcall(function()
            if Move.setNoclip then Move.setNoclip(true, false) end
        end)
        local okGlide = pcall(function()
            return Move.glide(goalFrame, position)
        end)
        if okGlide then
            S.tpState = {
                goal = goalFrame,
                holdUntil = os.clock() + ((TP and TP.hold) or 0.35),
            }
            return true
        end
    end

    local applied = false
    pcall(function()
        if FM.applyPivot and FM.applyPivot(goalFrame) then
            applied = true
        end
    end)
    if not applied then
        pcall(function()
            root.CFrame = goalFrame
            root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
            applied = true
        end)
    end

    S.tpState = {
        goal = goalFrame,
        holdUntil = os.clock() + ((TP and TP.hold) or 0.35),
    }
    return applied or true
end

function FM.goHomePulse()
    local remote = GoHome
    if not remote then
        remote = FM.findRemote and FM.findRemote("GoHome")
    end
    if remote then
        pcall(function()
            remote:FireServer("home")
        end)
        return true
    end
    return false
end

function FM.secureTeleportTo(position, lookAt)
    if typeof(position) == "CFrame" then
        lookAt = lookAt or position.LookVector
        position = position.Position
    end
    if typeof(position) ~= "Vector3" then
        return false
    end

    if not S._forceTeleport and (S.farmMode or "Tween") ~= "Teleport" then
        lookAt = lookAt or position
        pcall(function()
            if Move and Move.setNoclip then
                Move.setNoclip(true, false)
            end
        end)
        if Move and type(Move.glide) == "function" then
            local ok, result = pcall(function()
                return Move.glide(CFrame.lookAt(position, typeof(lookAt) == "Vector3" and lookAt or position))
            end)
            if ok then
                return result ~= false
            end
        end
        return FM.hardTeleport(position)
    end

    local character = LocalPlayer.Character
    local root = FM.getRoot()
    if not root or not character then
        return false
    end

    if Move and Move.glideStop then
        pcall(Move.glideStop)
    end

    local look = lookAt
    local cf
    if typeof(look) == "Vector3" then
        local dir = look - position
        if dir.Magnitude > 0.05 then
            cf = CFrame.new(position, position + dir.Unit)
        else
            cf = CFrame.new(position)
        end
    else
        cf = CFrame.new(position)
    end

    FM.goHomePulse()
    if FM.applyPivot then
        local ok = FM.applyPivot(cf)
        if ok then
            return true
        end
    end
    pcall(function()
        root.CFrame = cf
        root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
        root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
    end)
    return true
end

function FM.hardTeleport(target)
    
    if not S._forceTeleport and (S.farmMode or "Tween") ~= "Teleport" then
        return FM.moveTo(target, target)
    end
    local position
    if typeof(target) == "Vector3" then
        position = target
    elseif typeof(target) == "Instance" and target.Parent then
        position = target.Position
    end

    if not position then
        return false
    end

    local character = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not character then
        return false
    end

    local root = FM.getRoot()
    if not root then
        return false
    end

    FM.finishTeleport()
    if Move and Move.glideStop then
        pcall(Move.glideStop)
    end

    local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if humanoid then
        pcall(function()
            if humanoid.SeatPart or humanoid.Sit then
                humanoid.Sit = false
            end
            humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown, false)
            humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll, false)
        end)
    end

    local ignore = { character }
    if typeof(target) == "Instance" then
        ignore[#ignore + 1] = target
    end

    local goalPos = FM.findClearGoal(position + TP.offset, ignore)
    local goalFrame = CFrame.new(goalPos, goalPos + Vector3.new(0, 0, -1))

    local mode = (S and S.farmMode) or "Tween"
    if mode == "Teleport" then
        local ok = FM.secureTeleportTo(goalPos, position)
        if not ok then
            FM.goHomePulse()
            if not FM.applyPivot(goalFrame) then
                return false
            end
        end
        S.tpState = {
            goal = goalFrame,
            holdUntil = os.clock() + TP.hold,
            usingGlide = false,
        }
        return true
    end

    if Move and Move.glide then
        local ok = Move.glide(goalFrame, position)
        if ok then
            S.tpState = {
                goal = goalFrame,
                holdUntil = os.clock() + 2.5,
                usingGlide = true,
            }
            task.spawn(function()
                local deadline = os.clock() + 4
                while os.clock() < deadline do
                    local r = FM.getRoot()
                    if r and (r.Position - goalPos).Magnitude <= 6 then
                        break
                    end
                    if Move.glide then
                        pcall(Move.glide, goalFrame, position)
                    end
                    task.wait(0.05)
                end
                if Move.glideStop then
                    pcall(Move.glideStop)
                end
                FM.applyPivot(goalFrame)
                FM.finishTeleport()
            end)
            return true
        end
    end

    FM.goHomePulse()
    if not FM.applyPivot(goalFrame) then
        return false
    end

    S.tpState = {
        goal = goalFrame,
        holdUntil = os.clock() + TP.hold,
    }

    return true
end

local pendingActions = {}
local promptRestores = {}
local claimed = {}

function FM.schedule(delay, fn)
    pendingActions[#pendingActions + 1] = { at = os.clock() + delay, fn = fn }
end

local promptCache = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })

function FM.crystalPrompt(inst)
    local cached = promptCache[inst]
    if cached and cached.Parent then
        return cached
    end

    local ok, prompt = pcall(inst.FindFirstChildOfClass, inst, "ProximityPrompt")

    if not (ok and prompt) then
        ok, prompt = pcall(inst.FindFirstChildWhichIsA, inst, "ProximityPrompt", true)
    end

    if ok and prompt then
        promptCache[inst] = prompt
        return prompt
    end

    promptCache[inst] = nil

    return nil
end

function FM.surfaceDistance(part, origin)
    local ok, distance = pcall(function()
        local point = part.CFrame:PointToObjectSpace(origin)
        local half = part.Size * 0.5
        local clamped = Vector3.new(
            math.clamp(point.X, -half.X, half.X),
            math.clamp(point.Y, -half.Y, half.Y),
            math.clamp(point.Z, -half.Z, half.Z)
        )
        return (point - clamped).Magnitude
    end)

    if ok and distance then
        return distance
    end

    return (part.Position - origin).Magnitude
end

function FM.firePrompt(prompt)
    if not promptRestores[prompt] then
        promptRestores[prompt] = {
            hold = prompt.HoldDuration,
            sight = prompt.RequiresLineOfSight,
            enabled = prompt.Enabled,
            range = prompt.MaxActivationDistance,
        }
    end

    pcall(function()
        prompt.HoldDuration = 0
        prompt.RequiresLineOfSight = false
        prompt.Enabled = true
        prompt.MaxActivationDistance = 1000
    end)

    local fired = false

    if typeof(fireproximityprompt) == "function" then
        fired = pcall(fireproximityprompt, prompt, 1)
        if not fired then
            fired = pcall(fireproximityprompt, prompt)
        end
    end

    if not fired then
        fired = pcall(function()
            prompt:InputHoldBegin()
            prompt:InputHoldEnd()
        end)
    end

    FM.schedule(PICK.restore, function()
        local saved = promptRestores[prompt]
        if not saved then
            return
        end

        promptRestores[prompt] = nil

        if prompt.Parent then
            prompt.HoldDuration = saved.hold
            prompt.RequiresLineOfSight = saved.sight
            prompt.Enabled = saved.enabled
            prompt.MaxActivationDistance = saved.range
        end
    end)

    return fired
end

local pickupParams = OverlapParams.new()
pickupParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude

pcall(function()
    pickupParams.MaxParts = 120
    pickupParams.RespectCanCollide = false
end)

local pickupFound = {}
local pickupSeen = {}

function FM.pickupCandidates(free, origin, filterFunc)
    local found = pickupFound
    local seen = pickupSeen
    table.clear(found)
    table.clear(seen)
    local now = os.clock()

    local function consider(child)
        if not child or seen[child] then
            return
        end
        seen[child] = true

        if not child.Parent or not FM.isCrystal(child) or FM.getAttr(child, "Collected") == true then
            return
        end

        local claim = claimed[child]
        if claim and now - claim < PICK.retry then
            return
        end

        if filterFunc and not filterFunc(child) then
            return
        end

        local value = FM.crystalValue(child)

        if not filterFunc then
            if not FM.meetsFilter(child, value) then
                return
            end
        end

        local weight = FM.crystalWeight(child)
        if weight > free then
            return
        end

        local distance = FM.surfaceDistance(child, origin)
        if distance > PICK.range then
            return
        end

        found[#found + 1] = {
            inst = child,
            prompt = FM.crystalPrompt(child),
            value = value,
            weight = weight,
            distance = distance,
        }
    end

    pickupParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = { LocalPlayer.Character or LocalPlayer }

    local ok, hits = pcall(function()
        return Workspace:GetPartBoundsInRadius(origin, PICK.range + PICK.pad, pickupParams)
    end)

    if ok and hits then
        for _, part in ipairs(hits) do
            consider(part)
        end
    end

    FM.eachContainer(function(container)
        for _, child in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
            if child:IsA("BasePart") then
                consider(child)
            elseif child:IsA("Model") then
                for _, inner in ipairs(child:GetChildren()) do
                    consider(inner)
                end
            end
        end
    end)

    for inst in pairs(S.registry) do
        consider(inst)
    end

    table.sort(found, function(a, b)
        if a.value == b.value then
            return a.distance < b.distance
        end
        return a.value > b.value
    end)

    return found
end

function FM.markPickupBusy(seconds)
    seconds = tonumber(seconds) or 0.55
    local untilT = os.clock() + math.max(0.15, seconds)
    if (S._pickupBusyUntil or 0) < untilT then
        S._pickupBusyUntil = untilT
    end
end

function FM.isPickupBusy()
    -- Pickup busy must never stall active farm features (AutoBoulder / AutoMoney / Vein dig)
    if S.boulderFarmActive or S.moneyFarmActive or S.farmActive or S.miningBoulder then
        return false
    end
    if S.veinFarmActive and not S._veinLootPhase then
        return false
    end
    if S.veinFarmActive and S._veinLootPhase then
        return true
    end
    return os.clock() < (S._pickupBusyUntil or 0)
end

function FM.grabCrystal(inst, prompt)
    local sent = false
    FM.markPickupBusy(0.35)

    if HoldComplete then
        sent = pcall(function()
            HoldComplete:FireServer(inst)
        end)
    end

    if not prompt then
        prompt = FM.crystalPrompt(inst)
    end

    if prompt and prompt.Parent and FM.firePrompt(prompt) then
        sent = true
    end

    if not sent and typeof(fireclickdetector) == "function" then
        local ok, detector = pcall(inst.FindFirstChildWhichIsA, inst, "ClickDetector", true)
        if ok and detector then
            sent = pcall(fireclickdetector, detector, 0)
        end
    end

    return sent
end

function FM.instantPromptPatch(prompt)
    if not prompt or not prompt.Parent then
        return
    end
    if not S.instantPatched[prompt] and not promptRestores[prompt] then
        S.instantPatched[prompt] = {
            hold = prompt.HoldDuration,
            sight = prompt.RequiresLineOfSight,
            enabled = prompt.Enabled,
            maxDist = prompt.MaxActivationDistance,
            clickable = prompt.ClickablePrompt,
        }
    end

    pcall(function()
        prompt.HoldDuration = 0
        prompt.RequiresLineOfSight = false
        prompt.Enabled = true
        prompt.ClickablePrompt = true
    end)
end

function FM.restoreInstantPrompts()
    for prompt, saved in pairs(S.instantPatched) do
        if prompt.Parent then
            pcall(function()
                prompt.HoldDuration = saved.hold
                prompt.RequiresLineOfSight = saved.sight
                prompt.Enabled = saved.enabled
                if saved.maxDist ~= nil then
                    prompt.MaxActivationDistance = saved.maxDist
                end
                if saved.clickable ~= nil then
                    prompt.ClickablePrompt = saved.clickable
                end
            end)
        end
    end

    table.clear(S.instantPatched)
end

function FM.nearbyCrystalParts(origin, radius)
    pickupParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = { LocalPlayer.Character or LocalPlayer }

    local ok, hits = pcall(function()
        return Workspace:GetPartBoundsInRadius(origin, radius, pickupParams)
    end)

    if ok and hits then
        return hits
    end

    return nil
end

function FM.isRuneLike(inst)
    if not inst then
        return false
    end
    if FM.getAttr(inst, "IsRune") == true then
        return true
    end
    if FM.getAttr(inst, "RuneId") ~= nil or FM.getAttr(inst, "RuneName") ~= nil then
        return true
    end
    if type(inst.Name) == "string" and inst.Name:find("Rune", 1, true) then
        return true
    end
    return false
end

function FM.refreshInstantPrompts()
    local root = FM.getRoot()
    if not root then
        return
    end

    for prompt in pairs(S.instantPatched) do
        if not prompt.Parent then
            S.instantPatched[prompt] = nil
        end
    end

    local radius = PICK.instantRadius or 55
    local maxParts = PICK.instantMaxParts or 50
    local maxPatch = PICK.instantMaxPatch or 20
    local patched = 0

    local function tryPatchFrom(obj)
        if patched >= maxPatch then
            return
        end
        if not obj then
            return
        end
        local prompt = obj:FindFirstChildOfClass("ProximityPrompt")
        if not prompt then
            prompt = obj:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ProximityPrompt", true)
        end
        if prompt and not promptRestores[prompt] then
            FM.instantPromptPatch(prompt)
            patched += 1
        end
    end

    local origin = root.Position
    for inst in pairs(S.registry) do
        if patched >= maxPatch then
            break
        end
        if inst and inst.Parent and inst:IsA("BasePart") then
            local dist = (inst.Position - origin).Magnitude
            if dist <= radius then
                tryPatchFrom(inst)
                if inst.Parent and inst.Parent ~= Workspace then
                    tryPatchFrom(inst.Parent)
                end
            end
        end
    end

    if patched < maxPatch then
        local params = OverlapParams.new()
        params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
        params.FilterDescendantsInstances = { LocalPlayer.Character or LocalPlayer }
        pcall(function()
            params.MaxParts = maxParts
        end)

        local ok, parts = pcall(function()
            return Workspace:GetPartBoundsInRadius(origin, radius, params)
        end)

        if ok and parts then
            for _, part in ipairs(parts) do
                if patched >= maxPatch then
                    break
                end
                if part and part.Parent then
                    tryPatchFrom(part)
                    local parent = part.Parent
                    if parent and parent ~= Workspace and parent ~= LocalPlayer.Character then
                        tryPatchFrom(parent)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
end

function FM.isBackpackCrystalTool(tool)
    if not tool or not tool:IsA("Tool") then
        return false
    end
    if FM.getAttr(tool, "Tier") ~= nil and FM.getAttr(tool, "WeightKg") ~= nil then
        return true
    end
    if FM.getAttr(tool, "Value") ~= nil and FM.getAttr(tool, "WeightKg") ~= nil then
        return true
    end
    return false
end

function FM.collectBackpackCrystals()
    local list = {}
    local function scan(container)
        if not container then
            return
        end
        for _, child in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
            if FM.isBackpackCrystalTool(child) then
                list[#list + 1] = child
            end
        end
    end
    scan(LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack"))
    scan(LocalPlayer.Character)
    return list
end

function FM.dropAllCrystals()
    local remote = FM.findRemote("CrystalDropRequest")
    if not remote then
        WindUI:Notify({
            Title = "Drop Crystals",
            Content = "CrystalDropRequest missing",
            Duration = 3,
            Icon = "alert-circle",
        })
        return
    end

    local crystals = FM.collectBackpackCrystals()
    if #crystals == 0 then
        WindUI:Notify({
            Title = "Drop Crystals",
            Content = "No crystals in backpack",
            Duration = 2,
            Icon = "alert-circle",
        })
        return
    end

    if S._droppingCrystals then
        return
    end
    S._droppingCrystals = true
    S._dropLockAt = os.clock()
    S._stopCrystalOps = false

    task.spawn(function()
        local timing = FM.getDropPickupTiming()
        local humanoid = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local dropped = 0

        for _, tool in ipairs(crystals) do
            if S._stopCrystalOps then
                break
            end
            if not tool or not tool.Parent then
                continue
            end

            FM.rememberDroppedCrystal(tool)

            if humanoid and tool.Parent ~= LocalPlayer.Character then
                pcall(function()
                    humanoid:EquipTool(tool)
                end)
                task.wait(timing.dropEquip)
            end

            local held = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild(tool.Name)
            local name = (held and held.Name) or tool.Name

            local ok = pcall(function()
                remote:FireServer(name)
            end)
            if ok then
                dropped += 1
            end
            task.wait(timing.dropGap)
        end

        S._droppingCrystals = false
        WindUI:Notify({
            Title = "Drop Crystals",
            Content = string.format("Dropped %d (%s)", dropped, S.pickupMode or "Legit"),
            Duration = 2,
            Icon = dropped > 0 and "check" or "alert-circle",
        })
    end)
end


function FM.pickupAllDroppedCrystals()
    FM.markPickupBusy(2.5)
    local root = FM.getRoot()
    if not root then return end
    if S._pickingDropped then return end
    S._pickingDropped = true
    S._stopCrystalOps = false
    task.spawn(function()
        local origin = root.Position
        local radius = 120
        local targets, seen = {}, {}
        local function addPart(part)
            if not part or seen[part] or not part:IsA("BasePart") then return end
            if not FM.isInDroppedFolder(part) then return end
            if FM.getAttr(part, "Collected") == true then return end
            if not (FM.isCrystal(part) or FM.getAttr(part, "Value") ~= nil or FM.getAttr(part, "Tier") ~= nil) then return end
            if (part.Position - origin).Magnitude > radius then return end
            seen[part] = true
            targets[#targets + 1] = part
        end
        local function scan(folder)
            if not folder then return end
            for _, d in ipairs(folder:GetDescendants()) do
                if d:IsA("BasePart") then addPart(d) end
            end
        end
        scan(Workspace:FindFirstChild("DroppedCrystals"))
        local things = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Things")
        if things then scan(things:FindFirstChild("DroppedCrystals")) end
        for _, child in ipairs(Workspace:GetDescendants()) do
            if child.Name == "DroppedCrystals" then scan(child) end
        end
        table.sort(targets, function(a, b)
            return (a.Position - origin).Magnitude < (b.Position - origin).Magnitude
        end)
        local picked = 0
        for _, crystal in ipairs(targets) do
            if S._stopCrystalOps then break end
            if crystal.Parent and FM.getAttr(crystal, "Collected") ~= true then
                local prompt = FM.crystalPrompt(crystal)
                local ok = false
                local Hold = HoldComplete or (FM.findRemote and FM.findRemote("CrystalHoldComplete"))
                if Hold then
                    ok = pcall(function() Hold:FireServer(crystal) end)
                end
                if not ok then
                    ok = FM.grabCrystal(crystal, prompt)
                end
                if not ok and prompt then
                    ok = FM.firePrompt(prompt)
                end
                if ok then picked += 1 end
                task.wait(0.08)
            end
        end
        S._pickingDropped = false
        WindUI:Notify({ Title = "Pickup Dropped", Content = string.format("Picked %d", picked), Duration = 3, Icon = picked > 0 and "check" or "alert-circle" })
    end)
end

function FM.setInstantPrompt(value)
    S.instantPromptActive = value == true
    S.instantAccumulator = math.huge

    if S._instantPromptHoldConn then
        S._instantPromptHoldConn:Disconnect()
