        S._instantPromptHoldConn = nil
    end
    if S._instantPromptShownConn then
        S._instantPromptShownConn:Disconnect()
        S._instantPromptShownConn = nil
    end
    if S._instantDescendantConn then
        S._instantDescendantConn:Disconnect()
        S._instantDescendantConn = nil
    end
    if S._instantPromptServiceConn then
        S._instantPromptServiceConn:Disconnect()
        S._instantPromptServiceConn = nil
    end

    if not S.instantPromptActive then
        return
    end

    local ok, PPS = pcall(function()
        return game:GetService("ProximityPromptService")
    end)
    if not ok or not PPS then
        return
    end

    local lastFire = 0

    local function fireManual(prompt)
        if not prompt or not prompt.Parent then
            return
        end
        local now = os.clock()
        if now - lastFire < 0.08 then
            return
        end
        lastFire = now

        FM.firePrompt(prompt)

        local inst = prompt.Parent
        if inst and not inst:IsA("BasePart") then
        end
        local crystal = nil
        local node = prompt.Parent
        for _ = 1, 6 do
            if not node then
                break
            end
            if FM.isCrystal and FM.isCrystal(node) then
                crystal = node
                break
            end
            if node:IsA("BasePart") and FM.isCrystal and FM.isCrystal(node) then
                crystal = node
                break
            end
            if FM.getAttr and FM.getAttr(node, "Value") ~= nil then
                crystal = node
                break
            end
            node = node.Parent
        end
        if crystal and HoldComplete then
            pcall(function()
                HoldComplete:FireServer(crystal)
            end)
        end
    end

    S._instantPromptHoldConn = PPS.PromptButtonHoldBegan:Connect(function(prompt, player)
        if not S.instantPromptActive then
            return
        end
        if player ~= nil and player ~= LocalPlayer then
            return
        end
        fireManual(prompt)
    end)

    pcall(function()
        S._instantPromptShownConn = PPS.PromptTriggered:Connect(function(prompt, player)
            if not S.instantPromptActive then
                return
            end
            if player ~= nil and player ~= LocalPlayer then
                return
            end
            fireManual(prompt)
        end)
    end)
end

function FM.instantGrab()
    if not S.instantPromptActive then
        return
    end

    local root = FM.getRoot()
    if not root then
        return
    end

    local origin = root.Position
    local look = root.CFrame.LookVector
    local best, bestScore = nil, -1e9
    local maxDist = 14

    local function consider(prompt)
        if not prompt or not prompt:IsA("ProximityPrompt") or not prompt.Parent then
            return
        end
        if prompt.Enabled == false then
            return
        end
        local parent = prompt.Parent
        local pos = nil
        if parent:IsA("BasePart") then
            pos = parent.Position
        elseif parent:IsA("Model") then
            local ok, pivot = pcall(function()
                return parent:GetPivot().Position
            end)
            pos = ok and pivot or nil
            if not pos then
                local p = parent.PrimaryPart or parent:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
                pos = p and p.Position
            end
        end
        if not pos then
            return
        end
        local offset = pos - origin
        local dist = offset.Magnitude
        if dist > maxDist then
            return
        end
        local dir = dist > 0.05 and offset.Unit or look
        local facing = look:Dot(dir)
        local score = facing * 12 - dist
        if score > bestScore then
            bestScore = score
            best = prompt
        end
    end

    if S.registry then
        for inst in pairs(S.registry) do
            if inst and inst.Parent then
                local prompt = FM.crystalPrompt and FM.crystalPrompt(inst) or nil
                if prompt then
                    consider(prompt)
                end
            end
        end
    end

    local params = OverlapParams.new()
    params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
    params.FilterDescendantsInstances = { LocalPlayer.Character or LocalPlayer }
    pcall(function()
        params.MaxParts = 40
    end)
    local ok, parts = pcall(function()
        return Workspace:GetPartBoundsInRadius(origin, maxDist, params)
    end)
    if ok and parts then
        for _, part in ipairs(parts) do
            if part and part.Parent then
                local prompt = part:FindFirstChildOfClass("ProximityPrompt")
                    or part:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ProximityPrompt", true)
                if not prompt and part.Parent then
                    prompt = part.Parent:FindFirstChildOfClass("ProximityPrompt")
                end
                if prompt then
                    consider(prompt)
                end
            end
        end
    end

    if best then
        FM.firePrompt(best)
        local node = best.Parent
        for _ = 1, 6 do
            if not node then
                break
            end
            if HoldComplete and FM.getAttr and FM.getAttr(node, "Value") ~= nil then
                pcall(function()
                    HoldComplete:FireServer(node)
                end)
                break
            end
            if HoldComplete and FM.isCrystal and FM.isCrystal(node) then
                pcall(function()
                    HoldComplete:FireServer(node)
                end)
                break
            end
            node = node.Parent
        end
    end
end

function FM.pickupStep(filterFunc)
    local now = os.clock()

    local cooldown, burstLimit
    if filterFunc then
        cooldown, burstLimit = FM.getPickupTiming()
    else
        cooldown = PICK.cooldown
        burstLimit = PICK.burst
    end

    if now - S.lastPickup < cooldown then
        return
    end

    local root = FM.getRoot()
    if not root then
        return
    end

    local free = FM.backpackFree()
    if free <= 0 then
        if now - S.lastBagWarn >= 12 then
            S.lastBagWarn = now
            WindUI:Notify({
                Title = "FayyMeng",
                Content = "Backpack full - pickup paused",
                Duration = 2,
                Icon = "alert-circle",
            })
        end
        return
    end

    for inst, stamp in pairs(claimed) do
        if now - stamp >= PICK.forget or not inst.Parent then
            claimed[inst] = nil
        end
    end

    local list = FM.pickupCandidates(free, root.Position, filterFunc)
    if #list == 0 then
        if free > 0.5 then
            FM.requestStream(root.Position)
        end
        return
    end

    local budget = free
    local grabs = 0

    for _, entry in ipairs(list) do
        if grabs >= burstLimit then
            break
        end
        if entry.weight <= budget then
            claimed[entry.inst] = now
            if FM.grabCrystal(entry.inst, entry.prompt) then
                budget -= entry.weight
                grabs += 1
            end
        end
    end

    if grabs > 0 then
        S.lastPickup = now
        if not S.farmActive and not S.miningBoulder then
            FM.markPickupBusy(0.3 + grabs * 0.05)
        end
    end
end

function FM.tryFilteredCrystalPickup(origin, free)
    if free == nil then
        free = FM.backpackFree()
    end
    if free <= 0 then
        return false
    end
    if not S.pickupValueActive and not S.pickupLuckActive and not S.pickupRarityActive then
        return false
    end

    local now = os.clock()
    local cooldown, burstLimit = FM.getPickupTiming()
    if now - (S.lastFilteredPickup or 0) < cooldown then
        return false
    end

    local list = FM.pickupCandidates(free, origin, FM.valueLuckFilter)
    if not list or #list == 0 then
        return false
    end

    local grabs = 0
    for _, entry in ipairs(list) do
        if grabs >= burstLimit then
            break
        end
        if entry.inst and entry.inst.Parent and FM.getAttr(entry.inst, "Collected") ~= true then
            if entry.weight <= free then
                claimed[entry.inst] = now
                if FM.grabCrystal(entry.inst, entry.prompt) then
                    free -= entry.weight
                    grabs += 1
                end
            end
        end
    end

    if grabs > 0 then
        S.lastFilteredPickup = now
        S.lastPickup = now
        if not S.farmActive and not S.miningBoulder then
            FM.markPickupBusy(0.3 + grabs * 0.05)
        end
        return true
    end
    return false
end

function FM.updateBackpackLabel()
    if not S.BackpackLabel then
        return
    end

    local capacity = FM.backpackCapacity()
    local used = FM.backpackWeight()

    if capacity == math.huge then
        S.BackpackLabel:SetText(string.format("Bag %.1f / \u{221E} kg", used))
        return
    end

    local free = math.max(0, capacity - used)
    S.BackpackLabel:SetText(string.format("Bag %.1f / %.1f kg\nFree %.1f kg", used, capacity, free))
end

function FM.getBaseSpeed()
    local n = LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("BaseSpeed")
    if type(n) == "number" and n > 0 then
        return n
    end
    local folder = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("GamepassesOwned")
    local flag = folder and folder:FindFirstChild("Speed2x")
    if flag and flag:IsA("BoolValue") and flag.Value then
        return 40
    end
    return 25
end

function FM.getBaseJump()
    local n = LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("BaseJump")
    if type(n) == "number" and n > 0 then
        return n
    end
    return 50
end

function FM.enforceSpeed(humanoid)
    if not humanoid then
        return
    end
    local target = S.speedActive and (PACE.boost or 40) or FM.getBaseSpeed()
    if humanoid.WalkSpeed ~= target then
        pcall(function()
            humanoid.WalkSpeed = target
        end)
    end
end

function FM.restoreUpright(character)
    character = character or LocalPlayer.Character
    if not character then
        return
    end
    local root = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not root then
        return
    end
    local constraint = root:FindFirstChild("UprightConstraint")
    if constraint then
        pcall(function()
            constraint.Enabled = character:GetAttribute("Ragdolled") ~= true
        end)
    end
end

function FM.hasFastMineBoost()
    local data = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerData")
    local stats = data and data:FindFirstChild("RealStats")
    local val = stats and stats:FindFirstChild("FastMineBoostSeconds")
    if val and (val:IsA("NumberValue") or val:IsA("IntValue") or val:IsA("NumberValue")) then
        return (tonumber(val.Value) or 0) > 0, tonumber(val.Value) or 0
    end
    return false, 0
end

function FM.watchSpeed(humanoid)
    if S.speedHooked == humanoid then
        return
    end

    if S.speedConn then
        S.speedConn:Disconnect()
        S.speedConn = nil
    end

    S.speedHooked = humanoid
    if not humanoid then
        return
    end

    local ok, connection = pcall(function()
        return humanoid:GetPropertyChangedSignal("WalkSpeed"):Connect(function()
            if S.speedActive then
                FM.enforceSpeed(humanoid)
            end
        end)
    end)

    if ok then
        S.speedConn = connection
    end
end

function FM.setSpeedBoost(value)
    S.speedActive = value == true
    S._speedWanted = S.speedActive

    local character = LocalPlayer.Character
    local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

    if S.speedActive then
        FM.watchSpeed(humanoid)
        FM.enforceSpeed(humanoid)
        return
    end

    FM.watchSpeed(nil)

    if humanoid then
        pcall(function()
            humanoid.WalkSpeed = FM.getBaseSpeed()
        end)
    end
end

pcall(function()
    Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function()
        if S.playerEspActive then
            task.defer(function()
                pcall(FM.updatePlayerEsp)
            end)
        end
    end)
    Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(player)
        if S.playerEspActive then
            pcall(function()
                if S.playerCache and S.playerCache[player] then
                end
                FM.updatePlayerEsp()
            end)
        end
    end)
end)

local schedulerConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(deltaTime)
    if S.tpState then
        local ok, err = pcall(function()
            if not S.tpState.usingGlide then
                if not FM.applyPivot(S.tpState.goal) then
                    FM.finishTeleport()
                    return
                end
            end

            if os.clock() >= S.tpState.holdUntil then
                if S.tpState.usingGlide and Move and Move.glideStop then
                    pcall(Move.glideStop)
                end
                if S.tpState.goal then
                    FM.applyPivot(S.tpState.goal)
                end
                FM.finishTeleport()
            end
        end)

        if not ok then
            FM.finishTeleport()
            FM.reportError("teleport", err)
        end
    end

    -- Isolated systems own crystal pickup; skip legacy global crystal pickup worker
    if FM.BoulderPickupSystem or FM.MoneyPickupSystem then
        -- legacy crystal auto-pickup disabled (use Boulder/Money tab toggles)
    elseif S.autoPickupActive or S.pickupValueActive or S.pickupLuckActive or S.pickupRarityActive then
        S._pickupAcc = (S._pickupAcc or 0) + deltaTime
        local tickRate = PACE.pickup or 0.28
        if S.pickupValueActive or S.pickupLuckActive or S.pickupRarityActive then
            tickRate = ((S.pickupMode or "Legit") == "Burst") and 0.14 or 0.26
        end
        if S._pickupAcc >= tickRate then
            S._pickupAcc = 0
            local filterFunc = nil
            if S.pickupValueActive or S.pickupLuckActive or S.pickupRarityActive then
                filterFunc = FM.valueLuckFilter
            end
            local ok, err = pcall(FM.pickupStep, filterFunc)
            if not ok then
                FM.reportError("pickup", err)
            end
        end
    end

    if S.speedActive then
        S._speedAcc = (S._speedAcc or 0) + deltaTime
        if S._speedAcc >= 0.2 then
            S._speedAcc = 0
            local body = LocalPlayer.Character
            local mover = body and body:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if mover then
                FM.watchSpeed(mover)
                FM.enforceSpeed(mover)
            end
        end
    end

    S._watchAcc = (S._watchAcc or 0) + deltaTime
    if S._watchAcc >= 6 then
        S._watchAcc = 0
        local now = os.clock()
        if S._placingCrystals and S._placeLockAt and (now - S._placeLockAt) > 45 then
            S._placingCrystals = false
        end
        if S._pickingDropped and S._pickLockAt and (now - S._pickLockAt) > 45 then
            S._pickingDropped = false
        end
        if S._droppingCrystals and S._dropLockAt and (now - S._dropLockAt) > 45 then
            S._droppingCrystals = false
        end
        if S._placingRunes and S._runeLockAt and (now - S._runeLockAt) > 60 then
            S._placingRunes = false
        end
        if S._flyWanted and Move and Move.setFly and not Move.isFlyOn() then
            pcall(Move.setFly, true)
        end
        if S._noclipWanted and Move and Move.setNoclip and not Move.isNoclipOn() then
            pcall(Move.setNoclip, true)
        end
    end

    if S.playerEspActive then
        S._playerEspAcc = (S._playerEspAcc or 0) + deltaTime
        if S._playerEspAcc >= (PACE.playerEsp or 0.25) then
            S._playerEspAcc = 0
            local ok, err = pcall(FM.updatePlayerEsp)
            if not ok then
                FM.reportError("playerEsp", err)
            end
        end
    end

    S.statsAccumulator += deltaTime
    if S.statsAccumulator >= PACE.stats then
        S.statsAccumulator = 0
        local ok, err = pcall(FM.updateBackpackLabel)
        if not ok then
            FM.reportError("backpack", err)
        end
    end

    if #pendingActions == 0 then
        return
    end

    local now = os.clock()
    for index = #pendingActions, 1, -1 do
        local job = pendingActions[index]
        if now >= job.at then
            table.remove(pendingActions, index)
            local ok, err = pcall(job.fn)
            if not ok then
                FM.reportError("action", err)
            end
        end
    end
end)

function FM.sortedByScore(scoreFn)
    local scored = {}
    local seen = {}

    local function consider(inst)
        if seen[inst] or not inst.Parent then
            return
        end

        if FM.getAttr(inst, "Collected") == true then
            return
        end

        seen[inst] = true

        local ok, score = pcall(scoreFn, inst)
        scored[#scored + 1] = { inst = inst, score = ok and score or 0 }
    end

    for inst in pairs(S.registry) do
        consider(inst)
    end

    FM.eachContainer(function(container)
        for _, child in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
            if FM.isCrystal(child) then
                consider(child)
            end
        end
    end)

    table.sort(scored, function(a, b)
        return a.score > b.score
    end)

    return scored
end

local Mountain = {}
local mountainConn

local aimParams = RaycastParams.new()
aimParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
aimParams.IgnoreWater = true

function FM.getAimedCrystal()
    local unitRay = Mouse.UnitRay
    local origin = unitRay.Origin
    local direction = unitRay.Direction.Unit

    local character = LocalPlayer.Character
    aimParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = character and { character } or {}

    local hit = Workspace:Raycast(origin, direction * PICK.aimRange, aimParams)
    if hit and hit.Instance and (S.registry[hit.Instance] or FM.isCrystal(hit.Instance)) then
        return hit.Instance
    end

    local best, bestDot
    local seen = {}

    local function consider(inst)
        if seen[inst] or not inst.Parent then
            return
        end

        seen[inst] = true

        local offset = (inst.Position + ESP.offset) - origin
        local magnitude = offset.Magnitude
        if magnitude > 0 then
            local dot = direction:Dot(offset / magnitude)
            if not bestDot or dot > bestDot then
                bestDot = dot
                best = inst
            end
        end
    end

    for inst in pairs(S.espCache) do
        consider(inst)
    end

    FM.eachContainer(function(container)
        for _, child in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
            if FM.isCrystal(child) then
                consider(child)
            end
        end
    end)

    if best and bestDot and bestDot >= PICK.aimDot then
        return best
    end
    return nil
end

function FM.aimTeleport()
    if not S.espActive then
        WindUI:Notify({
            Title = "FayyMeng",
            Content = "Enable Crystal ESP first",
            Duration = 3,
            Icon = "alert-circle",
        })
        return
    end

    local inst = FM.getAimedCrystal()
    if not inst then
        WindUI:Notify({
            Title = "FayyMeng",
            Content = "No crystal aimed",
            Duration = 2,
            Icon = "alert-circle",
        })
        return
    end

    FM.teleportTo(inst)
end

function FM.tpToRank(scoreFn, rank, formatter)
    local entry = FM.sortedByScore(scoreFn)[rank]
    if not entry or entry.score <= 0 then
        pcall(function()
            WindUI:Notify({
                Title = "FayyMeng",
                Content = string.format("No crystal for #%d", rank),
                Duration = 3,
            })
        end)
        return
    end

    
    local moved = false
    pcall(function()
        moved = FM.teleportTo(entry.inst) == true
    end)
    
    if not moved then
        pcall(function()
            local pos = FM.resolveWorldPosition and FM.resolveWorldPosition(entry.inst)
            local root = FM.getRoot and FM.getRoot()
            if pos and root and (root.Position - pos).Magnitude < 25 then
                moved = true
            end
        end)
    end

    local name = "Crystal"
    pcall(function()
        name = (FM.crystalName and FM.crystalName(entry.inst)) or name
    end)
    local label = tostring(entry.score or "")
    pcall(function()
        if type(formatter) == "function" then
            
            local ok, text = pcall(formatter, entry.inst, entry.score)
            if ok and text ~= nil then
                label = tostring(text)
            end
        end
    end)

    pcall(function()
        WindUI:Notify({
            Title = "FayyMeng",
            Content = moved
                and string.format("TP #%d %s (%s)", rank, name, label)
                or "Teleport failed",
            Duration = 3,
        })
    end)
end

function FM.fireRemote(remote, ...)
    if not remote then
        return false
    end

    local args = table.pack(...)
    local ok = pcall(function()
        remote:FireServer(table.unpack(args, 1, args.n))
    end)

    return ok
end

function FM.unfavoriteAll()
    local cleared = 0

    local function scan(container)
        if not container then
            return
        end

        for _, child in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
            if child:IsA("Tool") and child:GetAttribute("Favorited") == true then
                pcall(function()
                    child:SetAttribute("Favorited", false)
                end)
                FM.fireRemote(ToggleFavorite, child, false)
                cleared += 1
            end
        end
    end

    scan(LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack"))
    scan(LocalPlayer.Character)

    return cleared
end

function FM.listBoulderPositions()
    local positions = {}
    local seen = {}
    local function push(pos)
        if typeof(pos) ~= "Vector3" then
            return
        end
        local key = string.format("%d:%d:%d", math.floor(pos.X / 8), math.floor(pos.Y / 8), math.floor(pos.Z / 8))
        if seen[key] then
            return
        end
        seen[key] = true
        positions[#positions + 1] = pos
    end
    local function addModel(model)
        if not model then
            return
        end
        local ok, pivot = pcall(function()
            return model:GetPivot().Position
        end)
        if ok and typeof(pivot) == "Vector3" then
            push(pivot)
            return
        end
        local part = model.PrimaryPart or model:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
        if part then
            push(part.Position)
        end
    end
    local function scanContainer(container)
        if not container then
            return
        end
        for _, child in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
            local name = child.Name
            if type(name) == "string" then
                local lower = string.lower(name)
                if lower:find("boulder", 1, true) or lower:find("rock", 1, true) then
                    addModel(child)
                elseif child:IsA("Folder") or child:IsA("Model") then
                    for _, sub in ipairs(child:GetChildren()) do
                        local sn = string.lower(tostring(sub.Name or ""))
                        if sn:find("boulder", 1, true) or sn:find("rock", 1, true) then
                            addModel(sub)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    pcall(function()
        local things = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Things")
        if things then
            scanContainer(things:FindFirstChild("Boulders"))
            local zones = things:FindFirstChild("Zones")
            if zones then
                for _, z in ipairs(zones:GetChildren()) do
                    scanContainer(z:FindFirstChild("Boulders"))
                end
            end
            scanContainer(things)
        end
    end)
    pcall(function()
        scanContainer(Workspace:FindFirstChild("Boulders"))
    end)
    
    local root = FM.getRoot and FM.getRoot()
    if root then
        local origin = root.Position
        table.sort(positions, function(a, b)
            return (a - origin).Magnitude < (b - origin).Magnitude
        end)
    end
    return positions
end

function FM.isCrystalTool(tool)
    if not tool or not tool:IsA("Tool") then
        return false
    end
    if tool:GetAttribute("Tier") ~= nil then
        return true
    end
    if tool:GetAttribute("Value") ~= nil then
        return true
    end
    if tool:GetAttribute("CrystalName") ~= nil then
        return true
    end
    return false
end

-- ============================================================================
-- AUTO FAVORITE — pick up → favorite (Cobalt: ToggleFavorite:FireServer(tool, true))
-- ============================================================================

local function FM_resolveToggleFavorite()
    local remote = ToggleFavorite
    if typeof(remote) == "Instance" and remote.Parent then
        return remote
    end
    remote = nil
    pcall(function()
        local folder = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
        if folder then
            remote = folder:FindFirstChild("ToggleFavorite")
        end
    end)
    if not remote then
        pcall(function()
            remote = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("ToggleFavorite", true)
        end)
    end
    if not remote and FM.findRemote then
        remote = FM.findRemote("ToggleFavorite")
    end
    if remote then
        ToggleFavorite = remote
    end
    return remote
end

function FM.setFavoriteTool(tool, favored)
    -- Exact Cobalt path:
    -- ReplicatedStorage.Remotes.ToggleFavorite:FireServer(tool, true/false)
    if not tool or not tool:IsA("Tool") then
        return false
    end
    favored = favored == true
    local remote = FM_resolveToggleFavorite()
    if not remote then
        return false
    end
    local ok = pcall(function()
        remote:FireServer(tool, favored)
    end)
    if ok then
        pcall(function()
            tool:SetAttribute("Favorited", favored)
        end)
    end
    return ok == true
end

local function FM_isAutoFavCrystal(tool)
    if not tool or not tool:IsA("Tool") then
        return false
    end
    local name = tostring(tool.Name or "")
    if name:find("Rune", 1, true) then
        return false
    end
    if name:lower():find("pickaxe", 1, true) or name:lower():find("shovel", 1, true) then
        return false
    end
    -- Game crystal names look like: [M] Violetstream [21.5kg]
    if name:find("%[.-kg%]") or name:find("%d+%.?%d*%s*kg") then
        return true
    end
    local okTier = false
    pcall(function()
        if tool:GetAttribute("Tier") ~= nil then okTier = true end
    end)
    if okTier then return true end
    local okVal = false
    pcall(function()
        if tool:GetAttribute("Value") ~= nil then okVal = true end
        if tool:GetAttribute("WeightKg") ~= nil then okVal = true end
    end)
    if okVal then return true end
    if FM.isCrystalTool and FM.isCrystalTool(tool) then
        return true
    end
    return false
end

function FM.crystalMatchesFavFilter(tool)
    return true
end

function FM.favoriteMatching(wantFavorite)
    wantFavorite = wantFavorite ~= false
    local changed = 0
    local function consider(child)
        if not FM_isAutoFavCrystal(child) then
            return
        end
        local isFav = child:GetAttribute("Favorited") == true
        if wantFavorite and not isFav then
            if FM.setFavoriteTool(child, true) then
                changed += 1
            end
        elseif (not wantFavorite) and isFav then
            if FM.setFavoriteTool(child, false) then
                changed += 1
            end
        end
    end
    local function scan(container)
        if not container then return end
        for _, child in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
            consider(child)
        end
    end
    scan(LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack"))
    scan(LocalPlayer.Character)
    return changed
end

local _autoFavGen = 0

local function FM_setAutoFavToggleUI(on)
    -- Safe single UI write; never recurse into Callback storms
    on = on == true
    if S._autoFavUiLock then
        return
    end
    local toggle = FM._autoFavToggle or S._autoFavToggle
    if type(toggle) ~= "table" then
        pcall(function()
            local opts = FluentLibrary and FluentLibrary.Options
            if type(opts) == "table" then
                toggle = opts.cfg_AutoFavorite or opts["cfg_AutoFavorite"]
            end
        end)
    end
    if type(toggle) ~= "table" then
        return
    end
    local cur = toggle.Value
    if cur == on then
        return
    end
    S._autoFavUiLock = true
    pcall(function()
        if toggle.SetValue then
            toggle:SetValue(on)
        elseif toggle.Set then
            toggle:Set(on)
        else
            toggle.Value = on
        end
    end)
    task.defer(function()
        S._autoFavUiLock = false
    end)
end

function FM.setAutoFavToggleUI(on)
    return FM_setAutoFavToggleUI(on)
end

function FM.setAutoFavorite(on)
    on = on == true
    -- Always bump gen so old workers die
    _autoFavGen += 1
    local myGen = _autoFavGen
    S.autoFavActive = on

    if not on then
        S._autoFavRunning = false
        return
    end

    -- PATH: AutoFav ↔ Money only. Boulder never controls Auto Fav.
    if FM.MoneySystem and (FM.MoneySystem.Enabled == true) then
        S.autoFavActive = false
        S._autoFavRunning = false
        return
    end
    -- BoulderSystem / BoulderPickupSystem intentionally ignored here.

    if S._autoFavRunning then
        -- worker still winding down; allow new worker below
    end
    S._autoFavRunning = true

    task.spawn(function()
        local bpConn, charConn, charChildConn
        local lastFire = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })

        local function favNow(tool)
            if not S.autoFavActive or myGen ~= _autoFavGen then
                return
            end
            if not tool or not tool:IsA("Tool") then
                return
            end
            if not FM_isAutoFavCrystal(tool) then
                return
            end
            local now = os.clock()
            local prev = lastFire[tool]
            local isFav = tool:GetAttribute("Favorited") == true
            if isFav and prev and (now - prev) < 8 then
                return
            end
            if prev and (now - prev) < 0.5 then
                return
            end
            lastFire[tool] = now
            local delaySec = tonumber(S.autoFavDelay) or 0.5
            if delaySec < 0 then delaySec = 0 end
            if delaySec > 5 then delaySec = 5 end
            task.delay(delaySec, function()
                if not S.autoFavActive or myGen ~= _autoFavGen then
                    return
                end
                if tool and tool.Parent and FM_isAutoFavCrystal(tool) then
                    FM.setFavoriteTool(tool, true)
                end
            end)
        end

        local function hookBp()
            if bpConn then
                pcall(function() bpConn:Disconnect() end)
                bpConn = nil
            end
            local bp = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
            if not bp then
                return
            end
            bpConn = bp.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
                favNow(child)
            end)
            for _, child in ipairs(bp:GetChildren()) do
                favNow(child)
            end
        end

        local function hookChar(char)
            if charChildConn then
                pcall(function() charChildConn:Disconnect() end)
                charChildConn = nil
            end
            if not char then
                return
            end
            charChildConn = char.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
                favNow(child)
            end)
            for _, child in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
                favNow(child)
            end
            hookBp()
        end

        hookBp()
        charConn = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
            if myGen == _autoFavGen then
                hookChar(char)
            end
        end)
        if LocalPlayer.Character then
            hookChar(LocalPlayer.Character)
        end

        while S.autoFavActive and myGen == _autoFavGen do
            -- Money path only (Boulder does not stop Auto Fav)
            if FM.MoneySystem and FM.MoneySystem.Enabled == true then
                S.autoFavActive = false
                break
            end

            local bp = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
            if bp and not bpConn then
                hookBp()
            end

            task.wait(1.0)
        end

        if bpConn then pcall(function() bpConn:Disconnect() end) end
        if charConn then pcall(function() charConn:Disconnect() end) end
        if charChildConn then pcall(function() charChildConn:Disconnect() end) end
        if myGen == _autoFavGen then
            S._autoFavRunning = false
        end
    end)
end

function FM.mountainSpot()
    local centerX = Workspace:GetAttribute("MountainCenterX")
    local centerZ = Workspace:GetAttribute("MountainCenterZ")

    if typeof(centerX) == "number" and typeof(centerZ) == "number" then
        local base = Workspace:GetAttribute("MountainBaseY")
        local peak = Workspace:GetAttribute("MountainPeakY")
        local height = 700

        if typeof(base) == "number" and typeof(peak) == "number" then
            height = base + (peak - base) * 0.55
        end

        return Vector3.new(centerX, height, centerZ)
    end

    local things = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Things")
    local zones = things and things:FindFirstChild("MountainZones")

    if zones then
        for _, child in ipairs(zones:GetChildren()) do
            if child:IsA("BasePart") and child.Name == "MountainZone" then
                return child.Position
            end
        end
    end

    return nil
end

function FM.mountainSpan()
    local radius = Workspace:GetAttribute("MountainRadius")

    if typeof(radius) == "number" and radius > 20 then
        return radius
    end

    return 150
end

function FM.isPointInPart(point, part)
    if not (part and part.Parent and part:IsA("BasePart")) then
        return false
    end
    local localPos = part.CFrame:PointToObjectSpace(point)
    local half = part.Size * 0.5
    return math.abs(localPos.X) <= half.X
        and math.abs(localPos.Y) <= half.Y
        and math.abs(localPos.Z) <= half.Z
end

function FM.distanceToPart(point, part)
    if not (part and part.Parent and part:IsA("BasePart")) then
        return math.huge
    end
    local localPos = part.CFrame:PointToObjectSpace(point)
    local half = part.Size * 0.5
    local dx = math.max(math.abs(localPos.X) - half.X, 0)
    local dy = math.max(math.abs(localPos.Y) - half.Y, 0)
    local dz = math.max(math.abs(localPos.Z) - half.Z, 0)
    return math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz)
end

function FM.isInMountainZone(point)
    if typeof(point) ~= "Vector3" then
        return false
    end

    local cx = Workspace:GetAttribute("MountainCenterX")
    local cz = Workspace:GetAttribute("MountainCenterZ")
    local radius = Workspace:GetAttribute("MountainRadius")
    local baseY = Workspace:GetAttribute("MountainBaseY")
    local peakY = Workspace:GetAttribute("MountainPeakY")

    if typeof(cx) == "number" and typeof(cz) == "number" and typeof(radius) == "number"
        and typeof(baseY) == "number" and typeof(peakY) == "number" then
        local dx = point.X - cx
        local dz = point.Z - cz
        local dist = math.sqrt(dx * dx + dz * dz)
        return dist <= (radius + 60)
            and point.Y >= (baseY - 120)
            and point.Y <= (peakY + 120)
    end

    local things = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Things")
    local zones = things and things:FindFirstChild("MountainZones")
    if zones then
        for _, child in ipairs(zones:GetChildren()) do
            if child:IsA("BasePart") and child.Name == "MountainZone" and FM.isPointInPart(point, child) then
                return true
            end
        end
    end

    return false
end

function FM.isInNoDigZone(point)
    local things = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Things")
    if not things then
        return false
    end

    local noDig = things:FindFirstChild("NoDigging")
    if noDig then
        if noDig:IsA("BasePart") and FM.isPointInPart(point, noDig) then
            return true
        end
        for _, child in ipairs(noDig:GetChildren()) do
            if child:IsA("BasePart") and FM.isPointInPart(point, child) then
                return true
            end
        end
    end

    local zones = things:FindFirstChild("Zones")
    if zones then
        for _, child in ipairs(zones:GetChildren()) do
            if child:IsA("BasePart") and child.Name == "NoDigZone" and FM.isPointInPart(point, child) then
                return true
            end
        end
    end

    return false
end

function FM.isAtBase()
    local root = FM.getRoot()
    if not root then
        return true
    end

    local pos = root.Position

    if FM.isInMountainZone(pos) then
        S.wasAtBase = false
        return false
    end

    local spot = FM.mountainSpot()
    if not spot then
        S.wasAtBase = pos.Y < 80
        return S.wasAtBase
    end

    local flat = Vector2.new(pos.X - spot.X, pos.Z - spot.Z)
    local dist = flat.Magnitude
    local span = FM.mountainSpan()

    if S.wasAtBase then
        S.wasAtBase = dist > (span + 20)
    else
        S.wasAtBase = dist > (span + 55)
    end

    return S.wasAtBase == true
end

local sellClock = 0

function FM.unlockMovement()
    local character = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not character then
        return
    end
    local root = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if root then
        pcall(function()
            root.Anchored = false
            root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
        end)
    end
    if humanoid then
        pcall(function()
            humanoid.PlatformStand = false
            humanoid.Sit = false
            humanoid.AutoRotate = true
            humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running, true)
            humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.RunningNoPhysics, true)
            humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall, true)
            humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping, true)
            humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp, true)
            humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
            if humanoid.WalkSpeed <= 0 then
                humanoid.WalkSpeed = (FM.getBaseSpeed and FM.getBaseSpeed()) or 25
            end
            if humanoid.JumpPower ~= nil and humanoid.JumpPower <= 0 then
                humanoid.JumpPower = 50
            end
            if humanoid.JumpHeight ~= nil and humanoid.JumpHeight <= 0 then
                humanoid.JumpHeight = 7.2
            end
            if S.speedActive or S._speedWanted then
                if FM.enforceSpeed then
                    FM.enforceSpeed(humanoid)
                end
            end
        end)
        pcall(function()
            local seat = humanoid.SeatPart
            if seat then
                humanoid.Sit = false
            end
        end)
    end
end

function FM.unlockMovementBurst(duration)
    duration = tonumber(duration) or 3
    task.spawn(function()
        local untilT = os.clock() + duration
        while os.clock() < untilT do
            FM.unlockMovement()
            task.wait(0.2)
        end
        FM.unlockMovement()
    end)
end

function FM.doSell()
    local now = os.clock()
    if now - sellClock < 1.2 then
        return false
    end

    sellClock = now

    pcall(function()
        if GoHome then
            FM.fireRemote(GoHome, "sell")
        elseif FM.goHomePulse then
            FM.goHomePulse()
        end
    end)

    FM.schedule(0.35, function()
        local remote = SellRequest or (FM.findRemote and FM.findRemote("SellRequest"))
        if remote then
            pcall(function()
                remote:FireServer("all")
            end)
        else
            FM.fireRemote(SellRequest, "all")
        end
        FM.unlockMovement()
    end)

    FM.unlockMovementBurst(3.5)
    return true
end

do
    local function install()
        local BOULDER_INFO = {
            Mossite = {
                rarity = "Common",
                pickaxe = "Titanium Spike",
                crystals = "8-11",
                runes = "Luck / Haste",
                color = Color3.fromRGB(150, 220, 120),
            },
            Voltite = {
                rarity = "Uncommon",
                pickaxe = "Celestial Apex",
                crystals = "10-14",
                runes = "Storm / Weight",
                color = Color3.fromRGB(110, 190, 240),
            },
            Gildrite = {
                rarity = "Rare",
                pickaxe = "Eclipse Fang",
                crystals = "11-15",
                runes = "Fortune / Detonation",
                color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 200, 60),
            },
            Rimeveil = {
                rarity = "Epic",
                pickaxe = "Voidreign",
                crystals = "13-18",
                runes = "Preservation / Warmth",
                color = Color3.fromRGB(170, 100, 255),
            },
            Nocturnite = {
                rarity = "Legendary",
                pickaxe = "The Terminus",
                crystals = "16-22",
                runes = "Excavator / Colossus",
                color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 80, 180),
            },
        }

        local BOULDER_OFFSET = Vector3.new(0, 7, 0)
        local BOULDER_WIDTH = 220
        local BOULDER_HEIGHT = 36
        local BOULDER_STEP = 0.75

        local GRAB_RANGE = 60
        local GRAB_STEP = 0.2
        local GRAB_LIMIT = 6
        local GRAB_RETRY = 0.15

        local boulderEsp = false
        local autoGrab = false
        local autoGrabCrystal = false

        local boulderCache = {}
        local grabbed = {}

        local boulderClock = 0
        local grabClock = 0
        local grabCrystalClock = 0

        local scanParams = OverlapParams.new()
        scanParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude

        local function textSize()
            local t = (ESP and tonumber(ESP.text)) or 18
            local s = tonumber(S.boulderScale) or 0.4
            return math.max(6, math.floor(t * s + 0.5))
        end

        local function anchorPart(inst)
            if inst:IsA("BasePart") then
                return inst
            end
            if inst:IsA("Model") then
                return inst.PrimaryPart or inst:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
            end
            return nil
        end

        local function createCard(anchor, offset, colors, width, height)
            local billboard = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
            billboard.Name = "FayyMengMountainEsp"
            billboard.Adornee = anchor
            billboard.AlwaysOnTop = true
            billboard.ResetOnSpawn = false
            billboard.LightInfluence = 0
            billboard.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(width * S.boulderScale, height * S.boulderScale)
            billboard.StudsOffsetWorldSpace = offset
            billboard.MaxDistance = math.huge
            billboard.Parent = EspHolder

            local total = #colors
            local size = textSize()
            local labels = {}
            local constraints = {}

            for index, color in ipairs(colors) do
                local label, constraint = FM.newLabel("Line" .. index, billboard, index - 1, total, color, false, size)
                labels[index] = label
                constraints[index] = constraint
            end

            local highlight = nil
            local model = anchor
            if anchor and anchor.Parent and anchor.Parent:IsA("Model") then
                model = anchor.Parent
            elseif anchor and not anchor:IsA("Model") then
                model = anchor
            end
            pcall(function()
                highlight = Instance.new("Highlight")
                highlight.Name = "FayyMengBoulderOutline"
                highlight.Adornee = model
                highlight.FillColor = colors[1] or Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                highlight.OutlineColor = colors[1] or Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                highlight.FillTransparency = 0.85
                highlight.OutlineTransparency = 0
                highlight.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
                highlight.Parent = EspHolder
            end)

            return {
                gui = billboard,
                labels = labels,
                constraints = constraints,
                width = width,
                height = height,
                text = {},
                highlight = highlight,
            }
        end

        local function scaleCard(entry)
            if not entry or not entry.gui then
                return
            end
            local scale = tonumber(S.boulderScale) or 0.4
            local w = tonumber(entry.width) or 220
            local h = tonumber(entry.height) or 36
            pcall(function()
                entry.gui.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w * scale, h * scale)
            end)

            local size = textSize()
            if entry.constraints then
                for _, constraint in ipairs(entry.constraints) do
                    pcall(function()
                        constraint.MaxTextSize = size
                    end)
                end
            end
        end

        local function setLine(entry, index, text)
            if not entry or not entry.text or not entry.labels then
                return
            end
            if entry.text[index] == text then
                return
            end
            entry.text[index] = text
            local label = entry.labels[index]
            if label then
                pcall(function() label.Text = text end)
            end
        end

        local function dropCard(cache, key)
            local entry = cache[key]
            if not entry then
                return
            end

            if entry.highlight then
                pcall(function()
                    entry.highlight:Destroy()
                end)
                entry.highlight = nil
            end
            if entry.selection then
                pcall(function()
                    entry.selection:Destroy()
                end)
                entry.selection = nil
            end
            if entry.box then
                pcall(function()
                    entry.box:Destroy()
                end)
                entry.box = nil
            end
            if entry.gui then
                entry.gui:Destroy()
            end
            cache[key] = nil
        end

        local function clearCache(cache)
            for key in pairs(cache) do
                dropCard(cache, key)
            end
        end

        local function boulderKind(inst)
            if not inst then
                return nil
            end
            local name = inst.Name
            if type(name) ~= "string" then
                return nil
            end
            for kind in pairs(BOULDER_INFO) do
                if name == kind or name:sub(1, #kind) == kind then
                    return kind
                end
            end
            for kind in pairs(BOULDER_INFO) do
                if name:find(kind, 1, true) then
                    return kind
                end
            end
            return nil
        end

        local function boulderRoots()
            local roots = {}
            local seen = {}
            local function push(inst)
                if inst and not seen[inst] then
                    seen[inst] = true
                    roots[#roots + 1] = inst
                end
            end

            local decorations = Workspace:FindFirstChild("MountainDecorations")
            push(decorations and decorations:FindFirstChild("Boulders"))
            push(Workspace:FindFirstChild("BoulderTest"))
            local things = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Things")
            if things then
                push(things:FindFirstChild("Boulders"))
                push(things:FindFirstChild("MountainBoulders"))
            end
            local zones = things and things:FindFirstChild("MountainZones")
            if zones then
                for _, z in ipairs(zones:GetChildren()) do
                    push(z:FindFirstChild("Boulders"))
                end
            end
            return roots
        end

        local function eachBoulder(fn)
            for _, container in ipairs(boulderRoots()) do
                for _, child in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
                    local kind = boulderKind(child)
                    if kind then
                        fn(child, kind)
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        local function isRune(inst)
            if not inst then
                return false
            end
            if FM.getAttr(inst, "RuneId") ~= nil then
                return true
            end
            if FM.getAttr(inst, "IsRune") == true then
                return true
            end
            if FM.getAttr(inst, "RuneName") ~= nil then
                return true
            end
            if FM.getAttr(inst, "RuneType") ~= nil then
                return true
            end
            local n = tostring(inst.Name or "")
            if n:find("Rune", 1, true) then
                return true
            end
            local lower = string.lower(n)
            if lower:find("haste", 1, true) or lower:find("luck", 1, true) or lower:find("weight", 1, true) then
                if inst:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ProximityPrompt", true) then
                    return true
                end
            end
            return false
        end

        local function runeTitle(inst)
            local id = FM.getAttr(inst, "RuneId") or FM.getAttr(inst, "RuneName")
            if type(id) == "string" and id ~= "" then
                if id:find("Rune", 1, true) then
                    return id
                end
                return id .. " Rune"
            end
            return inst.Name
        end

        local function eachRune(origin, radius, fn)
            if typeof(origin) ~= "Vector3" then
                return
            end
            radius = tonumber(radius) or 0
            if radius <= 0 then
                return
            end

            local seen = {}

            local function offer(owner, part)
                if not owner or seen[owner] then
                    return
                end

                local anchor = part or anchorPart(owner)
                if not anchor or not anchor.Parent then
                    return
                end

                local pos
                local okPos, result = pcall(function()
                    return anchor.Position
                end)
                if not okPos or typeof(result) ~= "Vector3" then
                    return
                end
                pos = result

                if (pos - origin).Magnitude > radius then
                    return
                end

                seen[owner] = true
                pcall(fn, owner, anchor)
            end

            local function scanFolder(container)
                if not container then
                    return
                end

                for _, child in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
                    if isRune(child) then
                        offer(child, anchorPart(child))
                    end
                end
            end

            scanFolder(Workspace)
            scanFolder(Workspace:FindFirstChild("Things"))
            scanFolder(Workspace:FindFirstChild("DroppedCrystals"))
            local things = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Things")
            if things then
                scanFolder(things:FindFirstChild("Runes"))
                scanFolder(things:FindFirstChild("DroppedRunes"))
                scanFolder(things:FindFirstChild("Pickups"))
            end
            scanFolder(Workspace:FindFirstChild("Runes"))
            scanFolder(Workspace:FindFirstChild("DroppedRunes"))

            local character = LocalPlayer.Character
            scanParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = character and { character } or {}

            local ok, parts = pcall(function()
                return Workspace:GetPartBoundsInRadius(origin, radius, scanParams)
            end)

            if not ok or not parts then
                return
            end

            for _, part in ipairs(parts) do
                if isRune(part) then
                    offer(part, part)
                else
                    local parent = part.Parent
                    if parent and parent ~= Workspace and isRune(parent) then
                        offer(parent, anchorPart(parent) or part)
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        local function syncBoulders()
            local root = FM.getRoot()
            local origin = root and root.Position or nil
            local seen = {}

            eachBoulder(function(model, kind)
                local anchor = anchorPart(model)
                if not anchor then
                    return
                end

                seen[model] = true
                local info = BOULDER_INFO[kind]
                if not info then
                    return
                end
                local entry = boulderCache[model]

                if entry and not entry.gui.Parent then
                    dropCard(boulderCache, model)
                    entry = nil
                end

                if not entry then
                    entry = createCard(
                        anchor,
                        BOULDER_OFFSET,
                        { info.color },
                        BOULDER_WIDTH,
                        BOULDER_HEIGHT
                    )
                    local hl = Instance.new("Highlight")
                    hl.Name = "FayyMengBoulderHighlight"
                    hl.Adornee = model
                    hl.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
                    hl.FillTransparency = 0.72
                    hl.OutlineTransparency = 0
                    hl.FillColor = info.color
                    hl.OutlineColor = info.color
                    hl.Parent = EspHolder
                    entry.highlight = hl

                    boulderCache[model] = entry
                end

                if entry.gui.Adornee ~= anchor then
                    entry.gui.Adornee = anchor
                end
                if entry.highlight then
                    entry.highlight.Adornee = model
                    entry.highlight.FillColor = info.color
                    entry.highlight.OutlineColor = info.color
                end

                scaleCard(entry)
                setLine(entry, 1, kind)
            end)

            local stale
            for model in pairs(boulderCache) do
                if not seen[model] then
                    stale = stale or {}
                    stale[#stale + 1] = model
                end
            end

            if stale then
                for _, model in ipairs(stale) do
                    dropCard(boulderCache, model)
                end
            end
        end

        local function grabRunes(radius)
            local root = FM.getRoot()
            if not root then
                return 0
            end

            local now = os.clock()
            local fired = 0

            for prompt, stamp in pairs(grabbed) do
                local age = (type(stamp) == "number") and (now - stamp) or 999
                if age > 5 or not prompt or not prompt.Parent then
                    grabbed[prompt] = nil
                end
            end

            eachRune(root.Position, radius or GRAB_RANGE, function(owner, part)
                if fired >= GRAB_LIMIT then
                    return
                end

                local prompt = owner:FindFirstChildOfClass("ProximityPrompt")
                if not prompt then
                    prompt = FM.crystalPrompt(owner)
                end
                if not prompt and part ~= owner then
                    prompt = FM.crystalPrompt(part)
                end

                if not prompt or not prompt.Parent then
                    return
                end

                local last = grabbed[prompt]
                if last and now - last < GRAB_RETRY then
                    return
                end

                grabbed[prompt] = now

                if FM.firePrompt(prompt) then
                    fired += 1
                    local title = tostring(runeTitle(owner) or "Rune")
                    S._runeGrabSession = S._runeGrabSession or {}
                    S._runeGrabSession[title] = (S._runeGrabSession[title] or 0) + 1
                    S._runeGrabTotal = (S._runeGrabTotal or 0) + 1
                end
            end)

            return fired
        end

        function Mountain.applyScale()
            for _, entry in pairs(boulderCache) do
                scaleCard(entry)
            end
        end

        function Mountain.boulderList()
            local list = {}

            for _, kind in ipairs({ "Mossite", "Voltite", "Gildrite", "Rimeveil", "Nocturnite" }) do
                local info = BOULDER_INFO[kind]
                list[#list + 1] = string.format("%s  \u{2022}  %s", kind, info.pickaxe)
            end

            return list
        end

        function Mountain.findBoulder(kind, preferNearest)
            if type(kind) ~= "string" or kind == "" then
                return nil, nil
            end
            local root = FM.getRoot()
            local origin = root and root.Position
            local best, bestPos, bestDist
            eachBoulder(function(model, k)
                if k ~= kind then
                    return
                end
                local part = model
                if model:IsA("Model") then
                    part = model.PrimaryPart or model:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
                end
                if not part or not part:IsA("BasePart") then
                    return
                end
                local pos = part.Position
                if preferNearest and origin then
                    local d = (pos - origin).Magnitude
                    if not bestDist or d < bestDist then
                        best, bestPos, bestDist = model, pos, d
                    end
                elseif not best then
                    best, bestPos = model, pos
                end
            end)
            return best, bestPos
        end

        function Mountain.gotoBoulder(kind, mode)
            local model, pos = Mountain.findBoulder(kind, true)
            if not pos then
                if WindUI then
                    WindUI:Notify({
                        Title = "Boulder",
                        Content = "No " .. tostring(kind) .. " boulder found nearby",
                        Duration = 3,
                        Icon = "alert-circle",
                    })
                end
                return false
            end
            local dest = pos + Vector3.new(0, 5, 0)
            mode = tostring(mode or S.farmMode or "Tween")
            if mode == "Teleport" and FM.secureTeleportTo then
                FM.secureTeleportTo(dest, pos)
            elseif FM.moveTo then
                FM.moveTo(dest, pos)
            elseif FM.teleportTo then
                FM.teleportTo(dest)
            end
            if WindUI then
                WindUI:Notify({
                    Title = "Boulder",
                    Content = mode .. " to " .. tostring(kind),
                    Duration = 2,
                    Icon = "check",
                })
            end
            return true
        end

        local revealedBoulderParts = {}

        local function revealBoulderParts()
            local decorations = Workspace:FindFirstChild("MountainDecorations")
            local folder = decorations and decorations:FindFirstChild("Boulders")
            if not folder then
                return 0
            end
            local n = 0
            for _, v in ipairs(folder:GetDescendants()) do
                if v:IsA("BasePart") and v.Transparency >= 0.99 then
                    if revealedBoulderParts[v] == nil then
                        revealedBoulderParts[v] = v.Transparency
                    end
                    pcall(function()
                        v.Transparency = 0
                        v.LocalTransparencyModifier = 0
                    end)
                    n += 1
                end
            end
            return n
        end

        local function restoreBoulderParts()
            for part, orig in pairs(revealedBoulderParts) do
                if part and part.Parent then
                    pcall(function()
                        part.Transparency = typeof(orig) == "number" and orig or 1
                    end)
                end
            end
            table.clear(revealedBoulderParts)
        end

        function Mountain.setBoulderEsp(value)
            boulderEsp = value == true
            if boulderEsp then
                boulderClock = math.huge
                task.defer(revealBoulderParts)
            else
                clearCache(boulderCache)
                restoreBoulderParts()
            end
        end

        function Mountain.setAutoGrab(value)
            autoGrab = value
            if value then
                grabClock = math.huge
                S._runeGrabSession = {}
                S._runeGrabTotal = 0
            else
                table.clear(grabbed)
                local total = tonumber(S._runeGrabTotal) or 0
                local session = S._runeGrabSession or {}
                if total > 0 then
                    local lines = {}
                    local keys = {}
                    for name in pairs(session) do
                        keys[#keys + 1] = name
                    end
                    table.sort(keys)
                    for _, name in ipairs(keys) do
                        lines[#lines + 1] = string.format("%s x%d", name, session[name] or 0)
                    end
                    WindUI:Notify({
                        Title = "Rune Pickup Summary",
                        Content = string.format("Collected %d rune(s)\n%s", total, table.concat(lines, "\n")),
                        Duration = 6,
                        Icon = "check",
                    })
                end
                S._runeGrabSession = {}
                S._runeGrabTotal = 0
            end
        end

        local function grabCrystalsNear(radius)
            local fired = 0
            local root = FM.getRoot and FM.getRoot()
            if not root then
                return 0
            end
            radius = radius or (GRAB_RANGE + 12)
            local origin = root.Position

            
            local list = {}
            local function consider(inst)
                if not inst or not inst.Parent then
                    return
                end
                local part = inst
                if not inst:IsA("BasePart") then
                    part = inst:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
                end
                if not part then
                    return
                end
                local target = inst
                if FM.isCrystal then
                    if FM.isCrystal(inst) then
                        target = inst
                    elseif FM.isCrystal(part) then
                        target = part
                    else
                        return
                    end
                end
                if FM.getAttr(target, "Collected") == true then
                    return
                end
                local pos = part.Position
                if (pos - origin).Magnitude > radius then
                    return
                end
                list[#list + 1] = target
            end

            pcall(function()
                if Mountain.crystalsNear then
                    for _, c in ipairs(Mountain.crystalsNear(radius) or {}) do
                        consider(c)
                    end
                end
            end)
            if #list == 0 then
                pcall(function()
                    if FM.eachContainer then
                        FM.eachContainer(function(container)
                            for _, ch in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
                                consider(ch)
                            end
                        end)
                    end
                end)
            end

            
            local useFilter = (S.pickupValueActive == true)
                or (S.pickupLuckActive == true)
                or (S.pickupRarityActive == true)

            if useFilter and type(FM.valueLuckFilter) == "function" then
                local filtered = {}
                for _, crystal in ipairs(list) do
                    local ok, pass = pcall(FM.valueLuckFilter, crystal)
                    if ok and pass then
                        filtered[#filtered + 1] = crystal
                    end
                end
                list = filtered
            end

            if #list == 0 then
                return 0
            end

            
            table.sort(list, function(a, b)
                if useFilter then
                    if S.pickupValueActive then
                        local va = FM.crystalValue(a) or 0
                        local vb = FM.crystalValue(b) or 0
                        if va ~= vb then
                            return va > vb
                        end
                    end
                    if S.pickupLuckActive then
                        local la = select(2, pcall(FM.crystalLuck, a)) or 0
                        local lb = select(2, pcall(FM.crystalLuck, b)) or 0
                        if la ~= lb then
                            return la > lb
                        end
                    end
                end
                local pa = (FM.resolveWorldPosition and FM.resolveWorldPosition(a)) or (a.Position)
                local pb = (FM.resolveWorldPosition and FM.resolveWorldPosition(b)) or (b.Position)
                if typeof(pa) ~= "Vector3" then pa = origin end
                if typeof(pb) ~= "Vector3" then pb = origin end
                return (pa - origin).Magnitude < (pb - origin).Magnitude
            end)

            local limit = GRAB_LIMIT or 4
            for i = 1, math.min(#list, limit) do
                local crystal = list[i]
                if crystal and crystal.Parent then
                    
                    local allowed = true
                    if useFilter and type(FM.valueLuckFilter) == "function" then
                        local ok, pass = pcall(FM.valueLuckFilter, crystal)
                        allowed = ok and pass == true
                    end
                    if allowed then
                        local prompt = FM.crystalPrompt and FM.crystalPrompt(crystal)
                        local okGrab = false
                        if FM.grabCrystal then
                            okGrab = FM.grabCrystal(crystal, prompt) == true
                        end
                        if not okGrab and prompt and FM.firePrompt then
                            okGrab = FM.firePrompt(prompt) == true
                        end
                        if okGrab then
                            fired += 1
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            return fired
        end

        function Mountain.setAutoGrabCrystal(value)
            -- Deprecated: crystal grab is handled by BoulderPickupSystem / MoneyPickupSystem
            value = false
            if type(value) == "table" then
                value = value.CurrentValue or value[1] or value.Value
            end
            autoGrabCrystal = value == true
            S.autoGrabCrystal = autoGrabCrystal
            if autoGrabCrystal then
                grabCrystalClock = math.huge
            end
        end

        function Mountain.crystalsNear(radius)
            local list = {}
            local root = FM.getRoot and FM.getRoot()
            if not root then return list end
            local origin = root.Position
            radius = radius or 32
            pcall(function()
                if FM.eachContainer then
                    FM.eachContainer(function(container)
                        for _, ch in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
                            local part = ch:IsA("BasePart") and ch or ch:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
                            if part and FM.isCrystal and FM.isCrystal(part) then
                                if (part.Position - origin).Magnitude <= radius then
                                    list[#list + 1] = part
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end)
            return list
        end

        function Mountain.grabRange()
            return GRAB_RANGE
        end

        function Mountain.grabRunes(radius)
            local ok, fired = pcall(grabRunes, radius)
            if not ok then
                FM.reportError("runeGrab", fired)
                return 0
            end
            return fired or 0
        end

        function Mountain.grabNear(radius)
            return Mountain.grabRunes(radius)
        end

        function Mountain.runesNear(radius)
            local root = FM.getRoot()
            if not root then
                return 0
            end

            local count = 0

            eachRune(root.Position, radius or GRAB_RANGE, function()
                count += 1
            end)

            return count
        end

        function Mountain.shutdown()
            boulderEsp = false
            autoGrab = false
            autoGrabCrystal = false
            S.autoGrabCrystal = false
            clearCache(boulderCache)
            table.clear(grabbed)
        end

        mountainConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(deltaTime)
            if not boulderEsp and not autoGrab and not autoGrabCrystal then
                return
            end

            if boulderEsp then
                boulderClock += deltaTime
                if boulderClock >= BOULDER_STEP then
                    boulderClock = 0
                    local ok, err = pcall(syncBoulders)
                    if not ok then
                        FM.reportError("boulder", err)
                    end
                    pcall(revealBoulderParts)
                end
            end

            if autoGrab then
                grabClock += deltaTime
                if grabClock >= GRAB_STEP then
                    grabClock = 0
                    local ok, err = pcall(grabRunes, math.max(GRAB_RANGE, 80))
                    if not ok then
                        FM.reportError("runeGrab", err)
                    end
                end
            end

            -- Auto Grab Crystal removed: use Boulder/Money Auto Pickup with presets
            if autoGrabCrystal then
                autoGrabCrystal = false
                S.autoGrabCrystal = false
            end
        end)
    end

    install()
end

local Move = {}

do
    local function install()
        local flyActive = false
        local noclipActive = false
        local jumpActive = false
        local iyflyspeed = 2 
        local QEfly = true

        local flyConn
        local flyKeyDown
        local flyKeyUp
        local mflyRender
        local mflyChar
        local noclipConn
        local jumpConn
        local charHookConn
        local collisions = {}
        local controlModule = nil
        local velName = "FM_IYFlyVel"
        local gyroName = "FM_IYFlyGyro"
        local infJumpDebounce = false
        local bindInfJump 

        local function isMobileDevice()
            local ok, platform = pcall(function()
                return UserInputService:GetPlatform()
            end)
            if ok and (platform == Enum.Platform.Android or platform == Enum.Platform.IOS) then
                return true
            end
            return UserInputService.TouchEnabled and not UserInputService.KeyboardEnabled
        end

        local function humanoidOf()
            local character = LocalPlayer.Character
            return character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        end

        local function getRootPart(char)
            char = char or LocalPlayer.Character
            if not char then
                return nil
            end
            return char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or char:FindFirstChild("Torso") or char.PrimaryPart
        end

        task.spawn(function()
            pcall(function()
                local ps = LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerScripts", 10)
                if not ps then
                    return
                end
                local pm = ps:FindFirstChild("PlayerModule") or ps:WaitForChild("PlayerModule", 5)
                if not pm then
                    return
                end
                local cm = pm:FindFirstChild("ControlModule") or pm:WaitForChild("ControlModule", 5)
                if cm then
                    controlModule = require(cm)
                end
            end)
        end)

        local function dropFlyMovers(root)
            root = root or getRootPart()
            if root then
                for _, name in ipairs({ velName, gyroName, "FayyMengFlyVelocity", "FayyMengFlyGyro" }) do
                    local obj = root:FindFirstChild(name)
                    if obj then
                        pcall(function()
                            obj:Destroy()
                        end)
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        local function disconnectFlyInputs()
            if flyKeyDown then
                pcall(function()
                    flyKeyDown:Disconnect()
                end)
                flyKeyDown = nil
            end
            if flyKeyUp then
                pcall(function()
                    flyKeyUp:Disconnect()
                end)
                flyKeyUp = nil
            end
            if flyConn then
                pcall(function()
                    flyConn:Disconnect()
                end)
                flyConn = nil
            end
            if mflyRender then
                pcall(function()
                    mflyRender:Disconnect()
                end)
                mflyRender = nil
            end
            if mflyChar then
                pcall(function()
                    mflyChar:Disconnect()
                end)
                mflyChar = nil
            end
        end

        local function startPcFly()
            disconnectFlyInputs()
            dropFlyMovers()

            local char = LocalPlayer.Character
            if not char then
                return
            end
            local humanoid = humanoidOf()
            local T = getRootPart(char)
            if not T then
                return
            end

            local CONTROL = { F = 0, B = 0, L = 0, R = 0, Q = 0, E = 0 }
            local lCONTROL = { F = 0, B = 0, L = 0, R = 0, Q = 0, E = 0 }
            local SPEED = 0

            local BG = Instance.new("BodyGyro")
            local BV = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
            BG.Name = gyroName
            BV.Name = velName
            BG.P = 9e4
            BG.Parent = T
            BV.Parent = T
            BG.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(9e9, 9e9, 9e9)
            BG.CFrame = T.CFrame
            BV.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
            BV.MaxForce = Vector3.new(9e9, 9e9, 9e9)

            task.spawn(function()
                while flyActive do
                    task.wait()
                    local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
                    humanoid = humanoidOf()
                    T = getRootPart()
                    if not T or not camera then
                    elseif not T:FindFirstChild(velName) then
                        break
                    else
                        if humanoid then
                            humanoid.PlatformStand = true
                        end

                        if CONTROL.L + CONTROL.R ~= 0 or CONTROL.F + CONTROL.B ~= 0 or CONTROL.Q + CONTROL.E ~= 0 then
                            SPEED = 50 * iyflyspeed
                        elseif SPEED ~= 0 then
                            SPEED = 0
                        end

                        local bv = T:FindFirstChild(velName)
                        local bg = T:FindFirstChild(gyroName)
                        if not bv or not bg then
                            break
                        end

                        if (CONTROL.L + CONTROL.R) ~= 0 or (CONTROL.F + CONTROL.B) ~= 0 or (CONTROL.Q + CONTROL.E) ~= 0 then
                            bv.Velocity = ((camera.CFrame.LookVector * (CONTROL.F + CONTROL.B))
                                + ((camera.CFrame * CFrame.new(CONTROL.L + CONTROL.R, (CONTROL.F + CONTROL.B + CONTROL.Q + CONTROL.E) * 0.2, 0).Position) - camera.CFrame.Position))
                                * SPEED
                            lCONTROL = { F = CONTROL.F, B = CONTROL.B, L = CONTROL.L, R = CONTROL.R, Q = CONTROL.Q, E = CONTROL.E }
                        elseif SPEED ~= 0 then
                            bv.Velocity = ((camera.CFrame.LookVector * (lCONTROL.F + lCONTROL.B))
                                + ((camera.CFrame * CFrame.new(lCONTROL.L + lCONTROL.R, (lCONTROL.F + lCONTROL.B + CONTROL.Q + CONTROL.E) * 0.2, 0).Position) - camera.CFrame.Position))
                                * SPEED
                        else
                            bv.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
                        end
                        bg.CFrame = camera.CFrame
                    end
                end
                dropFlyMovers()
                if humanoid then
                    humanoid.PlatformStand = false
                end
            end)

            flyKeyDown = UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, processed)
                if processed then
                    return
                end
                local code = input.KeyCode
                if code == Enum.KeyCode.W then
                    CONTROL.F = iyflyspeed
                elseif code == Enum.KeyCode.S then
                    CONTROL.B = -iyflyspeed
                elseif code == Enum.KeyCode.A then
                    CONTROL.L = -iyflyspeed
                elseif code == Enum.KeyCode.D then
