                    CONTROL.R = iyflyspeed
                elseif code == Enum.KeyCode.E and QEfly then
                    CONTROL.Q = iyflyspeed * 2
                elseif code == Enum.KeyCode.Q and QEfly then
                    CONTROL.E = -iyflyspeed * 2
                end
            end)

            flyKeyUp = UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input, processed)
                if processed then
                    return
                end
                local code = input.KeyCode
                if code == Enum.KeyCode.W then
                    CONTROL.F = 0
                elseif code == Enum.KeyCode.S then
                    CONTROL.B = 0
                elseif code == Enum.KeyCode.A then
                    CONTROL.L = 0
                elseif code == Enum.KeyCode.D then
                    CONTROL.R = 0
                elseif code == Enum.KeyCode.E then
                    CONTROL.Q = 0
                elseif code == Enum.KeyCode.Q then
                    CONTROL.E = 0
                end
            end)
        end

        local function startMobileFly()
            disconnectFlyInputs()
            dropFlyMovers()

            local root = getRootPart()
            if not root then
                return
            end

            local function ensureMovers(r)
                if not r then
                    return
                end
                if not r:FindFirstChild(velName) then
                    local bv = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
                    bv.Name = velName
                    bv.MaxForce = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
                    bv.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
                    bv.Parent = r
                end
                if not r:FindFirstChild(gyroName) then
                    local bg = Instance.new("BodyGyro")
                    bg.Name = gyroName
                    bg.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(9e9, 9e9, 9e9)
                    bg.P = 1000
                    bg.D = 50
                    bg.Parent = r
                end
            end

            ensureMovers(root)

            mflyChar = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
                task.wait(0.3)
                if not flyActive then
                    return
                end
                root = getRootPart(char)
                ensureMovers(root)
            end)

            mflyRender = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
                if not flyActive then
                    return
                end
                root = getRootPart()
                local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
                local humanoid = humanoidOf()
                if not root or not camera or not humanoid then
                    return
                end
                ensureMovers(root)
                local VelocityHandler = root:FindFirstChild(velName)
                local GyroHandler = root:FindFirstChild(gyroName)
                if not VelocityHandler or not GyroHandler then
                    return
                end

                VelocityHandler.MaxForce = Vector3.new(9e9, 9e9, 9e9)
                GyroHandler.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(9e9, 9e9, 9e9)
                humanoid.PlatformStand = true
                GyroHandler.CFrame = camera.CFrame
                VelocityHandler.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)

                local direction = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
                if controlModule then
                    local ok, vec = pcall(function()
                        return controlModule:GetMoveVector()
                    end)
                    if ok and typeof(vec) == "Vector3" then
                        direction = vec
                    end
                end
                if direction.Magnitude < 0.01 then
                    direction = humanoid.MoveDirection
                    if direction.Magnitude > 0.01 then
                        local look = camera.CFrame.LookVector * Vector3.new(1, 0, 1)
                        local right = camera.CFrame.RightVector * Vector3.new(1, 0, 1)
                        if look.Magnitude > 0 and right.Magnitude > 0 then
                            look = look.Unit
                            right = right.Unit
                            direction = Vector3.new(direction:Dot(right), 0, -direction:Dot(look))
                        end
                    end
                end

                local spd = iyflyspeed * 50
                if direction.X ~= 0 then
                    VelocityHandler.Velocity = VelocityHandler.Velocity + camera.CFrame.RightVector * (direction.X * spd)
                end
                if direction.Z ~= 0 then
                    VelocityHandler.Velocity = VelocityHandler.Velocity - camera.CFrame.LookVector * (direction.Z * spd)
                end
            end)
        end

        local function stopFly()
            flyActive = false
            disconnectFlyInputs()
            dropFlyMovers()
            local humanoid = humanoidOf()
            if humanoid then
                pcall(function()
                    humanoid.PlatformStand = false
                    humanoid.AutoRotate = true
                end)
            end
            pcall(function()
                Workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
            end)
        end

        local function noclipStep()
            local character = LocalPlayer.Character
            if not character then
                return
            end
            for _, part in ipairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
                if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                    if collisions[part] == nil then
                        collisions[part] = part.CanCollide
                    end
                    if part.CanCollide then
                        part.CanCollide = false
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        function Move.setFlySpeed(value)
            local n = tonumber(value)
            if n and n > 0 then
                iyflyspeed = math.clamp(n / 50, 0.2, 12)
            end
        end

        function Move.setFly(value)
            if value == true then
                stopFly()
                flyActive = true
                S._flyWanted = true
                if isMobileDevice() then
                    startMobileFly()
                else
                    startPcFly()
                end
                return
            end
            S._flyWanted = false
            stopFly()
        end

        if not charHookConn then
            charHookConn = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
                infJumpDebounce = false
                task.defer(function()
                    task.wait(0.25)
                    if S._flyWanted then
                        pcall(Move.setFly, true)
                    end
                    if S._jumpWanted then
                        jumpActive = true
                        bindInfJump()
                    end
                end)
                task.defer(function()
                    local hum = char and char:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 8)
                    if not hum then
                        return
                    end
                    hum.Died:Connect(function()
                        infJumpDebounce = false
                    end)
                end)
            end)
        end

        function Move.setNoclip(value, remember)
            noclipActive = value == true
            if remember ~= false then
                S._noclipWanted = noclipActive
            end

            if noclipActive then
                if not noclipConn then
                    noclipConn = RunService.Stepped:Connect(function()
                        if not noclipActive then
                            return
                        end
                        pcall(noclipStep)
                    end)
                end
                pcall(noclipStep)
                return
            end

            -- FULL DISABLE
            noclipActive = false
            if noclipConn then
                pcall(function() noclipConn:Disconnect() end)
                noclipConn = nil
            end

            -- Restore original CanCollide for every part we touched
            for part, state in pairs(collisions) do
                if typeof(part) == "Instance" and part.Parent then
                    pcall(function()
                        part.CanCollide = state and true or false
                    end)
                end
            end
            table.clear(collisions)

            local character = LocalPlayer.Character
            if character then
                local hrp = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if hrp and hrp:IsA("BasePart") then
                    pcall(function() hrp.CanCollide = true end)
                end
            end
        end


        function Move.forceClearFarmMovement()
            noclipActive = false
            if noclipConn then
                pcall(function()
                    noclipConn:Disconnect()
                end)
                noclipConn = nil
            end
            pcall(Move.glideStop)

            for part, state in pairs(collisions) do
                if part and part.Parent then
                    pcall(function()
                        part.CanCollide = state and true or false
                    end)
                end
            end
            table.clear(collisions)

            local character = LocalPlayer.Character
            if character then
                local root = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if root then
                    pcall(function()
                        root.CanCollide = true
                        root.Anchored = false
                        root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                        root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    end)
                end
                for _, part in ipairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
                    if part:IsA("BasePart") and part.Name == "HumanoidRootPart" then
                        pcall(function()
                            part.CanCollide = true
                        end)
                    end
                end
                local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if humanoid then
                    pcall(function()
                        humanoid.PlatformStand = false
                        humanoid.Sit = false
                        if humanoid.WalkSpeed <= 0 then
                            humanoid.WalkSpeed = (FM.getBaseSpeed and FM.getBaseSpeed()) or 25
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end

            if S._noclipWanted == true then
                Move.setNoclip(true, false)
            end
            if S._flyWanted == true then
                pcall(function()
                    Move.setFly(true)
                end)
            else
                pcall(function()
                    Move.setFly(false)
                end)
            end
        end

        function Move.isFlyOn()
            return flyActive
        end
        function Move.isNoclipOn()
            return noclipActive
        end

        local jumpHoldConn
        bindInfJump = function()
            if jumpConn then
                pcall(function()
                    jumpConn:Disconnect()
                end)
                jumpConn = nil
            end
            if jumpHoldConn then
                pcall(function()
                    jumpHoldConn:Disconnect()
                end)
                jumpHoldConn = nil
            end
            infJumpDebounce = false
            if not jumpActive then
                return
            end

            jumpConn = UserInputService.JumpRequest:Connect(function()
                if not jumpActive or not S._jumpWanted then
                    return
                end
                local humanoid = humanoidOf()
                if not humanoid or humanoid.Health <= 0 then
                    return
                end
                pcall(function()
                    humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
                end)
            end)

            local lastHoldJump = 0
            jumpHoldConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                if not jumpActive or not S._jumpWanted then
                    return
                end
                local humanoid = humanoidOf()
                if not humanoid or humanoid.Health <= 0 then
                    return
                end
                local holding = false
                if not UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() then
                    if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) then
                        holding = true
                    end
                end
                if humanoid.Jump then
                    holding = true
                end
                if not holding then
                    return
                end
                local now = os.clock()
                if now - lastHoldJump < 0.12 then
                    return
                end
                lastHoldJump = now
                pcall(function()
                    humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
                end)
            end)
        end

        function Move.setInfJump(value)
            jumpActive = value == true
            S._jumpWanted = jumpActive
            bindInfJump()
        end

        function Move.shutdown()
            Move.setFly(false)
            Move.setNoclip(false, false)
            Move.setInfJump(false)
            Move.glideStop()
        end
    end

    install()
end

do
    local function install()
        local GLIDE_SPEED = 520
        local AIM_RATE = 9
        local SNAP_GAP = 0.35
        local RESPONSE = 200
        local STREAM_GAP = 0.5
        local HOLD_FORCE = 1e7
        local HOLD_TORQUE = 1e7

        local attachment
        local mover
        local aligner
        local cursor
        local facing
        local goalFrame
        local aimSpot
        local streamClock = 0
        local glideConn
        local running = false

        local function humanoidOf()
            local character = LocalPlayer.Character
            return character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        end

        local function detach()
            if mover then
                pcall(function()
                    mover:Destroy()
                end)
                mover = nil
            end

            if aligner then
                pcall(function()
                    aligner:Destroy()
                end)
                aligner = nil
            end

            if attachment then
                pcall(function()
                    attachment:Destroy()
                end)
                attachment = nil
            end
        end

        local function attach(root)
            detach()

            local ok = pcall(function()
                local point = Instance.new("Attachment")
                point.Name = "FayyMengGlidePoint"
                point.Parent = root

                local position = Instance.new("AlignPosition")
                position.Name = "FayyMengGlidePosition"
                position.Mode = Enum.PositionAlignmentMode.OneAttachment
                position.Attachment0 = point
                position.RigidityEnabled = false
                position.ApplyAtCenterOfMass = true
                position.ReactionForceEnabled = false
                position.MaxForce = HOLD_FORCE
                position.MaxVelocity = math.huge
                position.Responsiveness = RESPONSE
                position.Position = root.Position
                position.Parent = root

                local orientation = Instance.new("AlignOrientation")
                orientation.Name = "FayyMengGlideOrientation"
                orientation.Mode = Enum.OrientationAlignmentMode.OneAttachment
                orientation.Attachment0 = point
                orientation.RigidityEnabled = false
                orientation.ReactionTorqueEnabled = false
                orientation.MaxTorque = HOLD_TORQUE
                orientation.MaxAngularVelocity = math.huge
                orientation.Responsiveness = RESPONSE
                orientation.CFrame = root.CFrame.Rotation
                orientation.Parent = root

                attachment = point
                mover = position
                aligner = orientation
            end)

            if not ok then
                detach()
            end

            return ok
        end

        local function step(deltaTime)
            if not goalFrame then
                return
            end

            local root = FM.getRoot()
            if not root then
                return
            end

            if not mover or mover.Parent ~= root or not aligner or aligner.Parent ~= root then
                if not attach(root) then
                    return
                end

                cursor = root.Position
                facing = root.CFrame.Rotation
            end

            local humanoid = humanoidOf()
            if humanoid and not humanoid.PlatformStand then
                pcall(function()
                    humanoid.PlatformStand = true
                end)
            end

            cursor = cursor or root.Position
            facing = facing or root.CFrame.Rotation

            local delta = goalFrame.Position - cursor
            local span = GLIDE_SPEED * deltaTime

            if delta.Magnitude <= math.max(span, SNAP_GAP) then
                cursor = goalFrame.Position
            else
                cursor += delta.Unit * span
            end

            streamClock += deltaTime
            if streamClock >= STREAM_GAP then
                streamClock = 0
                FM.requestStream(goalFrame.Position)
            end

            local look = goalFrame.Rotation

            if aimSpot then
                local gap = aimSpot - cursor
                if gap.Magnitude > 0.1 then
                    look = CFrame.lookAt(cursor, aimSpot).Rotation
                end
            end

            facing = facing:Lerp(look, 1 - math.exp(-AIM_RATE * deltaTime))

            mover.Position = cursor
            aligner.CFrame = facing
        end

        function Move.glide(goal, aim)
            if typeof(goal) ~= "CFrame" then
                return false
            end

            goalFrame = goal
            aimSpot = typeof(aim) == "Vector3" and aim or nil

            if not running then
                running = true

                local root = FM.getRoot()
                if root then
                    cursor = root.Position
                    facing = root.CFrame.Rotation
                    attach(root)
                end
            end

            if not glideConn then
                glideConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(deltaTime)
                    if not running then
                        return
                    end

                    local ok, err = pcall(step, deltaTime)
                    if not ok then
                        FM.reportError("glide", err)
                    end
                end)
            end

            return true
        end

        function Move.glideStop()
            running = false
            goalFrame = nil
            aimSpot = nil
            cursor = nil
            facing = nil
            streamClock = 0

            if glideConn then
                glideConn:Disconnect()
                glideConn = nil
            end

            detach()

            local root = FM.getRoot()
            if root then
                pcall(function()
                    root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                end)
            end

            local humanoid = humanoidOf()
            if humanoid then
                pcall(function()
                    humanoid.PlatformStand = false
                    humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
                end)
            end
        end
    end

    install()
end

-- ==================== MOVEMENT OWNERSHIP GATE (ROOT-CAUSE FIX) ====================
-- Root cause: AutoBoulder / AutoMoney / AutoVein all called Move.setNoclip and
-- Move.setFly directly. MovementOwner existed but was never checked before those
-- calls, so any feature's stop() could silently disable noclip/fly out from under
-- another feature that was still actively farming (e.g. AutoMoney OFF killing
-- AutoBoulder's noclip mid-mine). The farm loop kept running (no console error),
-- but the character got physically stuck against terrain, which looked like the
-- feature "doing nothing."
--
-- These helpers make every farm feature go through MovementOwner before touching
-- the shared Move module. If another feature currently owns movement, the call is
-- a safe no-op instead of silently overriding it.

function FM.ownedSetNoclip(name, value, remember)
    if value then
        MovementOwner:Acquire(name)
        pcall(function()
            Move.setNoclip(true, false)
        end)
        return true
    end
    MovementOwner:Release(name)
    if MovementOwner:HasAny() then
        return false
    end
    pcall(function()
        Move.setNoclip(false, false)
    end)
    return true
end

function FM.ownedSetFly(name, value)
    if value then
        MovementOwner:Acquire(name)
        pcall(function()
            Move.setFly(true)
        end)
        return true
    end
    MovementOwner:Release(name)
    if MovementOwner:HasAny() then
        return false
    end
    pcall(function()
        Move.setFly(false)
    end)
    return true
end

function FM.ownedClearFarmMovement(name)
    -- Never clear shared movement while any other owner is active
    if MovementOwner:HasAny() and not MovementOwner:Is(name) then
        return false
    end
    if MovementOwner:HasAny() and MovementOwner:Is(name) then
        -- still owned by us (and maybe others after multi-owner) — only clear if sole
        local owners = MovementOwner.owners or {}
        local count = 0
        for _ in pairs(owners) do count += 1 end
        if count > 1 then
            return false
        end
    end
    pcall(function()
        Move.forceClearFarmMovement()
    end)
    return true
end
-- ==================== END MOVEMENT OWNERSHIP GATE ====================

function FM.mountainPeakPosition()
    local cx = Workspace:GetAttribute("MountainCenterX")
    local cz = Workspace:GetAttribute("MountainCenterZ")
    local peakY = Workspace:GetAttribute("MountainPeakY")
    local baseY = Workspace:GetAttribute("MountainBaseY")
    local radius = Workspace:GetAttribute("MountainRadius")

    if typeof(cx) ~= "number" or typeof(cz) ~= "number" then
        local spot = FM.mountainSpot()
        if not spot then
            return nil
        end
        cx, cz = spot.X, spot.Z
    end
    if typeof(radius) ~= "number" or radius < 20 then
        radius = 150
    end
    if typeof(peakY) ~= "number" then
        peakY = (typeof(baseY) == "number" and baseY or 0) + 900
    end
    if typeof(baseY) ~= "number" then
        baseY = 0
    end

    local rayParams = RaycastParams.new()
    rayParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    if char then
        rayParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = { char }
    end
    rayParams.IgnoreWater = true

    local bestPos = Vector3.new(cx, peakY, cz)
    local bestY = -math.huge
    local samples = {
        Vector3.new(0, 0, 0),
        Vector3.new(1, 0, 0), Vector3.new(-1, 0, 0),
        Vector3.new(0, 0, 1), Vector3.new(0, 0, -1),
        Vector3.new(0.7, 0, 0.7), Vector3.new(-0.7, 0, 0.7),
        Vector3.new(0.7, 0, -0.7), Vector3.new(-0.7, 0, -0.7),
    }
    local rings = { 0, radius * 0.05, radius * 0.1, radius * 0.18, radius * 0.28 }

    for _, ring in ipairs(rings) do
        for _, dir in ipairs(samples) do
            local ox = cx + dir.X * ring
            local oz = cz + dir.Z * ring
            local origin = Vector3.new(ox, peakY + 250, oz)
            local hit = Workspace:Raycast(origin, Vector3.new(0, -(peakY - baseY + 400), 0), rayParams)
            if hit and hit.Position.Y > bestY then
                bestY = hit.Position.Y
                bestPos = hit.Position
            end
        end
    end

    if bestY < -1e8 then
        bestPos = Vector3.new(cx, peakY, cz)
    end

    return bestPos + Vector3.new(0, 6, 0)
end

function FM.teleportToMountainPeak()
    local peak = FM.mountainPeakPosition()
    if not peak then
        WindUI:Notify({
            Title = "Mine A Mountain",
            Content = "Mountain peak not found",
            Duration = 3,
            Icon = "alert-circle",
        })
        return false
    end

    local startRoot = FM.getRoot()
    local startPos = startRoot and startRoot.Position or nil

    S._peakTweening = true
    task.spawn(function()
        local deadline = os.clock() + 60
        local attempts = 0
        local maxAttempts = 12

        while S._peakTweening and os.clock() < deadline and attempts < maxAttempts do
            attempts += 1
            local goal = FM.mountainPeakPosition() or peak
            peak = goal
            FM.requestStream(goal)
            pcall(function()
                LocalPlayer:RequestStreamAroundAsync(goal, 5)
            end)

            FM.moveTo(goal, goal)

            local arrived = false
            for _ = 1, 40 do
                if not S._peakTweening then
                    break
                end
                local root = FM.getRoot()
                if root then
                local dist = (root.Position - goal).Magnitude
                if dist <= 16 then
                    local recheck = FM.mountainPeakPosition()
                    if recheck and recheck.Y > goal.Y + 8 and (root.Position - recheck).Magnitude > 20 then
                        peak = recheck
                        break
                    end
                    arrived = true
                    break
                end
                if startPos and (root.Position - startPos).Magnitude <= 25 and dist > 80 then
                    arrived = false
                    break
                end
                end 
                task.wait(0.15)
            end

            if arrived then
                task.wait(0.45)
                local root = FM.getRoot()
                if root then
                    local stillNear = (root.Position - goal).Magnitude <= 22
                    local bounced = startPos and (root.Position - startPos).Magnitude <= 30
                    if stillNear and not bounced then
                        break
                    end
                    arrived = false
                else
                    break
                end
            end

            task.wait(0.2)
        end

        S._peakTweening = false
        pcall(function()
            if Move and Move.glideStop then Move.glideStop() end
        end)
        pcall(function()
            if Move and Move.setNoclip then Move.setNoclip(false, false) end
        end)

        local root = FM.getRoot()
        local finalPeak = FM.mountainPeakPosition() or peak
        local dist = root and (root.Position - finalPeak).Magnitude or 999
        WindUI:Notify({
            Title = "Mine A Mountain",
            Content = dist <= 22
                and string.format("At peak Y=%.0f", finalPeak.Y)
                or string.format("Peak retry done (%.0fm left)", dist),
            Duration = 3,
            Icon = dist <= 22 and "check" or "alert-circle",
        })
    end)

    WindUI:Notify({
        Title = "Mine A Mountain",
        Content = "Moving to peak (" .. tostring(S.farmMode or "Tween") .. ")...",
        Duration = 2,
        Icon = "check",
    })
    return true
end

function FM.stopAllTeleport()
    S._peakTweening = false

    pcall(function()
        if Move and Move.glideStop then
            Move.glideStop()
        end
    end)
    pcall(function()
        if Move and Move.setNoclip then
            Move.setNoclip(false, false)
        end
    end)
    pcall(function()
        if Move and Move.setFly then
            Move.setFly(false)
        end
    end)
    pcall(function()
        if finishTeleport then
            FM.finishTeleport()
        end
    end)

    local character = LocalPlayer.Character
    local root = FM.getRoot()
    local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

    if root then
        for _, inst in ipairs(root:GetChildren()) do
            if inst:IsA("AlignPosition") or inst:IsA("AlignOrientation")
                or inst:IsA("BodyVelocity") or inst:IsA("BodyPosition")
                or inst:IsA("BodyGyro") or inst:IsA("LinearVelocity")
                or inst:IsA("AngularVelocity") or inst:IsA("VectorForce") then
                pcall(function()
                    inst:Destroy()
                end)
            end
            if inst:IsA("Attachment") and inst.Name:find("FayyMeng", 1, true) then
                pcall(function()
                    inst:Destroy()
                end)
            end
        end
        pcall(function()
            root.Anchored = false
            root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
        end)
    end

    if humanoid then
        pcall(function()
            humanoid.PlatformStand = false
            humanoid.AutoRotate = true
            humanoid.Sit = false
            if humanoid.WalkSpeed < 8 then
                humanoid.WalkSpeed = 16
            end
            local state = humanoid:GetState()
            if state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics
                or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.PlatformStanding then
                humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
            end
        end)
    end

    WindUI:Notify({
        Title = "Mine A Mountain",
        Content = "All TP / tween stopped",
        Duration = 2,
        Icon = "check",
    })
end

do
    local function install()
        local CrystalBox = Tabs.esp

        CrystalBox:Dropdown({
            Title = "ESP Value Preset",
            Desc = "Quick min value for crystal ESP",
            Values = { "Off", "500K", "500M", "500B", "1T" },
            Value = "Off",
            Callback = function(opt)
                if type(opt) == "table" then opt = opt[1] or opt.Value or "Off" end
                opt = tostring(opt or "Off")
                local map = { Off = 0, ["500K"] = 5e5, ["500M"] = 5e8, ["500B"] = 5e11, ["1T"] = 1e12 }
                S.minValue = map[opt] or 0
                S.valueFilter = S.minValue > 0
                if FM.requestRefresh then FM.requestRefresh() end
            end
        })

        CrystalBox:Dropdown({
            Title = "ESP Luck Preset",
            Desc = "Minimum luck filter.",
            Values = { "Off", "1%", "5%", "10%", "25%", "50%", "100%", "200%", "500%" },
            Value = "Off",
            Callback = function(opt)
                if type(opt) == "table" then opt = opt[1] or opt.Value or "Off" end
                opt = tostring(opt or "Off")
                local map = {
                    Off = 0,
                    ["1%"] = 1,
                    ["5%"] = 5,
                    ["10%"] = 10,
                    ["25%"] = 25,
                    ["50%"] = 50,
                    ["100%"] = 100,
                    ["200%"] = 200,
                    ["500%"] = 500,
                }
                S.minLuck = map[opt] or tonumber((opt:gsub("%%", ""))) or 0
                if S.espActive then
                    pcall(function() FM.clearEsp() end)
                end
                if S.luckEspActive then
                    pcall(function() clearLuckEsp() end)
                    pcall(function() FM.refreshLuckEsp() end)
                end
                if FM.requestRefresh then FM.requestRefresh() end
            end
        })

        CrystalBox:Dropdown({
            Title = "ESP By Rarity",
            Desc = "ESP rarity filter.",
            Values = { "All", "Common", "Uncommon", "Rare", "Epic", "Legendary", "Mythic", "Empyrean", "Pulsar", "Quasar" },
            Multi = true,
            AllowNone = true,
            Value = {},
            Callback = function(map)
                local normalized = {}
                if type(map) == "table" then
                    for k, v in pairs(map) do
                        if type(k) == "string" and (v == true or v == "true") then
                            normalized[k] = true
                        elseif type(v) == "string" and v ~= "" then
                            normalized[v] = true
                        end
                    end
                elseif type(map) == "string" and map ~= "" then
                    normalized[map] = true
                end
                if normalized["All"] then
                    normalized = { All = true }
                end
                S.rarityFilter = normalized
                if S.espActive then
                    pcall(function()
                        FM.clearEsp()
                    end)
                end
                if FM.requestRefresh then FM.requestRefresh() end
            end
        })

        CrystalBox:Toggle({
            Title = "Crystal ESP",
            Desc = "Crystal labels.",
            Value = false,
            Callback = function(value)
                S.espActive = value
                if not value then
                    FM.clearEsp()
                end
                FM.updateTracking()
            end
        })

        local function setMinValue(text)
            local parsed = FM.parseValue(text)
            if not parsed then
                return
            end

            S.minValue = math.max(parsed, 0)
            S.valueFilter = S.minValue > 0
            FM.requestRefresh()
        end

        CrystalBox:Input({
            Title = "Min Value",
            Desc = "Minimum value filter.",
            Value = "2m",
            Placeholder = "2m",
            Type = "Input",
            Callback = setMinValue
        })

        S.StatsLabel = CrystalBox:Paragraph({
            Title = "Stats",
        })

        S.StatsLabel.SetText = function(self, text)
            self.Desc = text
        end

        local LuckBox = Tabs.esp

        LuckBox:Toggle({
            Title = "Luck ESP",
            Desc = "Show luck % + rarity on crystals",
            Value = false,
            Callback = function(value)
                S.luckEspActive = value == true
                if S.luckEspActive then
                    S.sweepAccumulator = math.huge
                    FM.updateTracking()
                    task.defer(function()
                        pcall(FM.sweep)
                        pcall(FM.refreshLuckEsp)
                    end)
                else
                    clearLuckEsp()
                    if not S.espActive then
                        FM.updateTracking()
                    end
                end
            end
        })

        LuckBox:Input({
            Title = "Min Luck %",
            Desc = "Minimum luck filter.",
            Value = "0",
            Placeholder = "e.g. 5",
            Type = "Input",
            Callback = function(text)
                local v = tonumber(text)
                if v then
                    S.minLuck = math.max(v, 0)
                    if S.luckEspActive then
                        clearLuckEsp()
                        FM.refreshLuckEsp()
                    end
                end
            end
        })
    end

    install()
end

            Desc = "Player labels.",

            Callback = function(value)

do
    local function install()
        local TopBox = Tabs.teleports

        local function fmtValue(_, score) return FM.formatShort(score, "$") end
        local function fmtLuck(_, score) return FM.formatLuck(score) end
        local function fmtWeight(inst, score)
            if type(score) == "number" then
                return FM.formatWeight(score)
            end
            return FM.formatWeight(FM.crystalWeight(inst))
        end

        local TP_OPTIONS = {
            ["Top 1 Value"]  = function() FM.tpToRank(FM.crystalValue,  1, fmtValue)  end,
            ["Top 1 Luck"]   = function() FM.tpToRank(FM.crystalLuck,   1, fmtLuck)   end,
            ["Top 1 Weight"] = function() FM.tpToRank(FM.crystalWeight, 1, fmtWeight) end,
        }

        local selectedTopTarget = "Top 1 Value"

        TopBox:Dropdown({
            Title = "Top Target",
            Desc = "Choose target type, then press Teleport",
            Values = {
                "Top 1 Value",
                "Top 1 Luck",
                "Top 1 Weight",
            },
            Value = "Top 1 Value",
            Callback = function(v)
                if type(v) == "string" and v ~= "" then
                    selectedTopTarget = v
                end
            end,
        })

        TopBox:Button({
            Title = "Teleport Top Crystal",
            Desc = "Teleport to top target.",
            Callback = function()
                local key = selectedTopTarget or "Top 1 Value"
                local fn = TP_OPTIONS[key]
                if fn then
                    fn()
                else
                    pcall(function()
                        WindUI:Notify({
                            Title = "FayyMeng",
                            Content = "Select Top 1 Value, Luck, or Weight first",
                            Duration = 3,
                        })
                    end)
                end
            end,
        })
    end

    install()
end


do
    local function install()
        local PickupBox = Tabs.pickup
        if not PickupBox then return end

        pcall(function()
            PickupBox:Section({ Title = "Pickup", TextSize = 17 })
        end)

        PickupBox:Toggle({
            Title = "Instant Prompt",
            Flag = "cfg_InstantPrompt",
            Desc = "Skip hold time on proximity prompts",
            Value = true,
            Callback = function(v)
                if FM.setInstantPrompt then
                    FM.setInstantPrompt(v == true)
                end
            end
        })
        task.defer(function()
            if FM.setInstantPrompt then pcall(FM.setInstantPrompt, true) end
        end)

        PickupBox:Toggle({
            Title = "Auto Pickup Rune",
            Flag = "cfg_AutoPickupRune",
            Desc = "Rune collection (world runes near you).",
            Value = false,
            Callback = function(v)
                v = v == true
                -- Always wire BOTH isolated system + Mountain auto-grab (Pickup tab)
                pcall(function()
                    if FM.RunePickupSystem and FM.RunePickupSystem.SetEnabled then
                        FM.RunePickupSystem:SetEnabled(v)
                    end
                end)
                pcall(function()
                    if Mountain and Mountain.setAutoGrab then
                        Mountain.setAutoGrab(v)
                    end
                end)
                S.autoRunePickup = v
            end
        })

        pcall(function()
            PickupBox:Section({ Title = "Crystal Drop / Pickup", TextSize = 17 })
        end)

        PickupBox:Button({
            Title = "Drop All Crystals",
            Desc = "Drop inventory crystals.",
            Callback = function()
                if FM.dropAllCrystals then
                    FM.dropAllCrystals()
                else
                    local remote = FM.findRemote and FM.findRemote("CrystalDropRequest")
                    if not remote then
                        return
                    end
                    local backpack = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
                    local character = LocalPlayer.Character
                    local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    local tools = {}
                    local function consider(container)
                        if not container then return end
                        for _, t in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
                            if t:IsA("Tool") then
                                if FM.isCrystal and FM.isCrystal(t) then
                                    tools[#tools + 1] = t
                                elseif t:GetAttribute("Value") ~= nil or t:GetAttribute("Tier") ~= nil then
                                    tools[#tools + 1] = t
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    consider(backpack)
                    consider(character)
                    task.spawn(function()
                        for _, tool in ipairs(tools) do
                            if humanoid and tool.Parent ~= character then
                                pcall(function() humanoid:EquipTool(tool) end)
                                task.wait(0.05)
                            end
                            pcall(function() remote:FireServer(tool.Name) end)
                            task.wait(0.08)
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end,
        })

        PickupBox:Button({
            Title = "Pickup All Dropped Crystals",
            Desc = "Pick up dropped crystals.",
            Callback = function()
                if FM.pickupAllDroppedCrystals then
                    FM.pickupAllDroppedCrystals()
                else
                    local Hold = FM.findRemote and FM.findRemote("CrystalHoldComplete")
                    if not Hold then return end
                    local root = FM.getRoot and FM.getRoot()
                    if not root then return end
                    local origin = root.Position
                    local targets, seen = {}, {}
                    local function add(part)
                        if not part or seen[part] or not part:IsA("BasePart") then return end
                        if FM.getAttr and FM.getAttr(part, "Collected") == true then return end
                        local okCrystal = (FM.isCrystal and FM.isCrystal(part))
                            or part:GetAttribute("Value") ~= nil
                            or part:GetAttribute("Tier") ~= nil
                        if not okCrystal then return end
                        if (part.Position - origin).Magnitude > 150 then return end
                        seen[part] = true
                        targets[#targets + 1] = part
                    end
                    local function scan(folder)
                        if not folder then return end
                        for _, d in ipairs(folder:GetDescendants()) do
                            if d:IsA("BasePart") then add(d) end
                        end
                    end
                    scan(Workspace:FindFirstChild("DroppedCrystals"))
                    local things = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Things")
                    if things then scan(things:FindFirstChild("DroppedCrystals")) end
                    task.spawn(function()
                        for _, crystal in ipairs(targets) do
                            if crystal.Parent then
                                pcall(function() Hold:FireServer(crystal) end)
                                task.wait(0.08)
                            end
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end,
        })

        PickupBox:Paragraph({
            Title = "Note",
            Desc = "Inventory drop and pickup.",
        })
    end
    install()
end


do
    local godStateConn = nil
    local godEnabled = false
    local godHealthConn = nil
    local godCharConn = nil

    local function clearGodConnections()
        if godHealthConn then
            pcall(function() godHealthConn:Disconnect() end)
            godHealthConn = nil
        end
        if godStateConn then
            pcall(function() godStateConn:Disconnect() end)
            godStateConn = nil
        end
    end

    -- God Mode: prefer non-destructive harden first; fallback clone without
    -- leaving LocalPlayer.Character nil longer than necessary. Always rebind CharacterManager.
    local function applyGodToCharacter(char)
        if not char or not godEnabled then
            return
        end
        if not char.Parent then
            return
        end

        clearGodConnections()

        local human = char:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Humanoid")
        if not human then
            return
        end

        -- Method A: soft god (keeps same Humanoid instance — best for lifecycle)
        local softOk = pcall(function()
            human.MaxHealth = math.huge
            human.Health = math.huge
            human.BreakJointsOnDeath = false
            human:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead, false)
            human:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown, false)
            human:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll, false)
        end)

        -- Method B: clone Humanoid only if soft path unavailable (avoid Character=nil race)
        if not softOk or human.MaxHealth < 1e9 then
            pcall(function()
                local Cam = Workspace.CurrentCamera
                local Pos = Cam and Cam.CFrame
                local old = human
                local nHuman = old:Clone()
                nHuman.BreakJointsOnDeath = false
                nHuman:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead, false)
                nHuman:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown, false)
                nHuman:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll, false)
                nHuman:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics, false)
                nHuman.MaxHealth = math.huge
                nHuman.Health = math.huge
                nHuman.Parent = char
                old:Destroy()
                human = nHuman
                if Cam then
                    Cam.CameraSubject = nHuman
                    if Pos then Cam.CFrame = Pos end
                end
                local anim = char:FindFirstChild("Animate")
                if anim then
                    anim.Disabled = true
                    task.defer(function()
                        if anim and anim.Parent then
                            anim.Disabled = false
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end)
            human = char:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Humanoid")
        end

        if human then
            pcall(function()
                human.MaxHealth = math.huge
                human.Health = math.huge
                human.BreakJointsOnDeath = false
                human:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead, false)
            end)
            godHealthConn = human.HealthChanged:Connect(function()
                if not godEnabled then return end
                if human and human.Parent and human.Health < human.MaxHealth then
                    human.Health = human.MaxHealth
                end
            end)
            pcall(function()
                godStateConn = human.StateChanged:Connect(function(_, new)
                    if not godEnabled or not human or not human.Parent then return end
                    if new == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead
                        or new == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown
                        or new == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll then
                        pcall(function()
                            human:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
                            human.Health = human.MaxHealth
                        end)
                    end
                end)
            end)
        end

        -- CRITICAL: refresh CharacterManager so Farm/Money get live Root/Humanoid
        pcall(function()
            if FM.CharacterManager and FM.CharacterManager.Refresh then
                FM.CharacterManager:Refresh(char)
            else
                FM.bindCharacter(char)
            end
        end)
    end

    local function setGodMode(value)
        godEnabled = value == true
        S.godMode = godEnabled

        if godEnabled then
            local char = LocalPlayer.Character
            if char then
                applyGodToCharacter(char)
            end
            -- CharacterManager.OnReady also re-applies via FM._godReapply
            if not godCharConn then
                godCharConn = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
                    task.delay(0.4, function()
                        if godEnabled and char and char.Parent then
                            applyGodToCharacter(char)
                        end
                    end)
                end)
            end
            pcall(function()
                WindUI:Notify({
                    Title = "God Mode",
                    Content = "God Mode enabled",
                    Duration = 2,
                    Icon = "check",
                })
            end)
        else
            clearGodConnections()
            local human = FM.getHumanoid and FM.getHumanoid() or (LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Humanoid"))
            if human then
                pcall(function()
                    human.MaxHealth = 100
                    human.Health = math.min(human.Health, 100)
                    human.BreakJointsOnDeath = true
                    human:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead, true)
                    human:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown, true)
                    human:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll, true)
                end)
            end
            pcall(function()
                WindUI:Notify({
                    Title = "God Mode",
                    Content = "God Mode disabled",
                    Duration = 2,
                    Icon = "x",
                })
            end)
        end
    end

    FM.setGodMode = setGodMode
    FM._godReapply = applyGodToCharacter

    local function install()
        local MoveBox = Tabs.movement

        MoveBox:Section({ Title = "Movement", TextSize = 17 })

        MoveBox:Toggle({
            Title = "Speed Boost",
            Desc = "Increased walk speed.",
            Value = true,
            Callback = FM.setSpeedBoost
        })

        task.defer(function()
            if S._speedWanted ~= false then
                FM.setSpeedBoost(true)
            end
        end)

        MoveBox:Toggle({
            Title = "Noclip",
            Desc = "Walk through walls and terrain",
            Value = false,
            Callback = Move.setNoclip
        })

        MoveBox:Toggle({
            Title = "Inf Jump",
            Desc = "Infinite jump.",
            Value = false,
            Callback = Move.setInfJump
        })

        MoveBox:Toggle({
            Title = "Fly",
            Desc = "Flight movement.",
            Value = false,
            Callback = Move.setFly
        })

        MoveBox:Slider({
            Title = "Fly Speed",
            Desc = "How fast you fly when Fly is enabled",
            Step = 1,
            Value = {
                Min = 20,
                Max = 300,
                Default = 100,
            },
            Callback = Move.setFlySpeed
        })

        MoveBox:Toggle({
            Title = "God Mode",
            Desc = "Damage protection.",
            Value = false,
            Callback = setGodMode,
        })
    end

    install()
end

do
    local MiscState = {
        antiFling = false,
        clickTp = false,
        autoRejoin = false,
        fpsBoost = false,
        clickTpConn = nil,
        antiFlingConn = nil,
        rejoinConn = nil,
        originalLighting = nil,
    }

    local function setAntiFling(value)
        MiscState.antiFling = value == true
        if MiscState.antiFlingConn then
            pcall(function() MiscState.antiFlingConn:Disconnect() end)
            MiscState.antiFlingConn = nil
        end
        if not MiscState.antiFling then
            return
        end
        MiscState.antiFlingConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if not MiscState.antiFling then
                return
            end
            local char = LocalPlayer.Character
            if not char then
                return
            end
            local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not root then
                return
            end
            local vel = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
            local maxH = 90
            local maxV = 120
            local hx, hz = vel.X, vel.Z
            local hy = vel.Y
            local hMag = math.sqrt(hx * hx + hz * hz)
            if hMag > maxH then
                local scale = maxH / hMag
                hx, hz = hx * scale, hz * scale
            end
            if hy > maxV then
                hy = maxV
            elseif hy < -maxV then
                hy = -maxV
            end
            if hx ~= vel.X or hy ~= vel.Y or hz ~= vel.Z then
                root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(hx, hy, hz)
            end
            for _, child in ipairs(root:GetChildren()) do
                if child:IsA("BodyVelocity") or child:IsA("BodyForce") or child:IsA("BodyAngularVelocity") then
                    if not child:GetAttribute("FM_Keep") then
                        pcall(function() child:Destroy() end)
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
    end

    local function setClickTp(value)
        MiscState.clickTp = value == true
        if MiscState.clickTpConn then
            pcall(function() MiscState.clickTpConn:Disconnect() end)
            MiscState.clickTpConn = nil
        end
        if not MiscState.clickTp then
            return
        end
        MiscState.clickTpConn = UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gameProcessed)
            if gameProcessed or not MiscState.clickTp then
                return
            end
            if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
                return
            end
            local cam = Workspace.CurrentCamera
            local root = FM.getRoot and FM.getRoot() or (LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"))
            if not cam or not root then
                return
            end
            local mouse = LocalPlayer:GetMouse()
            local unitRay = cam:ScreenPointToRay(mouse.X, mouse.Y)
            local params = RaycastParams.new()
            params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
            params.FilterDescendantsInstances = { LocalPlayer.Character }
            local result = Workspace:Raycast(unitRay.Origin, unitRay.Direction * 2000, params)
            if result and result.Position then
                local dest = result.Position + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
                if FM.teleportTo then
                    FM.teleportTo(dest)
                else
                    root.CFrame = CFrame.new(dest)
                end
            end
        end)
    end

    local function setAutoRejoin(value)
        MiscState.autoRejoin = value == true
        if MiscState.rejoinConn then
            pcall(function() MiscState.rejoinConn:Disconnect() end)
            MiscState.rejoinConn = nil
        end
        if not MiscState.autoRejoin then
            return
        end
        local GuiService = game:GetService("GuiService")
        local TeleportService = game:GetService("TeleportService")
        MiscState.rejoinConn = GuiService.ErrorMessageChanged:Connect(function()
            if not MiscState.autoRejoin then
                return
            end
            task.delay(1.5, function()
                if not MiscState.autoRejoin then
                    return
                end
                pcall(function()
                    TeleportService:Teleport(game.PlaceId, LocalPlayer)
                end)
            end)
        end)
        pcall(function()
            LocalPlayer.OnTeleport:Connect(function(state)
                if state == Enum.TeleportState.Failed and MiscState.autoRejoin then
                    task.delay(2, function()
                        pcall(function()
                            TeleportService:Teleport(game.PlaceId, LocalPlayer)
                        end)
                    end)
                end
            end)
        end)
    end

    local function setFpsBoost(value)
        MiscState.fpsBoost = value == true
        local Lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
        local Terrain = Workspace:FindFirstChildOfClass("Terrain")

        if MiscState.fpsBoost then
            if not MiscState.originalLighting then
                MiscState.originalLighting = {
                    GlobalShadows = Lighting.GlobalShadows,
                    FogEnd = Lighting.FogEnd,
                    Brightness = Lighting.Brightness,
                    QualityLevel = settings().Rendering.QualityLevel,
                }
            end
            pcall(function()
                Lighting.GlobalShadows = false
                Lighting.FogEnd = 9e9
                settings().Rendering.QualityLevel = Enum.QualityLevel.Level01
            end)
            pcall(function()
                if Terrain then
                    Terrain.Decoration = false
                end
            end)
            pcall(function()
                for _, v in ipairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do
                    if v:IsA("BlurEffect") or v:IsA("SunRaysEffect") or v:IsA("BloomEffect")
                        or v:IsA("ColorCorrectionEffect") or v:IsA("DepthOfFieldEffect") then
                        v.Enabled = false
                    end
                end
            end)
        else
            if MiscState.originalLighting then
                pcall(function()
                    Lighting.GlobalShadows = MiscState.originalLighting.GlobalShadows
                    Lighting.FogEnd = MiscState.originalLighting.FogEnd
                    settings().Rendering.QualityLevel = MiscState.originalLighting.QualityLevel
                end)
                MiscState.originalLighting = nil
            end
        end
    end

    FM.setAntiFling = setAntiFling
    FM.setClickTp = setClickTp
    FM.setAutoRejoin = setAutoRejoin
    FM.setFpsBoost = setFpsBoost

    local function installMisc()
        if not Tabs.misc then
            return
        end
        local MiscBox = Tabs.misc

        MiscBox:Section({ Title = "Protection", TextSize = 17 })

        MiscBox:Toggle({
            Title = "No Camera Shake",
            Desc = "Disable camera shake effects",
            Value = true,
            Callback = function(value)
                pcall(function()
                    if type(FM_setNoCameraShake) == "function" then
                        FM_setNoCameraShake(value == true)
                    end
                end)
            end
        })

        MiscBox:Toggle({
            Title = "Anti Ragdoll",
            Desc = "Anti-ragdoll.",
            Value = true,
            Callback = FM.setAntiRagdoll,
        })

        MiscBox:Toggle({
            Title = "Anti Fling",
            Desc = "Anti-fling.",
            Value = true,
            Callback = setAntiFling,
        })

        task.defer(function()
            pcall(setAntiFling, true)
        end)

        MiscBox:Section({ Title = "Utility", TextSize = 17 })

        MiscBox:Toggle({
            Title = "Click Teleport",
            Desc = "Click teleport.",
            Value = false,
            Callback = setClickTp,
        })

        MiscBox:Toggle({
            Title = "Auto Rejoin On Kick",
            Desc = "Auto-rejoin.",
            Value = false,
            Callback = setAutoRejoin,
        })

        MiscBox:Toggle({
            Title = "FPS Boost",
            Desc = "Low graphics mode.",
            Value = false,
            Callback = setFpsBoost,
        })
    end

    task.defer(installMisc)
end

local aimInputConn

do
    local function install()
        local MOUSE_KEYS = {
            [Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1] = "MB1",
            [Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2] = "MB2",
            [Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton3] = "MB3",
        }

        local function pressedKeyName(input)
            local pressed = MOUSE_KEYS[input.UserInputType]
            if not pressed and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
                pressed = input.KeyCode.Name
            end
            return pressed
        end

        local aimKeybind = "F"

        aimInputConn = UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, processed)
            if processed then
                return
            end
            if UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() then
                return
            end

            local pressed = pressedKeyName(input)
            if not pressed then
                return
            end

            if S.instantPromptActive and pressed == "E" then
                local ok, err = pcall(FM.instantGrab)
                if not ok then
                    FM.reportError("instant", err)
                end
            end

            if not S.aimTpEnabled then
                return
            end

            if pressed == aimKeybind then
                local ok, err = pcall(FM.aimTeleport)
                if not ok then
                    FM.reportError("aim", err)
                end
            end
        end)

        local function updateKeybind(key)
            aimKeybind = key
        end

    end

    install()
end

local Net = {}
local netConns = {}

do
    local function install()
        local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")
        local TeleportService = game:GetService("TeleportService")
        local GuiService = game:GetService("GuiService")
        local PLACE = game.PlaceId
        local PAGES = 1
        local POOL_TARGET = 20
        local RETRY_STEP = 1.5
        local RETRY_LIMIT = 10
        local BACK_DELAY = 3
        local REFILL_MARK = 8
        local WARM_STEP = 30

        local visited = {}
        local pool = {}
        local hopping = false
        local reviving = false
        local lastCode = 0
        local alive = true

        local function note(text)
            pcall(function()
                WindUI:Notify({
                    Title = "Hop",
                    Content = text,
                    Duration = 4,
                    Icon = "globe",
                })
            end)
        end

        local function grab(link)
            local sender = (syn and syn.request) or (http and http.request) or http_request or request

            if type(sender) == "function" then
                local ok, response = pcall(function()
                    return sender({ Url = link, Method = "GET" })
                end)

                if ok and type(response) == "table" then
                    local code = tonumber(response.StatusCode) or 0

                    if code >= 200 and code < 300 and type(response.Body) == "string" then
                        return response.Body, code
                    end

                    return nil, code
                end
            end

            local ok, body = pcall(function()
                return game:HttpGet(link)
            end)

            if ok and type(body) == "string" then
                return body, 200
            end

            return nil, 0
        end

        local function fetch(cursor)
            local link = string.format("https://games.roblox.com/v1/games/%d/servers/Public?sortOrder=Desc&excludeFullGames=true&limit=100", PLACE)

            if type(cursor) == "string" and cursor ~= "" then
                link ..= "&cursor=" .. cursor
            end

            local body, code = grab(link)

            if type(body) ~= "string" then
                lastCode = code or 0
                return nil, nil
            end

            lastCode = 200

            local parsed, data = pcall(function()
                return HttpService:JSONDecode(body)
            end)

            if not parsed or type(data) ~= "table" then
                lastCode = -1
                return nil, nil
            end

            return data.data, data.nextPageCursor
        end

        local function refill()
            table.clear(pool)

            local cursor

            for _ = 1, PAGES do
                local entries, nextCursor = fetch(cursor)

                if type(entries) == "table" then
                    for _, entry in ipairs(entries) do
                        local id = type(entry) == "table" and entry.id or nil

                        if type(id) == "string" and id ~= tostring(game.JobId) and not visited[id] then
                            local skip = false
                            pcall(function()
                                local g = typeof(getgenv) == "function" and getgenv().FayyMeng_VisitedJobs
                                if g and g[id] and type(g[id]) == "number" and (os.time() - g[id]) < 1800 then
                                    skip = true
                                end
                            end)
                            if not skip then
                                local playing = tonumber(entry.playing) or 0
                                local room = tonumber(entry.maxPlayers) or 0
                                if room > 0 and playing < room then
                                    pool[#pool + 1] = id
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end

                cursor = nextCursor

                if type(cursor) ~= "string" or cursor == "" or #pool >= POOL_TARGET then
                    break
                end
            end

            for index = #pool, 2, -1 do
                local swap = math.random(1, index)
                pool[index], pool[swap] = pool[swap], pool[index]
            end

            return #pool
        end

        function Net.rejoin()
            if reviving then
                return
            end

            reviving = true
            local jobId = game.JobId

            task.spawn(function()
                for _ = 1, RETRY_LIMIT do
                    local sent = pcall(function()
                        TeleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(PLACE, jobId, LocalPlayer)
                    end)

                    if not sent then
                        pcall(function()
                            TeleportService:Teleport(PLACE, LocalPlayer)
                        end)
                    end

                    task.wait(RETRY_STEP)
                end

                reviving = false
            end)
        end

        function Net.hop()
            if hopping then
                return false
            end

            hopping = true
            local currentJob = tostring(game.JobId)
            visited[currentJob] = true
            pcall(function()
                if typeof(getgenv) == "function" then
                    getgenv().FayyMeng_VisitedJobs = getgenv().FayyMeng_VisitedJobs or {}
                    getgenv().FayyMeng_VisitedJobs[currentJob] = os.time()
                end
            end)

            task.spawn(function()
                pcall(FM_queueReloadAfterTeleport)

                for round = 1, RETRY_LIMIT do
                    if #pool == 0 and refill() == 0 then
                        table.clear(visited)
                        visited[tostring(game.JobId)] = true
                        refill()
                    end

                    local choice = nil
                    while #pool > 0 do
                        local cand = table.remove(pool)
                        if cand and cand ~= tostring(game.JobId) and not visited[cand] then
                            choice = cand
                            break
                        end
                    end

                    if choice then
                        visited[choice] = true
                        pcall(function()
                            if typeof(getgenv) == "function" then
                                getgenv().FayyMeng_VisitedJobs[choice] = os.time()
                            end
                        end)
                        note(string.format("try %d  %s", round, string.sub(choice, 1, 8)))

                        local sent, err = pcall(function()
                            TeleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(PLACE, choice, LocalPlayer)
                        end)

                        if not sent then
                            note("blocked  " .. tostring(err))
                        else
                            task.wait(RETRY_STEP)
                            if tostring(game.JobId) ~= currentJob then
                                hopping = false
                                return
                            end
                        end

                        if #pool <= REFILL_MARK then
                            task.spawn(refill)
                        end
                    else
                        note(string.format("no servers  http %d", lastCode))
                    end

                    task.wait(RETRY_STEP)
                end

                hopping = false
            end)

            return true
        end

        function Net.forget()
            table.clear(visited)
            table.clear(pool)
            visited[game.JobId] = true
        end

        function Net.busy()
            return hopping
        end

        function Net.ready()
            return #pool
        end

        function Net.stop()
            alive = false
        end

        task.spawn(function()
            while alive do
                if #pool < POOL_TARGET and not hopping then
                    refill()
                end

                task.wait(WARM_STEP)
            end
        end)

        netConns[#netConns + 1] = TeleportService.TeleportInitFailed:Connect(function()
            hopping = false
        end)

        netConns[#netConns + 1] = GuiService.ErrorMessageChanged:Connect(function()
            local ok, message = pcall(function()
                return GuiService:GetErrorMessage()
            end)

            if ok and type(message) == "string" and message ~= "" then
                task.delay(BACK_DELAY, Net.rejoin)
            end
        end)

        local prompts = CoreGui:FindFirstChild("RobloxPromptGui")
        local overlay = prompts and prompts:FindFirstChild("promptOverlay")

        if overlay then
            netConns[#netConns + 1] = overlay.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
                if child.Name:find("ErrorPrompt") then
                    task.delay(BACK_DELAY, Net.rejoin)
                end
            end)
        end
    end

    install()
end

function FM.resolveUserId(input)
    if input == nil then
        return nil
    end
    input = tostring(input):gsub("%s+", "")
    if input == "" then
        return nil
    end
    local asNum = tonumber(input)
    if asNum and asNum > 0 then
        return math.floor(asNum)
    end
    local ok, userId = pcall(function()
        return Players:GetUserIdFromNameAsync(input)
    end)
    if ok and type(userId) == "number" and userId > 0 then
        return userId
    end
    return nil
end

local RADAR_DISPLAY = {
    CrystalRadar = "Crystal Radar",
    CaveRadar = "Cave Radar",
    LavaRadar = "Lava Radar",
    BombveinsRadar = "Bombveins Radar",
    AetherstoneRadar = "Aetherstone Radar",
    BoulderRadar = "Boulder Radar",
}

function FM.queryRadarStock()
    local query = RadarShopQuery or FM.findRemote("RadarShopQuery")
    if not query then return nil end
    local ok, data = pcall(function() return query:InvokeServer() end)
    if ok and type(data) == "table" then return data end
    return nil
end

function FM.checkRadarStockNotify()
    task.spawn(function()
        local data = FM.queryRadarStock()
        if not data or type(data.stock) ~= "table" then
            WindUI:Notify({ Title = "Radar Stock", Content = "Could not read radar stock", Duration = 3, Icon = "alert-circle" })
            return
        end
        local lines = {}
        for id, amount in pairs(data.stock) do
            local n = tonumber(amount) or 0
            if n > 0 then
                lines[#lines + 1] = string.format("%s x%d", RADAR_DISPLAY[id] or id, n)
            end
        end
        table.sort(lines)
        local restock = tonumber(data.secondsToRestock) or 0
        local extra = restock > 0 and string.format("\nRestock %dm %ds", math.floor(restock/60), restock%60) or ""
        if #lines == 0 then
            WindUI:Notify({ Title = "Radar Stock", Content = "No radars in stock" .. extra, Duration = 5, Icon = "alert-circle" })
        else
            WindUI:Notify({ Title = "Radar Stock", Content = table.concat(lines, "\n") .. extra, Duration = 7, Icon = "check" })
        end
    end)
end

function FM.buyAllRadars()
    task.spawn(function()
        local buy = RadarBuyRequest or FM.findRemote("RadarBuyRequest")
        if not buy then
            WindUI:Notify({ Title = "Radar", Content = "RadarBuyRequest missing", Duration = 3, Icon = "alert-circle" })
            return
        end
        local data = FM.queryRadarStock()
        if not data or type(data.stock) ~= "table" then
            WindUI:Notify({ Title = "Radar", Content = "No stock data", Duration = 3, Icon = "alert-circle" })
            return
        end
        local bought = 0
        for id, amount in pairs(data.stock) do
            local n = tonumber(amount) or 0
            for _ = 1, n do
                local ok, res = pcall(function() return buy:InvokeServer(id, 1) end)
                if ok and type(res) == "table" and res.ok then
                    bought += 1
                else
                    break
                end
                task.wait(0.15)
            end
        end
        WindUI:Notify({ Title = "Radar", Content = "Bought " .. tostring(bought) .. " radar(s)", Duration = 3, Icon = bought > 0 and "check" or "alert-circle" })
    end)
end

function FM.findRadarTool(preferName)
    preferName = preferName or "BoulderRadar"
    local function scan(container)
        if not container then return nil end
        for _, child in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
            if child:IsA("Tool") and type(child.Name) == "string" then
                if child.Name == preferName or (child.Name:find("Boulder", 1, true) and child.Name:find("Radar", 1, true)) then
                    return child
                end
            end
        end
        return nil
    end
    return scan(LocalPlayer.Character) or scan(LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack"))
end

function FM.activateGameRadar(radarName)
    radarName = radarName or "BoulderRadar"
    local remote = FM.findRemote("RadarActivate")
    if not remote then
        return false, "RadarActivate missing"
    end
    local tool = FM.findRadarTool(radarName)
    if tool then
        local hum = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if hum and tool.Parent ~= LocalPlayer.Character then
            pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(tool) end)
            task.wait(0.2)
        end
        radarName = tool.Name
    end
    local ok = pcall(function()
        remote:FireServer(radarName, true)
    end)
    if ok then
        S._activeGameRadar = radarName
        pcall(function()
            LocalPlayer:SetAttribute("ActiveRadarId", radarName)
        end)
    end
    return ok
end

function FM.deactivateGameRadar()
    local remote = FM.findRemote("RadarActivate")
    if remote then
        pcall(function()
            remote:FireServer(nil, false)
        end)
    end
    S._activeGameRadar = nil
    pcall(function()
        LocalPlayer:SetAttribute("ActiveRadarId", nil)
    end)
end

function FM.upgradeWeight(tier, silent)
    local remote = UpgradeBuy or FM.findRemote("UpgradeBuy")
    if not remote then
        if not silent then
            WindUI:Notify({ Title = "Upgrade", Content = "UpgradeBuy remote missing", Duration = 3, Icon = "alert-circle" })
        end
        return false
    end
    tier = tonumber(tier) or 1
    local ok = pcall(function() remote:FireServer("Weight", tier) end)
    if not silent then
        local label = ({[1]="1kg",[2]="5kg",[3]="10kg"})[tier] or tostring(tier)
        WindUI:Notify({ Title = "Upgrade Weight", Content = "Requested " .. label, Duration = 2, Icon = "check" })
    end
    return ok == true
end

function FM.buyWeightBulk(tier, times)
    tier = tonumber(tier) or 1
    times = math.max(1, math.floor(tonumber(times) or 1))
    local label = ({[1]="1kg",[2]="5kg",[3]="10kg"})[tier] or tostring(tier)
    local success = 0
    for i = 1, times do
        if FM.upgradeWeight(tier, true) then
            success += 1
        end
        if i < times then
            task.wait(0.35)
        end
    end
    WindUI:Notify({
        Title = "Upgrade Weight",
        Content = string.format("Successfully bought %dx %s weight upgrade", success, label),
        Duration = 4,
        Icon = success > 0 and "check" or "alert-circle",
    })
    return success
end

function FM.captureFeatureSnapshot()
    local snap = {}
    pcall(function()
        snap.boulderFarm = Farm and Farm.isActive and Farm.isActive() == true
    end)
    pcall(function()
        snap.moneyFarm = Money and Money.isActive and Money.isActive() == true
    end)
    snap.veinFarm = S.veinFarmActive == true
    snap.fastDig = S.fastDigActive == true
    snap.esp = S.espActive == true
    snap.playerEsp = S.playerEspActive == true
    snap.luckEsp = S.luckEspActive == true
    snap.pickupValue = S.pickupValueActive == true
    snap.pickupLuck = S.pickupLuckActive == true
    snap.pickupRarity = S.pickupRarityActive == true
    snap.autoPickup = S.autoPickupActive == true
    snap.autoGrabCrystal = S.autoGrabCrystal == true
    snap.skipVein = S.skipVeinActive ~= false
    snap.speed = S.speedActive == true or S._speedWanted == true
    snap.noCameraShake = S.noCameraShake == true
    snap.antiRagdoll = S.antiRagdoll == true
    snap.farmMode = S.farmMode
    snap.pickupMode = S.pickupMode
    snap.digSpeedMult = S.digSpeedMult
    snap.safeMode = S.safeMode == true
    return snap
end

function FM.stopAllRuntimeFeatures()
    pcall(function()
        if Farm and Farm.stop then Farm.stop() end
    end)
    pcall(function()
        if Money and Money.stop then Money.stop() end
    end)
    pcall(function()
        if FM.setVeinFarm then FM.setVeinFarm(false) end
    end)
    pcall(function()
        S.fastDigActive = false
    end)
    pcall(function()
        if Mountain and Mountain.setAutoGrab then Mountain.setAutoGrab(false) end
    end)
    pcall(function()
        if Mountain and Mountain.setAutoGrabCrystal then Mountain.setAutoGrabCrystal(false) end
    end)
    pcall(function()
        if Move and Move.glideStop then Move.glideStop() end
    end)
    pcall(function()
        if Move and Move.forceClearFarmMovement then Move.forceClearFarmMovement() end
    end)
    pcall(function()
