        if Move and Move.setFly then Move.setFly(false) end
    end)
    pcall(function()
        if Move and Move.setNoclip then Move.setNoclip(false, false) end
    end)
    pcall(function()
        if FM.stopAllTeleport then FM.stopAllTeleport() end
    end)
    pcall(function()
        if FM.finishTeleport then FM.finishTeleport() end
    end)
end

function FM.resetRuntimeCaches()
    S._refreshSession = (S._refreshSession or 0) + 1
    S._filterCandCache = nil
    S._veinDeposits = nil
    S._veinFarmTarget = nil
    S._veinLootPhase = false
    S._veinFarmStatus = nil
    S._slowMove = false
    S.miningBoulder = nil
    S.safeFleeing = false
    S.safeReturnPos = nil
    S.lastFilteredPickup = 0
    S.lastPickup = 0
    S._pickupBusyUntil = 0
    S.farmActive = false

    pcall(function()
        if FM.clearRemoteCache then FM.clearRemoteCache() end
    end)

    
    pcall(function()
        if S.dirty and table.clear then
            table.clear(S.dirty)
        end
    end)
    pcall(function()
        if S.candidates and table.clear then
            table.clear(S.candidates)
        end
    end)
    pcall(function()
        if S.recentDrops and table.clear then
            table.clear(S.recentDrops)
        end
    end)

    
    S.rootPart = nil
    pcall(function()
        if FM.getRoot then FM.getRoot() end
    end)

    
    pcall(function()
        SellRequest = FM.findRemote("SellRequest") or SellRequest
        GoHome = FM.findRemote("GoHome") or GoHome
        HoldComplete = FM.findRemote("CrystalHoldComplete") or HoldComplete
        ToggleFavorite = FM.findRemote("ToggleFavorite") or ToggleFavorite
        UpgradeBuy = FM.findRemote("UpgradeBuy") or UpgradeBuy
    end)
end

function FM.restoreFeatureSnapshot(snap)
    if type(snap) ~= "table" then
        return
    end

    if snap.farmMode then
        S.farmMode = snap.farmMode
    end
    if snap.pickupMode then
        S.pickupMode = snap.pickupMode
    end
    if snap.digSpeedMult then
        S.digSpeedMult = snap.digSpeedMult
    end
    S.safeMode = snap.safeMode == true
    S.skipVeinActive = snap.skipVein ~= false

    
    if snap.esp then
        S.espActive = true
        pcall(function()
            if FM.requestRefresh then FM.requestRefresh() end
        end)
    end
    if snap.playerEsp then
        S.playerEspActive = true
    end
    if snap.luckEsp then
        S.luckEspActive = true
    end

    S.pickupValueActive = snap.pickupValue == true
    S.pickupLuckActive = snap.pickupLuck == true
    S.pickupRarityActive = snap.pickupRarity == true
    S.autoPickupActive = snap.autoPickup == true

    if snap.autoGrabCrystal then
        pcall(function()
            if Mountain and Mountain.setAutoGrabCrystal then
                Mountain.setAutoGrabCrystal(true)
            else
                S.autoGrabCrystal = true
            end
        end)
    end

    if snap.fastDig then
        S.fastDigActive = true
    end

    if snap.speed then
        S._speedWanted = true
        S.speedActive = true
        pcall(function()
            local hum = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if hum and FM.watchSpeed then
                FM.watchSpeed(hum)
                if FM.enforceSpeed then FM.enforceSpeed(hum) end
            end
        end)
    end

    if snap.noCameraShake and FM_setNoCameraShake then
        pcall(FM_setNoCameraShake, true)
    end

    if snap.antiRagdoll then
        pcall(function()
            if FM.setAntiRagdoll then FM.setAntiRagdoll(true) end
            S.antiRagdoll = true
        end)
    end

    
    if snap.boulderFarm then
        pcall(function()
            if Farm and Farm.setActive then
                Farm.setActive(true)
            end
        end)
    end
    if snap.moneyFarm then
        pcall(function()
            if Money and Money.setActive then
                Money.setActive(true)
            end
        end)
    end
    if snap.veinFarm then
        pcall(function()
            if FM.setVeinFarm then
                FM.setVeinFarm(true)
            end
        end)
    end
end

function FM.systemReset()
    if S._refreshBusy then
        WindUI:Notify({
            Title = "System Reset",
            Content = "Reset already running",
            Duration = 2,
            Icon = "alert-circle",
        })
        return false
    end
    S._refreshBusy = true

    local ok, err = pcall(function()
        WindUI:Notify({
            Title = "System Reset",
            Content = "Stopping all features and clearing runtime...",
            Duration = 2,
            Icon = "refresh",
        })

        
        
        
        S._globalSession = (S._globalSession or 0) + 1
        S._refreshSession = (S._refreshSession or 0) + 1

        
        
        
        
        pcall(function()
            if FeatureManager and FeatureManager.order then
                for _, name in ipairs(FeatureManager.order) do
                    local feat = FeatureManager.features and FeatureManager.features[name]
                    if feat then
                        feat.enabled = false
                        feat.active = false
                        feat.session = (feat.session or 0) + 1
                        pcall(function()
                            if FeatureManager.StopRuntime then
                                FeatureManager:StopRuntime(name)
                            elseif FeatureManager.Stop then
                                FeatureManager:Stop(name)
                            end
                        end)
                        pcall(function()
                            if FeatureManager.ResetRuntime then
                                FeatureManager:ResetRuntime(name)
                            elseif FeatureManager.Reset then
                                FeatureManager:Reset(name)
                            end
                        end)
                        pcall(function()
                            if FeatureManager.DisconnectAll then
                                FeatureManager:DisconnectAll(name)
                            end
                        end)
                    end
                end
            end
        end)

        
        
        
        pcall(function()
            if Farm and Farm.setActive then Farm.setActive(false) end
        end)
        pcall(function()
            if Farm and Farm.stop then Farm.stop() end
        end)
        pcall(function()
            if Money and Money.setActive then Money.setActive(false) end
        end)
        pcall(function()
            if Money and Money.stop then Money.stop() end
        end)
        pcall(function()
            if FM.setVeinFarm then FM.setVeinFarm(false) end
        end)
        pcall(function()
            if Mountain and Mountain.setAutoGrab then Mountain.setAutoGrab(false) end
        end)
        pcall(function()
            if Mountain and Mountain.setAutoGrabCrystal then Mountain.setAutoGrabCrystal(false) end
        end)
        pcall(function()
            if FM.setVeinScanner then FM.setVeinScanner(false) end
        end)
        pcall(function()
            if FM.stopAllRuntimeFeatures then FM.stopAllRuntimeFeatures() end
        end)

        
        
        
        S.farmActive = false
        S.boulderFarmActive = false
        S.moneyFarmActive = false
        S.veinFarmActive = false
        S.fastDigActive = false
        S.espActive = false
        S.playerEspActive = false
        S.luckEspActive = false
        S.pickupValueActive = false
        S.pickupLuckActive = false
        S.pickupRarityActive = false
        S.autoPickupActive = false
        S.autoGrabCrystal = false
        S.miningBoulder = nil
        S._speedWanted = false
        S.speedActive = false
        S._noclipWanted = false
        S._flyWanted = false
        S.safeFleeing = false
        S.safeReturnPos = nil
        S._slowMove = false
        S._veinLootPhase = false
        S._veinFarmTarget = nil
        S._veinFarmStatus = nil
        S._forceTeleport = false

        
        
        
        pcall(function()
            if MovementOwner and MovementOwner.Clear then MovementOwner:Clear() end
        end)
        pcall(function()
            if Move and Move.glideStop then Move.glideStop() end
        end)
        pcall(function()
            if Move and Move.forceClearFarmMovement then Move.forceClearFarmMovement() end
        end)
        pcall(function()
            if Move and Move.setFly then Move.setFly(false) end
        end)
        pcall(function()
            if Move and Move.setNoclip then Move.setNoclip(false, false) end
        end)
        pcall(function()
            if FM.stopAllTeleport then FM.stopAllTeleport() end
        end)
        pcall(function()
            if FM.finishTeleport then FM.finishTeleport() end
        end)

        
        
        
        pcall(function()
            if FM.resetRuntimeCaches then FM.resetRuntimeCaches() end
        end)
        pcall(function()
            if FM.clearRemoteCache then FM.clearRemoteCache() end
        end)
        S._filterCandCache = nil
        S._veinDeposits = nil
        S.rootPart = nil
        S.lastFilteredPickup = 0
        S.lastPickup = 0
        S._pickupBusyUntil = 0

        pcall(function()
            if S.dirty and table.clear then table.clear(S.dirty) end
        end)
        pcall(function()
            if S.candidates and table.clear then table.clear(S.candidates) end
        end)
        pcall(function()
            if S.recentDrops and table.clear then table.clear(S.recentDrops) end
        end)

        
        
        
        
        task.wait(0.12)
        pcall(function()
            if FM.getRoot then FM.getRoot() end
        end)

        
        

        WindUI:Notify({
            Title = "System Reset",
            Content = "All features OFF — runtime cleared (GUI kept). Toggle features again to start.",
            Duration = 4,
            Icon = "check",
        })
    end)

    S._refreshBusy = false
    if not ok then
        warn("[FayyMeng] systemReset error:", err)
        WindUI:Notify({
            Title = "System Reset",
            Content = "Reset finished with errors (see console)",
            Duration = 4,
            Icon = "alert-circle",
        })
        return false
    end
    return true
end

function FM.globalRefresh()
    return FM.systemReset()
end

function FM.kickThenRejoin(delaySec)
    local ts = game:GetService("TeleportService")
    local placeId = game.PlaceId
    delaySec = tonumber(delaySec)
    if not delaySec or delaySec < 0 then
        delaySec = 5 + math.random() * 3
    end

    pcall(function()
        if typeof(getgenv) == "function" then
            getgenv().FM_KickRejoin = {
                placeId = placeId,
                wait = delaySec,
                at = os.time(),
            }
        end
    end)

    pcall(FM_queueReloadAfterTeleport)

    -- Schedule rejoin BEFORE kick so the wait can still run on most executors
    task.spawn(function()
        task.wait(delaySec)
        pcall(function()
            ts:Teleport(placeId, LocalPlayer)
        end)
        pcall(function()
            ts:Teleport(placeId)
        end)
    end)

    -- Kick self (diskonek)
    task.defer(function()
        pcall(function()
            LocalPlayer:Kick(string.format(
                "Boulder empty. Rejoining in %.0f seconds...",
                delaySec
            ))
        end)
    end)

    return true
end

function FM.rejoinPlace(delaySec)
    return FM.kickThenRejoin(delaySec)
end

function FM.hopRandomServer()
    local ts = game:GetService("TeleportService")
    local placeId = game.PlaceId
    local http = game:GetService("HttpService")
    local currentJob = tostring(game.JobId)

    pcall(FM_queueReloadAfterTeleport)

    local visited = {}
    pcall(function()
        if typeof(getgenv) == "function" then
            getgenv().FayyMeng_VisitedJobs = getgenv().FayyMeng_VisitedJobs or {}
            for id, ts0 in pairs(getgenv().FayyMeng_VisitedJobs) do
                if type(ts0) == "number" and (os.time() - ts0) < 1800 then
                    visited[tostring(id)] = true
                end
            end
        end
    end)
    visited[currentJob] = true

    local function fetchServers()
        local list = {}
        local urls = {
            string.format("https://games.roblox.com/v1/games/%d/servers/Public?sortOrder=Desc&limit=100&excludeFullGames=true", placeId),
            string.format("https://games.roblox.com/v1/games/%d/servers/Public?sortOrder=Asc&limit=100&excludeFullGames=true", placeId),
        }
        local req = (syn and syn.request) or (http_request) or (request) or (fluxus and fluxus.request)

        for _, url in ipairs(urls) do
            local body = nil
            if typeof(req) == "function" then
                local ok, res = pcall(function()
                    return req({ Url = url, Method = "GET" })
                end)
                if ok and res and res.Body then
                    body = res.Body
                end
            end
            if not body and typeof(game.HttpGet) == "function" then
                pcall(function()
                    body = game:HttpGet(url)
                end)
            end
            if body then
                local data
                pcall(function()
                    data = http:JSONDecode(body)
                end)
                if type(data) == "table" and type(data.data) == "table" then
                    for _, server in ipairs(data.data) do
                        local id = server and server.id
                        local playing = tonumber(server.playing) or 0
                        local maxP = tonumber(server.maxPlayers) or 0
                        if type(id) == "string"
                            and id ~= currentJob
                            and not visited[id]
                            and playing < maxP
                            and maxP > 0 then
                            list[#list + 1] = id
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            if #list >= 15 then
                break
            end
        end
        for i = #list, 2, -1 do
            local j = math.random(1, i)
            list[i], list[j] = list[j], list[i]
        end
        return list
    end

    local servers = fetchServers()
    if #servers == 0 then
        WindUI:Notify({
            Title = "Server Hop",
            Content = "No different server found",
            Duration = 3,
            Icon = "alert-circle",
        })
        return false
    end

    task.spawn(function()
        for i, job in ipairs(servers) do
            if tostring(game.JobId) ~= currentJob then
                return 
            end
            visited[job] = true
            pcall(function()
                if typeof(getgenv) == "function" then
                    getgenv().FayyMeng_VisitedJobs[job] = os.time()
                end
            end)
            pcall(FM_queueReloadAfterTeleport)
            local sent = pcall(function()
                ts:TeleportToPlaceInstance(placeId, job, LocalPlayer)
            end)
            if not sent then
                task.wait(0.8)
            else
                task.wait(2.5)
            end
            if tostring(game.JobId) == currentJob and i < #servers then
            else
                break
            end
        end
    end)
    return true
end

function FM.joinServerByUser(input)
    local userId = FM.resolveUserId(input)
    if not userId then
        WindUI:Notify({
            Title = "Join Server",
            Content = "Invalid username / userId",
            Duration = 3,
            Icon = "alert-circle",
        })
        return
    end

    task.spawn(function()
        local ts = game:GetService("TeleportService")
        local placeId, jobId
        local errMsg

        local ok = pcall(function()
            local a, b, c = ts:GetPlayerPlaceInstanceAsync(userId)
            if type(a) == "boolean" then
                if not a then
                    errMsg = tostring(b)
                    error(errMsg or "not in game")
                end
                placeId, jobId = b, c
            elseif type(a) == "number" or (type(a) == "string" and tonumber(a)) then
                placeId, jobId = tonumber(a) or a, b
            elseif type(a) == "string" and type(b) == "string" then
                if #a > 10 and not tonumber(a) then
                    jobId, placeId = a, tonumber(b) or game.PlaceId
                else
                    placeId, jobId = tonumber(a) or a, b
                end
            else
                placeId, jobId = a, b
            end
        end)

        if not ok or not jobId then
            local httpOk, data = pcall(function()
                local req = (syn and syn.request) or (http and http.request) or request or http_request
                if not req then
                    error("no request")
                end
                local res = req({
                    Url = "https://apis.roblox.com/presence-api/v1/presence/users",
                    Method = "POST",
                    Headers = { ["Content-Type"] = "application/json" },
                    Body = game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONEncode({ userIds = { userId } }),
                })
                return game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONDecode(res.Body)
            end)

            if httpOk and data and data.userPresences then
                for _, presence in ipairs(data.userPresences) do
                    if presence.userPresenceType == 2 and presence.placeId and presence.gameId then
                        placeId = presence.placeId
                        jobId = presence.gameId
                        break
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        placeId = tonumber(placeId) or placeId
        jobId = jobId and tostring(jobId) or nil

        if not jobId or jobId == "" then
            WindUI:Notify({
                Title = "Join Server",
                Content = errMsg or "Player offline / privacy blocked",
                Duration = 3,
                Icon = "alert-circle",
            })
            return
        end

        if placeId and tonumber(placeId) and tonumber(placeId) ~= game.PlaceId then
            WindUI:Notify({
                Title = "Join Server",
                Content = "They are in a different place (" .. tostring(placeId) .. ")",
                Duration = 3,
                Icon = "alert-circle",
            })
            return
        end

        if jobId == game.JobId then
            WindUI:Notify({
                Title = "Join Server",
                Content = "Already in the same server",
                Duration = 2,
                Icon = "check",
            })
            return
        end

        WindUI:Notify({
            Title = "Join Server",
            Content = "Joining server...",
            Duration = 3,
            Icon = "globe",
        })

        local tpOk, tpErr = pcall(function()
            ts:TeleportToPlaceInstance(game.PlaceId, jobId, LocalPlayer)
        end)
        if not tpOk then
            pcall(function()
                ts:TeleportToPlaceInstance(tonumber(placeId) or game.PlaceId, jobId, LocalPlayer)
            end)
        end
    end)
end

function FM.nearestOtherPlayer(origin)
    local best, bestDist = nil, math.huge
    for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if plr ~= LocalPlayer then
            local char = plr.Character
            local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if hrp then
                local d = (hrp.Position - origin).Magnitude
                if d < bestDist then
                    bestDist = d
                    best = hrp
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return best, bestDist
end

function FM.safeModeStep()
    if not S.safeMode then
        if S.safeFleeing then
            S.safeFleeing = false
            S.safeReturnPos = nil
        end
        return false
    end

    local farming = S.farmActive or S.miningBoulder ~= nil
    if not farming and not S.safeFleeing then
        return false
    end

    local root = FM.getRoot()
    if not root then
        return false
    end

    local threat, dist = FM.nearestOtherPlayer(root.Position)
    local SAFE_RADIUS = 200
    local FLEE_DIST = 1000

    if S.safeFleeing then
        if not threat or dist >= SAFE_RADIUS + 50 then
            local ret = S.safeReturnPos
            S.safeFleeing = false
            S.safeReturnPos = nil
            if ret then
                if Move and Move.glide then
                    pcall(Move.glide, CFrame.new(ret), ret)
                    task.delay(2.5, function()
                        if Move and Move.glideStop then
                            pcall(Move.glideStop)
                        end
                    end)
                else
                    pcall(FM.teleportTo, ret)
                end
            end
            return false
        end
        return true 
    end

    if threat and dist <= SAFE_RADIUS then
        S.safeReturnPos = root.Position
        S.safeFleeing = true
        local away = root.Position - threat.Position
        if away.Magnitude < 1 then
            away = Vector3.new(1, 0, 0)
        end
        away = Vector3.new(away.X, 0, away.Z).Unit
        local fleePos = root.Position + away * FLEE_DIST + Vector3.new(0, 40, 0)
        if Move and Move.glide then
            pcall(Move.setNoclip, true)
            pcall(Move.glide, CFrame.new(fleePos), fleePos)
        else
            pcall(FM.teleportTo, fleePos)
        end
        return true
    end

    return false
end

local Farm = {}
local farmConn

do
    local function install()
        local FARM_KINDS = { "Mossite", "Voltite", "Gildrite", "Rimeveil", "Nocturnite" }

        local SCAN_SPOTS = {
            CFrame.new(
                -12.7105675, 459.090942, 818.847412,
                0.993408799, -0.00500036497, -0.11451605,
                0.000644713698, 0.999275982, -0.0380407833,
                0.114623353, 0.0377162211, 0.992692769
            ),
            CFrame.new(
                13.0506754, 318.450409, -488.078888,
                -0.99998939, 0.000884758658, -0.00452193478,
                -0.000498382491, 0.954864502, 0.297041386,
                0.00458064489, 0.297040492, -0.954853892
            ),
            CFrame.new(
                74.3923645, 610.789368, 210.838226,
                -0.94896102, -0.27110818, 0.161162555,
                2.26557495e-06, 0.51098305, 0.859590769,
                -0.315393418, 0.815718472, -0.484902382
            ),
        }

        local TeleportService = game:GetService("TeleportService")
        local PLACE_ID = game.PlaceId

        local HOLD_DIST = 6
        local HOLD_SLACK = 0.75
        local HOLD_AIM = 0.999
        local SCAN_HOLD = 1.4
        local SWING_GAP = 0.04
        local SWING_BURST = 6
        local SWING_FLOOR = 0.02
        local COOLDOWN_KEYS = { "SwingCooldown", "DigCooldown", "Cooldown", "SwingSpeed", "DigSpeed" }
        local AIM_ANGLES = { 0, 35, -35, 70, -70, 110, -110, 145, -145, 180 }
        local AIM_LIFT = { 0, 5, -4, 12 }
        local SIGHT_GRACE = 1.5
        local SIGHT_STEPS = 8
        local LOST_GRACE = 2.5
        local DRY_ROUNDS = 4
        local DRY_TIME = 1.1
        local PROBE_DIST = { 0, -3, 4, -5, 8 }
        local SWEEP_PARTS = 3
        local RUNE_SWEEP = 90
        local LOOT_TIME = 3.5
        local EQUIP_STEP = 1
        local RESET_WAIT = 2

        local PICK_NAMES = {
            ["Rusty Scrapper"] = true,
            ["Weathered Wood"] = true,
            ["Chipped Stone"] = true,
            ["Hardened Iron"] = true,
            ["Copper Pick"] = true,
            ["Reinforced Steel"] = true,
            ["Titanium Spike"] = true,
            ["Frostbite Pick"] = true,
            ["Emerald Carver"] = true,
            ["Volcano Basalt"] = true,
            ["Obsidian Edge"] = true,
            ["Tempest Pick"] = true,
            ["Celestial Apex"] = true,
            ["Astral Rend"] = true,
            ["Eclipse Fang"] = true,
            ["Nebular Throne"] = true,
            Voidreign = true,
            Singularity = true,
            ["The Terminus"] = true,
            ["Admin Pickaxe"] = true,
            ["Shark Pickaxe"] = true,
            ["Diamond Pickaxe"] = true,
        }

        local digRemote
        local active = false
        local targets = {}
        local phase = "idle"
        local target, anchor
        local swingClock = 0
        local equipClock = 0
        local waitUntil = 0
        local lastSpot
        local hpMark
        local dryRounds = 0
        local scanned = false
        local scanIndex = 0
        local heldPick
        local spotFrame
        local aimTurn = 0
        local blindClock = 0
        local lostClock = 0
        local dryClock = 0
        local probeIndex = 0
        local partCursor = 0
        local pendingFinish = false
        local lootUntil = 0
        local statusText = "Idle"
        local noTargetClock
        local emptyRejoinDelay = 0
        local filterPickClock = 0

        local function toggleValue(name)
            return false
        end

        local function digEvent()
            if digRemote and digRemote.Parent then
                return digRemote
            end

            digRemote = FM.findRemote("DigRequest")
            return digRemote
        end

        local function isPickaxe(tool)
            if not tool or not tool:IsA("Tool") then
                return false
            end

            if FM.getAttr(tool, "IsPickaxe") == true then
                return true
            end

            if PICK_NAMES[tool.Name] then
                return true
            end

            return FM.getAttr(tool, "DigPower") ~= nil and FM.getAttr(tool, "Tier") == nil
        end

        local function pickScore(tool)
            if not isPickaxe(tool) then
                return 0
            end

            local score = 1
            local power = tonumber(FM.getAttr(tool, "DigPower"))
            if power then
                score += power
            end

            return score
        end

        local function equipPick()
            local character = LocalPlayer.Character
            local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if not character or not humanoid then
                return nil
            end

            local held = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
            local choice
            local best = 0

            local function consider(tool)
                local score = pickScore(tool)
                if score > best then
                    best = score
                    choice = tool
                end
            end

            consider(held)

            local backpack = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
            if backpack then
                for _, tool in ipairs(backpack:GetChildren()) do
                    consider(tool)
                end
            end

            if not choice then
                return nil
            end

            if choice == held then
                return held
            end

            pcall(function()
                humanoid:EquipTool(choice)
            end)

            local now = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
            if now and isPickaxe(now) then
                return now
            end

            return nil
        end

        local rayParams = RaycastParams.new()
        rayParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
        rayParams.IgnoreWater = true
        rayParams.RespectCanCollide = false

        local JUNK_WORDS = { "vfx", "effect", "fx", "debris", "particle", "shard", "chunk", "dust", "smoke" }

        local function junkName(instance)
            local name = string.lower(instance.Name)

            for _, word in ipairs(JUNK_WORDS) do
                if string.find(name, word, 1, true) then
                    return true
                end
            end

            return false
        end

        local function ignorable(instance, model)
            if not instance then
                return true
            end

            if model and instance:IsDescendantOf(model) then
                return true
            end

            if instance:IsA("Terrain") then
                return true
            end

            if not instance:IsA("BasePart") then
                return true
            end

            if instance.Transparency >= 0.5 or not instance.CanCollide or not instance.CanQuery then
                return true
            end

            if not instance.Anchored or instance.Massless then
                return true
            end

            if junkName(instance) then
                return true
            end

            local owner = instance:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
            if owner and Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(owner) then
                return true
            end

            return false
        end

        local function sightClear(origin, part, model)
            if not part or not part.Parent then
                return true
            end

            local delta = part.Position - origin
            local distance = delta.Magnitude
            if distance < 1 then
                return true
            end

            local skip = { LocalPlayer.Character, Workspace.Terrain }

            for _ = 1, SIGHT_STEPS do
                rayParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = skip

                local hit = Workspace:Raycast(origin, delta.Unit * (distance + 2), rayParams)
                if not hit then
                    return true
                end

                local instance = hit.Instance

                if instance == part or (model and instance:IsDescendantOf(model)) then
                    return true
                end

                if not ignorable(instance, model) then
                    return false
                end

                skip[#skip + 1] = instance
            end

            return true
        end

        local function usablePart(item)
            if not item:IsA("BasePart") then
                return false
            end

            if not item.Anchored or item.Transparency >= 0.9 or item.Massless then
                return false
            end

            return not junkName(item)
        end

        local function partList(model)
            local list = {}

            if model:IsA("BasePart") then
                list[1] = model
                return list
            end

            local spare = {}

            for _, item in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
                if item:IsA("BasePart") then
                    if usablePart(item) then
                        list[#list + 1] = item
                    else
                        spare[#spare + 1] = item
                    end
                end
            end

            if #list > 0 then
                return list
            end

            return spare
        end

        local function anchorSpot(model, part)
            if part and part.Parent then
                return part.Position
            end

            if not model or not model.Parent then
                return nil
            end

            local ok, pivot = pcall(model.GetPivot, model)
            if ok and pivot then
                return pivot.Position
            end

            return nil
        end

        local function coreSpot(model)
            if not model or not model.Parent then
                return nil
            end

            if model:IsA("BasePart") then
                return model.Position
            end

            local boxed, box = pcall(model.GetBoundingBox, model)
            if boxed and box then
                return box.Position
            end

            local ok, pivot = pcall(model.GetPivot, model)
            if ok and pivot then
                return pivot.Position
            end

            return nil
        end

        local function coreFrame(core, part)
            local look = part and part.Parent and part.Position or nil

            if not look or (look - core).Magnitude < 1 then
                look = core + Vector3.new(0, -2, 0)
            end

            return CFrame.new(core, look)
        end

        local function visibleAnchor(model)
            local root = FM.getRoot()
            local origin = root and root.Position
            local pick, pickDistance, fallback, fallbackDistance

            for _, part in ipairs(partList(model)) do
                local distance = origin and (part.Position - origin).Magnitude or 0

                if not fallback or distance < fallbackDistance then
                    fallback = part
                    fallbackDistance = distance
                end

                if origin and sightClear(origin, part, model) then
                    if not pick or distance < pickDistance then
                        pick = part
                        pickDistance = distance
                    end
                end
            end

            return pick or fallback
        end

        local function freshAnchor(model)
            local list = partList(model)
            local count = #list

            if count == 0 then
                return nil
            end

            for _ = 1, count do
                partCursor = partCursor % count + 1

                local item = list[partCursor]
                if item and item.Parent then
                    return item
                end
            end

            return nil
        end

        local function hitSpot(part, model)
            local root = FM.getRoot()
            if not root then
                return part.Position
            end

            local origin = root.Position
            local delta = part.Position - origin
            local distance = delta.Magnitude
            if distance < 1 then
                return part.Position
            end

            local skip = { LocalPlayer.Character, Workspace.Terrain }

            for _ = 1, SIGHT_STEPS do
                rayParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = skip

                local hit = Workspace:Raycast(origin, delta.Unit * (distance + 2), rayParams)
                if not hit then
                    break
                end

                if hit.Instance == part or hit.Instance:IsDescendantOf(model) then
                    return hit.Position
                end

                skip[#skip + 1] = hit.Instance
            end

            return part.Position
        end

        local function swingGap(tool)
            local pick = tool or heldPick
            if pick then
                for _, key in ipairs(COOLDOWN_KEYS) do
                    local value = tonumber(FM.getAttr(pick, key))
                    if value and value > 0 then
                        return math.max(value, SWING_FLOOR)
                    end
                end
            end

            return SWING_GAP
        end

        local digRayParams = RaycastParams.new()
        digRayParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Include
        digRayParams.IgnoreWater = true

        local function refreshDigFilter()
            local filter = { Workspace.Terrain }
            local decorations = Workspace:FindFirstChild("MountainDecorations")
            local boulders = decorations and decorations:FindFirstChild("Boulders")
            if boulders then
                filter[#filter + 1] = boulders
            end
            local test = Workspace:FindFirstChild("BoulderTest")
            if test then
                filter[#filter + 1] = test
            end
            digRayParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = filter
        end

        local function resolveDigHit(preferPos)
            local root = FM.getRoot()
            if not root then
                return nil
            end
            refreshDigFilter()
            local maxReach = 14
            if typeof(preferPos) == "Vector3" then
                local origin = root.Position + Vector3.new(0, 1.5, 0)
                local delta = preferPos - origin
                local dist = delta.Magnitude
                if dist > 0.05 then
                    local dir = delta.Unit
                    local hit = Workspace:Raycast(origin, dir * math.min(dist + 2, maxReach), digRayParams)
                    if hit then
                        return hit.Position
                    end
                    
                    local down = Workspace:Raycast(preferPos + Vector3.new(0, 8, 0), Vector3.new(0, -40, 0), digRayParams)
                    if down then
                        return down.Position
                    end
                    if dist <= maxReach then
                        return preferPos
                    end
                    return origin + dir * (maxReach - 0.5)
                end
            end
            local cam = Workspace.CurrentCamera
            if cam then
                local look = cam.CFrame.LookVector
                local hit = Workspace:Raycast(cam.CFrame.Position, look * maxReach, digRayParams)
                if hit then
                    return hit.Position
                end
                hit = Workspace:Raycast(root.Position, look * maxReach, digRayParams)
                if hit then
                    return hit.Position
                end
            end
            local down = Workspace:Raycast(root.Position + Vector3.new(0, 2, 0), Vector3.new(0, -12, 0), digRayParams)
            if down then
                return down.Position
            end
            return root.Position + root.CFrame.LookVector * 6
        end

        local function swing(part, model, center)
            local event = digEvent()
            if not event or not heldPick then
                return false
            end

            local name = heldPick.Name
            local core = center or (part and part.Parent and part.Position)
            local prefer = core
            if part and part.Parent then
                prefer = hitSpot(part, model) or core
            end
            prefer = prefer or core

            local hitPos = resolveDigHit(prefer)
            if typeof(hitPos) ~= "Vector3" then
                return false
            end
            if type(name) ~= "string" or name == "" then
                return false
            end

            local ok = pcall(function()
                event:FireServer(name, hitPos)
            end)
            return ok
        end

        local function hold(goal, aim)
            if typeof(goal) ~= "CFrame" then
                return false
            end
            if S.farmMode == "Teleport" then
                local root = FM.getRoot()
                if root then
                    pcall(function()
                        root.CFrame = goal
                    end)
                end
                return true
            end
            return Move.glide(goal, aim)
        end

        local function restart()
            task.spawn(function()
                pcall(function()
                    LocalPlayer:Kick("FayyMeng: restarting server")
                end)

                for _ = 1, 20 do
                    task.wait(1.5)

                    local sent = pcall(function()
                        TeleportService:Teleport(PLACE_ID, LocalPlayer)
                    end)

                    if not sent then
                        pcall(function()
                            TeleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(PLACE_ID, game.JobId, LocalPlayer)
                        end)
                    end
                end
            end)
        end

        local function boulderRoots()
            local roots = {}

            local decorations = Workspace:FindFirstChild("MountainDecorations")
            local folder = decorations and decorations:FindFirstChild("Boulders")
            if folder then
                roots[#roots + 1] = folder
            end

            local test = Workspace:FindFirstChild("BoulderTest")
            if test then
                roots[#roots + 1] = test
            end

            return roots
        end

        local function boulderKind(inst)
            for _, kind in ipairs(FARM_KINDS) do
                if inst.Name:find(kind, 1, true) then
                    return kind
                end
            end
            return nil
        end

        local function anchorOf(model)
            if model:IsA("BasePart") then
                return model
            end

            local list = partList(model)
            if list[1] then
                return list[1]
            end

            local ok, part = pcall(model.FindFirstChildWhichIsA, model, "BasePart", true)
            if ok and part then
                return part
            end

            return nil
        end

        local function boulderHealth(model)
            local hp = FM.getAttr(model, "Health")
            if hp == nil then
                hp = FM.getAttr(model, "Hp")
            end
            if hp == nil then
                hp = FM.getAttr(model, "CurrentHealth")
            end
            return tonumber(hp)
        end

        -- Cached boulder list (periodic refresh, large effective radius via streaming)
        local _bCache = {}
        local _bCacheAt = 0
        local _bStreamAt = 0
        local B_CACHE_TTL = 1.25
        local B_STREAM_GAP = 2.5
        local B_MAX_DIST = 2500

        local function refreshBoulderCache(force)
            local now = os.clock()
            if not force and (now - _bCacheAt) < B_CACHE_TTL and #_bCache > 0 then
                return
            end
            _bCacheAt = now
            table.clear(_bCache)
            for _, container in ipairs(boulderRoots()) do
                if container and container.Parent then
                    for _, child in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
                        local kind = boulderKind(child)
                        if kind and targets[kind] then
                            local part = anchorOf(child)
                            if part and part.Parent then
                                _bCache[#_bCache + 1] = { model = child, kind = kind, part = part }
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        local function pickTarget()
            local root = FM.getRoot()
            if not root then
                return nil
            end

            local now = os.clock()
            if (now - _bStreamAt) >= B_STREAM_GAP then
                _bStreamAt = now
                pcall(function()
                    LocalPlayer:RequestStreamAroundAsync(root.Position, 10)
                end)
            end

            refreshBoulderCache(false)

            local best, bestAnchor, bestScore
            local origin = root.Position
            local alive = {}
            for i = 1, #_bCache do
                local e = _bCache[i]
                local model, part = e.model, e.part
                if model and model.Parent and part and part.Parent then
                    alive[#alive + 1] = e
                    local distance = (part.Position - origin).Magnitude
                    if distance <= B_MAX_DIST then
                        local hp = boulderHealth(model) or 0
                        local score = hp * 2 + distance
                        if not best or score < bestScore then
                            best = model
                            bestAnchor = part
                            bestScore = score
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            _bCache = alive

            return best, bestAnchor
        end

        local function approach(part, model, turn, spot, pad)
            local root = FM.getRoot()
            if not root then
                return nil
            end

            local center = spot or (part and part.Parent and part.Position)
            if not center then
                return nil
            end

            local away = root.Position - center
            away = Vector3.new(away.X, 0, away.Z)

            if away.Magnitude < 0.5 then
                away = Vector3.new(0, 0, 1)
            end

            away = away.Unit

            local span = part and part.Parent and part.Size.Magnitude * 0.5 or 6
            local reach = math.max(span + HOLD_DIST + (pad or 0), span + 3)
            local skip = turn or 0

            for _, lift in ipairs(AIM_LIFT) do
                for _, angle in ipairs(AIM_ANGLES) do
                    local dir = (CFrame.fromAxisAngle(Vector3.yAxis, math.rad(angle)) * away).Unit
                    local candidate = center + dir * reach + Vector3.new(0, lift, 0)

                    if sightClear(candidate, part, model) then
                        if skip <= 0 then
                            return CFrame.new(candidate, center)
                        end

                        skip -= 1
                    end
                end
            end

            return CFrame.new(center + away * reach + Vector3.new(0, AIM_LIFT[2], 0), center)
        end

        local function beginLoot(finish)
            pendingFinish = finish == true
            lootUntil = os.clock() + LOOT_TIME
            phase = "loot"
            statusText = "Looting runes"
        end

        local function stop()
            active = false
            phase = "idle"
            target = nil
            anchor = nil
            waitUntil = 0
            Move.glideStop()
            hpMark = nil
            dryRounds = 0
            pendingFinish = false
            lootUntil = 0
            spotFrame = nil
            aimTurn = 0
            blindClock = 0
            lostClock = 0
            dryClock = 0
            probeIndex = 0
            statusText = "Idle"
            S.miningBoulder = nil
            S.boulderFarmActive = false
            pcall(FM.syncFarmActiveFlag)
            
            pcall(function()
                local feat = FeatureManager:Get("AutoBoulder")
                if feat then feat.active = false end
            end)
            pcall(function()
                if MovementOwner and MovementOwner.Release then
                    -- ROOT-CAUSE FIX: setActive(true)/step() acquire ownership
                    -- under BOTH "Boulder" (direct MovementOwner:Acquire calls)
                    -- AND "AutoBoulder" (via FM.ownedSetNoclip/step() every tick).
                    -- Only releasing "Boulder" here left "AutoBoulder" stuck in
                    -- MovementOwner.owners forever, which made HasAny() always
                    -- return true and permanently blocked noclip from ever being
                    -- disabled again (character stays in noclip after OFF).
                    MovementOwner:Release("Boulder")
                    MovementOwner:Release("AutoBoulder")
                end
            end)
            
            if farmConn then
                pcall(function() farmConn:Disconnect() end)
                farmConn = nil
            end

            pcall(function()
                if not MovementOwner or not (
                    (type(MovementOwner.HasAny) == "function" and MovementOwner:HasAny())
                    or MovementOwner.owner
                ) then
                    Move.forceClearFarmMovement()
                    Move.setNoclip(false, false)
                end
            end)
            pcall(function()
                if Move and Move.glideStop then Move.glideStop() end
            end)
            if S._noclipWanted then
                pcall(function()
                    Move.setNoclip(true, false)
                end)
            end
            -- Do not kill RunePickup path when stopping Boulder engine
            pcall(function()
                if FM.RunePickupSystem and FM.RunePickupSystem.Active then
                    return
                end
                if Mountain and Mountain.setAutoGrab then
                    Mountain.setAutoGrab(false)
                end
            end)
            pcall(function()
                local hum = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if hum then
                    hum:Move(Vector3.zero, false)
                end
            end)
        end

        local function step(deltaTime)
            local root = FM.getRoot()
            if not root then
                statusText = "Waiting for character"
                return
            end

            local now = os.clock()

            if FM.safeModeStep() then
                statusText = "Safe Mode: avoiding player"
                return
            end

            -- ROOT-CAUSE FIX: filtered pickup must NEVER abort AutoBoulder farm step.
            -- Previously: tryFilteredCrystalPickup success → return → silent dead mining
            -- while toggle stayed ON (no console error). Pickup runs as side-effect only.
            if S.pickupValueActive or S.pickupLuckActive or S.pickupRarityActive then
                filterPickClock = (filterPickClock or 0) + deltaTime
                local filterTick = ((S.pickupMode or "Legit") == "Burst") and 0.15 or 0.35
                if filterPickClock >= filterTick then
                    filterPickClock = 0
                    local free = FM.backpackFree()
                    if free > 0 then
                        pcall(function()
                            FM.tryFilteredCrystalPickup(root.Position, free)
                        end)
                    end
                end
            end

            if phase == "scan" then
                if not scanned then
                    local spot = FM.mountainSpot() or root.Position
                    FM.requestStream(spot)
                    pcall(function()
                        LocalPlayer:RequestStreamAroundAsync(spot, 3)
                    end)
                    scanned = true
                    noTargetClock = 0
                    statusText = "Scanning targets"
                end

                local model = pickTarget()
                if not model then
                    noTargetClock = (noTargetClock or 0) + deltaTime
                    local spot = FM.mountainSpot() or root.Position
                    -- short confirm empty (~1.2s) then leave path; join waits 5–8s
                    if noTargetClock < 1.2 then
                        if math.floor(noTargetClock * 2) % 2 == 0 then
                            FM.requestStream(spot)
                        end
                        statusText = string.format("Checking boulders %.1fs", noTargetClock)
                        return
                    end
                    if S.autoRejoinOnEmpty then
                        local joinDelay = 5 + math.random() * 3
                        statusText = "Boulder habis — stop → kick → rejoin"
                        stop()
                        pcall(function()
                            if Move and Move.forceClearFarmMovement then
                                Move.forceClearFarmMovement()
                            end
                        end)
                        pcall(function()
                            if Move and Move.setFly then Move.setFly(false) end
                            if Move and Move.setNoclip then Move.setNoclip(false, false) end
                        end)
                        pcall(function()
                            if WindUI and WindUI.Notify then
                                WindUI:Notify({
                                    Title = "Boulder",
                                    Content = string.format(
                                        "Kick → tunggu %.0fs → join kembali",
                                        joinDelay
                                    ),
                                    Duration = 4,
                                    Icon = "check",
                                })
                            end
                        end)
                        pcall(function()
                            if FM.kickThenRejoin then
                                FM.kickThenRejoin(joinDelay)
                            elseif FM.rejoinPlace then
                                FM.rejoinPlace(joinDelay)
                            end
                        end)
                        return
                    end

                    if S.autoHopOnEmpty then
                        statusText = "Boulder habis — hopping server..."
                        stop()
                        pcall(function()
                            if Move and Move.forceClearFarmMovement then
                                Move.forceClearFarmMovement()
                            end
                        end)
                        pcall(function()
                            if WindUI and WindUI.Notify then
                                WindUI:Notify({
                                    Title = "Boulder",
                                    Content = "No boulders — server hop",
                                    Duration = 3,
                                    Icon = "check",
                                })
                            end
                        end)
                        task.spawn(function()
                            task.wait(0.35)
                            pcall(function()
                                if FM.hopRandomServer then
                                    FM.hopRandomServer()
                                end
                            end)
                        end)
                        return
                    end
                    beginLoot(true)
                    statusText = "Final rune sweep"
                    return
                end

                noTargetClock = 0
                target = model
                anchor = visibleAnchor(model)
                spotFrame = nil
                aimTurn = 0
                blindClock = 0
                lostClock = 0
                dryClock = 0
                probeIndex = 0
                hpMark = nil
                dryRounds = 0
                phase = "mine"
                return
            end

            if phase == "mine" then
                if not target or not target.Parent then
                    S.miningBoulder = nil
                    lastSpot = root.CFrame
                    beginLoot(false)
                    return
                end

                S.miningBoulder = target

                local kind = boulderKind(target) or "Boulder"
                local hp = boulderHealth(target)

                if hp and hp <= 0 then
                    lastSpot = root.CFrame
                    beginLoot(false)
                    return
                end

                if not anchor or not anchor.Parent or not anchor:IsDescendantOf(target) then
                    anchor = visibleAnchor(target) or anchorOf(target)
                    spotFrame = nil
                end

                local core = coreSpot(target)
                local center = core or anchorSpot(target, anchor)

                if not center then
                    lostClock += deltaTime

                    if lostClock >= LOST_GRACE then
                        lastSpot = root.CFrame
                        beginLoot(false)
                        return
                    end

                    statusText = string.format("Holding %s", kind)
                    return
                end

                lostClock = 0

                if not spotFrame then
                    if core and probeIndex == 0 then
                        spotFrame = coreFrame(core, anchor)
                    else
                        spotFrame = approach(anchor, target, aimTurn, center, PROBE_DIST[probeIndex % #PROBE_DIST + 1])
                    end
                end

                if spotFrame then
                    FM.ownedSetNoclip("AutoBoulder", true, false)
                    hold(spotFrame, center)
                    statusText = string.format("Mining %s", kind)
                end

                if heldPick == nil or heldPick.Parent ~= LocalPlayer.Character then
                    equipClock = 0
                    heldPick = equipPick()
                else
                    equipClock += deltaTime
                    if equipClock >= EQUIP_STEP then
                        equipClock = 0
                        heldPick = equipPick() or heldPick
                    end
                end

                if not heldPick then
                    statusText = "No pickaxe"
                    return
                end

                swingClock += deltaTime

                local gap = swingGap()
                local swung = 0

                if swingClock >= gap then
                    swung = math.min(math.floor(swingClock / gap), 4)
                    swingClock -= swung * gap

                    for _ = 1, swung do
                        swing(anchor, target, center)
                    end
                end

                if hp then
                    if hpMark == nil or hp < hpMark - 0.001 then
                        hpMark = hp
                        dryRounds = 0
                        dryClock = 0
                        blindClock = 0
                    else
                        dryClock += deltaTime
                        dryRounds += swung

                        if dryClock >= DRY_TIME or dryRounds >= DRY_ROUNDS then
                            dryClock = 0
                            dryRounds = 0
                            probeIndex += 1
                            aimTurn = (aimTurn + 1) % #AIM_ANGLES
                            anchor = freshAnchor(target) or visibleAnchor(target) or anchor
                            spotFrame = nil
                        end
                    end

                    statusText = string.format("Mining %s  %.0f hp", kind, hp)
                    return
                end

                if sightClear(root.Position, anchor, target) then
                    blindClock = 0
                else
                    blindClock += deltaTime

                    if blindClock >= SIGHT_GRACE then
                        blindClock = 0
                        aimTurn = (aimTurn + 1) % #AIM_ANGLES
                        anchor = visibleAnchor(target) or anchor
                        spotFrame = nil
                    end
                end

                statusText = string.format("Mining %s", kind)

                return
            end

            if phase == "loot" then
                if lastSpot then
                    hold(lastSpot)
                end

                Mountain.grabNear(RUNE_SWEEP)

                if now < lootUntil then
                    statusText = string.format("Looting runes  %.1fs", lootUntil - now)
                    return
                end

                if pendingFinish then
                    phase = "reset"
                    waitUntil = now + RESET_WAIT
                    statusText = "Runes collected"
                else
                    target = nil
                    anchor = nil
                    phase = "scan"
                    statusText = "Next boulder"
                end

                return
            end

            if phase == "reset" then
                if now < waitUntil then
                    return
                end

                statusText = "Restarting server"
                WindUI:Notify({
                    Title = "FayyMeng",
                    Content = "No boulders left - kicking and rejoining",
                    Duration = 3,
                    Icon = "alert-circle",
                })
                stop()
                restart()
            end
        end

        local PICKAXE_REQ = {
            Mossite = "Titanium Spike",
            Voltite = "Celestial Apex",
            Gildrite = "Eclipse Fang",
            Rimeveil = "Voidreign",
            Nocturnite = "The Terminus",
        }

        local function setTargets(value)
            table.clear(targets)

            if type(value) == "table" then
                for key, flag in pairs(value) do
                    if type(key) == "string" and (flag == true or flag == "true") then
                        targets[key] = true
                    elseif type(flag) == "string" and flag ~= "" then
                        targets[flag] = true
                    elseif type(key) == "number" and type(flag) == "string" then
                        targets[flag] = true
                    end
                end
            elseif type(value) == "string" and value ~= "" then
                targets[value] = true
            end
        end

        local function ensureFarmHeartbeat()
            
            if farmConn then
                pcall(function() farmConn:Disconnect() end)
                farmConn = nil
            end
            farmConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(deltaTime)
                if not active then
                    return
                end
                local ok, err = pcall(step, deltaTime)
                if not ok then
                    FM.reportError("boulderFarm", err)
                end
            end)
        end

        local function setActive(value)
            if type(value) == "table" then
                value = value.CurrentValue or value[1] or value.Value
            end
            value = value == true

            if not value then
                -- Engine stop only — do NOT write FeatureManager.enabled (owned by BoulderSystem UI)
                pcall(function()
                    local feat = FeatureManager:Get("AutoBoulder")
                    if feat then feat.active = false end
                end)
                stop()
                return
            end

            if not next(targets) then
                for _, kind in ipairs(FARM_KINDS) do
                    targets[kind] = true
                end
            end

            
            pcall(function()
                local feat = FeatureManager:Get("AutoBoulder")
                if feat then feat.active = true end
            end)
            digRemote = nil
            heldPick = nil
            ensureFarmHeartbeat()
            active = true
            S.boulderFarmActive = true
            pcall(FM.syncFarmActiveFlag)
            pcall(function()
                if MovementOwner and MovementOwner.Acquire then
                    MovementOwner:Acquire("Boulder")
                end
            end)
            phase = "scan"
            noTargetClock = 0
            waitUntil = 0
            target = nil
            anchor = nil
            lastSpot = nil
            swingClock = 0
            equipClock = 0
            hpMark = nil
            dryRounds = 0
            spotFrame = nil
            aimTurn = 0
            blindClock = 0
            lostClock = 0
            dryClock = 0
            probeIndex = 0
            scanned = false
            scanIndex = 0
            heldPick = nil
            pendingFinish = false
            lootUntil = 0
            statusText = "Starting"

            pcall(function() Move.glideStop() end)
            pcall(function() FM.ownedClearFarmMovement("AutoBoulder") end)
            pcall(function() Move.setFly(false) end)
            pcall(function() FM.ownedSetNoclip("AutoBoulder", true, false) end)
            pcall(function() Mountain.setAutoGrab(true) end)
        end

        local FarmBox = Tabs.boulder

        FarmBox:Section({ Title = "Auto Farm", TextSize = 17 })


        FarmBox:Dropdown({
            Title = "Targets",
            Desc = "Boulder type filter.",
            Values = FARM_KINDS,
            Multi = true,
            AllowNone = true,
            Callback = setTargets
        })

        local StatusLabel = FarmBox:Paragraph({
            Title = "Status",
            Desc = "Idle",
        })

        local labelClock = 0

        
        if farmConn then
            pcall(function() farmConn:Disconnect() end)
            farmConn = nil
        end
        local statusConn
        statusConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(deltaTime)
            labelClock += deltaTime
            if labelClock >= 0.6 then
                labelClock = 0
                if StatusLabel then
                    StatusLabel.Desc = statusText
                end
            end
        end)
        
        farmConn = nil

        Farm.stop = stop
        Farm.setActive = setActive
        FM.Farm = Farm
        Farm.isActive = function()
            return active == true
        end
        Farm.equipPick = equipPick
        Farm.swingGap = swingGap
        Farm.digEvent = digEvent

        FeatureManager:Register("AutoBoulder", {
            enabled = false,
            active = false,
            Initialize = function()
                digRemote = nil
                heldPick = nil
            end,
            StartRuntime = function(_, session)
                
                if not next(targets) then
                    for _, kind in ipairs(FARM_KINDS) do
                        targets[kind] = true
                    end
                end
                digRemote = nil
                heldPick = nil
                ensureFarmHeartbeat()
                active = true
                S.boulderFarmActive = true
                pcall(FM.syncFarmActiveFlag)
                phase = "scan"
                statusText = "Starting"
                pcall(function()
                    if MovementOwner and MovementOwner.Acquire then
                        MovementOwner:Acquire("Boulder")
                    end
                end)
                pcall(function() FM.ownedSetNoclip("AutoBoulder", true, false) end)
                pcall(function() Mountain.setAutoGrab(true) end)
            end,
            StopRuntime = function()
                
                active = false
                S.boulderFarmActive = false
                S.miningBoulder = nil
                pcall(FM.syncFarmActiveFlag)
                pcall(function()
                    if MovementOwner and MovementOwner.Release then
                        MovementOwner:Release("Boulder")
                        MovementOwner:Release("AutoBoulder")
                    end
                end)
                pcall(function() Move.glideStop() end)
                pcall(function()
                    if not MovementOwner or not (
                    (type(MovementOwner.HasAny) == "function" and MovementOwner:HasAny())
                    or MovementOwner.owner
                ) then
                        Move.forceClearFarmMovement()
                        Move.setNoclip(false, false)
                    end
                end)
                pcall(function() Mountain.setAutoGrab(false) end)
            end,
            Cleanup = function()
                digRemote = nil
                heldPick = nil
                target = nil
                phase = "idle"
            end,
            ResetRuntime = function()
                digRemote = nil
                heldPick = nil
                target = nil
                phase = "idle"
                pendingFinish = false
                lootUntil = 0
                statusText = "Idle"
                active = false
            end,
        })
    end

    install()
end

local Money = {}
local moneyConn

do
    local function install()
        local SCAN_SPOTS = {
            CFrame.new(
                -12.7105675,
                459.090942,
                818.847412,
                0.993408799,
                -0.00500036497,
                -0.11451605,
                0.000644713698,
                0.999275982,
                -0.0380407833,
                0.114623353,
                0.0377162211,
                0.992692769
            ),
            CFrame.new(
                13.0506754,
                318.450409,
                -488.078888,
                -0.99998939,
                0.000884758658,
                -0.00452193478,
                -0.000498382491,
                0.954864502,
                0.297041386,
                0.00458064489,
                0.297040492,
                -0.954853892
            ),
            CFrame.new(
                74.3923645,
                610.789368,
                210.838226,
                -0.94896102,
                -0.27110818,
                0.161162555,
                2.26557495e-06,
                0.51098305,
                0.859590769,
                -0.315393418,
                0.815718472,
                -0.484902382
            ),
        }

        local SCAN_HOLD = 1.4
        local PEAK_GAP = 10
        local PEAK_STEP = 48
        local PEAK_RINGS = 12
        local COLUMN_STEP = 8
        local RING_MAX = 6
        local RAY_TOP = 120
        local RAY_DROP = 60
        local ZONE_PAD = 12
        local SURFACE_GAP = 0.15
        local COLUMN_DRY = 40
        local DIG_BURST = 7
        local DIG_SINK = 1.2
        local DIG_LIFT = 6
        local DIG_SLACK = 1.5
        local DIG_AIM = 0.999
        local EQUIP_STEP = 1
        local SELL_MARK = 0.5
        local SELL_WAIT = 2.2
        local DIG_REACH = 12
        local DIG_REFRESH = 5
        local COLLECT_RANGE = 32000
        local COLLECT_LIFT = 5
        local COLLECT_GAP = 0.15
        local GRAB_GAP = 0.05

        local OFFSETS = { Vector2.new(0, 0) }
        local PEAK_OFFSETS = { Vector2.new(0, 0) }

        for ring = 1, RING_MAX do
            local slices = ring * 6

            for slice = 0, slices - 1 do
                local angle = slice / slices * math.pi * 2
                local reach = ring * COLUMN_STEP
                OFFSETS[#OFFSETS + 1] = Vector2.new(math.cos(angle) * reach, math.sin(angle) * reach)
            end
        end

        for ring = 1, PEAK_RINGS do
            local slices = ring * 3

            for slice = 0, slices - 1 do
                local angle = slice / slices * math.pi * 2
                local reach = ring * PEAK_STEP
                PEAK_OFFSETS[#PEAK_OFFSETS + 1] =
                    Vector2.new(math.cos(angle) * reach, math.sin(angle) * reach)
            end
        end

        local surfaceParams = RaycastParams.new()
        surfaceParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Include
        surfaceParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = { Workspace.Terrain }
        surfaceParams.IgnoreWater = true

        local digParams = RaycastParams.new()
        digParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Include
        digParams.IgnoreWater = true

        local digClock = 0

        local function digFilter(now)
            if digClock > 0 and now - digClock < DIG_REFRESH then
                return
            end

            digClock = now

            local list = { Workspace.Terrain }
            local decorations = Workspace:FindFirstChild("MountainDecorations")
            local boulders = decorations and decorations:FindFirstChild("Boulders")

            if boulders then
                list[#list + 1] = boulders
            end

            local test = Workspace:FindFirstChild("BoulderTest")

            if test then
                list[#list + 1] = test
            end

            digParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = list
        end

        local function pickReach(tool)
            local override = tool and tonumber(FM.getAttr(tool, "OverrideMaxReach"))
            return (override or DIG_REACH) + 3
        end

        local function aimPoint(origin, spot, reach, now)
            digFilter(now)

            local goals = {
                spot,
                spot - Vector3.new(0, 2, 0),
                origin - Vector3.new(0, reach, 0),
            }

            for _, goal in ipairs(goals) do
                local delta = goal - origin
                local distance = delta.Magnitude

                if distance > 0.05 then
                    local span = math.min(distance + 4, reach)
                    local hit = Workspace:Raycast(origin, delta.Unit * span, digParams)

                    if hit then
                        return hit.Position
                    end
                end
            end

            local delta = spot - origin

            if delta.Magnitude <= reach then
                return spot
            end

            return origin + delta.Unit * reach
        end

        local active = false
        local autoSell = false
        local focusLuck = false
        local minLuckPoints = 10
        local autoPlantLuck = false
        local plantClock = 0
        local heldPick
        local loot
        local lootClock = 0
        local grabClock = 0
        local lootHp
        local lootMax
        local target
        local columnY
        local columnDry = 0
        local columnSwings = 0
        local surfaceClock = 0
        local peakClock = 0
        local scanIndex = 0
        local scanUntil = 0
        local loaded = false
        local swingClock = 0
        local equipClock = 0
        local sellSpot
        local sellUntil = 0
        local lootBlocked = false
        local statusText = "Idle"

        local function toggleValue(name)
            return false
        end

        local function zoneBase()
            local base = Workspace:GetAttribute("MountainBaseY")
            if typeof(base) == "number" then
                return base
            end
            return 0
        end

        local function zonePeak()
            local peak = Workspace:GetAttribute("MountainPeakY")
            if typeof(peak) == "number" then
                return peak
            end
            return zoneBase() + 900
        end

        local function zoneCenter()
            local spot = FM.mountainSpot()
            if spot then
                return Vector2.new(spot.X, spot.Z)
            end
            return nil
        end

        local function insideZone(x, z)
            local center = zoneCenter()
            if not center then
                return false
            end

            return (Vector2.new(x, z) - center).Magnitude <= FM.mountainSpan() + ZONE_PAD
        end

        local function surfaceAt(x, z)
            if not insideZone(x, z) then
                return nil
            end

            local base = zoneBase()
            local top = zonePeak() + RAY_TOP
            local hit = Workspace:Raycast(
                Vector3.new(x, top, z),
                Vector3.new(0, -(top - base + RAY_DROP), 0),
                surfaceParams
            )

            if not hit or hit.Position.Y <= base + 1 then
                return nil
            end

            return hit.Position
        end

        local function farmOrigin(root)
            if insideZone(root.Position.X, root.Position.Z) then
                return root.Position
            end

            return FM.mountainSpot() or root.Position
        end

        local function highestColumn(origin, offsets)
            local best

            for _, offset in ipairs(offsets) do
                local spot = surfaceAt(origin.X + offset.X, origin.Z + offset.Y)

                if spot and (not best or spot.Y > best.Y) then
                    best = spot
                end
            end

            return best
        end

        local function pickTarget(origin, now)
            local center = FM.mountainSpot()

            if center and now - peakClock >= PEAK_GAP then
                peakClock = now

                local high = highestColumn(center, PEAK_OFFSETS)
                if high then
                    return high
                end
            end

            local spot = highestColumn(origin, OFFSETS)
            if spot then
                return spot
            end

            if center then
                peakClock = now
                return highestColumn(center, PEAK_OFFSETS)
            end

            return nil
        end

        local function holdAt(goal, aim)
            if typeof(goal) ~= "CFrame" then
                return false
            end
            if S.farmMode == "Teleport" then
                local root = FM.getRoot()
                if root then
                    pcall(function()
                        root.CFrame = goal
                    end)
                end
                return true
            end
            return Move.glide(goal, aim)
        end

        local function swing(spot)
            local event = Farm.digEvent()
            if not event or not heldPick then
                return false
            end

            local name = heldPick.Name
            if type(name) ~= "string" or name == "" then
                return false
            end
            local root = FM.getRoot()
            local aim = spot

            if root and typeof(spot) == "Vector3" then
                aim = aimPoint(root.Position, spot, pickReach(heldPick), os.clock()) or spot
            end
            if typeof(aim) ~= "Vector3" then
                return false
            end

            return pcall(function()
                for step = 0, DIG_BURST - 1 do
                    local pos = aim - Vector3.new(0, step * DIG_SINK, 0)
                    event:FireServer(name, pos)
                end
            end)
        end

        local function bagRatio()
            local capacity = FM.backpackCapacity()
            if capacity == math.huge or capacity <= 0 then
                return 0
            end

            return FM.backpackWeight() / capacity
        end

        local function findLoot(free, origin, luckFilterFn)
            local best, bestValue, bestDistance, bestLuck
            local blocked = false
            local seen = {}

            local function consider(inst)
                if not inst or seen[inst] then
                    return
                end
                seen[inst] = true

                if not inst.Parent or not FM.isCrystal(inst) or FM.getAttr(inst, "Collected") == true then
                    return
                end

                local value = FM.crystalValue(inst)
                if not FM.meetsFilter(inst, value) then
                    return
                end

                if luckFilterFn and not luckFilterFn(inst) then
                    return
                end

                local distance = (inst.Position - origin).Magnitude
                if distance > COLLECT_RANGE then
                    return
                end

                if FM.crystalWeight(inst) > free then
                    blocked = true
                    return
                end

                local luckPts = math.floor((FM.crystalLuck(inst) or 0) * 100 + 0.5)
                local better = false
                if focusLuck then
                    better = not best or luckPts > (bestLuck or 0) or (luckPts == (bestLuck or 0) and value > (bestValue or 0))
                else
                    better = not best or value > bestValue
                    if not better and value == bestValue and distance < bestDistance then
                        better = true
                    end
                end

                if better then
