                    best = inst
                    bestValue = value
                    bestDistance = distance
                    bestLuck = luckPts
                end
            end

            FM.eachContainer(function(container)
                for _, child in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
                    if child:IsA("BasePart") then
                        consider(child)
                    elseif child:IsA("Model") then
                        for _, inner in ipairs(child:GetChildren()) do
                            if inner:IsA("BasePart") then
                                consider(inner)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)

            for inst in pairs(S.registry) do
                consider(inst)
            end

            return best, blocked
        end

        local function stop()
            active = false
            target = nil
            columnY = nil
            loaded = false
            Move.glideStop()
            scanIndex = 0
            loot = nil
            lootHp = nil
            lootMax = nil
            lootBlocked = false
            sellSpot = nil
            sellUntil = 0
            heldPick = nil
            statusText = "Idle"
            S.moneyFarmActive = false
            pcall(FM.syncFarmActiveFlag)
            pcall(function()
                local feat = FeatureManager:Get("AutoMoney")
                if feat then feat.active = false end
            end)
            pcall(function()
                if MovementOwner and MovementOwner.Release then
                    MovementOwner:Release("Money")
                end
            end)
            if moneyConn then
                pcall(function() moneyConn:Disconnect() end)
                moneyConn = nil
            end

            pcall(function()
                if not MovementOwner or not (
                    (type(MovementOwner.HasAny) == "function" and MovementOwner:HasAny())
                    or MovementOwner.owner
                ) then
                    Move.forceClearFarmMovement()
                end
            end)
            pcall(function()
                if Move and Move.glideStop then Move.glideStop() end
            end)
            -- ROOT-CAUSE FIX: this used to call Move.setNoclip(false, false)
            -- unconditionally, which would silently turn off noclip even while
            -- AutoBoulder (or AutoVein) was still actively farming and relying
            -- on it, causing it to get stuck against terrain with no error.
            pcall(function()
                FM.ownedSetNoclip("AutoMoney", false, false)
            end)
            if S._noclipWanted then
                pcall(function()
                    Move.setNoclip(true, false)
                end)
            end
            Mountain.setAutoGrab(false)
        end

        local function step(deltaTime)
            local root = FM.getRoot()
            if not root then
                statusText = "Waiting for character"
                return
            end

            local now = os.clock()

            if FM.safeModeStep() then
                statusText = "Safe Mode: avoiding player"
                return
            end

            if not loaded then
                local spot = FM.mountainSpot()
                if spot then
                    FM.requestStream(spot)
                    pcall(function()
                        LocalPlayer:RequestStreamAroundAsync(spot, 2)
                    end)
                end
                loaded = true
                peakClock = 0
                statusText = "Terrain loaded (no character scan)"
            end

            if autoPlantLuck and now - plantClock >= 3.5 then
                plantClock = now
                local tools = {}
                local function scanTools(container)
                    if not container then return end
                    for _, child in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
                        if FM.isCrystalTool(child) then
                            local luck = 0
                            pcall(function()
                                luck = FM.crystalLuck(child) or 0
                            end)
                            if luck * 100 >= (minLuckPoints or 5) then
                                tools[#tools + 1] = child
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
                scanTools(LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack"))
                scanTools(LocalPlayer.Character)
                local _, region = FM.getOwnedPlotRegion()
                if region and #tools > 0 then
                    FM.goPlotUnderground(region)
                    if FM.placeCrystalTool(tools[1], region) then
                        statusText = "Planted luck at plot"
                    end
                end
            end

            if sellUntil > 0 then
                if now < sellUntil then
                    statusText = "Selling"
                    if S.autoPickupActive then
                        FM.pickupStep()
                    end
                    return
                end

                sellUntil = 0

                if sellSpot then
                    FM.applyPivot(sellSpot)
                    sellSpot = nil
                end

                if FM.unlockMovement then
                    FM.unlockMovement()
                end

                target = nil
                columnY = nil
                surfaceClock = 0
            end

            if autoSell and (bagRatio() >= SELL_MARK or lootBlocked) then
                sellSpot = root.CFrame

                if FM.doSell() then
                    lootBlocked = false
                    sellUntil = now + SELL_WAIT
                    statusText = "Selling"
                    return
                end
            end

            -- ROOT-CAUSE FIX: rate-limit pickup during AutoMoney so it cannot starve
            -- pickaxe equip / dig for entire frames. Pickup never blocks dig path.
            do
                local nowP = os.clock()
                if (nowP - (S._moneyFarmPickupAt or 0)) >= 0.28 then
                    S._moneyFarmPickupAt = nowP
                    if S.autoPickupActive then
                        pcall(function() FM.pickupStep() end)
                    elseif S.pickupValueActive or S.pickupLuckActive or S.pickupRarityActive then
                        pcall(function() FM.pickupStep(FM.valueLuckFilter) end)
                    end
                end
            end

            if heldPick == nil or heldPick.Parent ~= LocalPlayer.Character then
                equipClock = 0
                heldPick = Farm.equipPick()
            else
                equipClock += deltaTime
                if equipClock >= EQUIP_STEP then
                    equipClock = 0
                    heldPick = Farm.equipPick() or heldPick
                end
            end

            if not heldPick then
                -- Re-try equip next frame; do not treat as permanent failure
                statusText = "No pickaxe"
                pcall(function()
                    heldPick = Farm.equipPick()
                end)
                return
            end

            swingClock += deltaTime

            local swingNeed = math.max(0.02, Farm.swingGap(heldPick) * 0.4)
            local canSwing = swingClock >= swingNeed
            local free = FM.backpackFree()

            if loot and (not loot.Parent or FM.getAttr(loot, "Collected") == true) then
                loot = nil
                lootHp = nil
                lootMax = nil
            end

            if loot then
                local hp = tonumber(FM.getAttr(loot, "MinedHP"))

                if hp and (lootMax == nil or hp > lootMax) then
                    lootMax = hp
                end

                lootHp = hp
            end

            if not loot and now - lootClock >= COLLECT_GAP then
                lootClock = now
                local luckFn = nil
                if focusLuck then
                    luckFn = function(inst)
                        local luckPts = math.floor((FM.crystalLuck(inst) or 0) * 100 + 0.5)
                        return luckPts >= minLuckPoints
                    end
                end
                loot, lootBlocked = findLoot(free, root.Position, luckFn)

                if loot then
                    lootHp = tonumber(FM.getAttr(loot, "MinedHP"))
                    lootMax = lootHp
                end
            end

            if loot then
                local spot = loot.Position

                holdAt(CFrame.new(spot + Vector3.new(0, COLLECT_LIFT, 0), spot), spot)

                local digSpot = spot

                if not lootHp or lootHp <= 0 then
                    local ground = surfaceAt(spot.X, spot.Z)

                    if ground then
                        digSpot = ground
                    end
                end

                FM.requestStream(spot)

                if canSwing then
                    swingClock -= swingNeed
                    swing(digSpot)
                end

                if now - grabClock >= GRAB_GAP then
                    grabClock = now
                    FM.grabCrystal(loot, FM.crystalPrompt(loot))
                end

                if lootHp and lootHp > 0 then
                    local ratio = 0

                    if lootMax and lootMax > 0 then
                        ratio = math.clamp(1 - lootHp / lootMax, 0, 1)
                    end

                    statusText = string.format("Breaking crystal %d%%", math.floor(ratio * 100))
                else
                    statusText = "Collecting crystals"
                end

                return
            end

            local origin = farmOrigin(root)

            if target and now - surfaceClock >= SURFACE_GAP then
                surfaceClock = now

                local spot = surfaceAt(target.X, target.Z)

                if not spot then
                    target = nil
                    columnY = nil
                    columnDry = 0
                    columnSwings = 0
                else
                    if not columnY or spot.Y < columnY - 0.05 then
                        columnDry = 0
                    else
                        columnDry += columnSwings
                    end

                    columnSwings = 0
                    columnY = spot.Y
                    target = spot

                    if columnDry >= COLUMN_DRY then
                        target = nil
                        columnY = nil
                        columnDry = 0
                    end
                end
            end

            if not target then
                local spot = pickTarget(origin, now)

                if not spot then
                    spot = surfaceAt(origin.X, origin.Z)
                end

                if not spot then
                    FM.requestStream(origin)

                    if canSwing then
                        swingClock -= swingNeed
                        swing(root.Position - Vector3.new(0, DIG_REACH * 0.5, 0))
                    end

                    statusText = "Loading terrain"
                    return
                end

                target = spot
                columnY = spot.Y
                columnDry = 0
                columnSwings = 0
                surfaceClock = now
            end

            holdAt(CFrame.new(target + Vector3.new(0, DIG_LIFT, 0), target), target)

            if canSwing then
                swingClock -= swingNeed
                columnSwings += 1
                swing(target)
            end

            statusText = string.format("Mining surface at %dm", math.floor(target.Y))
        end

        local function ensureMoneyHeartbeat()
            if moneyConn then
                pcall(function() moneyConn:Disconnect() end)
                moneyConn = nil
            end
            moneyConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(deltaTime)
                if not active then
                    return
                end
                local ok, err = pcall(step, deltaTime)
                if not ok then
                    FM.reportError("moneyFarm", err)
                end
            end)
        end

        local function setActive(value)
            if type(value) == "table" then
                value = value.CurrentValue or value[1] or value.Value
            end
            value = value == true

            if not value then
                -- Engine stop only — FeatureManager.enabled owned by MoneySystem UI
                pcall(function()
                    local feat = FeatureManager:Get("AutoMoney")
                    if feat then feat.active = false end
                end)
                stop()
                return
            end

            pcall(function()
                local feat = FeatureManager:Get("AutoMoney")
                if feat then feat.active = true end
            end)
            digRemote = nil
            heldPick = nil
            ensureMoneyHeartbeat()
            active = true
            S.moneyFarmActive = true
            pcall(FM.syncFarmActiveFlag)
            pcall(function()
                if MovementOwner and MovementOwner.Acquire then
                    MovementOwner:Acquire("Money")
                end
            end)
            target = nil
            columnY = nil
            columnDry = 0
            columnSwings = 0
            surfaceClock = 0
            peakClock = 0
            scanIndex = 0
            scanUntil = 0
            loaded = false
            loot = nil
            lootClock = 0
            lootHp = nil
            lootMax = nil
            lootBlocked = false
            swingClock = 0
            equipClock = 0
            sellSpot = nil
            sellUntil = 0
            heldPick = nil
            statusText = "Starting"

            pcall(function() Move.glideStop() end)
            pcall(function() FM.ownedClearFarmMovement("AutoMoney") end)
            pcall(function() Move.setFly(false) end)
            pcall(function() FM.ownedSetNoclip("AutoMoney", true, false) end)
            pcall(function() Mountain.setAutoGrab(true) end)
        end

        local MoneyBox = Tabs.money


        MoneyBox:Toggle({
            Title = "Auto Sell At 50%",
            Desc = "Sell when backpack is about half full",
            Value = false,
            Callback = function(value)
                autoSell = value
            end
        })

        local StatusLabel = MoneyBox:Paragraph({
            Title = "Status",
        })

        local labelClock = 0

        if moneyConn then
            pcall(function() moneyConn:Disconnect() end)
            moneyConn = nil
        end
        local moneyStatusConn
        moneyStatusConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(deltaTime)
            labelClock += deltaTime
            if labelClock >= 0.6 then
                labelClock = 0
                if StatusLabel then
                    StatusLabel.Desc = statusText
                end
            end
        end)
        moneyConn = nil

        Money.stop = stop
        Money.setActive = setActive
        FM.Money = Money
        Money.isActive = function()
            return active == true
        end

        FeatureManager:Register("AutoMoney", {
            enabled = false,
            active = false,
            Initialize = function()
                heldPick = nil
            end,
            StartRuntime = function()
                digRemote = nil
                heldPick = nil
                ensureMoneyHeartbeat()
                active = true
                S.moneyFarmActive = true
                pcall(FM.syncFarmActiveFlag)
                statusText = "Starting"
                pcall(function()
                    if MovementOwner and MovementOwner.Acquire then
                        MovementOwner:Acquire("Money")
                    end
                end)
            end,
            StopRuntime = function()
                active = false
                S.moneyFarmActive = false
                pcall(FM.syncFarmActiveFlag)
                pcall(function()
                    if MovementOwner and MovementOwner.Release then
                        MovementOwner:Release("Money")
                    end
                end)
                pcall(function() Move.glideStop() end)
                pcall(function()
                    if not MovementOwner or not (
                    (type(MovementOwner.HasAny) == "function" and MovementOwner:HasAny())
                    or MovementOwner.owner
                ) then
                        Move.forceClearFarmMovement()
                    end
                end)
            end,
            Cleanup = function()
                target = nil
                heldPick = nil
            end,
            ResetRuntime = function()
                target = nil
                heldPick = nil
                loot = nil
                statusText = "Idle"
                active = false
            end,
        })
    end

    install()
end

local fastDigConn

do
    local swingClock = 0
    local digRemote = nil

    local digParams = RaycastParams.new()
    digParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Include
    digParams.IgnoreWater = true

    local function refreshDigFilter()
        local list = { Workspace.Terrain }
        local decorations = Workspace:FindFirstChild("MountainDecorations")
        local boulders = decorations and decorations:FindFirstChild("Boulders")
        if boulders then
            list[#list + 1] = boulders
        end
        local test = Workspace:FindFirstChild("BoulderTest")
        if test then
            list[#list + 1] = test
        end
        digParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = list
    end

    local function getDigRemote()
        if digRemote and digRemote.Parent then
            return digRemote
        end
        if Farm and Farm.digEvent then
            digRemote = Farm.digEvent()
        end
        if not digRemote then
            digRemote = FM.findRemote("DigRequest")
        end
        return digRemote
    end

    local function isDigTool(tool)
        if not tool or not tool:IsA("Tool") then
            return false
        end
        local n = tostring(tool.Name or "")
        if tool:GetAttribute("DigPower") ~= nil then
            return true
        end
        if n:find("Pick", 1, true) or n:find("Axe", 1, true) or n == "Sledge Hammer" then
            return true
        end
        if tool:GetAttribute("BitSpinAxis") ~= nil or n:find("Drill", 1, true) then
            return true
        end
        return false
    end

    local function getHeldPick()
        
        local char = LocalPlayer.Character
        if not char then
            return nil
        end
        for _, tool in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
            if isDigTool(tool) then
                return tool
            end
        end
        return nil
    end

    local function findDigTarget(tool)
        local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
        local root = FM.getRoot()
        if not root then
            return nil
        end
        refreshDigFilter()
        local maxReach = 28
        if tool then
            local override = tonumber(FM.getAttr(tool, "OverrideMaxReach"))
            maxReach = math.max((override or 10) + 8, 20)
        end
        local look = (camera and camera.CFrame.LookVector) or root.CFrame.LookVector
        local flat = Vector3.new(look.X, 0, look.Z)
        if flat.Magnitude < 0.001 then
            flat = root.CFrame.RightVector
        end
        flat = flat.Unit

        local function tryRay(origin, dir)
            local hit = Workspace:Raycast(origin, dir, digParams)
            if hit then
                return hit.Position
            end
            return nil
        end

        if camera then
            local hit = tryRay(camera.CFrame.Position, look * maxReach)
            if hit then return hit end
        end
        local hit = tryRay(root.Position + Vector3.new(0, 1.5, 0), look * maxReach)
        if hit then return hit end
        hit = tryRay(root.Position + Vector3.new(0, 1.5, 0), flat * maxReach)
        if hit then return hit end
        hit = tryRay(root.Position + Vector3.new(0, 2, 0), Vector3.new(0, -1, 0) * maxReach)
        if hit then return hit end
        hit = tryRay(root.Position + flat * 3 + Vector3.new(0, 2, 0), (look + Vector3.new(0, -0.35, 0)).Unit * maxReach)
        if hit then return hit end
        local hit = Workspace:Raycast(root.Position + Vector3.new(0, 1.5, 0), look * maxReach, digParams)
        if hit then
            return hit.Position
        end
        hit = Workspace:Raycast(root.Position + flat * 2, look * maxReach, digParams)
        if hit then
            return hit.Position
        end
        
        hit = Workspace:Raycast(root.Position, Vector3.new(0, -maxReach * 1.5, 0), digParams)
        if hit then
            return hit.Position
        end
        hit = Workspace:Raycast(root.Position + flat * 3, Vector3.new(0, -maxReach * 1.5, 0), digParams)
        if hit then
            return hit.Position
        end
        
        local hybrid = (look * 0.55 + Vector3.new(0, -0.85, 0))
        if hybrid.Magnitude > 0.05 then
            hybrid = hybrid.Unit
            hit = Workspace:Raycast(root.Position + Vector3.new(0, 1, 0), hybrid * maxReach, digParams)
            if hit then
                return hit.Position
            end
        end
        return root.Position + Vector3.new(0, -4, 0) + flat * 4
    end

    local function fireDig(toolName, pos)
        if type(toolName) ~= "string" or toolName == "" then
            return false
        end
        if typeof(pos) ~= "Vector3" then
            return false
        end
        local event = getDigRemote()
        if not event or not event.Parent then
            digRemote = nil
            event = getDigRemote()
        end
        if not event then
            digRemote = FM.findRemote("DigRequest")
            event = digRemote
        end
        if not event then
            return false
        end
        local ok = pcall(function()
            event:FireServer(toolName, pos)
        end)
        if not ok then
            digRemote = nil
            event = FM.findRemote("DigRequest")
            if event then
                digRemote = event
                ok = pcall(function()
                    event:FireServer(toolName, pos)
                end)
            end
        end
        return ok == true
    end

    function FM.fireDig(toolName, pos)
        return fireDig(toolName, pos)
    end

    local function fastDigStep(deltaTime)
        if not S.fastDigActive then
            return
        end

        if not S.farmActive and not S.miningBoulder then
            if FM.isPickupBusy and FM.isPickupBusy() then
                swingClock = 0
                return
            end
        end

        local pick = getHeldPick()
        if not pick or type(pick.Name) ~= "string" or pick.Name == "" then
            -- no force-equip: wait until player holds a dig tool
            swingClock = 0
            return
        end

        local root = FM.getRoot()
        if not root then
            return
        end

        -- Isolated AutoDig path: respect base/zone (must not dig at base / outside mountain)
        if FM.isAtBase and FM.isAtBase() then
            swingClock = 0
            return
        end
        do
            local cx = Workspace:GetAttribute("MountainCenterX")
            local cz = Workspace:GetAttribute("MountainCenterZ")
            local radius = Workspace:GetAttribute("MountainRadius")
            if typeof(cx) == "number" and typeof(cz) == "number" and typeof(radius) == "number" then
                if FM.isInMountainZone and not FM.isInMountainZone(root.Position) then
                    swingClock = 0
                    return
                end
            end
        end

        local mult = tonumber(S.digSpeedMult) or 0.4
        mult = math.clamp(mult, 0.08, 1)
        local baseGap = 0.04
        if Farm and Farm.swingGap then
            local okGap, gap = pcall(Farm.swingGap, pick)
            if okGap and type(gap) == "number" then
                baseGap = gap
            end
        end
        local swingNeed = math.max(0.03, baseGap * mult)

        swingClock = swingClock + (deltaTime or 0.016)
        if swingClock < swingNeed then
            return
        end
        swingClock = 0

        local aim = findDigTarget(pick)
        if typeof(aim) ~= "Vector3" then
            return
        end
        if FM.isInNoDigZone and FM.isInNoDigZone(aim) then
            return
        end

        
        local look = Workspace.CurrentCamera and Workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame.LookVector or root.CFrame.LookVector
        local right = Workspace.CurrentCamera and Workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame.RightVector or root.CFrame.RightVector
        fireDig(pick.Name, aim)
        fireDig(pick.Name, aim - Vector3.new(0, 1.2, 0))
        fireDig(pick.Name, aim + look * 1.5)
        fireDig(pick.Name, aim - look * 1.2)
        fireDig(pick.Name, aim + right * 1.4)
        fireDig(pick.Name, aim - right * 1.4)
        fireDig(pick.Name, aim + Vector3.new(0, 1.5, 0))
        fireDig(pick.Name, aim - Vector3.new(0, 2.4, 0))

        
        refreshDigFilter()
        local origin = root.Position + Vector3.new(0, 1.2, 0)
        local maxReach = 12
        local dirs = {
            look,
            -look,
            right,
            -right,
            Vector3.new(0, -1, 0),
            Vector3.new(0, 1, 0),
            (look + right).Unit,
            (look - right).Unit,
            (-look + right).Unit,
            (-look - right).Unit,
            (look + Vector3.new(0, 0.6, 0)).Unit,
            (look + Vector3.new(0, -0.6, 0)).Unit,
        }
        for i = 1, #dirs do
            local d = dirs[i]
            if typeof(d) == "Vector3" and d.Magnitude > 0.01 then
                local hit = Workspace:Raycast(origin, d.Unit * maxReach, digParams)
                if hit then
                    fireDig(pick.Name, hit.Position)
                else
                    fireDig(pick.Name, origin + d.Unit * math.min(6, maxReach))
                end
            end
        end
    end

    local function setFastDig(value)
        if type(value) == "table" then
            value = value.CurrentValue or value[1] or value.Value
        end
        -- Isolated AutoDig: never force-equip tools; only dig if player already holds a dig tool
        if value == true then
            S.fastDigActive = true
            S.digSpeedMult = tonumber(S.digSpeedMult) or 0.28
            swingClock = 0
            digRemote = nil
        else
            S.fastDigActive = false
            swingClock = 0
            digRemote = nil
        end
    end

    local function install()
        local DigBox = Tabs.dig
        DigBox:Toggle({
            Title = "Auto Dig",
            Desc = "Automatic dig.",
            Value = false,
            Callback = setFastDig,
        })
    end

    install()

    fastDigConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(deltaTime)
        local ok, err = pcall(fastDigStep, deltaTime)
        if not ok then
            FM.reportError("fastDig", err)
        end
    end)
end

do
    function FM.setAntiRagdoll(value)
    S.antiRagdoll = value == true

    if S._antiRagdollConn then
        S._antiRagdollConn:Disconnect()
        S._antiRagdollConn = nil
    end
    if S._antiRagdollAttrConn then
        S._antiRagdollAttrConn:Disconnect()
        S._antiRagdollAttrConn = nil
    end
    if S._antiRagdollCharConn then
        S._antiRagdollCharConn:Disconnect()
        S._antiRagdollCharConn = nil
    end
    if S._antiRagdollStateConn then
        S._antiRagdollStateConn:Disconnect()
        S._antiRagdollStateConn = nil
    end
    if S._antiRagdollHealthConn then
        S._antiRagdollHealthConn:Disconnect()
        S._antiRagdollHealthConn = nil
    end

    if not S.antiRagdoll then
        return
    end

    S._lastSafeHealth = nil

    local function getDeactivateRemote()
        local pkg = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("RagdollSystemPackage")
        local sys = pkg and pkg:FindFirstChild("RagdollSystem")
        local remotes = sys and sys:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
        return remotes and remotes:FindFirstChild("DeactivateRagdollRemote")
    end

    local function zeroDamageAndUnragdoll()
        local character = LocalPlayer.Character
        if not character then
            return
        end

        local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if humanoid then
            local safe = S._lastSafeHealth or humanoid.MaxHealth
            if humanoid.Health < safe then
                pcall(function()
                    humanoid.Health = safe
                end)
            end
            if humanoid.Health > humanoid.MaxHealth then
                pcall(function()
                    humanoid.Health = humanoid.MaxHealth
                end)
            end
        end

        local remote = getDeactivateRemote()
        if remote then
            pcall(function()
                remote:FireServer()
            end)
        end

        local alt = FM.findRemote and FM.findRemote("RagdollRequest")
        if alt then
            pcall(function()
                alt:FireServer("disable")
            end)
            pcall(function()
                alt:FireServer("deactivate")
            end)
        end

        pcall(function()
            character:SetAttribute("Ragdolled", false)
        end)

        local root = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if humanoid then
            pcall(function()
                humanoid.PlatformStand = false
                if humanoid.WalkSpeed <= 0 then
                    if S._speedWanted or S.speedActive then
                        humanoid.WalkSpeed = (PACE and PACE.boost) or 40
                    else
                        humanoid.WalkSpeed = FM.getBaseSpeed()
                    end
                end
                local state = humanoid:GetState()
                if state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics
                    or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown
                    or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll then
                    humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
                end
            end)
        end

        if root then
            pcall(function()
                root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
            end)
        end

        for _, inst in ipairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
            if inst:IsA("AnimationConstraint") then
                pcall(function()
                    inst.Enabled = true
                    inst.IsKinematic = true
                end)
            end
        end

        pcall(function()
            FM.restoreUpright(character)
        end)
        if humanoid then
            pcall(function()
                if S._speedWanted or S.speedActive then
                    S.speedActive = true
                    FM.watchSpeed(humanoid)
                    FM.enforceSpeed(humanoid)
                else
                    humanoid.WalkSpeed = FM.getBaseSpeed()
                end
            end)
        end
    end

    local function hookCharacter(character)
        if S._antiRagdollAttrConn then
            S._antiRagdollAttrConn:Disconnect()
            S._antiRagdollAttrConn = nil
        end
        if S._antiRagdollHealthConn then
            S._antiRagdollHealthConn:Disconnect()
            S._antiRagdollHealthConn = nil
        end
        if not character then
            return
        end

        local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if humanoid then
            S._lastSafeHealth = humanoid.Health
            S._antiRagdollHealthConn = humanoid.HealthChanged:Connect(function(newHealth)
                if not S.antiRagdoll then return end
                local isRag = character:GetAttribute("Ragdolled") == true
                    or humanoid:GetState() == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics
                    or humanoid:GetState() == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll
                    or humanoid.PlatformStand
                if isRag then
                    local safe = S._lastSafeHealth or humanoid.MaxHealth
                    if newHealth < safe then
                        pcall(function()
                            humanoid.Health = safe
                        end)
                    end
                    zeroDamageAndUnragdoll()
                else
                    if newHealth > 0 then
                        S._lastSafeHealth = newHealth
                    end
                end
            end)
        end

        S._antiRagdollAttrConn = character:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Ragdolled"):Connect(function()
            if S.antiRagdoll and character:GetAttribute("Ragdolled") then
                zeroDamageAndUnragdoll()
            end
        end)

        if humanoid then
            if S._antiRagdollStateConn then
                S._antiRagdollStateConn:Disconnect()
            end
            S._antiRagdollStateConn = humanoid.StateChanged:Connect(function(_, newState)
                if not S.antiRagdoll then
                    return
                end
                if newState == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics
                    or newState == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll
                    or newState == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown then
                    zeroDamageAndUnragdoll()
                end
            end)
        end

        if character:GetAttribute("Ragdolled") then
            zeroDamageAndUnragdoll()
        end
    end

    hookCharacter(LocalPlayer.Character)
    S._antiRagdollCharConn = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(hookCharacter)

    local clock = 0
    S._antiRagdollConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
        if not S.antiRagdoll then
            return
        end
        clock += dt
        if clock < 0.12 then
            return
        end
        clock = 0

        local character = LocalPlayer.Character
        if not character then
            return
        end

        local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local ragdolled = character:GetAttribute("Ragdolled") == true
        local physics = humanoid and (
            humanoid:GetState() == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics
            or humanoid:GetState() == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll
            or humanoid.PlatformStand == true
        )

        if ragdolled or physics then
            zeroDamageAndUnragdoll()
        elseif humanoid and humanoid.Health > 0 then
            S._lastSafeHealth = humanoid.Health
        end
    end)
end

local function install()
        local PlotBox = Tabs.runes

        PlotBox:Button({
            Title = "PLACE ALL SELECTED RUNE",
            Desc = "Place runes on plot.",
            Callback = function()
                task.spawn(function()
                    if FM.placeAllSelectedRunes then
                        FM.placeAllSelectedRunes()
                    else
                        WindUI:Notify({ Title = "Runes", Content = "Use rune tools from backpack on plot", Duration = 3 })
                    end
                end)
            end
        })

        local RUNE_LIST = {
            "Luck Rune", "Haste Rune", "Storm Rune", "Weight Rune",
            "Fortune Rune", "Detonation Rune", "Preservation Rune", "Warmth Rune",
            "Excavator Rune", "Colossus Rune",
        }

        local selectedRunes = {}
        local placeAmount = 1
        local dropAmount = 1
        local selectedDropRunes = {}

        local function getOwnedPlotModel()
            local things = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Things")
            local plots = things and things:FindFirstChild("Plots")
            local slots = plots and plots:FindFirstChild("Slots")
            if not slots then
                return nil
            end

            local byName = slots:FindFirstChild(LocalPlayer.Name)
            if byName then
                return byName
            end

            for _, slot in ipairs(slots:GetChildren()) do
                if slot:IsA("Model") then
                    local owner = slot:GetAttribute("Owner") or slot:GetAttribute("OwnerName")
                    if owner == LocalPlayer.Name or owner == LocalPlayer.UserId then
                        return slot
                    end
                end
            end

            for _, child in ipairs(slots:GetChildren()) do
                local sign = child:FindFirstChild("Sign")
                if sign then
                    local text = ""
                    if sign:IsA("BasePart") then
                        local surface = sign:FindFirstChildOfClass("SurfaceGui") or sign:FindFirstChildOfClass("BillboardGui")
                        if surface then
                            local label = surface:FindFirstChildOfClass("TextLabel")
                            if label then text = tostring(label.Text or "") end
                        end
                    elseif sign:IsA("Model") then
                        local label = sign:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("TextLabel", true)
                        if label then text = tostring(label.Text or "") end
                    end
                    if text ~= "" and (text:find(LocalPlayer.Name, 1, true) or text:find(LocalPlayer.DisplayName, 1, true)) then
                        return child
                    end
                end
            end

            return nil
        end

        local function collectRuneTools(selectedOnly, maxPerType)
            local list = {}
            local counts = {}
            local limit = tonumber(maxPerType) or math.huge

            local function scan(container)
                if not container then
                    return
                end
                for _, child in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
                    if child:IsA("Tool") and child.Name:find("Rune", 1, true) then
                        if not selectedOnly or selectedRunes[child.Name] then
                            local n = counts[child.Name] or 0
                            if n < limit then
                                counts[child.Name] = n + 1
                                list[#list + 1] = child
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            scan(LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack"))
            scan(LocalPlayer.Character)
            return list
        end

        local function findRuneToolByName(runeName)
            local function scan(container)
                if not container then
                    return nil
                end
                for _, child in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
                    if child:IsA("Tool") and child.Name == runeName then
                        return child
                    end
                end
                return nil
            end
            return scan(LocalPlayer.Character) or scan(LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack"))
        end

        local function parseAmount(value)
            if type(value) == "number" then
                return math.max(1, math.floor(value))
            end
            if type(value) == "string" then
                local n = tonumber(value:match("[%d%.]+"))
                if n and n > 0 then
                    return math.floor(n)
                end
            end
            if type(value) == "table" then
                local v = value.Text or value.Value or value[1] or value.Input
                return parseAmount(v)
            end
            return math.max(1, math.floor(tonumber(placeAmount) or 1))
        end

        local function placeSelectedRunes()
            if next(selectedRunes) == nil then
                WindUI:Notify({
                    Title = "Place Runes",
                    Content = "Select at least 1 rune first",
                    Duration = 3,
                    Icon = "alert-circle",
                })
                return
            end

            local amount = parseAmount(placeAmount)
            placeAmount = amount

            local plot = getOwnedPlotModel()
            if not plot then
                WindUI:Notify({
                    Title = "Plot",
                    Content = "Own plot not found",
                    Duration = 3,
                    Icon = "alert-circle",
                })
                return
            end

            local region = plot:FindFirstChild("Region")
            if not (region and region:IsA("BasePart")) then
                WindUI:Notify({
                    Title = "Plot",
                    Content = "Plot Region missing",
                    Duration = 2,
                    Icon = "alert-circle",
                })
                return
            end

            local remote = FM.findRemote("PlotPlaceRequest")
            if not remote then
                WindUI:Notify({
                    Title = "Plot",
                    Content = "PlotPlaceRequest missing",
                    Duration = 2,
                    Icon = "alert-circle",
                })
                return
            end

            local names = {}
            for name in pairs(selectedRunes) do
                names[#names + 1] = name
            end
            table.sort(names)

            local cf = region.CFrame
            local size = region.Size
            local half = size * 0.5
            local padX = math.max(half.X - 1.5, 1)
            local padZ = math.max(half.Z - 1.5, 1)

            local function placementWorldPos(objectSize)
                local oh = (objectSize or Vector3.new(2, 2, 2)) * 0.5
                for _ = 1, 16 do
                    local lx = (math.random() - 0.5) * 2 * padX
                    local lz = (math.random() - 0.5) * 2 * padZ
                    local ly = oh.Y + 0.05
                    local bottomLocal = Vector3.new(lx, ly - half.Y, lz)
                    return cf:PointToWorldSpace(bottomLocal)
                end
                local bottomLocal = Vector3.new(0, oh.Y + 0.05 - half.Y, 0)
                return cf:PointToWorldSpace(bottomLocal)
            end

            local function toolSize(tool)
                local handle = tool and tool:FindFirstChild("Handle")
                if handle and handle:IsA("BasePart") then
                    return handle.Size
                end
                local fx = tonumber(tool and tool:GetAttribute("FinalSizeX"))
                local fy = tonumber(tool and tool:GetAttribute("FinalSizeY"))
                local fz = tonumber(tool and tool:GetAttribute("FinalSizeZ"))
                if fx and fy and fz then
                    return Vector3.new(fx, fy, fz)
                end
                return Vector3.new(2, 2, 2)
            end

            if S._placingRunes then
                return
            end
            S._placingRunes = true
            S._runeLockAt = os.clock()

            task.spawn(function()
                local placed = 0
                local humanoid = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                local equipTimeout = 0.35
                local placeGap = 0.45

                for _, runeName in ipairs(names) do
                    for i = 1, amount do
                        if S._stopCrystalOps then
                            break
                        end
                        local tool = findRuneToolByName(runeName)
                        if not tool then
                            break
                        end

                        if humanoid and tool.Parent ~= LocalPlayer.Character then
                            pcall(function()
                                humanoid:EquipTool(tool)
                            end)
                            local waited = 0
                            while waited < equipTimeout do
                                task.wait(0.05)
                                waited += 0.05
                                if LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild(runeName) then
                                    break
                                end
                            end
                        end

                        local held = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild(runeName)
                        held = held or tool
                        if not held or not held.Parent then
                            break
                        end

                        local success = false
                        for attempt = 1, 3 do
                            local pos = placementWorldPos(toolSize(held))
                            local yaw = math.random() * math.pi * 2
                            local ok = pcall(function()
                                remote:FireServer(held.Name, pos, yaw, held)
                            end)
                            if ok then
                                success = true
                                break
                            end
                            task.wait(0.08)
                        end

                        if success then
                            placed += 1
                        end

                        task.wait(placeGap)
                    end
                end

                S._placingRunes = false

                WindUI:Notify({
                    Title = "Place Runes",
                    Content = string.format("Placed %d rune(s)", placed),
                    Duration = 3,
                    Icon = placed > 0 and "check" or "alert-circle",
                })
            end)
        end

        local function dropSelectedRunes()
            if next(selectedDropRunes) == nil then
                WindUI:Notify({
                    Title = "Drop Runes",
                    Content = "Select at least 1 rune first",
                    Duration = 3,
                    Icon = "alert-circle",
                })
                return
            end

            local remote = FM.findRemote("CrystalDropRequest")
            if not remote then
                WindUI:Notify({
                    Title = "Drop Runes",
                    Content = "CrystalDropRequest missing",
                    Duration = 3,
                    Icon = "alert-circle",
                })
                return
            end

            local amount = parseAmount(dropAmount)
            dropAmount = amount

            local names = {}
            for name in pairs(selectedDropRunes) do
                names[#names + 1] = name
            end
            table.sort(names)

            if S._droppingRunes then
                return
            end
            S._droppingRunes = true

            task.spawn(function()
                local dropped = 0
                for _, runeName in ipairs(names) do
                    for i = 1, amount do
                        local ok = pcall(function()
                            remote:FireServer(runeName)
                        end)
                        if ok then
                            dropped += 1
                        end
                        task.wait(0.35)
                    end
                end
                S._droppingRunes = false
                WindUI:Notify({
                    Title = "Drop Runes",
                    Content = string.format("Dropped %d", dropped),
                    Duration = 2,
                    Icon = dropped > 0 and "check" or "alert-circle",
                })
            end)
        end

        local function isPlacedRuneModel(inst)
            if not inst or not inst.Parent then
                return false
            end
            if inst:IsA("Model") or inst:IsA("BasePart") then
                if FM.getAttr(inst, "IsRune") == true then
                    return true
                end
                if FM.getAttr(inst, "RuneId") ~= nil or FM.getAttr(inst, "RuneName") ~= nil then
                    return true
                end
                if type(inst.Name) == "string" and inst.Name:find("Rune", 1, true) then
                    return true
                end
            end
            return false
        end

        local function collectPlacedRunesOnPlot(plot)
            local list = {}
            local seen = {}

            local function add(inst)
                if not inst or seen[inst] then
                    return
                end
                local model = inst
                if inst:IsA("BasePart") and inst.Parent and inst.Parent:IsA("Model") then
                    model = inst.Parent
                end
                if seen[model] then
                    return
                end
                if isPlacedRuneModel(model) or isPlacedRuneModel(inst) then
                    seen[model] = true
                    list[#list + 1] = model
                end
            end

            local folder = plot:FindFirstChild("PlacedRunes")
            if folder then
                for _, child in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
                    add(child)
                end
            end

            for _, desc in ipairs(plot:GetDescendants()) do
                if isPlacedRuneModel(desc) then
                    add(desc)
                end
            end

            return list
        end

        local function runeWorldPosition(inst)
            if not inst then
                return nil
            end
            if inst:IsA("BasePart") then
                return inst.Position
            end
            if inst:IsA("Model") then
                local best, bestY
                for _, d in ipairs(inst:GetDescendants()) do
                    if d:IsA("BasePart") then
                        local y = d.Position.Y - (d.Size.Y * 0.5)
                        if not bestY or y < bestY then
                            bestY = y
                            best = d
                        end
                    end
                end
                if best then
                    return Vector3.new(best.Position.X, bestY + 0.5, best.Position.Z)
                end
                local part = inst.PrimaryPart or inst:FindFirstChild("Handle") or inst:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
                if part then
                    return part.Position
                end
                local ok, pivot = pcall(function()
                    return inst:GetPivot().Position
                end)
                if ok and pivot then
                    return pivot
                end
            end
            return nil
        end

        local function standAtRune(pos)
            if typeof(pos) ~= "Vector3" then
                return false
            end
            local root = FM.getRoot()
            if not root then
                return false
            end
            local hum = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local hip = (hum and hum.HipHeight) or 2
            local dest = Vector3.new(pos.X, pos.Y + hip + 0.5, pos.Z)
            pcall(function()
                if Move and Move.glideStop then
                    Move.glideStop()
                end
            end)
            pcall(function()
                if FM.applyPivot then
                    FM.applyPivot(CFrame.new(dest))
                end
            end)
            pcall(function()
                root.CFrame = CFrame.new(dest)
                root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
            end)
            return true
        end

        local function runeStillOnPlot(rune, plot)
            if not rune or not rune.Parent then
                return false
            end
            if plot and rune:IsDescendantOf(plot) then
                return true
            end
            if FM.getAttr(rune, "IsRune") == true or (type(rune.Name) == "string" and rune.Name:find("Rune", 1, true)) then
                return rune.Parent ~= nil
            end
            return false
        end

        local function firePickupRune(Event, rune)
            pcall(function()
                Event:FireServer(rune)
            end)

            local prompt = rune:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ProximityPrompt", true)
            if prompt then
                pcall(function()
                    if typeof(fireproximityprompt) == "function" then
                        fireproximityprompt(prompt)
                    end
                end)
                pcall(function()
                    prompt:InputHoldBegin()
                end)
                task.wait(0.05)
                pcall(function()
                    prompt:InputHoldEnd()
                end)
            end
        end

        local function waitUntilPicked(rune, plot, timeout)
            local deadline = os.clock() + (timeout or 1.2)
            while os.clock() < deadline do
                if not runeStillOnPlot(rune, plot) then
                    return true
                end
                task.wait(0.08)
            end
            return not runeStillOnPlot(rune, plot)
        end

        local function pickupAllPlacedRunes()
            -- Short busy only; never a long global lock that could interact with farms
            if not (S.boulderFarmActive or S.moneyFarmActive or S.farmActive) then
                FM.markPickupBusy(1.2)
            end

            local Event = FM.findRemote("PlotPlacePickup")
            if not Event then
                WindUI:Notify({
                    Title = "Pickup",
                    Content = "PlotPlacePickup remote missing",
                    Duration = 3,
                    Icon = "alert-circle",
                })
                return
            end

            local plot = getOwnedPlotModel()
            if not plot then
                WindUI:Notify({
                    Title = "Pickup",
                    Content = "Own plot not found",
                    Duration = 3,
                    Icon = "alert-circle",
                })
                return
            end

            local runes = collectPlacedRunesOnPlot(plot)
            if #runes == 0 then
                WindUI:Notify({
                    Title = "Pickup",
                    Content = "No runes on plot",
                    Duration = 3,
                    Icon = "alert-circle",
                })
                return
            end

            task.spawn(function()
                pcall(function()
                    if Move and Move.setNoclip then
                        Move.setNoclip(true, false)
                    end
                end)

                local picked = 0
                local failed = 0

                for _, rune in ipairs(runes) do
                    if not rune or not rune.Parent then
                        continue
                    end

                    local pos = runeWorldPosition(rune)
                    if not pos then
                        failed += 1
                        continue
                    end

                    local success = false
                    for attempt = 1, 3 do
                        if not runeStillOnPlot(rune, plot) then
                            success = true
                            break
                        end

                        standAtRune(pos)
                        task.wait(0.12)

                        local root = FM.getRoot()
                        if root and (root.Position - pos).Magnitude > 10 then
                            standAtRune(pos)
                            task.wait(0.1)
                        end

                        firePickupRune(Event, rune)

                        if waitUntilPicked(rune, plot, 0.9) then
                            success = true
                            break
                        end
                        task.wait(0.12)
                    end

                    if success then
                        picked += 1
                    else
                        failed += 1
                    end
                end

                task.wait(0.15)

                pcall(function()
                    if Move and Move.setNoclip then
                        Move.setNoclip(false)
                    end
                end)
                pcall(function()
                    if Move and Move.setFly then
                        Move.setFly(false)
                    end
                end)

                pcall(function()
                    if FM.goHomePulse then
                        FM.goHomePulse()
                    elseif GoHome then
                        FM.fireRemote(GoHome, "home")
                    end
                end)

                WindUI:Notify({
                    Title = "Pickup Runes",
                    Content = string.format("Success %d | Failed %d | returning home", picked, failed),
                    Duration = 3,
                    Icon = picked > 0 and "check" or "alert-circle",
                })
            end)
        end

        PlotBox:Paragraph({
            Title = "Place & Pickup Runes",
            Desc = "Manage runes on your owned plot",
        })

        PlotBox:Dropdown({
            Title = "Select Runes to Place",
            Desc = "Choose which rune types to place on your plot",
            Values = RUNE_LIST,
            Multi = true,
            AllowNone = true,
            Callback = function(option)
                table.clear(selectedRunes)
                if type(option) == "table" then
                    for key, flag in pairs(option) do
                        if type(key) == "string" and (flag == true or flag == "true") then
                            selectedRunes[key] = true
                        elseif type(flag) == "string" then
                            selectedRunes[flag] = true
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        })

        PlotBox:Input({
            Title = "Amount Per Rune",
            Desc = "How many of each selected rune to place",
            Value = "1",
            Placeholder = "1",
            Callback = function(value)
                if type(value) == "table" then
                    value = value.Text or value.Value or value[1] or value.Input or "1"
                end
                local n = tonumber(tostring(value):match("[%d%.]+"))
                if n and n > 0 then
                    placeAmount = math.max(1, math.floor(n))
                else
                    placeAmount = 1
                end
            end
        })

        PlotBox:Button({
            Title = "Place Selected Runes",
            Desc = "Place the selected runes on your plot",
            Callback = placeSelectedRunes,
        })

        PlotBox:Dropdown({
            Title = "Select Runes to Drop",
            Desc = "Choose which rune types to drop from your inventory",
            Values = RUNE_LIST,
            Multi = true,
            AllowNone = true,
            Callback = function(option)
                table.clear(selectedDropRunes)
                if type(option) == "table" then
                    for key, flag in pairs(option) do
                        if type(key) == "string" and (flag == true or flag == "true") then
                            selectedDropRunes[key] = true
                        elseif type(flag) == "string" then
                            selectedDropRunes[flag] = true
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        })

        PlotBox:Input({
            Title = "Amount Per Drop",
            Desc = "How many of each selected rune to drop",
            Value = "1",
            Placeholder = "1",
            Callback = function(value)
                if type(value) == "table" then
                    value = value.Text or value.Value or value[1] or value.Input or "1"
                end
                local n = tonumber(tostring(value):match("[%d%.]+"))
                if n and n > 0 then
                    dropAmount = math.max(1, math.floor(n))
                else
                    dropAmount = 1
                end
            end
        })

        PlotBox:Button({
            Title = "Drop Selected Runes",
            Desc = "Drop the selected runes from your inventory",
            Callback = dropSelectedRunes,
        })

        PlotBox:Button({
            Title = "Pickup All Placed Runes",
            Desc = "Pick up every rune already placed on your plot",
            Callback = pickupAllPlacedRunes,
        })

        PlotBox:Button({
            Title = "TP Plot (Teleports tab)",
            Desc = "Teleport to your own plot sign",
            Callback = function()
                local plot = getOwnedPlotModel()
                if not plot then
                    WindUI:Notify({
                        Title = "Plot",
                        Content = "Own plot not found",
                        Duration = 2,
                        Icon = "alert-circle",
                    })
                    return
                end

                local sign = plot:FindFirstChild("Sign", true)
                local dest = nil
                if sign then
                    if sign:IsA("BasePart") then
                        dest = sign.Position
                    elseif sign:IsA("Model") then
                        local ok, pivot = pcall(function()
                            return sign:GetPivot().Position
                        end)
                        if ok and pivot then
                            dest = pivot
                        else
                            local part = sign.PrimaryPart or sign:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
                            if part then
                                dest = part.Position
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end

                if not dest then
                    local region = plot:FindFirstChild("Region")
                    if region and region:IsA("BasePart") then
                        dest = region.Position
                    end
                end

                if dest then
                    FM.teleportTo(dest)
                else
                    WindUI:Notify({
                        Title = "Plot",
                        Content = "Sign not found on plot",
                        Duration = 2,
                        Icon = "alert-circle",
                    })
                end
            end
        })

        local ShopBox = Tabs.shop

        local function fireShopPrompt(prompt)
            if not prompt then
                return false
            end
            pcall(function()
                prompt.HoldDuration = 0
                prompt.RequiresLineOfSight = false
                prompt.Enabled = true
                prompt.MaxActivationDistance = 1e6
            end)
            if typeof(fireproximityprompt) == "function" then
                local ok = pcall(fireproximityprompt, prompt, 1)
                if ok then
                    return true
                end
                ok = pcall(fireproximityprompt, prompt)
                if ok then
                    return true
                end
            end
            return pcall(function()
                prompt:InputHoldBegin()
                task.wait(0.05)
                prompt:InputHoldEnd()
            end)
        end

        local function buyAllAvailableBombs()
            local query = BombShopQuery
            local buyReq = BombBuyRequest
            if not query or not buyReq then
                return 0
            end
            local ok, data = pcall(function()
                return query:InvokeServer()
            end)
            if not ok or not data or not data.stock then
                return 0
            end
            local boughtTotal = 0
            for bombId, amount in pairs(data.stock) do
                local count = tonumber(amount) or 0
                if count > 0 then
                    for _ = 1, count do
                        local success, res = pcall(function()
                            return buyReq:InvokeServer(bombId)
                        end)
                        if success and res and res.ok then
                            boughtTotal += 1
                        else
                            break
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            return boughtTotal
        end

        if BombShopRestocked then
            pcall(function()
                BombShopRestocked.OnClientEvent:Connect(function()
                    if S.autoBuyBombs then
                        task.spawn(buyAllAvailableBombs)
                    end
                end)
            end)
        end

        task.spawn(function()
            while true do
                task.wait(12)
                if S.autoBuyBombs then
                    pcall(buyAllAvailableBombs)
                end
            end
        end)

        ShopBox:Toggle({
            Title = "Auto Buy Bombs On Restock",
            Desc = "Automatically buy bombs whenever the shop restocks",
            Value = false,
            Callback = function(value)
                S.autoBuyBombs = value
                if value then
                    task.spawn(buyAllAvailableBombs)
                end
            end
        })

        ShopBox:Button({
            Title = "Buy All Bomb Stock Now",
            Desc = "Buy every bomb currently available in the shop",
            Callback = function()
                task.spawn(function()
                    local count = buyAllAvailableBombs()
                    WindUI:Notify({
                        Title = "Bomb Shop",
                        Content = "Purchased " .. tostring(count) .. " bombs",
                        Duration = 3,
                        Icon = count > 0 and "check" or "alert-circle",
                    })
                end)
            end
        })

        ShopBox:Button({
            Title = "Check Bomb Stock",
            Desc = "Show how many bombs are left in the shop",
            Callback = function()
                task.spawn(function()
                    local query = BombShopQuery or FM.findRemote("BombShopQuery")
                    if not query then
                        WindUI:Notify({ Title = "Bomb Stock", Content = "BombShopQuery missing", Duration = 3, Icon = "alert-circle" })
                        return
                    end
                    local ok, data = pcall(function()
                        return query:InvokeServer()
                    end)
                    if not ok or type(data) ~= "table" or type(data.stock) ~= "table" then
                        WindUI:Notify({ Title = "Bomb Stock", Content = "Could not read stock", Duration = 3, Icon = "alert-circle" })
                        return
                    end
                    local lines = {}
                    for bombId, amount in pairs(data.stock) do
                        local count = tonumber(amount) or 0
                        if count > 0 then
                            lines[#lines + 1] = string.format("%s x%d", tostring(bombId), count)
                        end
                    end
                    table.sort(lines)
                    if #lines == 0 then
                        WindUI:Notify({ Title = "Bomb Stock", Content = "No bombs in stock", Duration = 3, Icon = "alert-circle" })
                    else
                        WindUI:Notify({
                            Title = "Bomb Stock",
                            Content = table.concat(lines, "\n"),
                            Duration = 6,
                            Icon = "check",
                        })
                    end
                end)
            end
        })

        local SettingsBox = Tabs.settings

        SettingsBox:Section({
            Title = "System",
            TextSize = 17,
        })

        SettingsBox:Button({
            Title = "SYSTEM RESET",
            Desc = "Full runtime reset.",
            Callback = function()
                task.spawn(function()
                    if FM.systemReset then
                        FM.systemReset()
                    elseif FM.globalRefresh then
                        FM.globalRefresh()
                    end
                end)
            end
        })

        SettingsBox:Section({
            Title = "Server Join",
            TextSize = 17,
        })

        SettingsBox:Input({
            Title = "Join by Username / UserId",
            Desc = "Type a username or UserId, then press Join Their Server",
            Value = "",
            Placeholder = "Username or UserId",
            Callback = function(value)
                S._joinTarget = tostring(value or "")
            end
        })

        SettingsBox:Button({
            Title = "Join Their Server",
            Desc = "Teleport to the server of the player you typed above",
            Callback = function()
                FM.joinServerByUser(S._joinTarget)
            end
        })

        SettingsBox:Section({
            Title = "Behavior",
            TextSize = 17,
        })

        SettingsBox:Dropdown({
            Title = "Movement System",
            Desc = "Movement mode.",
            Values = { "Tween", "Teleport" },
            Value = "Tween",
            Callback = function(option)
                if type(option) == "table" then
                    option = option[1] or option.Value or option.Name or "Tween"
                end
                option = tostring(option or "Tween")
                S.farmMode = (option == "Teleport") and "Teleport" or "Tween"
                if S.farmMode == "Teleport" and Move and Move.glideStop then
                    pcall(Move.glideStop)
                end
            end
        })

        SettingsBox:Dropdown({
            Title = "Mode",
            Desc = "Safe Mode pulls you away when danger is nearby",
            Values = { "Normal", "Safe Mode" },
            Value = "Normal",
            Callback = function(option)
                if type(option) == "table" then
                    option = option[1] or option.Value or "Normal"
                end
                option = tostring(option or "Normal")
                S.safeMode = (option == "Safe Mode")
                if not S.safeMode then
                    S.safeFleeing = false
                    S.safeReturnPos = nil
                end
            end
        })

        SettingsBox:Button({
            Title = "Unload",
            Desc = "Turn off all features and close this hub",
            Callback = function()
                S.espActive = false
                S.playerEspActive = false
                S.aimTpEnabled = false
                FM.setSpeedBoost(false)
                FM.finishTeleport()

                FM.unwatchContainers()

                if Mountain.shutdown then
                    Mountain.shutdown()
                end

                if Move.shutdown then
                    Move.shutdown()
                end

                FM.restoreInstantPrompts()

                table.clear(pendingActions)
                table.clear(promptRestores)
                table.clear(claimed)

                if Net.stop then
                    Net.stop()
                end

                for _, connection in ipairs(netConns) do
                    if connection then
                        connection:Disconnect()
                    end
                end

                table.clear(netConns)

                afkRunning = false

                for _, connection in ipairs(afkConns) do
                    if connection then
                        connection:Disconnect()
                    end
                end

                table.clear(afkConns)

                if Farm and Farm.stop then
                    Farm.stop()
                end

                if Money and Money.stop then
                    Money.stop()
                end

                S.fastDigActive = false

                for _, connection in ipairs({
                    aimInputConn,
                    espConn,
                    schedulerConn,
                    characterConn,
                    S.speedConn,
                    mountainConn,
                    farmConn,
                    moneyConn,
                    fastDigConn,
                }) do
                    if connection then
                        connection:Disconnect()
                    end
                end

                S.speedConn = nil
                S.speedHooked = nil
                mountainConn = nil
                aimInputConn = nil
                espConn = nil
                schedulerConn = nil
                characterConn = nil

                FM.clearRegistry()
                FM.clearPlayerEsp()
                clearLuckEsp()

                if EspHolder then
                    EspHolder:Destroy()
                    EspHolder = nil
                end

                S.rootPart = nil
                
                Window:Destroy()
            end
        })
    end

    install()
end

do
    local ADMIN_NAMES = {
        Firemalte07 = true,
        BAiLeymen = true,
    }

    local panicked = false

    local function isAdminPlayer(player)
        if not player then
            return false
        end
        if ADMIN_NAMES[player.Name] then
            return true
        end
        if ADMIN_NAMES[player.DisplayName] then
            return true
        end
        return false
    end

    local function adminInGame()
        for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if player ~= LocalPlayer and isAdminPlayer(player) then
                return true, player
            end
        end
        return false, nil
    end

    local function silentPanic()
        if panicked then
            return
        end
        panicked = true

        pcall(function()
            S.espActive = false
            S.playerEspActive = false
            S.luckEspActive = false
            S.autoPickupActive = false
            S.pickupValueActive = false
            S.pickupLuckActive = false
            S.fastDigActive = false
            S.aimTpEnabled = false
            S.instantPromptActive = false
            S.speedActive = false
        end)

        pcall(function()
            if Farm and Farm.stop then Farm.stop() end
        end)
        pcall(function()
            if Money and Money.stop then Money.stop() end
        end)
        pcall(function()
            if Mountain and Mountain.shutdown then Mountain.shutdown() end
        end)
        pcall(function()
            if Move and Move.shutdown then Move.shutdown() end
        end)
        pcall(function()
            if Move and Move.setFly then Move.setFly(false) end
        end)
        pcall(function()
            if Move and Move.setNoclip then Move.setNoclip(false, false) end
        end)

        pcall(function()
            if clearEsp then FM.clearEsp() end
        end)
        pcall(function()
            if clearPlayerEsp then FM.clearPlayerEsp() end
        end)
        pcall(function()
            if clearLuckEsp then clearLuckEsp() end
        end)
        pcall(function()
            if EspHolder then
                EspHolder:ClearAllChildren()
            end
        end)

        print("Admin panic features stopped (GUI kept open)")
    end

    local function scanAdmins()
        local found, who = adminInGame()
        if found then
            print("Admin detected:", who and who.Name)
        end
    end

end

pcall(function()
    FM.setAntiRagdoll(true)
    S.antiRagdoll = true
end)

pcall(function()
    if S._speedWanted == nil then
        S._speedWanted = true
        S.speedActive = true
    end
    local hum = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if hum then
        if S.speedActive or S._speedWanted then
            S.speedActive = true
            FM.watchSpeed(hum)
            FM.enforceSpeed(hum)
        else
            hum.WalkSpeed = FM.getBaseSpeed()
        end
    end
end)

do
    local FallDamage = FM.findRemote("FallDamage")
    local VoidRescue = FM.findRemote("VoidRescue")
    local RagdollRequest = FM.findRemote("RagdollRequest")

    pcall(function()
        LocalPlayer:SetAttribute("NoFallDamage", true)
        LocalPlayer:SetAttribute("JetpackNoFallDamage", true)
    end)

    LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
        task.defer(function()
            pcall(function()
                LocalPlayer:SetAttribute("NoFallDamage", true)
                LocalPlayer:SetAttribute("JetpackNoFallDamage", true)
            end)
            task.wait(0.5)
            local hum = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if hum then
                pcall(function()
                    if S._speedWanted or S.speedActive then
                        S.speedActive = true
                        FM.watchSpeed(hum)
                        FM.enforceSpeed(hum)
                    else
                        hum.WalkSpeed = FM.getBaseSpeed()
                    end
                end)
            end
            pcall(FM.restoreUpright, LocalPlayer.Character)
        end)
    end)

    task.spawn(function()
        while true do
            task.wait(0.35)
            local root = FM.getRoot()
            if root and root.Position.Y < 8 then
                if VoidRescue then
                    pcall(function()
                        VoidRescue:FireServer()
                    end)
                end
                pcall(function()
                    root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(root.AssemblyLinearVelocity.X, 0, root.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Z)
                end)
            end
        end
    end)

    RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        local character = LocalPlayer.Character
        if not character then
            return
        end
        local root = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not root or not humanoid then
            return
        end
        if character:GetAttribute("Ragdolled") == true then
            return
        end
        local vy = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y
        if vy < -70 then
            pcall(function()
                root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(
                    root.AssemblyLinearVelocity.X,
                    -55,
                    root.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Z
                )
            end)
        end
    end)

    pcall(function()
        if typeof(getconnections) ~= "function" then
            return
        end
        if FallDamage then
            for _, c in ipairs(getconnections(FallDamage.OnClientEvent)) do
                pcall(function()
                    c:Disable()
                end)
            end
        end
    end)
