    task.spawn(function()
        while true do
            task.wait(1)
            local active, secs = FM.hasFastMineBoost()
            if active then
                if S.digSpeedMult and S.digSpeedMult > 0.25 then
                    S._digMultBeforeBoost = S._digMultBeforeBoost or S.digSpeedMult
                    S.digSpeedMult = math.min(S.digSpeedMult, 0.28)
                end
            elseif S._digMultBeforeBoost then
                S.digSpeedMult = S._digMultBeforeBoost
                S._digMultBeforeBoost = nil
            end
        end
    end)
end

task.defer(function()
    if not Tabs or not Window then
        return
    end

    local ShopTab = Tabs.shop
    local TpTab = Tabs.teleports
    if not ShopTab or not TpTab then
        return
    end

    if S._guiShopTpBuilt then
        return
    end
    S._guiShopTpBuilt = true

    pcall(function()
        ShopTab:Section({ Title = "Weight Upgrade", TextSize = 17 })
    end)

    local weightReady = false
    task.defer(function()
        weightReady = true
    end)

        

    pcall(function()
        ShopTab:Section({ Title = "Radar Shop", TextSize = 17 })
    end)

    ShopTab:Button({
        Title = "Check Radar Stock",
        Desc = "Show available radar items in the shop",
        Callback = function()
            if FM.checkRadarStockNotify then
                FM.checkRadarStockNotify()
            end
        end,
    })

    ShopTab:Button({
        Title = "Buy All Radar Stock",
        Desc = "Buy every radar item currently in stock",
        Callback = function()
            if FM.buyAllRadars then
                FM.buyAllRadars()
            end
        end,
    })

    pcall(function()
        TpTab:Section({ Title = "Locations", TextSize = 17 })
    end)

    TpTab:Button({
        Title = "TP Home",
        Desc = "Teleport back to your home base",
        Callback = function()
            pcall(function()
                if FM.goHomePulse then FM.goHomePulse() end
            end)
            pcall(function()
                if GoHome then FM.fireRemote(GoHome, "home") end
            end)
        end,
    })

    TpTab:Button({
        Title = "TP Mountain Peak",
        Desc = "Teleport to the top of the mountain",
        Callback = function()
            if FM.teleportToMountainPeak then
                FM.teleportToMountainPeak()
            end
        end,
    })

    TpTab:Button({
        Title = "TP To Own Plot",
        Desc = "Teleport to the sign on your owned plot",
        Callback = function()
            local plot, region = FM.getOwnedPlotRegion and FM.getOwnedPlotRegion()
            if not plot then
                WindUI:Notify({ Title = "Plot", Content = "Own plot not found", Duration = 2, Icon = "alert-circle" })
                return
            end
            local dest = nil
            local sign = plot:FindFirstChild("Sign", true)
            if sign then
                if sign:IsA("BasePart") then
                    dest = sign.Position
                elseif sign:IsA("Model") then
                    local ok, pivot = pcall(function()
                        return sign:GetPivot().Position
                    end)
                    dest = ok and pivot or nil
                    if not dest then
                        local part = sign.PrimaryPart or sign:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
                        dest = part and part.Position
                    end
                end
            end
            if not dest and region then
                dest = region.Position
            end
            if dest then
                if FM.moveTo then
                    FM.moveTo(dest + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0), dest)
                elseif FM.teleportTo then
                    FM.teleportTo(dest)
                end
            else
                WindUI:Notify({ Title = "Plot", Content = "Sign not found", Duration = 2, Icon = "alert-circle" })
            end
        end,
    })

    TpTab:Button({
        Title = "Stop All TP",
        Desc = "Cancel any active teleport or tween movement",
        Callback = function()
            if FM.stopAllTeleport then
                FM.stopAllTeleport()
            end
        end,
    })

    TpTab:Button({
        Title = "Hop Random Server",
        Desc = "Join a different public server of this game",
        Callback = function()
            if FM.hopRandomServer then
                FM.hopRandomServer()
            end
        end,
    })

    TpTab:Button({
        Title = "Sell All",
        Desc = "Sell all crystals. Movement unlocks automatically after sell",
        Callback = function()
            if FM.doSell then
                FM.doSell()
            end
            if FM.unlockMovementBurst then
                FM.unlockMovementBurst(4)
            end
        end,
    })

    

    
    pcall(function()
        if not Tabs.shop then return end
        local ShopBox = Tabs.shop
        ShopBox:Dropdown({
            Title = "Weight Tier",
            Desc = "Backpack weight upgrade tier",
            Values = { "1kg (tier 1)", "5kg (tier 2)", "10kg (tier 3)" },
            Value = "1kg (tier 1)",
            Callback = function(opt)
                if type(opt) == "table" then opt = opt[1] or "" end
                opt = tostring(opt or "")
                if opt:find("10kg") then S._weightTier = 3
                elseif opt:find("5kg") then S._weightTier = 2
                else S._weightTier = 1 end
            end
        })
        ShopBox:Input({
            Title = "Buy Amount (Custom)",
            Desc = "How many times to buy the selected weight tier",
            Value = "1",
            Placeholder = "e.g. 1 / 25 / 100",
            Callback = function(text)
                if type(text) == "table" then
                    text = text.Text or text.Value or text[1] or text.Input or "1"
                end
                local n = tonumber(tostring(text or ""):match("[%d%.]+")) or 1
                S._weightTimes = math.max(1, math.floor(n))
            end
        })
        ShopBox:Button({
            Title = "Buy Weight",
            Desc = "Buy selected tier N times (one notify when finished)",
            Callback = function()
                task.spawn(function()
                    local tier = S._weightTier or 1
                    local times = math.max(1, math.floor(tonumber(S._weightTimes) or 1))
                    if FM.buyWeightBulk then
                        FM.buyWeightBulk(tier, times)
                    elseif FM.upgradeWeight then
                        FM.upgradeWeight(tier, false)
                    end
                end)
            end
        })
    end)

    WindUI:Notify({
        Title = "Mine A Mountain",
        Content = "Successful Injection",
        Duration = 4,
        Icon = "check",
    })
end)

function FM.setPickupButtonGui(enabled)
    S.pickupButtonGui = enabled == true

    if S._pickupBtnGui then
        pcall(function()
            S._pickupBtnGui:Destroy()
        end)
        S._pickupBtnGui = nil
    end
    if S._pickupBtnMoveConn then
        pcall(function() S._pickupBtnMoveConn:Disconnect() end)
        S._pickupBtnMoveConn = nil
    end
    if S._pickupBtnEndConn then
        pcall(function() S._pickupBtnEndConn:Disconnect() end)
        S._pickupBtnEndConn = nil
    end

    if not S.pickupButtonGui then
        return
    end

    local playerGui = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui") or LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui", 5)
    if not playerGui then
        return
    end

    local UIS = game:GetService("UserInputService")
    local gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    gui.Name = "FayyMengPickupButton"
    gui.ResetOnSpawn = false
    gui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
    gui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
    gui.DisplayOrder = 120
    pcall(function()
        gui.Parent = playerGui
    end)
    if not gui.Parent then
        pcall(function()
            gui.Parent = CoreGui
        end)
    end
    S._pickupBtnGui = gui

    local COL_BODY = Color3.fromRGB(18, 95, 95)
    local COL_TOP = Color3.fromRGB(55, 200, 200)
    local COL_SHADOW = Color3.fromRGB(5, 40, 42)
    local COL_SCREEN = Color3.fromRGB(70, 220, 220)
    local COL_TEXT = Color3.fromRGB(5, 35, 38)
    local COL_LOCK = Color3.fromRGB(255, 200, 80)
    local COL_UNLOCK = Color3.fromRGB(160, 255, 240)

    local BTN_W, BTN_H = 96, 96
    local defaultPos = UDim2.new(1, -70, 0.5, 0)
    local saved = S._pickupBtnPos

    local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
    frame.Name = "StudButton"
    frame.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    frame.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(BTN_W, BTN_H)
    frame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
    frame.Active = true
    frame.Parent = gui
    if saved and typeof(saved) == "UDim2" then
        frame.Position = saved
    else
        frame.Position = defaultPos
    end

    local body = Instance.new("Frame")
    body.Name = "Body"
    body.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
    body.BackgroundColor3 = COL_BODY
    body.BorderSizePixel = 0
    body.Active = false
    body.Parent = frame

    local bodyCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    bodyCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)
    bodyCorner.Parent = body

    local bodyStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    bodyStroke.Color = COL_SHADOW
    bodyStroke.Thickness = 3
    bodyStroke.Parent = body

    local function makeStud(name, xScale, yScale)
        local stud = Instance.new("Frame")
        stud.Name = name
        stud.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        stud.Position = UDim2.new(xScale, 0, yScale, 0)
        stud.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(26, 26)
        stud.BackgroundColor3 = COL_TOP
        stud.BorderSizePixel = 0
        stud.ZIndex = 2
        stud.Active = false
        stud.Parent = body
        local sc = Instance.new("UICorner")
        sc.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
        sc.Parent = stud
        local ss = Instance.new("UIStroke")
        ss.Color = COL_SHADOW
        ss.Thickness = 2
        ss.Parent = stud
        local hi = Instance.new("Frame")
        hi.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        hi.Position = UDim2.new(0.35, 0, 0.32, 0)
        hi.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 8)
        hi.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(180, 255, 255)
        hi.BackgroundTransparency = 0.35
        hi.BorderSizePixel = 0
        hi.ZIndex = 3
        hi.Active = false
        hi.Parent = stud
        local hc = Instance.new("UICorner")
        hc.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
        hc.Parent = hi
        return stud
    end

    makeStud("StudTL", 0.30, 0.28)
    makeStud("StudTR", 0.70, 0.28)
    makeStud("StudBL", 0.30, 0.58)
    makeStud("StudBR", 0.70, 0.58)

    local plate = Instance.new("Frame")
    plate.Name = "Plate"
    plate.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 1)
    plate.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, -8)
    plate.Size = UDim2.new(0.88, 0, 0, 22)
    plate.BackgroundColor3 = COL_SCREEN
    plate.BorderSizePixel = 0
    plate.ZIndex = 4
    plate.Active = false
    plate.Parent = body
    local pc = Instance.new("UICorner")
    pc.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
    pc.Parent = plate
    local ps = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    ps.Color = COL_SHADOW
    ps.Thickness = 1.5
    ps.Parent = plate

    local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    label.Name = "Label"
    label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    label.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
    label.Text = "PICK UP"
    label.TextColor3 = COL_TEXT
    label.TextSize = 12
    label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    label.ZIndex = 5
    label.Active = false
    label.Parent = plate

    local lockBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
    lockBtn.Name = "Lock"
    lockBtn.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0)
    lockBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, 6, 0, -10)
    lockBtn.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(24, 24)
    lockBtn.BackgroundColor3 = COL_SHADOW
    lockBtn.Text = S.pickupBtnLocked and "L" or "U"
    lockBtn.TextColor3 = S.pickupBtnLocked and COL_LOCK or COL_UNLOCK
    lockBtn.TextSize = 14
    lockBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    lockBtn.ZIndex = 12
    lockBtn.AutoButtonColor = true
    lockBtn.Parent = frame
    local lc = Instance.new("UICorner")
    lc.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
    lc.Parent = lockBtn
    local ls = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    ls.Color = COL_LOCK
    ls.Thickness = 1.5
    ls.Parent = lockBtn

    local function refreshLockVisual()
        local locked = S.pickupBtnLocked == true
        lockBtn.Text = locked and "L" or "U"
        lockBtn.TextColor3 = locked and COL_LOCK or COL_UNLOCK
        ls.Color = locked and COL_LOCK or COL_UNLOCK
        bodyStroke.Color = locked and COL_LOCK or COL_SHADOW
    end
    refreshLockVisual()

    lockBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        S.pickupBtnLocked = not S.pickupBtnLocked
        refreshLockVisual()
    end)
    pcall(function()
        lockBtn.Activated:Connect(function()
            S.pickupBtnLocked = not S.pickupBtnLocked
            refreshLockVisual()
        end)
    end)

    local hit = Instance.new("TextButton")
    hit.Name = "Hit"
    hit.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
    hit.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    hit.Text = ""
    hit.ZIndex = 10
    hit.AutoButtonColor = false
    hit.Active = true
    hit.Parent = frame

    local dragging = false
    local dragMoved = false
    local dragInput = nil
    local startMouse = nil
    local startFrame = nil
    local lastClick = 0
    local THRESH = 10

    local function clampToScreen(absX, absY)
        local cam = Workspace.CurrentCamera
        local vp = cam and cam.ViewportSize or Vector2.new(1280, 720)
        local halfW = BTN_W * 0.5
        local halfH = BTN_H * 0.5
        absX = math.clamp(absX, halfW + 4, vp.X - halfW - 4)
        absY = math.clamp(absY, halfH + 4, vp.Y - halfH - 4)
        return absX, absY
    end

    local function setAbsCenter(cx, cy)
        cx, cy = clampToScreen(cx, cy)
        frame.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(cx, cy)
        S._pickupBtnPos = frame.Position
    end

    local function beginDrag(input)
        if S.pickupBtnLocked then
            dragging = true
            dragMoved = false
            dragInput = input
            startMouse = Vector2.new(input.Position.X, input.Position.Y)
            startFrame = nil
            return
        end
        if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
            and input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            return
        end
        dragging = true
        dragMoved = false
        dragInput = input
        startMouse = Vector2.new(input.Position.X, input.Position.Y)
        local ap = frame.AbsolutePosition
        local as = frame.AbsoluteSize
        startFrame = Vector2.new(ap.X + as.X * 0.5, ap.Y + as.Y * 0.5)
    end

    hit.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
            or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            beginDrag(input)
        end
    end)

    S._pickupBtnMoveConn = UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if not dragging or not dragInput then return end
        if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
            and input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            return
        end
        if S.pickupBtnLocked or not startFrame or not startMouse then
            return
        end
        local cur = Vector2.new(input.Position.X, input.Position.Y)
        local delta = cur - startMouse
        if delta.Magnitude > THRESH then
            dragMoved = true
        end
        if dragMoved then
            setAbsCenter(startFrame.X + delta.X, startFrame.Y + delta.Y)
        end
    end)

    S._pickupBtnEndConn = UIS.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
        if not dragging then return end
        if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
            and input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            return
        end
        local wasDrag = dragMoved
        dragging = false
        dragMoved = false
        dragInput = nil
        startMouse = nil
        startFrame = nil

        if wasDrag then
            return
        end

        local now = os.clock()
        if now - lastClick < 0.25 then return end
        lastClick = now

        task.spawn(function()
            local root = FM.getRoot and FM.getRoot()
            if not root then return end
            local origin = root.Position
            local best, bestDist = nil, 28
            local function consider(inst)
                if not inst or not inst:IsA("BasePart") then return end
                if FM.getAttr and FM.getAttr(inst, "Collected") == true then return end
                local okCrystal = false
                pcall(function()
                    if FM.isCrystal and FM.isCrystal(inst) then okCrystal = true end
                end)
                if not okCrystal then
                    if FM.getAttr and (FM.getAttr(inst, "Value") ~= nil or FM.getAttr(inst, "Tier") ~= nil) then
                        okCrystal = true
                    end
                end
                if not okCrystal then return end
                local d = (inst.Position - origin).Magnitude
                if d < bestDist then
                    bestDist = d
                    best = inst
                end
            end
            if S.registry then
                for inst in pairs(S.registry) do
                    consider(inst)
                end
            end
            pcall(function()
                FM.eachContainer(function(container)
                    for _, child in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
                        if child:IsA("BasePart") then
                            consider(child)
                        elseif child:IsA("Model") then
                            for _, inner in ipairs(child:GetChildren()) do
                                consider(inner)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end)
            end)
            if best and FM.grabCrystal then
                local prompt = FM.crystalPrompt and FM.crystalPrompt(best)
                FM.grabCrystal(best, prompt)
            end
        end)
    end)
end

do
    local panelGui = nil
    local toggleFrames = {}
    local toggleStates = {}

    local function addCorner(obj, radius)
        local c = Instance.new("UICorner")
        c.CornerRadius = radius or UDim.new(0, 8)
        c.Parent = obj
        return c
    end

    local function addStroke(obj, transparency)
        local s = Instance.new("UIStroke")
        s.Color = Color3.fromRGB(80, 120, 200)
        s.Thickness = 1
        s.Transparency = transparency or 0.5
        s.Parent = obj
        return s
    end

    local function addGradient(obj, c1, c2, rot)
        local g = Instance.new("UIGradient")
        g.Color = ColorSequence.new(c1 or Color3.fromRGB(40, 80, 160), c2 or Color3.fromRGB(20, 40, 90))
        g.Rotation = rot or 25
        g.Parent = obj
        return g
    end

    local function tween(obj, props, dur)
        pcall(function()
            local tw = game:GetService("TweenService"):Create(
                obj,
                TweenInfo.new(dur or 0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
                props
            )
            tw:Play()
        end)
    end

    local function destroyShortcutPanel()
        if panelGui then
            pcall(function() panelGui:Destroy() end)
            panelGui = nil
        end
        table.clear(toggleFrames)
        table.clear(toggleStates)
        S.shortcutPanelOpen = false
    end

    local function updateToggleVisual(index, isOn, animate)
        local fr = toggleFrames[index]
        if not fr then return end
        fr.bg.BackgroundTransparency = isOn and 0 or 0.35
        fr.icon.TextTransparency = isOn and 0 or 0.2
        fr.label.TextTransparency = isOn and 0 or 0.2
        fr.indicatorBg.BackgroundTransparency = isOn and 0.1 or 0.5
        fr.indicatorText.Text = isOn and "✓" or "+"
    end

    local SHORTCUTS = {
        {
            name = "Auto Dig",
            oneShot = false,
            gradient = { Color3.fromRGB(255, 140, 60), Color3.fromRGB(180, 60, 20) },
            get = function() return S.fastDigActive == true end,
            apply = function(on)
                S.fastDigActive = on == true
            end,
        },
        {
            name = "Fly+Noclip",
            oneShot = false,
            gradient = { Color3.fromRGB(80, 180, 255), Color3.fromRGB(30, 80, 160) },
            get = function() return S._shortcutFly == true end,
            apply = function(on)
                S._shortcutFly = on == true
                pcall(function()
                    if Move and Move.setFly then Move.setFly(on) end
                end)
                pcall(function()
                    if Move and Move.setNoclip then Move.setNoclip(on, false) end
                end)
            end,
        },
        {
            name = "Auto Pickup",
            oneShot = false,
            gradient = { Color3.fromRGB(60, 220, 120), Color3.fromRGB(20, 120, 60) },
            get = function() return S._shortcutNearPickup == true end,
            apply = function(on)
                on = on == true
                S._shortcutNearPickup = on
                if S._shortcutNearPickupConn then
                    pcall(function() S._shortcutNearPickupConn:Disconnect() end)
                    S._shortcutNearPickupConn = nil
                end
                if not on then
                    return
                end
                local lastAt = 0
                S._shortcutNearPickupConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                    if not S._shortcutNearPickup then
                        return
                    end
                    local now = os.clock()
                    if now - lastAt < 0.25 then
                        return
                    end
                    lastAt = now
                    local root = FM.getRoot and FM.getRoot()
                    if not root then
                        return
                    end
                    local origin = root.Position
                    local free = FM.backpackFree and FM.backpackFree() or 8
                    -- Crystals near player (no farm preset filter — shortcut near grab)
                    if free > 0 and FM.pickupCandidates and FM.grabCrystal then
                        local list = FM.pickupCandidates(free, origin, function() return true end) or {}
                        for _, entry in ipairs(list) do
                            if entry.inst and entry.distance and entry.distance <= 40 then
                                pcall(FM.grabCrystal, entry.inst, entry.prompt)
                                break
                            elseif entry.inst then
                                local d = FM.surfaceDistance and FM.surfaceDistance(entry.inst, origin)
                                if d and d <= 40 then
                                    pcall(FM.grabCrystal, entry.inst, entry.prompt)
                                    break
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    -- Runes near player
                    if Mountain and Mountain.grabNear then
                        pcall(Mountain.grabNear, 35)
                    elseif Mountain and Mountain.grabRunes then
                        pcall(Mountain.grabRunes)
                    end
                end)
            end,
        },
        {
            name = "Sell All",
            oneShot = true,
            gradient = { Color3.fromRGB(255, 210, 60), Color3.fromRGB(160, 100, 20) },
            get = function() return false end,
            apply = function()
                if FM.doSell then FM.doSell() end
            end,
        },
        {
            name = "Hop Server",
            oneShot = true,
            gradient = { Color3.fromRGB(200, 100, 255), Color3.fromRGB(90, 40, 160) },
            get = function() return false end,
            apply = function()
                if FM.hopRandomServer then FM.hopRandomServer() end
            end,
        },
    }

    function FM.setShortcutPanel(enabled)
        enabled = enabled == true
        if not enabled then
            destroyShortcutPanel()
            return
        end
        if panelGui and panelGui.Parent then
            return
        end
        destroyShortcutPanel()

        local playerGui = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui") or LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui", 5)

        screenGui.ResetOnSpawn = false

        screenGui.DisplayOrder = 110

        screenGui.Parent = playerGui
        panelGui = screenGui
        S.shortcutPanelOpen = true

        local panel = Instance.new("Frame")

        panel.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5)
        panel.Position = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0.5, 0)
        panel.Size = UDim2.new(0, 200, 0, 0)
        panel.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(10, 18, 42)
        panel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        panel.BorderSizePixel = 0

            g.Parent = panel
        end

        local panelPadding = Instance.new("UIPadding")
        panelPadding.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 10)
        panelPadding.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 10)
        panelPadding.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 10)

        title.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(220, 230, 255)
        title.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        title.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold

        title.ZIndex = 2

        addStroke(selectAllBtn, 0.55)

        panel.ClipsDescendants = true

        local ROW_H = 40

        local function createToggleButton(index, config)
            local isOn = false
            pcall(function()
                isOn = config.get and config.get() or false
            end)

            local holder = Instance.new("Frame")
            holder.Name = "Toggle" .. index
            holder.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 40)

            icon.ZIndex = 4
            icon.Parent = bg

            local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")

            label.Position = UDim2.new(0, 34, 0.5, 0)
            label.Size = UDim2.new(1, -72, 1, 0)

            label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
            label.TextSize = 13
            label.ZIndex = 4
            label.Parent = bg

            local indicatorBg = Instance.new("Frame")
            indicatorBg.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5)
            indicatorBg.Position = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0.5, 0)
            indicatorBg.Size = UDim2.new(0, 22, 0, 22)
            indicatorBg.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            indicatorBg.BackgroundTransparency = isOn and 0.1 or 0.5
            indicatorBg.ZIndex = 4
            indicatorBg.Parent = bg
            addCorner(indicatorBg)

            local indicatorText = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            indicatorText.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
            indicatorText.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            indicatorText.Text = isOn and "✓" or "+"
            indicatorText.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            indicatorText.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
            indicatorText.TextSize = 16
            indicatorText.ZIndex = 5
            indicatorText.Parent = indicatorBg

            toggleFrames[index] = {
                bg = bg,
                icon = icon,
                label = label,
                indicatorBg = indicatorBg,
                indicatorText = indicatorText,
            }
            toggleStates[index] = { name = config.name, isOn = isOn, oneShot = config.oneShot == true }

            bg.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                local st = toggleStates[index]
                if st.oneShot then
                    updateToggleVisual(index, true, true)
                    pcall(config.apply, true)
                    task.delay(0.35, function()
                        if toggleStates[index] then
                            toggleStates[index].isOn = false
                            updateToggleVisual(index, false, true)
                        end
                    end)
                    return
                end
                st.isOn = not st.isOn
                updateToggleVisual(index, st.isOn, true)
                pcall(config.apply, st.isOn)
            end)
        end

        for i, config in ipairs(SHORTCUTS) do
            createToggleButton(i, config)
        end

        local allOn = false
        selectAllBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            allOn = not allOn
            for i, config in ipairs(SHORTCUTS) do
                local st = toggleStates[i]
                if st and not st.oneShot then
                    st.isOn = allOn
                    updateToggleVisual(i, allOn, true)
                    pcall(config.apply, allOn)
                end
            end
            selectAllBtn.Text = allOn and "Deselect all" or "Select all"
        end)

        local panelH = 34 + listH + 20
        tween(panel, {
            Size = UDim2.new(0, 200, 0, panelH),
            BackgroundTransparency = 0.08,
        }, 0.3)
    end

    task.defer(function()
        if not Tabs or not Tabs.button then
            return
        end
        if S._buttonTabBuilt then
            return
        end
        S._buttonTabBuilt = true

        local Box = Tabs.button
        pcall(function()
            Box:Section({ Title = "Shortcut Panel", TextSize = 17 })
        end)

        Box:Toggle({
            Title = "Show Shortcut Panel",
            Desc = "Shortcuts: Dig, Fly+Noclip, Pickup, Sell, Hop",
            Value = false,
            Callback = function(value)
                pcall(function()
                    FM.setShortcutPanel(value == true)
                end)
            end,
        })

        pcall(function()
            Box:Section({ Title = "Pickup Button", TextSize = 17 })
        end)

        Box:Toggle({
            Title = "Pickup Button GUI",
            Desc = "Studs Pick Up (teal). 1 nearest crystal, ignores filters",
            Value = false,
            Callback = function(value)
                pcall(function()
                    FM.setPickupButtonGui(value == true)
                end)
            end,
        })

        Box:Toggle({
            Title = "Lock Pickup Button",
            Desc = "Lock = cannot drag. Unlock = can drag (L/U on button)",
            Value = false,
            Callback = function(value)
                S.pickupBtnLocked = value == true
                pcall(function()
                    local gui = S._pickupBtnGui
                    if not gui then return end
                    local lockBtn = gui:FindFirstChild("StudButton") and gui.StudButton:FindFirstChild("Lock")
                    if lockBtn then
                        lockBtn.Text = S.pickupBtnLocked and "L" or "U"
                        lockBtn.TextColor3 = S.pickupBtnLocked and Color3.fromRGB(255, 200, 60) or Color3.fromRGB(160, 255, 240)
                    end
                end)
            end,
        })
    end)
end

    local VEIN_PRESETS = {

            distColor = Color3.fromRGB(220, 180, 255),
            beamA = Color3.fromRGB(180, 255, 255),

        },

            kind = "bomb_all",

        }
    end
