    for _, p in ipairs(VEIN_PRESETS) do
        PRESET_NAMES[#PRESET_NAMES + 1] = p.name
    end

    local markers = {}
    local pulseConn = nil
    local active = false
    local selectedName = "Aetherstone Vein"
    local scanRadius = 80
    local maxMarkers = 8
    local minCluster = 3
    local voxelRes = 4
    local scanInterval = 0.85
    local lastScanPos = nil
    local lastScanAt = 0
    local MAX_HITS = 180

    local function getPreset(name)
        for _, p in ipairs(VEIN_PRESETS) do
            if p.name == name then
                return p
            end
        end
        return VEIN_PRESETS[1]
    end

    local function clearMarkers()
        for i, m in pairs(markers) do
            pcall(function()
                if m.beam then m.beam:Destroy() end
                if m.beamA then m.beamA:Destroy() end
                if m.beamB then m.beamB:Destroy() end
                if m.part then m.part:Destroy() end
            end)
            markers[i] = nil
        end
    end

    local function sizeLabel(count)
        if count >= 60 then return "Massive" end
        if count >= 30 then return "Large" end
        if count >= 14 then return "Medium" end
        return "Small"
    end

    local function makeMarker(preset)
        preset = preset or getPreset(selectedName)
        local part = Instance.new("Part")
        part.Name = "FayyMengVeinAnchor"
        part.Anchored = true
        part.CanCollide = false
        part.CanQuery = false
        part.CanTouch = false
        part.Transparency = 1
        part.Size = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)
        part.Parent = Workspace

        local bb = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
        bb.Name = "FayyMengVeinBB"
        bb.Adornee = part
        bb.AlwaysOnTop = true
        bb.Size = UDim2.fromScale(6.5, 2.6)
        bb.MaxDistance = 210
        bb.Parent = part

        local layout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
        layout.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Vertical
        layout.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center
        layout.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center
        layout.Parent = bb

        local title = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        title.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        title.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 0.58)
        title.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
        title.TextScaled = true
        title.Text = string.upper(preset.name)
        title.TextColor3 = preset.color
        title.LayoutOrder = 1
        title.Parent = bb
        local ts = Instance.new("UIStroke")
        ts.Thickness = 2
        ts.Color = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
        ts.Transparency = 0.25
        ts.Parent = title
        local tc = Instance.new("UITextSizeConstraint")
        tc.MaxTextSize = 22
        tc.MinTextSize = 9
        tc.Parent = title

        local dist = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        dist.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        dist.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 0.42)
        dist.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        dist.TextScaled = true
        dist.TextColor3 = preset.distColor or Color3.fromRGB(235, 225, 215)
        dist.LayoutOrder = 2
        dist.Parent = bb
        local ds = Instance.new("UIStroke")
        ds.Thickness = 2
        ds.Color = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
        ds.Transparency = 0.25
        ds.Parent = dist
        local dc = Instance.new("UITextSizeConstraint")
        dc.MaxTextSize = 16
        dc.MinTextSize = 7
        dc.Parent = dist

        return { part = part, bb = bb, title = title, distLbl = dist, beam = nil, beamA = nil, beamB = nil }
    end

    local function ensureBeam(marker, color)
        local char = LocalPlayer.Character
        local hand = char and (char:FindFirstChild("RadarLeftHand") or char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"))
        if not (hand and hand:IsA("BasePart")) then
            if marker.beam then
                pcall(function() marker.beam:Destroy() end)
                marker.beam = nil
            end
            return
        end
        color = color or Color3.fromRGB(255, 170, 70)
        if marker.beam and marker.beam.Parent then
            if marker.beamA and marker.beamA.Parent ~= hand then
                marker.beamA.Parent = hand
            end
            marker.beam.Parent = hand
            marker.beam.Color = ColorSequence.new(color, color)
            return
        end
        marker.beamA = Instance.new("Attachment")
        marker.beamA.Parent = hand
        marker.beamB = Instance.new("Attachment")
        marker.beamB.Parent = marker.part
        local beam = Instance.new("Beam")
        beam.Attachment0 = marker.beamA
        beam.Attachment1 = marker.beamB
        beam.Color = ColorSequence.new(color, color)
        beam.LightEmission = 1
        beam.LightInfluence = 0
        beam.FaceCamera = true
        beam.Width0 = 0.4
        beam.Width1 = 0.18
        beam.CurveSize0 = 3
        beam.CurveSize1 = -3
        beam.Segments = 12
        beam.TextureSpeed = 2.2
        beam.TextureLength = 4
        beam.Parent = hand
        marker.beam = beam
    end

    local function isBombMat(mat)
        if BombMats and type(BombMats.isBombMaterial) == "function" then
            local ok, res = pcall(BombMats.isBombMaterial, mat)
            return ok and res == true
        end
        return false
    end

    local function bombInfo(mat)
        if BombMats and type(BombMats.infoFor) == "function" then
            local ok, info = pcall(BombMats.infoFor, mat)
            if ok then return info end
        end
        return nil
    end

    local function clusterPositions(positions, gap)
        gap = gap or 16
        local clusters = {}
        for _, pos in ipairs(positions) do
            local best, bestD = nil, gap
            for _, c in ipairs(clusters) do
                local d = ((c.sum / c.count) - pos).Magnitude
                if d < bestD then
                    best, bestD = c, d
                end
            end
            if best then
                best.sum = best.sum + pos
                best.count = best.count + 1
            else
                clusters[#clusters + 1] = { sum = pos, count = 1 }
            end
        end
        local out = {}
        for _, c in ipairs(clusters) do
            if c.count >= minCluster then
                out[#out + 1] = { pos = c.sum / c.count, count = c.count }
            end
        end
        return out
    end

    local function findDeposits(origin, preset)
        local half = scanRadius
        local region = Region3.new(
            origin - Vector3.new(half, half, half),
            origin + Vector3.new(half, half, half)
        ):ExpandToGrid(voxelRes)
        local ok, mats = pcall(function()
            local m, _ = Terrain:ReadVoxels(region, voxelRes)
            return m
        end)
        if not ok or not mats then
            mats = nil
        end
        local corner = region and (region.CFrame.Position - region.Size * 0.5) or origin

        if preset.kind == "prismarite" then
            local targets = {
                Enum.Material.WoodPlanks,
                Enum.Material.Glass, 
                Enum.Material.Ice,
                Enum.Material.Salt,
                Enum.Material.Marble,
                Enum.Material.Limestone, 
            }
            if type(S._prismariteMaterials) == "table" then
                targets = S._prismariteMaterials
            end
            if not mats then
                return {}
            end
            local hitsBy = {}
            for _, target in ipairs(targets) do
                hitsBy[target] = {}
            end
            local total = 0
            for x = 1, #mats do
                local yx = mats[x]
                for y = 1, #yx do
                    local zy = yx[y]
                    for z = 1, #zy do
                        local mat = zy[z]
                        local bucket = hitsBy[mat]
                        if bucket then
                            bucket[#bucket + 1] = corner + Vector3.new(
                                (x - 0.5) * voxelRes,
                                (y - 0.5) * voxelRes,
                                (z - 0.5) * voxelRes
                            )
                            total += 1
                            if total >= MAX_HITS then break end
                        end
                    end
                    if total >= MAX_HITS then break end
                end
                if total >= MAX_HITS then break end
            end
            local out = {}
            for mat, hits in pairs(hitsBy) do
                if #hits > 0 then
                    for _, c in ipairs(clusterPositions(hits, 14)) do
                        out[#out + 1] = {
                            pos = c.pos,
                            count = c.count,
                            label = "Prismarite Vein",
                            color = preset.color,
                        }
                    end
                end
            end
            return out
        end

        if not mats then
            return {}
        end

        if preset.kind == "terrain" then
            local hits = {}
            local target = preset.material
            local nx, done = #mats, false
            for x = 1, nx do
                local yx = mats[x]
                local ny = #yx
                for y = 1, ny do
                    local zy = yx[y]
                    local nz = #zy
                    for z = 1, nz do
                        if zy[z] == target then
                            hits[#hits + 1] = corner + Vector3.new(
                                (x - 0.5) * voxelRes,
                                (y - 0.5) * voxelRes,
                                (z - 0.5) * voxelRes
                            )
                            if #hits >= MAX_HITS then
                                done = true
                                break
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    if done then break end
                end
                if done then break end
            end
            local clusters = clusterPositions(hits, 14)
            for _, c in ipairs(clusters) do
                c.label = preset.name
                c.color = preset.color
            end
            return clusters
        end

        local byMat = {}
        local matCache = {} 
        local totalHits = 0
        local nx = #mats
        for x = 1, nx do
            local yx = mats[x]
            local ny = #yx
            for y = 1, ny do
                local zy = yx[y]
                local nz = #zy
                for z = 1, nz do
                    local mat = zy[z]
                    if mat and mat ~= Enum.Material.Air and mat ~= Enum.Material.Water then
                        local cached = matCache[mat]
                        if cached == nil then
                            if isBombMat(mat) then
                                local info = bombInfo(mat)
                                local matName = (info and (info.matName or info.name)) or "Bomb Vein"
                                cached = { name = matName }
                            else
                                cached = false
                            end
                            matCache[mat] = cached
                        end
                        if cached then
                            local matName = cached.name
                            if preset.kind == "bomb_all" or matName == preset.bombName or matName == preset.name then
                                local list = byMat[mat]
                                if not list then
                                    list = { _name = matName }
                                    byMat[mat] = list
                                end
                                list[#list + 1] = corner + Vector3.new(
                                    (x - 0.5) * voxelRes,
                                    (y - 0.5) * voxelRes,
                                    (z - 0.5) * voxelRes
                                )
                                totalHits += 1
                                if totalHits >= MAX_HITS then
                                    break
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
                if totalHits >= MAX_HITS then break end
            end
            if totalHits >= MAX_HITS then break end
        end

        local out = {}
        for mat, hits in pairs(byMat) do
            local name = hits._name or "Bomb Vein"
            hits._name = nil
            local clusters = clusterPositions(hits, 16)
            local col = BOMB_COLORS[name] or preset.color or Color3.fromRGB(255, 170, 70)
            for _, c in ipairs(clusters) do
                c.label = name
                c.color = col
                out[#out + 1] = c
            end
        end
        return out
    end

    local function scanOnce()
        local root = FM.getRoot and FM.getRoot() or nil
        if not root then
            clearMarkers()
            S._veinDeposits = {}
            return
        end
        local preset = getPreset(selectedName)
        local origin = root.Position
        local deposits = findDeposits(origin, preset)
        table.sort(deposits, function(a, b)
            return (a.pos - origin).Magnitude < (b.pos - origin).Magnitude
        end)
        S._veinDeposits = deposits
        local n = math.min(#deposits, maxMarkers)
        for i in pairs(markers) do
            if i > n then
                local m = markers[i]
                if m then
                    pcall(function()
                        if m.beam then m.beam:Destroy() end
                        if m.beamA then m.beamA:Destroy() end
                        if m.beamB then m.beamB:Destroy() end
                        if m.part then m.part:Destroy() end
                    end)
                    markers[i] = nil
                end
            end
        end
        for i = 1, n do
            local d = deposits[i]
            local col = d.color or preset.color
            local label = d.label or preset.name
            local m = markers[i]
            if not m then
                m = makeMarker(preset)
                markers[i] = m
            end
            m.part.Position = d.pos
            if m.title then
                m.title.Text = string.upper(label)
                m.title.TextColor3 = col
            end
            if m.distLbl then
                local dist = math.floor((d.pos - origin).Magnitude + 0.5)
                if preset.kind == "bomb" or preset.kind == "bomb_all" then
                    m.distLbl.Text = string.format("%s  ·  %dm away", sizeLabel(d.count), dist)
                else
                    m.distLbl.Text = string.format("%dm away", dist)
                end
                m.distLbl.TextColor3 = col
            end
            if i <= 3 then
                ensureBeam(m, col)
            elseif m.beam then
                pcall(function()
                    m.beam:Destroy()
                    if m.beamA then m.beamA:Destroy() end
                    if m.beamB then m.beamB:Destroy() end
                end)
                m.beam, m.beamA, m.beamB = nil, nil, nil
            end
        end
    end

    function FM.setVeinScanner(on)
        active = on == true
        S.veinScannerActive = active
        if pulseConn then
            pcall(function() pulseConn:Disconnect() end)
            pulseConn = nil
        end
        if not active then
            clearMarkers()
            return
        end
        task.spawn(function()
            while active do
                local root = FM.getRoot and FM.getRoot() or nil
                local now = os.clock()
                local should = true
                if root and lastScanPos and (now - lastScanAt) < scanInterval then
                    if (root.Position - lastScanPos).Magnitude < 12 then
                        should = false
                    end
                end
                if should then
                    pcall(scanOnce)
                    if root then
                        lastScanPos = root.Position
                        lastScanAt = now
                    end
                end
                task.wait(scanInterval)
            end
        end)
        local pulseAcc = 0
        pulseConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
            if not active then return end
            pulseAcc += dt
            if pulseAcc < 0.16 then return end
            pulseAcc = 0
            local t = os.clock() * 3
            local pulse = math.sin(t) * 0.25 + 1
            for _, m in pairs(markers) do
                if m.beam then
                    pcall(function()
                        m.beam.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
                            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.85),
                            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.15, 0.4 - (pulse - 1) * 0.12),
                            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.85, 0.5 - (pulse - 1) * 0.12),
                            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1),
                        })
                    end)
                end
            end
        end)
    end

    
    function FM.probePrismariteModules()
        local hits = {}
        local function scan(root, depth)
            if depth > 6 or not root then return end
            for _, ch in ipairs(root:GetChildren()) do
                local n = string.lower(ch.Name or "")
                if n:find("prism", 1, true) or n:find("rarityzone", 1, true) or n:find("aether", 1, true) then
                    hits[#hits + 1] = ch:GetFullName()
                end
                pcall(scan, ch, depth + 1)
            end
        end
        pcall(scan, ReplicatedStorage, 0)
        pcall(scan, game:GetService("StarterPlayer"), 0)
        pcall(scan, Workspace, 0)
        return hits
    end

    function FM.setVeinType(name)
        S._veinTypeName = tostring(name or "")
        selectedName = tostring(name or "Aetherstone Vein")
        clearMarkers()
        if active or S.veinFarmActive then
            pcall(scanOnce)
        end
    end

    local veinFarmConn = nil
    local veinDigClock = 0
    local veinMoveAt = 0
    local veinPickClock = 0

    
    local SKIP_VEIN_SEC = 3
    local SKIP_COOLDOWN = 12       
    local SKIP_MATCH_DIST = 14    
    local stuckVeins = {}         
    local digSession = nil        
    local skipGen = 0             
    local veinLastPrune = 0

    local function veinKey(pos)
        if typeof(pos) ~= "Vector3" then
            return nil
        end
        
        local q = 8
        return string.format("%d:%d:%d", math.floor(pos.X / q + 0.5), math.floor(pos.Y / q + 0.5), math.floor(pos.Z / q + 0.5))
    end

    local function pruneStuck(now)
        now = now or os.clock()
        for k, exp in pairs(stuckVeins) do
            if type(exp) ~= "number" or exp <= now then
                stuckVeins[k] = nil
            end
        end
    end

    local function isStuckKey(key, now)
        if not key then
            return false
        end
        local exp = stuckVeins[key]
        return type(exp) == "number" and exp > (now or os.clock())
    end

    local function markStuck(key, now)
        if not key then
            return
        end
        now = now or os.clock()
        stuckVeins[key] = now + SKIP_COOLDOWN
    end

    local function depositStillPresent(pos, deposits)
        if typeof(pos) ~= "Vector3" or type(deposits) ~= "table" then
            return false
        end
        for _, d in ipairs(deposits) do
            if d and typeof(d.pos) == "Vector3" then
                if (d.pos - pos).Magnitude <= SKIP_MATCH_DIST then
                    return true, d
                end
            end
        end
        return false, nil
    end

    local function abortVeinTarget(reason)
        
        digSession = nil
        S._veinFarmTarget = nil
        veinDigClock = 0
        if reason then
            
            S._veinFarmStatus = reason
        end
    end

    
    local veinDigParams = RaycastParams.new()
    veinDigParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Include
    veinDigParams.IgnoreWater = true

    local function refreshVeinDigFilter()
        local list = { Workspace.Terrain }
        local decorations = Workspace:FindFirstChild("MountainDecorations")
        local boulders = decorations and decorations:FindFirstChild("Boulders")
        if boulders then
            list[#list + 1] = boulders
        end
        local test = Workspace:FindFirstChild("BoulderTest")
        if test then
            list[#list + 1] = test
        end
        veinDigParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = list
    end

    local function veinIsDigTool(tool)
        if not tool or not tool:IsA("Tool") then
            return false
        end
        local n = tostring(tool.Name or "")
        if tool:GetAttribute("DigPower") ~= nil then
            return true
        end
        if n:find("Pick", 1, true) or n:find("Axe", 1, true) or n == "Sledge Hammer" then
            return true
        end
        if tool:GetAttribute("BitSpinAxis") ~= nil or n:find("Drill", 1, true) then
            return true
        end
        return false
    end

    local function veinGetPick()
        
        if Farm and type(Farm.equipPick) == "function" then
            local ok, pick = pcall(Farm.equipPick)
            if ok and pick and veinIsDigTool(pick) then
                return pick
            end
        end
        local char = LocalPlayer.Character
        if not char then
            return nil
        end
        for _, tool in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
            if veinIsDigTool(tool) then
                return tool
            end
        end
        local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local bp = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
        if bp and hum then
            for _, tool in ipairs(bp:GetChildren()) do
                if veinIsDigTool(tool) then
                    pcall(function()
                        hum:EquipTool(tool)
                    end)
                    return tool
                end
            end
        end
        return nil
    end

    local function veinAimPoint(pick, veinPos, root)
        
        refreshVeinDigFilter()
        local maxReach = 14
        if pick then
            local override = tonumber(FM.getAttr and FM.getAttr(pick, "OverrideMaxReach"))
            maxReach = (override or 10) + 4
        end
        local origin = root.Position + Vector3.new(0, 1.5, 0)
        local delta = veinPos - origin
        local dist = delta.Magnitude
        if dist < 0.05 then
            return veinPos
        end
        local dir = delta.Unit
        local hit = Workspace:Raycast(origin, dir * math.min(dist + 2, maxReach), veinDigParams)
        if hit then
            return hit.Position
        end
        
        if dist <= maxReach then
            return veinPos
        end
        return origin + dir * (maxReach - 0.5)
    end

    local function veinSwing(pick, veinPos, root)
        
        if not pick or type(pick.Name) ~= "string" or pick.Name == "" then
            return false
        end
        if typeof(veinPos) ~= "Vector3" or not root then
            return false
        end
        local aim = veinAimPoint(pick, veinPos, root)
        if typeof(aim) ~= "Vector3" then
            return false
        end
        if FM.isInNoDigZone and FM.isInNoDigZone(aim) then
            return false
        end
        local digger = FM.fireDig
        if type(digger) ~= "function" then
            return false
        end

        local toVein = veinPos - root.Position
        local look = toVein.Magnitude > 0.05 and toVein.Unit or (Workspace.CurrentCamera and Workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame.LookVector or root.CFrame.LookVector)
        local right = look:Cross(Vector3.new(0, 1, 0))
        if right.Magnitude < 0.05 then
            right = root.CFrame.RightVector
        else
            right = right.Unit
        end

        local fired = digger(pick.Name, aim)
        digger(pick.Name, aim - Vector3.new(0, 1.2, 0))
        digger(pick.Name, aim + look * 1.5)
        digger(pick.Name, aim - look * 1.2)
        digger(pick.Name, aim + right * 1.4)
        digger(pick.Name, aim - right * 1.4)
        digger(pick.Name, aim + Vector3.new(0, 1.5, 0))
        digger(pick.Name, aim - Vector3.new(0, 2.4, 0))
        digger(pick.Name, veinPos)
        digger(pick.Name, veinPos - Vector3.new(0, 1.5, 0))

        refreshVeinDigFilter()
        local origin = root.Position + Vector3.new(0, 1.2, 0)
        local maxReach = 12
        local dirs = {
            look, -look, right, -right,
            Vector3.new(0, -1, 0), Vector3.new(0, 1, 0),
            (look + right), (look - right), (-look + right), (-look - right),
            (look + Vector3.new(0, 0.7, 0)), (look + Vector3.new(0, -0.7, 0)),
        }
        for i = 1, #dirs do
            local d = dirs[i]
            if typeof(d) == "Vector3" and d.Magnitude > 0.01 then
                local u = d.Unit
                local hit = Workspace:Raycast(origin, u * maxReach, veinDigParams)
                if hit then
                    digger(pick.Name, hit.Position)
                else
                    digger(pick.Name, origin + u * 6)
                end
            end
        end
        return fired == true
    end

    local function veinSwingNeed(pick)
        
        local mult = tonumber(S.digSpeedMult) or 0.4
        mult = math.clamp(mult, 0.08, 1)
        local baseGap = 0.04
        if Farm and type(Farm.swingGap) == "function" then
            local okGap, gap = pcall(Farm.swingGap, pick)
            if okGap and type(gap) == "number" and gap > 0 then
                baseGap = gap
            end
        end
        return math.max(0.03, baseGap * mult)
    end

    local function pickNearestDeposit(origin, deposits, now)
        local best, bestD = nil, 1e12
        for _, d in ipairs(deposits) do
            if d and typeof(d.pos) == "Vector3" then
                local key = veinKey(d.pos)
                if not isStuckKey(key, now) then
                    local dist = (d.pos - origin).Magnitude
                    if dist < bestD then
                        best, bestD = d, dist
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        return best, bestD
    end

    local LOOT_RANGE = 42
    local prismOrbitIdx = 0
    local prismOrbitAt = 0
    local prismOrbitWaypoints = nil
    local prismOrbitBuiltAt = 0

    local function getMountainOrbitParams()
        local cx = Workspace:GetAttribute("MountainCenterX")
        local cz = Workspace:GetAttribute("MountainCenterZ")
        local peakY = Workspace:GetAttribute("MountainPeakY")
        local baseY = Workspace:GetAttribute("MountainBaseY")
        local radius = Workspace:GetAttribute("MountainRadius")
        if typeof(cx) ~= "number" or typeof(cz) ~= "number" then
            local spot = FM.mountainSpot and FM.mountainSpot()
            if spot then
                cx, cz = spot.X, spot.Z
            else
                local root = FM.getRoot and FM.getRoot()
                if root then
                    cx, cz = root.Position.X, root.Position.Z
                else
                    cx, cz = 0, 0
                end
            end
        end
        if typeof(radius) ~= "number" or radius < 40 then
            radius = 180
        end
        if typeof(baseY) ~= "number" then
            baseY = 0
        end
        if typeof(peakY) ~= "number" then
            peakY = baseY + 900
        end
        return cx, cz, baseY, peakY, radius
    end

    local function buildPrismOrbitWaypoints()
        local cx, cz, baseY, peakY, radius = getMountainOrbitParams()
        local points = {}
        
        local rayParams = RaycastParams.new()
        rayParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
        local char = LocalPlayer.Character
        if char then
            rayParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = { char }
        end
        rayParams.IgnoreWater = true

        local function snapSurface(x, z)
            local top = (typeof(peakY) == "number" and peakY or (baseY + 600)) + 250
            local origin = Vector3.new(x, top, z)
            local hit = Workspace:Raycast(origin, Vector3.new(0, -(top - baseY + 500), 0), rayParams)
            if hit and hit.Position then
                local y = hit.Position.Y + 4.5
                
                local maxY = (typeof(baseY) == "number" and baseY or 0) + 350
                if y > maxY then
                    y = maxY
                end
                return Vector3.new(hit.Position.X, y, hit.Position.Z)
            end
            
            return Vector3.new(x, (typeof(baseY) == "number" and baseY or 0) + 12, z)
        end

        
        local ringFracs = { 0.12, 0.22, 0.34, 0.46, 0.58 }
        local steps = 8
        for ri, rf in ipairs(ringFracs) do
            local r = math.max(25, radius * rf)
            local phase = (ri - 1) * 0.4
            for s = 0, steps - 1 do
                local ang = phase + (s / steps) * math.pi * 2
                local x = cx + math.cos(ang) * r
                local z = cz + math.sin(ang) * r
                points[#points + 1] = snapSurface(x, z)
            end
        end
        return points
    end

    local function isPrismariteVeinSelected()
        local name = tostring(S._veinTypeName or S.veinType or "")
        local lower = string.lower(name)
        if lower:find("prism", 1, true) then
            return true
        end
        local preset = nil
        pcall(function()
            if type(currentPreset) == "function" then
                preset = currentPreset()
            end
        end)
        if type(preset) == "table" and tostring(preset.kind or ""):lower():find("prism", 1, true) then
            return true
        end
        return false
    end

    local LOOT_STABLE = 0.85  
    local lootPhase = false
    local lootEmptySince = 0

    local function filterPickupEnabled()
        return S.pickupValueActive == true
            or S.pickupLuckActive == true
            or S.pickupRarityActive == true
    end

    local function countFilteredNear(origin)
        if not filterPickupEnabled() then
            return 0
        end
        if typeof(origin) ~= "Vector3" then
            return 0
        end
        local free = FM.backpackFree and FM.backpackFree() or 1
        if free and free <= 0 then
            free = 1 
        end
        local n = 0
        pcall(function()
            if FM.pickupCandidates and FM.valueLuckFilter then
                local list = FM.pickupCandidates(free, origin, FM.valueLuckFilter)
                if type(list) == "table" then
                    n = #list
                end
            end
        end)
        return n
    end

    local function doFilteredPickup(origin)
        if not filterPickupEnabled() then
            return false
        end
        local free = FM.backpackFree and FM.backpackFree() or 1
        if not free or free <= 0 then
            return false
        end
        local grabbed = false
        pcall(function()
            if FM.tryFilteredCrystalPickup then
                grabbed = FM.tryFilteredCrystalPickup(origin, free) == true
            elseif FM.pickupStep and FM.valueLuckFilter then
                FM.pickupStep(FM.valueLuckFilter)
                grabbed = true
            end
        end)
        if grabbed then
            pcall(function()
                if FM.markPickupBusy then
                    FM.markPickupBusy(0.45)
                end
            end)
        end
        return grabbed
    end

    function FM.setVeinFarm(on)
        if type(on) == "table" then
            on = on.CurrentValue or on[1] or on.Value
        end
        if on == true and (not FM.isWorldReady or not FM.isWorldReady()) then
            S.veinFarmActive = false
            FM.ensureWorldReadyThen(function()
                FM.setVeinFarm(true)
            end)
            return
        end
        S.veinFarmActive = on == true
        veinDigClock = 0
        veinMoveAt = 0
        veinPickClock = 0
        S._veinFarmTarget = nil
        digSession = nil
        lootPhase = false
        lootEmptySince = 0
        S._veinLootPhase = false
        skipGen += 1
        prismOrbitIdx = 1
        prismOrbitAt = 0
        prismOrbitWaypoints = nil
        prismOrbitBuiltAt = 0
        if veinFarmConn then
            pcall(function() veinFarmConn:Disconnect() end)
            veinFarmConn = nil
        end
        if not S.veinFarmActive then
            S._slowMove = false
            digSession = nil
            lootPhase = false
            lootEmptySince = 0
            S._veinLootPhase = false
            S._veinFarmTarget = nil
            S._pickupBusyUntil = 0

            local prevFly = S._veinPrevFly == true
            local prevNoclip = S._veinPrevNoclip == true
            S._veinPrevFly = nil
            S._veinPrevNoclip = nil

            pcall(function()
                if MovementOwner and MovementOwner.Release then
                    MovementOwner:Release("AutoVein")
                end
            end)

            -- Always stop vein movement helpers
            pcall(function()
                if Move and Move.glideStop then Move.glideStop() end
            end)
            pcall(function()
                local char = LocalPlayer.Character
                local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if hum then
                    hum.PlatformStand = false
                    pcall(function() hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp) end)
                end
            end)

            -- Fly: restore previous preference; if none, force OFF (vein always ends fly)
            pcall(function()
                if Move and Move.setFly then
                    Move.setFly(prevFly == true)
                end
            end)

            -- NoClip via ownership; force full off if nobody else owns movement
            pcall(function()
                if FM.ownedSetNoclip then
                    FM.ownedSetNoclip("AutoVein", false, false)
                end
            end)
            pcall(function()
                if MovementOwner and not MovementOwner:HasAny() then
                    if Move and Move.setNoclip then
                        Move.setNoclip(false, false)
                    end
                end
            end)
            return
        end

        S._filterCandCache = nil
        S._slowMove = true
        pruneStuck(os.clock())

        
        S._veinPrevFly = S._flyWanted == true
        S._veinPrevNoclip = S._noclipWanted == true
        
        -- ROOT-CAUSE FIX: acquire movement ownership before touching noclip/fly,
        -- and go through the gate so this doesn't silently override AutoBoulder
        -- or AutoMoney if either is currently active and owns movement.
        pcall(function()
            if MovementOwner and MovementOwner.Acquire then
                MovementOwner:Acquire("AutoVein")
            end
        end)
        pcall(function()
            FM.ownedSetNoclip("AutoVein", true, false)
            FM.ownedSetFly("AutoVein", true)
        end)

        if not active then
            pcall(function()
                FM.setVeinScanner(true)
            end)
        end

        veinFarmConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
            if not S.veinFarmActive then
                return
            end
            local root = FM.getRoot and FM.getRoot() or nil
            if not root then
                return
            end
            local now = os.clock()
            dt = dt or 0.016

            
            
            pcall(function()
                local v = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
                if v.Y < -2 or v.Y > 12 then
                    root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(v.X, 0, v.Z)
                end
            end)

            
            if S.farmActive or S.miningBoulder then
                return
            end

            
            if not veinLastPrune or (now - veinLastPrune) >= 4 then
                veinLastPrune = now
                pruneStuck(now)
            end

            
            
            
            
            local origin = root.Position
            local filterOn = filterPickupEnabled()
            if filterOn then
                veinPickClock = (veinPickClock or 0) + dt
                local tickRate = ((S.pickupMode or "Legit") == "Burst") and 0.12 or 0.28
                if veinPickClock >= tickRate then
                    veinPickClock = 0
                    doFilteredPickup(origin)
                end

                local near = countFilteredNear(origin)
                if near > 0 then
                    lootPhase = true
                    lootEmptySince = 0
                    S._veinLootPhase = true
                    S._veinFarmStatus = string.format("Pickup %d crystal(s)", near)
                elseif lootPhase then
                    if lootEmptySince == 0 then
                        lootEmptySince = now
                    elseif (now - lootEmptySince) >= LOOT_STABLE then
                        lootPhase = false
                        lootEmptySince = 0
                        S._veinLootPhase = false
                        S._veinFarmStatus = "Loot done — next vein"
                    else
                        S._veinLootPhase = true
                    end
                else
                    S._veinLootPhase = false
                end
            else
                lootPhase = false
                lootEmptySince = 0
                S._veinLootPhase = false
            end

            
            if lootPhase or S._veinLootPhase then
                return
            end

            local deposits = S._veinDeposits
            if type(deposits) ~= "table" or #deposits == 0 then
                if (now - (lastScanAt or 0)) > 1.0 then
                    pcall(scanOnce)
                    lastScanAt = now
                end
                
                if isPrismariteVeinSelected and isPrismariteVeinSelected() then
                    if not prismOrbitWaypoints or (now - (prismOrbitBuiltAt or 0)) > 20 then
                        local list = {}
                        pcall(function()
                            if FM.listBoulderPositions then
                                list = FM.listBoulderPositions() or {}
                            end
                        end)
                        if type(list) ~= "table" or #list == 0 then
                            
                            list = buildPrismOrbitWaypoints() or {}
                        end
                        prismOrbitWaypoints = list
                        prismOrbitBuiltAt = now
                        if not prismOrbitIdx or prismOrbitIdx < 1 then
                            prismOrbitIdx = 1
                        end
                    end
                    local wps = prismOrbitWaypoints
                    if type(wps) == "table" and #wps > 0 then
                        if prismOrbitIdx > #wps then
                            prismOrbitIdx = 1
                            prismOrbitBuiltAt = 0 
                        end
                        local wp = wps[prismOrbitIdx]
                        if typeof(wp) == "Vector3" then
                            local dist = (wp - origin).Magnitude
                            S._veinFarmStatus = string.format("Prism boulder %d/%d", prismOrbitIdx, #wps)
                            if dist > 14 then
                                if now - (prismOrbitAt or 0) > 1.35 then
                                    prismOrbitAt = now
                                    pcall(function()
                                        S._slowMove = true
                                        local stand = wp + Vector3.new(0, 4, 0)
                                        if FM.moveTo then
                                            FM.moveTo(stand, wp)
                                        elseif FM.travelTo then
                                            FM.travelTo(stand, wp)
                                        end
                                    end)
                                end
                            else
                                pcall(scanOnce)
                                lastScanAt = now
                                
                                local deps = S._veinDeposits
                                if type(deps) == "table" and #deps > 0 then
                                    S._veinFarmStatus = "Vein found near boulder"
                                    return
                                end
                                prismOrbitIdx = prismOrbitIdx + 1
                            end
                        else
                            prismOrbitIdx = prismOrbitIdx + 1
                        end
                    end
                end
                return
            end

            
            
            
            local skipOn = S.skipVeinActive ~= false
            local skipSec = tonumber(S.skipVeinSeconds) or SKIP_VEIN_SEC
            if skipSec < 1 then skipSec = 3 end

            if digSession and digSession.pos then
                local sess = digSession
                local still, refreshed = depositStillPresent(sess.pos, deposits)

                if not still then
                    
                    digSession = nil
                    S._veinFarmTarget = nil
                    S._veinFarmStatus = "Vein cleared — pickup"
                    if filterPickupEnabled() then
                        lootPhase = true
                        lootEmptySince = 0
                        S._veinLootPhase = true
                    end
                    
                elseif skipOn and sess.digStartedAt and (now - sess.digStartedAt) >= skipSec then
                    
                    local myGen = sess.gen
                    markStuck(sess.key, now)
                    abortVeinTarget("Skip stuck vein")
                    if filterPickupEnabled() then
                        lootPhase = true
                        lootEmptySince = 0
                        S._veinLootPhase = true
                        S._veinFarmStatus = "Skipped — pickup first"
                    end
                    if myGen ~= skipGen then
                        return
                    end
                    
                else
                    
                    if refreshed then
                        S._veinFarmTarget = refreshed
                        sess.pos = refreshed.pos
                        sess.key = veinKey(refreshed.pos) or sess.key
                    end
                end
            end

            local origin = root.Position
            local best, bestD

            
            if digSession and digSession.pos and S._veinFarmTarget then
                best = S._veinFarmTarget
                bestD = (best.pos - origin).Magnitude
            else
                best, bestD = pickNearestDeposit(origin, deposits, now)
                if not best then
                    
                    if (now - (lastScanAt or 0)) > 1.5 then
                        pcall(scanOnce)
                    end
                    return
                end
                
                local key = veinKey(best.pos)
                skipGen += 1
                digSession = {
                    key = key,
                    pos = best.pos,
                    gen = skipGen,
                    digStartedAt = nil, 
                    lastDigAt = nil,
                }
                S._veinFarmTarget = best
            end

            if not best or typeof(best.pos) ~= "Vector3" then
                return
            end

            local stand = best.pos + Vector3.new(0, 3.2, 0)
            if bestD > 7 then
                if now - veinMoveAt > 1.25 then
                    veinMoveAt = now
                    pcall(function()
                        S._slowMove = true
                        if FM.moveTo then
                            FM.moveTo(stand, best.pos)
                        elseif FM.travelTo then
                            FM.travelTo(stand, best.pos)
                        end
                    end)
                end
                if bestD > 45 then
                    return
                end
            else
                pcall(function()
                    root.CFrame = CFrame.new(root.Position, best.pos)
                    root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                end)
            end

            local pick = veinGetPick()
            if not pick then
                return
            end

            
            local swingNeed = veinSwingNeed(pick)
            veinDigClock = veinDigClock + dt
            if veinDigClock < swingNeed then
                return
            end
            veinDigClock = 0

            local fired = veinSwing(pick, best.pos, root)

            
            if fired and digSession and digSession.gen == skipGen then
                digSession.lastDigAt = now
                if not digSession.digStartedAt then
                    digSession.digStartedAt = now
                    S._veinFarmStatus = "Digging vein"
                end
            end
        end)
    end

    
    FeatureManager:Register("AutoVein", {
        enabled = false,
        active = false,
        Initialize = function()
            S._veinFarmTarget = nil
        end,
        StartRuntime = function()
            if FM.setVeinFarm then
                FM.setVeinFarm(true)
            end
        end,
        StopRuntime = function()
            if FM.setVeinFarm then
                FM.setVeinFarm(false)
            end
        end,
        Cleanup = function()
            S._veinFarmTarget = nil
            S._veinLootPhase = false
        end,
        ResetRuntime = function()
            S._veinFarmTarget = nil
            S._veinLootPhase = false
            S._veinFarmStatus = nil
            S._slowMove = false
        end,
    })

    FeatureManager:Register("AutoDig", {
        enabled = false,
        active = false,
        Initialize = function() end,
        StartRuntime = function()
            S.fastDigActive = true
        end,
        StopRuntime = function()
            S.fastDigActive = false
        end,
        Cleanup = function() end,
        ResetRuntime = function()
            S.fastDigActive = false
        end,
    })

    function FM.scanNearbyMaterials(opts)
        opts = opts or {}
        local root = FM.getRoot and FM.getRoot() or nil
        if not root then return {} end
        local origin = root.Position
        local chunk = tonumber(opts.chunk) or 48
        local steps = tonumber(opts.steps) or 2 
        local res = 4
        local counts = {}
        local matEnum = {}

        local function addGrid(mats, corner)
            if not mats then return end
            for x = 1, #mats do
                local yx = mats[x]
                for y = 1, #yx do
                    local zy = yx[y]
                    for z = 1, #zy do
                        local mat = zy[z]
                        if mat and mat ~= Enum.Material.Air then
                            local n = tostring(mat):gsub("Enum.Material.", "")
                            if mat == Enum.Material.Water then
                                n = "Water"
                            end
                            if isBombMat(mat) then
                                local info = bombInfo(mat)
                                if info and info.matName then
                                    n = info.matName .. " [bomb]"
                                else
                                    n = n .. " [bomb]"
                                end
                            end
                            counts[n] = (counts[n] or 0) + 1
                            matEnum[n] = mat
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        for ix = -steps, steps do
            for iy = -steps, steps do
                for iz = -steps, steps do
                    local center = origin + Vector3.new(ix * chunk, iy * chunk, iz * chunk)
                    local half = chunk * 0.5
                    local region = Region3.new(
                        center - Vector3.new(half, half, half),
                        center + Vector3.new(half, half, half)
                    ):ExpandToGrid(res)
                    local ok, mats = pcall(function()
                        local m, _ = Terrain:ReadVoxels(region, res)
                        return m
                    end)
                    if ok and mats then
                        addGrid(mats, region.CFrame.Position - region.Size * 0.5)
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        local list = {}
        for name, c in pairs(counts) do
            list[#list + 1] = {
                name = name,
                count = c,
                material = matEnum[name],
            }
        end
        local COMMON = {
            Grass = true, LeafyGrass = true, Ground = true, Rock = true,
            Sand = true, Sandstone = true, Snow = true, Mud = true,
            Water = true, Slate = true, Basalt = true,
        }
        table.sort(list, function(a, b)
            local ar = not COMMON[a.name:gsub(" %[bomb%]", "")]
            local br = not COMMON[b.name:gsub(" %[bomb%]", "")]
            if ar ~= br then
                return ar 
            end
            return a.count > b.count
        end)
        S._lastMaterialScan = list
        return list
    end

    task.defer(function()
        if not Tabs or not Tabs.vein then return end
        if S._veinTabBuilt then return end
        S._veinTabBuilt = true
        local Box = Tabs.vein

        pcall(function()
            Box:Section({ Title = "Vein Scanner", TextSize = 17 })
        end)

        Box:Dropdown({
            Title = "Vein Type",
            Desc = "Aetherstone / Lava / Prismarite / Bomb",
            Values = PRESET_NAMES,
            Value = "Aetherstone Vein",
            Callback = function(opt)
                if type(opt) == "table" then
                    opt = opt[1] or opt.Value or opt.Name or "Aetherstone Vein"
                end
                FM.setVeinType(tostring(opt or "Aetherstone Vein"))
            end,
        })

        Box:Toggle({
            Title = "Enable Scanner",
            Desc = "Vein markers.",
            Value = false,
            Callback = function(v)
                FM.setVeinScanner(v == true)
            end,
        })

        Box:Toggle({
            Title = "Auto Farm Vein",
            Desc = "Vein farm.",
            Value = false,
            Callback = function(v)
                if FM.setVeinFarm then
                    FM.setVeinFarm(v == true)
                end
            end,
        })

        Box:Toggle({
            Title = "Skip Vein",
            Desc = "Skip stuck veins.",
            Value = true,
            Callback = function(v)
                S.skipVeinActive = v == true
                if not S.skipVeinActive then
                    
                end
            end,
        })

        Box:Slider({
            Title = "Radius",
            Desc = "Scan radius.",
            Value = { Min = 40, Max = 120, Default = 80 },
            Callback = function(v)
                scanRadius = tonumber(v) or 80
            end,
        })

    end)
end

task.defer(function()
    if not Tabs or not Tabs.fav then
        return
    end
    if S._favTabBuilt then
        return
    end
    S._favTabBuilt = true
    local Box = Tabs.fav

    pcall(function()
        Box:Section({ Title = "Favorite", TextSize = 17 })
    end)

    local autoFavToggle = Box:Toggle({
        Title = "Auto Favorite",
        Flag = "cfg_AutoFavorite",
        Desc = "Auto-favorite on pickup.",
        Value = false,
        Callback = function(v)
            if S._autoFavUiLock then
                return
            end
            if v == true and FM.MoneySystem and FM.MoneySystem.Enabled then
                S.autoFavActive = false
                if FM.setAutoFavorite then
                    FM.setAutoFavorite(false)
                end
                -- flip UI off once without re-entry
                task.defer(function()
                    if S._autoFavUiLock then return end
                    S._autoFavUiLock = true
                    pcall(function()
                        if autoFavToggle and autoFavToggle.SetValue and autoFavToggle.Value ~= false then
                            autoFavToggle:SetValue(false)
                        end
                    end)
                    task.defer(function()
                        S._autoFavUiLock = false
                    end)
                end)
                return
            end
            if FM.setAutoFavorite then
                FM.setAutoFavorite(v == true)
            else
                S.autoFavActive = v == true
            end
        end,
    })
    FM._autoFavToggle = autoFavToggle
    S._autoFavToggle = autoFavToggle

    Box:Slider({
        Title = "Favorite Delay",
        Flag = "cfg_AutoFavDelay",
        Desc = "Favorite delay.",
        Value = { Min = 0, Max = 5, Default = 0.5 },
        Callback = function(v)
            local n = tonumber(v) or 0.5
            if n < 0 then n = 0 end
            if n > 5 then n = 5 end
            S.autoFavDelay = n
        end,
    })

    Box:Button({
        Title = "Unfavorite All",
        Desc = "Clear favorites.",
        Callback = function()
            task.spawn(function()
                local n = FM.unfavoriteAll and FM.unfavoriteAll() or 0
                pcall(function()
                    WindUI:Notify({
                        Title = "Favorite",
                        Content = string.format("Cleared %d", n or 0),
                        Duration = 3,
                        Icon = "check",
                    })
                end)
            end)
        end,
    })
end)

task.defer(function()
    if not Tabs or not Tabs.suggestions then
        return
    end
    if S._suggestionsTabBuilt then
        return
    end
    S._suggestionsTabBuilt = true

    local Box = Tabs.suggestions
    local SUGGEST_WEBHOOK = "https://discord.com/api/webhooks/1537020971557462156/RyQHUulqc6Mrupt8BGOcGkK0zg7O1vlv_wrKRULhD1Fe_JBuauD8MB8SQdmBM5w9MVD8"
    local draftMessage = ""
    local lastSendAt = 0
    local SEND_COOLDOWN = 20

    local function httpPostJson(url, payload)
        local body
        pcall(function()
            body = game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONEncode(payload)
        end)
        if type(body) ~= "string" then
            return false, "encode failed"
        end

        local req = (typeof(request) == "function" and request)
            or (typeof(http_request) == "function" and http_request)
            or (syn and syn.request)
            or (fluxus and fluxus.request)
            or (http and http.request)

        if typeof(req) == "function" then
            local ok, res = pcall(function()
                return req({
                    Url = url,
                    Method = "POST",
                    Headers = {
                        ["Content-Type"] = "application/json",
                    },
                    Body = body,
                })
            end)
            if not ok then
                return false, res
            end
            local code = type(res) == "table" and (res.StatusCode or res.Status or res.status_code) or nil
            if code and tonumber(code) and tonumber(code) >= 200 and tonumber(code) < 300 then
                return true
            end
            if code == nil then
                return true
            end
            return false, "HTTP " .. tostring(code)
        end

        return false, "no request function"
    end

    local function sendSuggestion()
        local msg = tostring(draftMessage or ""):gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
        if msg == "" then
            WindUI:Notify({
                Title = "Suggestions",
                Content = "Please write a suggestion first",
                Duration = 3,
                Icon = "alert-circle",
            })
            return
        end
        if #msg > 1500 then
            WindUI:Notify({
                Title = "Suggestions",
                Content = "Message is too long (max 1500 characters)",
                Duration = 3,
                Icon = "alert-circle",
            })
            return
        end

        local now = os.clock()
        if now - lastSendAt < SEND_COOLDOWN then
            local left = math.ceil(SEND_COOLDOWN - (now - lastSendAt))
            WindUI:Notify({
                Title = "Suggestions",
                Content = string.format("Please wait %ds before sending again", left),
                Duration = 3,
                Icon = "alert-circle",
            })
            return
        end

        local username = tostring(LocalPlayer and LocalPlayer.Name or "Unknown")
        local userId = LocalPlayer and LocalPlayer.UserId or 0
        local line = string.format("%s, %s", username, msg)

        local payload = {
            username = "Mine A Mountain Suggestions",
            content = line,
            embeds = {
                {
                    title = "New Suggestion",
                    description = msg,
                    color = 5793266,
                    fields = {
                        {
                            name = "Username",
                            value = username,
                            inline = true,
                        },
                        {
                            name = "UserId",
                            value = tostring(userId),
                            inline = true,
                        },
                    },
                    footer = {
                        text = "Mine A Mountain • FayyMeng",
                    },
                    timestamp = os.date("!%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ"),
                },
            },
            allowed_mentions = { parse = {} },
        }

        task.spawn(function()
            local ok, err = httpPostJson(SUGGEST_WEBHOOK, payload)
            if ok then
                lastSendAt = os.clock()
                WindUI:Notify({
                    Title = "Suggestions",
                    Content = "Suggestion sent. Thank you!",
                    Duration = 3,
                    Icon = "check",
                })
            else
                WindUI:Notify({
                    Title = "Suggestions",
                    Content = "Failed to send: " .. tostring(err),
                    Duration = 4,
                    Icon = "alert-circle",
                })
            end
        end)
    end

    pcall(function()
        Box:Section({ Title = "Suggestions", TextSize = 17 })
    end)

    Box:Paragraph({
        Title = "Suggestions",
        Desc = "Send a suggestion",
    })

    Box:Input({
        Title = "Your Suggestion",
        Desc = "Send a suggestion",
        Value = "",
        Placeholder = "Describe a feature to add or fix...",
        Callback = function(text)
            if type(text) == "table" then
                text = text.Text or text.Value or text[1] or text.Input or ""
            end
            draftMessage = tostring(text or "")
        end,
    })

    Box:Button({
        Title = "Send Suggestion",
        Desc = "Send a suggestion",
        Callback = sendSuggestion,
    })
end)

-- Begin watching terrain/mountain readiness (does not start farms by itself)
task.defer(function()
    pcall(function()
        FM.waitForWorldReady(120)
        print("[FM] World/terrain ready")
    end)
end)

print("Script fully loaded")



-- ============================================================================
-- ISOLATED SYSTEMS GUI (Boulder / Money) — Fluent controllers only
-- ============================================================================
task.defer(function()
    if not Tabs then return end
    if S._isolatedGuiBuilt then return end
    S._isolatedGuiBuilt = true

    local Sys = FM.Systems
    if not Sys then return end

    local function applyPresetValue(cfg, opt)
        local map = Sys.VALUE_PRESETS or {}
        local num, key = 0, "None"
        if type(opt) == "table" then
            opt = opt[1] or opt.Value or opt.Name or opt.Text or "None"
        end
        opt = tostring(opt or "None")
        local lower = string.lower(opt:gsub("%s+", ""))
        if lower == "none" or lower == "off" or lower == "" then
            num, key = 0, "None"
        elseif map[opt] ~= nil then
            num, key = map[opt], opt
        else
            for k, v in pairs(map) do
                if string.lower(tostring(k)) == lower then
                    num, key = v, tostring(k)
                    break
                end
            end
            if key == "None" and FM.parseValue then
                local p = FM.parseValue(opt)
                if p then num, key = p, opt end
            end
        end
        cfg.PresetValue = key
        cfg.MinValue = tonumber(num) or 0
        -- Keep legacy S flags in sync only for debug; pickup systems use Config
        S._lastPresetMinValue = cfg.MinValue
    end
    local function applyPresetLuck(cfg, opt)
        local map = Sys.LUCK_PRESETS or {}
        if type(opt) == "table" then
            opt = opt[1] or opt.Value or opt.Name or opt.Text or "None"
        end
        opt = tostring(opt or "None")
        local lower = string.lower(opt:gsub("%s+", ""))
        local num, key = 0, "None"
        if lower == "none" or lower == "off" or lower == "" then
            num, key = 0, "None"
        elseif map[opt] ~= nil then
            num, key = map[opt], opt
        else
            for k, v in pairs(map) do
                if string.lower(tostring(k)) == lower then
                    num, key = v, tostring(k)
                    break
                end
            end
            local n = tonumber(opt:match("[%d%.]+"))
            if key == "None" and n then num, key = n, opt end
        end
        cfg.PresetLuck = key
        cfg.MinLuck = tonumber(num) or 0
        S._lastPresetMinLuck = cfg.MinLuck
    end
    local function applyTier(cfg, map)
        local normalized = {}
        if type(map) == "table" then
            for k, v in pairs(map) do
                if type(k) == "string" and (v == true or v == "true") then
                    normalized[k] = true
                elseif type(v) == "string" and v ~= "" then
                    normalized[v] = true
                end
            end
        elseif type(map) == "string" and map ~= "" then
            normalized[map] = true
        end
        if normalized["All"] or not next(normalized) then
            normalized = { All = true }
        end
        cfg.Tier = normalized
    end
    local function parseNum(text)
        if type(text) == "table" then
            text = text.Text or text.Value or text[1] or "0"
        end
        text = tostring(text or "")
        if FM.parseValue and text:match("[%a]") then
            local p = FM.parseValue(text)
            if p then return p end
        end
        return tonumber((text:match("[%d%.]+"))) or 0
    end

    -- ----- BOULDER TAB -----
    -- Order: Toggle → Preset → Tier → Input
    local Box = Tabs.boulder
    if Box then
        local bCfg = FM.BoulderSystem.Config
        local bpCfg = FM.BoulderPickupSystem.Config

        pcall(function() Box:Section({ Title = "Boulder", TextSize = 17 }) end)

        Box:Toggle({
            Title = "Auto Boulder",
            Flag = "cfg_AutoBoulder",
            Desc = "Boulder farm.",
            Value = false,
            Callback = function(v)
                if FM.BoulderSystem then
                    FM.BoulderSystem:SetEnabled(v == true)
                end
            end,
        })

        Box:Toggle({
            Title = "Auto Pickup Boulder",
            Flag = "cfg_AutoPickupBoulder",
            Desc = "Filtered crystal pickup.",
            Value = false,
            Callback = function(v)
                if FM.BoulderPickupSystem then
                    FM.BoulderPickupSystem:SetEnabled(v == true)
                    if v and FM.BoulderSystem and FM.BoulderSystem.Enabled and not FM.BoulderPickupSystem.Active then
                        FM.BoulderPickupSystem:StartRuntime()
                    end
                end
            end,
        })

        Box:Dropdown({
            Title = "Preset Value",
            Flag = "cfg_BoulderPresetValue",
            Desc = "Value preset.",
            Values = { "None", "100k", "500k", "1m", "5m", "10m", "50m", "100m", "1b" },
            Value = "None",
            Callback = function(opt)
                applyPresetValue(bCfg, opt)
                applyPresetValue(bpCfg, opt)
                if FM.BoulderSystem.ApplyConfig then FM.BoulderSystem:ApplyConfig() end
            end,
        })

        Box:Dropdown({
            Title = "Preset Luck",
            Flag = "cfg_BoulderPresetLuck",
            Desc = "Luck preset.",
            Values = { "None", "1%", "5%", "10%", "25%", "50%", "100%" },
            Value = "None",
            Callback = function(opt)
                applyPresetLuck(bCfg, opt)
                applyPresetLuck(bpCfg, opt)
            end,
        })

        Box:Dropdown({
            Title = "Tier",
            Flag = "cfg_BoulderTier",
            Desc = "Rarity filter.",
            Values = Sys.TIER_OPTIONS,
            Multi = true,
            Value = {},
            AllowNone = true,
            Callback = function(map)
                applyTier(bCfg, map)
                applyTier(bpCfg, map)
            end,
        })

        Box:Input({
            Title = "Minimum Value",
            Flag = "cfg_BoulderMinValue",
            Desc = "Minimum value.",
            Value = "0",
            Placeholder = "0 / 1m",
            Callback = function(t)
                local n = parseNum(t)
                bCfg.MinValue = n
                bpCfg.MinValue = n
            end,
        })

        Box:Input({
            Title = "Minimum Luck",
            Flag = "cfg_BoulderMinLuck",
            Desc = "Minimum luck.",
            Value = "0",
            Placeholder = "0",
            Callback = function(t)
                local n = parseNum(t)
                bCfg.MinLuck = n
                bpCfg.MinLuck = n
            end,
        })
    end

    -- ----- MONEY TAB -----
    -- Order: Toggle → Preset → Tier → Input
    local MBox = Tabs.money
    if MBox then
        local mCfg = FM.MoneySystem.Config
        local mpCfg = FM.MoneyPickupSystem.Config

        pcall(function() MBox:Section({ Title = "Money", TextSize = 17 }) end)

        MBox:Toggle({
            Title = "Auto Money",
            Flag = "cfg_AutoMoney",
            Desc = "Money farm.",
            Value = false,
            Callback = function(v)
                if FM.MoneySystem then
                    FM.MoneySystem:SetEnabled(v == true)
                end
            end,
        })

        MBox:Toggle({
            Title = "Auto Pickup Money",
            Flag = "cfg_AutoPickupMoney",
            Desc = "Filtered crystal pickup.",
            Value = false,
            Callback = function(v)
                if FM.MoneyPickupSystem then
                    FM.MoneyPickupSystem:SetEnabled(v == true)
                    if v and FM.MoneySystem and FM.MoneySystem.Enabled and not FM.MoneyPickupSystem.Active then
                        FM.MoneyPickupSystem:StartRuntime()
                    end
                end
            end,
        })

        MBox:Dropdown({
            Title = "Preset Value",
            Flag = "cfg_MoneyPresetValue",
            Desc = "Value preset.",
            Values = { "None", "100k", "500k", "1m", "5m", "10m", "50m", "100m", "1b" },
            Value = "None",
            Callback = function(opt)
                applyPresetValue(mCfg, opt)
                applyPresetValue(mpCfg, opt)
                if FM.MoneySystem.ApplyConfig then FM.MoneySystem:ApplyConfig() end
            end,
        })

        MBox:Dropdown({
            Title = "Preset Luck",
            Flag = "cfg_MoneyPresetLuck",
            Desc = "Luck preset.",
            Values = { "None", "1%", "5%", "10%", "25%", "50%", "100%" },
            Value = "None",
            Callback = function(opt)
                applyPresetLuck(mCfg, opt)
                applyPresetLuck(mpCfg, opt)
            end,
        })

        MBox:Dropdown({
            Title = "Tier",
            Flag = "cfg_MoneyTier",
            Desc = "Rarity filter.",
            Values = Sys.TIER_OPTIONS,
            Multi = true,
            Value = {},
            AllowNone = true,
            Callback = function(map)
                applyTier(mCfg, map)
                applyTier(mpCfg, map)
            end,
        })

        MBox:Input({
            Title = "Minimum Value",
            Flag = "cfg_MoneyMinValue",
            Desc = "Minimum value.",
            Value = "0",
            Placeholder = "0 / 1m",
            Callback = function(t)
                local n = parseNum(t)
                mCfg.MinValue = n
                mpCfg.MinValue = n
            end,
        })

        MBox:Input({
            Title = "Minimum Luck",
            Flag = "cfg_MoneyMinLuck",
            Desc = "Minimum luck.",
            Value = "0",
            Placeholder = "0",
            Callback = function(t)
                local n = parseNum(t)
                mCfg.MinLuck = n
                mpCfg.MinLuck = n
            end,
        })
    end

    print("[FayyMeng] Isolated Boulder/Money GUI ready")
end)




-- ============================================================================
-- CONFIG MANAGER FIX (Fluent-Renewed SaveManager API)
-- Official SaveManager has: Save, Load, RefreshConfigList, LoadAutoloadConfig,
-- BuildConfigSection (Create/Load/Overwrite/Refresh/Set autoload) — NO Delete.
-- We add Delete + deferred AutoLoad after all Options are registered.
-- ============================================================================
task.defer(function()
    local SM = FluentSaveManager
    local Lib = FluentLibrary
    if not SM or not Lib then
        warn("[Config] SaveManager/Library missing")
        return
    end

    -- Keep Options pointer in sync (all controls created by now)
    pcall(function()
        SM:SetLibrary(Lib)
        SM:SetFolder("FayyMeng/MineAMountain")
        SM:BuildFolderTree()
    end)

    local function notify(title, content)
        pcall(function()
            Lib:Notify({
                Title = title or "Config",
                Content = content or "",
                Duration = 5,
            })
        end)
        pcall(function()
            if WindUI and WindUI.Notify then
                WindUI:Notify({ Title = title or "Config", Content = content or "", Duration = 5 })
            end
        end)
    end

    local function safeName(name)
        if type(name) ~= "string" then
            return nil
        end
        name = name:gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
        if name == "" then
            return nil
        end
        -- strip path separators
        name = name:gsub("[/\\]", "_")
        if #name > 64 then
            name = name:sub(1, 64)
        end
        return name
    end

    local function configPath(name)
        return SM.Folder .. "/settings/" .. name .. ".json"
    end

    local function autoloadPath()
        return SM.Folder .. "/settings/autoload.txt"
    end

    local function refreshList()
        local list = {}
        pcall(function()
            list = SM:RefreshConfigList() or {}
        end)
        local opt = SM.Options and SM.Options.SaveManager_ConfigList
        if opt and opt.SetValues then
            pcall(function()
                opt:SetValues(list)
                if opt.SetValue then
                    opt:SetValue(nil)
                end
            end)
        end
        return list
    end

    function SM:Delete(name)
        name = safeName(name)
        if not name then
            return false, "No config selected"
        end
        local path = configPath(name)
        if typeof(isfile) == "function" and not isfile(path) then
            return false, "Config file not found"
        end
        local ok = false
        if typeof(delfile) == "function" then
            ok = pcall(delfile, path)
        elseif typeof(writefile) == "function" then
            -- fallback: overwrite empty then try delfile style
            ok = pcall(writefile, path, "")
            if typeof(delfile) == "function" then
                pcall(delfile, path)
            end
        end
        -- clear autoload if it pointed at this config
        pcall(function()
            local ap = autoloadPath()
            if typeof(isfile) == "function" and isfile(ap) then
                local cur = readfile(ap)
                if tostring(cur) == name then
                    if typeof(delfile) == "function" then
                        delfile(ap)
                    else
                        writefile(ap, "")
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
        if not ok and typeof(isfile) == "function" and isfile(path) then
            return false, "Failed to delete file"
        end
        return true
    end

    -- GUI: Delete Config button on Config tab (after official section)
    local configTab = Tabs and Tabs.config and Tabs.config._raw
    if configTab then
        pcall(function()
            local section
            if configTab.AddSection then
                section = configTab:AddSection("Delete Config")
            elseif configTab.CreateSection then
                section = configTab:CreateSection("Delete Config")
            end
            local addBtn = function(opts)
                if section and section.AddButton then
                    section:AddButton(opts)
                elseif configTab.CreateButton then
                    configTab:CreateButton(opts)
                elseif configTab.AddButton then
                    configTab:AddButton(opts)
                end
            end
            addBtn({
                Title = "Delete selected config",
                Description = "Permanently deletes the selected config file from disk",
                Callback = function()
                    local opt = SM.Options and SM.Options.SaveManager_ConfigList
                    local name = opt and opt.Value
                    name = safeName(tostring(name or ""))
                    if not name then
                        notify("Config", "No config selected")
                        return
                    end
                    local success, err = SM:Delete(name)
                    if not success then
                        notify("Config", "Delete failed: " .. tostring(err))
                        return
                    end
                    refreshList()
                    notify("Config", string.format("Deleted config %q", name))
                end,
            })
            addBtn({
                Title = "Clear autoload",
                Description = "Disable auto load on next script start",
                Callback = function()
                    pcall(function()
                        local ap = autoloadPath()
                        if typeof(isfile) == "function" and isfile(ap) then
                            if typeof(delfile) == "function" then
                                delfile(ap)
                            else
                                writefile(ap, "")
                            end
                        end
                    end)
                    notify("Config", "Autoload cleared")
                end,
            })
        end)
    end

    -- Deferred AutoLoad: GUI + Options are ready
    local function runAutoLoad()
        pcall(function()
            SM:SetLibrary(Lib)
        end)
        local ap = autoloadPath()
        if typeof(isfile) ~= "function" or not isfile(ap) then
            print("[Config] No autoload set")
            return
        end
        local name = safeName(tostring(readfile(ap) or ""))
        if not name then
            print("[Config] Autoload empty — skip")
            return
        end
        local path = configPath(name)
        if typeof(isfile) == "function" and not isfile(path) then
            notify("Config", string.format("Autoload %q not found — skipped", name))
            -- clear stale autoload pointer
            pcall(function()
                if typeof(delfile) == "function" then
                    delfile(ap)
                end
            end)
            return
        end
        local success, err = SM:Load(name)
        if not success then
            notify("Config", "Autoload failed: " .. tostring(err))
            print("[Config] Autoload failed:", err)
            return
        end
        notify("Config", string.format("Auto loaded %q", name))
        print("[Config] Auto loaded:", name)
    end

    -- Small defer so last task.defer GUI (isolated systems) registers Options first
    task.defer(function()
        task.defer(runAutoLoad)
    end)

    FM.ConfigManager = {
        SaveManager = SM,
        Delete = function(name) return SM:Delete(name) end,
        Refresh = refreshList,
        AutoLoad = runAutoLoad,
        Folder = function() return SM.Folder end,
    }
    print("[Config] ConfigManager ready — Delete + deferred AutoLoad")
end)



-- Boot: ensure FastDig gamepass flag once functions exist
task.defer(function()
    task.wait(0.5)
    pcall(function()
        -- FastDig spoof removed; dig speed via S.digSpeedMult only
        if S.digSpeedMult == nil then
            S.digSpeedMult = 0.28
        end
    end)
end)
